# Mahāniddesa

> **Nikāya:** Khuddakanikāya
> **Book:** Mahāniddesa
> **Edition:** Pali Text Society (PTS)

<!-- PTS Page 001 -->
<a id="page-001"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 001]*

<p align="center">NAMO TASSA BHAGAVATO ARAHATO SAMMĀSAMBUDDHASSA.</p>


Mahāniddeso. Part I AṬṬHAKAVAGGIKO.[^001-2] Paṭhamo Kāmasuttaniddeso.[^001-2]

Sn_IV,1.1(=766): Kāmaṃ kāmayamānassa tassa ce taṃ samijjhati, addhā pītimano[^001-3] hoti laddhā macco yad icchati.  || Nidd_I.1:1 ||

Kāmaṃ kāmayamānassā[^001-4] ti. Kāmā ti uddānato[^001-5] dve kāmā, vatthukāmā ca kilesakāmā ca. Katame vatthukāmā? Manāpikā rūpā, manāpikā saddā, manāpikā gandhā, manāpikā rasā, manāpikā phoṭṭhabbā; attharaṇā pāpuraṇā; dāsidāsā ajeḷakā kukkuṭasūkarā[^001-6] hatthigavāssavaḷavā[^001-7] khettaṃ vatthu[^001-8] hiraññaṃ suvaṇṇaṃ gāmanigamarājadhāniyo raṭṭhaṃ ca janapado ca koso ca koṭṭhāgārañ ca, yaṃ kiñci rajanīyaṃ vatthu[^001-9], vatthukāmā. Api ca atītā kāmā, anāgatā kāmā, paccuppannā kāmā; ajjhattā kāmā, bahiddhā kāmā, ajjhattabahiddhā kāmā; hīnā kāmā, majjhimā kāmā, paṇītā kāmā; āpāyikā kāmā, mānusikā[^001-10] kāmā, dibbā kāmā; paccupaṭṭhitā kāmā, nimmitā kāmā, paranimmitā kāmā; pariggahitā kāmā, apa-


<!-- PTS Page 002 -->
<a id="page-002"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 002]*

riggahitā kāmā; mamāyitā[^002-1] kāmā, amamāyitā[^002-2] kāmā; sabbe pi kāmāvacarā dhammā, sabbe pi rūpāvacarā dhammā, sabbe pi arūpāvacarā dhammā; taṇhāvatthukā taṇhārammaṇā kāmanīyatthena rajanīyatthena madanīyatthena kāmā; ime vuccanti vatthukāmā. Katame kilesakāmā? Chando kāmo, rāgo kāmo, chandarāgo kāmo, saṃkappo kāmo, rāgo kāmo, saṃkapparāgo kāmo; yo kāmesu kāmachando[^002-3] kāmarāgo kāmanandi[^002-4] kāmataṇhā kāmasneho kāmapariḷāho kāmamucchā kāmajjhosānaṃ kāmogho kāmayogo kāmupādānam kāmachandanīvaraṇaṃ -- *Addasam kāma te mūlaṃ, saṃkappā kāma jāyasi; na taṃ saṃkappayissāmi, evaṃ kāma na hohisī[^002-5] ti; ime vuccanti kilesakāmā. Kāmaṃ[^002-6] kāmayamānassā ti kāmaṃ[^002-6] kāmayamānassa icchamānassa[^002-7] sādiyamānassa patthayamānassa pihayamānassa abhijappamānassā[^002-8] ti, kāmaṃ kāmayamānassa. Tassa ce taṃ samijjhatī ti. Tassa ce ti tassa khattiyassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā vessassa vā suddassa vā gahaṭṭhassa vā pabbajitassa vā devassa vā manussassa vā. Tan ti vatthukāmā vuccanti manāpikā rūpā, manāpikā saddā, manāpikā gandhā, manāpikā rasā, manāpikā phoṭṭhabbā. Samijjhatī[^002-9] ti ijjhati[^002-10] samijjhati labhati paṭilabhati adhigacchati vindatī ti, tassa ce taṃ samijjhati. Addhā pītimano hotī ti. Addhā ti ekaṃsavacanaṃ nissaṃsayavacanaṃ[^002-11] nikkaṃkhavacanaṃ[^002-12] advejjhavacanaṃ adveḷhakavacanaṃ niyogavacanaṃ apaṇṇakavacanaṃ[^002-13]


<!-- PTS Page 003 -->
<a id="page-003"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 003]*

avatthāpanavacanam etaṃ, addhā ti. Pītī ti yā pañcakāmaguṇapaṭisaṃyuttā[^003-1] pīti, pāmujjaṃ[^003-2], āmodanā, pamodanā, hāso, pahāso, vitti, tuṭṭhi, odagyaṃ, attamanatā[^003-3] cittassa[^003-4], abhipūraṇatā[^003-5] cittassa. Mano ti yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho, tajjā[^003-6] manoviññāṇadhātu; ayaṃ vuccati mano. Ayaṃ mano imāya pītiyā sahagato hoti, sahajāto saṃsaṭṭho sampayutto ekuppādo ekanirodho ekavatthuko ekārammaṇo. Pītimano hotī ti pītimano hotī[^003-4] tuṭṭhamano haṭṭhamano pahaṭṭhamano attamano udaggamano muditamano[^003-7] pamoditamano[^003-4] hotī ti, addhā pītimano hoti. Laddhā macco yad icchatī ti. Laddhā ti laddhā[^003-8] labhitvā paṭilabhitvā adhigantvā[^003-9] vinditvā. Macco[^003-10] ti satto naro māṇavo[^003-11] poso puggalo jīvo jagū[^003-12] jantu indagū[^003-13] manujo. Yad icchatī ti yaṃ icchati yaṃ sādiyati yaṃ pattheti yaṃ piheti[^003-14] yaṃ abhijappati rūpaṃ vā saddaṃ vā gandhaṃ vā rasaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbaṃ vā ti, laddhā macco[^003-15] yad icchati. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Kāmaṃ kāmayamānassa tassa ce taṃ samijjhati, addhā pītimano hoti laddhā macco yad icchatī ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,1.2(=767): Tassa ce kāmayānassa[^003-16] chandajātassa jantuno te kāmā parihāyanti, sallaviddho va ruppati.  || Nidd_I.1:2 ||


<!-- PTS Page 004 -->
<a id="page-004"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 004]*

Tassa ce kāmayānassā ti. Tassa ce ti tassa khattiyassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā vessassa vā suddassa vā gahaṭṭhassa vā pabbajitassa vā devassa vā manussassa vā. Kāmayānassā ti kāmayamānassa[^004-1] icchamānassa sādiyamānassa patthayamānassa[^004-2] pihayamānassa[^004-3] abhijappamānassa. Athavā kāmataṇhāya yāyati niyyati[^004-4] vuyhati[^004-5] saṃhariyati[^004-6]. Yathā hatthiyānena vā [^004-7]assayānena vā[^004-7] goyānena vā ajayānena vā meṇḍakayānena[^004-8] vā oṭṭhayānena vā kharayānena vā yāyati niyyati[^004-4] vuyhati saṃhariyati[^004-9], evam eva[^004-10] kāmataṇhāya yāyati niyyati[^004-4] vuyhati saṃhariyatī[^004-11] ti, tassa ce kāmayānassa. Chandajātassa jantuno ti. Chando ti yo kāmesu kāmachando kāmarāgo kāmanandi kāmataṇhā kāmasneho[^004-12] kāmapariḷāho kāmamucchā kāmajjhosānaṃ kāmogho kāmayogo kāmupādānaṃ kāmachandanīvaraṇaṃ. Tassa so kāmachando jāto hoti, sañjāto nibbatto abhinibbatto[^004-13] pātubhūto. Jantuno ti sattassa narassa māṇavassa posassa puggalassa jīvassa jagussa[^004-14] jantussa indagussa manujassā ti, chandajātassa[^004-15] jantuno. Te kāmā parihāyantī ti te vā[^004-16] kāmā parihāyanti, so vā kāmehi parihāyati. Kathaṃ te kāmā parihāyanti? Tassa tiṭṭhantass'; eva, te bhoge rājāno vā haranti, corā vā haranti, aggi vā ḍahati, udakaṃ vā vahati, *appiyā vā dāyādā haranti, nihitaṭṭhānā[^004-17] vā vigacchati,[^004-18] duppayuttā[^004-19] vā kammantā bhañjanti,[^004-20] kule


<!-- PTS Page 005 -->
<a id="page-005"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 005]*

vā kulajjhāpako[^005-1] uppajjati yo te bhoge vikirati vidhameti vidhaṃseti, aniccatā yeva aṭṭhamī[^005-2]; evaṃ te kāmā hāyanti parihāyanti paridhaṃsenti paripatanti[^005-3] antaradhāyanti vippalujjanti. Kathaṃ so kāmehi parihāyati? Tiṭṭhant'; eva te[^005-4] bhogā[^005-5], so cavati marati antaradhāyati vippalujjati; evaṃ so kāmehi hāyati parihāyati paridhaṃseti paripatati antaradhāyati vippalujjati. *Corā haranti rājāno, aggi ḍahati, nassati, atho antena jahati sarīraṃ sapariggahaṃ. Etad aññāya medhāvī bhuñjetha ca dadetha ca, datvā ca bhutvā[^005-6] ca yathānubhāvaṃ anindito saggam upeti ṭhānan ti, te kāmā[^005-7] parihāyanti. Sallaviddho va ruppatī ti yathā ayomayena vā sallena viddho, aṭṭhimayena vā sallena, dantamayena vā sallena, visāṇamayena vā sallena, kaṭṭhamayena vā sallena viddho, ruppati kuppati ghaṭṭiyati[^005-8] pīḷiyati, byādhito domanassito hoti; evam eva vatthukāmānaṃ vipariṇāmaññathābhāvā uppajjanti sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsā[^005-9], so kāmasallena [^005-10]ca sokasallena ca[^005-10] viddho ruppati kuppati ghaṭṭiyati[^005-8] pīḷiyati, byādhito domanassito hotī ti, sallaviddho va ruppati. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Tassa ce kamāyānassa chandajātassa jantuno te kāmā parihāyanti, sallaviddho va ruppatī ti.

<!-- PTS Page 006 -->
<a id="page-006"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 006]*

Sn_IV,1.3(=768): Yo kāme parivajjeti sappass'[^006-1] eva padā siro, so 'maṃ visattikaṃ loke sato samativattati.  || Nidd_I.1:3 ||

Yo kāme parivajjetī ti. Yo ti yo yādiso yathāyutto yathāvihito yathāpakāro yanthānapatto yaṃdhammasamannāgato khattiyo vā brāhmaṇo vā vesso vā suddo vā gahaṭṭho vā pabbajito vā devo vā manusso vā. Kāme parivajjetī ti kāmā ti uddānato[^006-2] dve kāmā, vatthukāmā ca kilesakāmā cā . . . pe . . . ime vuccanti vatthukāmā . . . pe . . . ime vuccanti kilesakāmā. Kāme parivajjetī ti dvīhi kāraṇehi kāme parivajjeti, vikkhambhanato vā samucchedato vā. Kathaṃ vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti? Aṭṭhikaṃkalūpamā[^006-3] kāmā appassādatthenā ti passanto vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti. Maṃsapesūpamā[^006-4] kāmā bahusādhāraṇatthenā ti passanto vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti. Tiṇukkūpamā[^006-5] kāmā anudahanatthenā ti passanto vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti. Aṅgārakāsūpamā[^006-6] kāmā mahāpariḷāhatthenā ti passanto vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti. Supinakūpamā kāmā ittarapaccupaṭṭhānatthenā[^006-7] ti passanto vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti. Yācitakūpamā kāmā tāvakālikatthenā ti passanto vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti. Rukkhaphalūpamā kāmā sambhañjanaparibhañjanatthenā[^006-8] ti passanto, vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti. Asisūnūpamā kāmā adhikuṭṭanatthenā[^006-9] ti passanto vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti. Sattisūlūpamā[^006-10] kāmā vinivijjanatthenā ti passanto vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti. Sappasirūpamā kāmā


<!-- PTS Page 007 -->
<a id="page-007"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 007]*

sappaṭibhayatthenā ti passanto vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti. Aggikhandhūpamā[^007-1] kāmā mahābhitāpanatthenā[^007-2] ti passanto vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti. Buddhānussatiṃ[^007-3] bhāvento pi[^007-4] vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti, dhammānussatiṃ bhāvento pi . . . pe . . . saṅghānussatiṃ bhāvento pi, sīlānussatiṃ bhāvento pi, cāgānussatiṃ bhāvento pi, devatānussatiṃ bhāvento pi, ānāpāṇassatiṃ[^007-5] bhāvento pi, maraṇassatiṃ[^007-6] bhāvento pi, kāyagatāsatiṃ bhāvento pi [^007-7]vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti[^007-7], upasamānussatiṃ bhāvento pi vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti, paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ bhāvento pi vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti, dutiyaṃ[^007-8] jhānaṃ bhāvento pi, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ bhāvento pi, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ bhāvento pi, ākāsānañcāyatanasamāpattiṃ[^007-9] bhāvento pi, viññānañcāyatanasamāpattiṃ bhāvento pi, ākiñcaññāyatanasamāpattiṃ bhāvento pi, nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasamāpattiṃ bhāvento pi vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti. Evaṃ vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeti. Kathaṃ samucchedato kāme parivajjeti? Sotāpattimaggaṃ bhāvento pi apāyagamanīve kāme samucchedato parivajjeti, sakadāgāmimaggaṃ[^007-10] bhāvento pi oḷārike kāme samucchedato parivajjeti, anāgāmimaggaṃ bhāvento pi aṇusahagate kāme samucchedato parivajjeti, arahattamaggaṃ bhāvento pi sabbena sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ asesaṃ nissesaṃ samucchedato kāme parivajjeti. Evaṃ samucchedato kāme parivajjetī ti, yo kāme parivajjeti. Sappass'[^007-11] eva padā siro ti. Sappo vuccati ahi. Ken' atthena sappo? Saṃsappanto gacchatī ti sappo. Bhu-


<!-- PTS Page 008 -->
<a id="page-008"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 008]*

janto gacchatī ti bhujago; ureṇa gacchatī ti urago; pannasiro gacchatī ti pannago; sireṇa[^008-1] supatī[^008-2] ti siriṃsapo[^008-3]; vile[^008-4] sayatī ti vilāsayo[^008-4]; guhāyaṃ setī[^008-5] ti guhāsayo; dāṭhā[^008-6] tassa āvudho ti dāṭhāvudho[^008-6]; visaṃ tassa ghoran ti ghoraviso; jivhā tassa duvidhā[^008-7] ti dujivho[^008-8]; dvīhi jivhāhi rasaṃ sāyatī ti dvirasaññū[^008-9]. Yathā puriso jīvitukāmo amaritukāmo sukhakāmo dukkhapaṭikkūlo[^008-10] pādena sappasiraṃ vajjeyya[^008-11] vivajjeyya parivajjeyya abhinivajjeyya, evam eva sukhakāmo dukkhapaṭikkūlo[^008-10] kāme vajjeyya vivajjeyya parivajjeyya abhinivajjeyyā ti, sappass'; eva padā siro. So 'maṃ visattikaṃ loke sato samatirattatī ti. So ti yo kāme parivajjeti. Visattikā vuccati taṇhā, yo rāgo sārāgo anunayo anurodho nandi[^008-12] nandirāgo[^008-12], cittassa sārāgo, icchā mucchā[^008-13] ajjhosānaṃ gedho[^008-14] paligedho[^008-15] saṅgo[^008-16] paṅko ejā māyā janikā sañjananī sibbinī[^008-17] jālinī saritā visattikā suttaṃ visaṭā āyūhanī[^008-18] dutiyā paṇidhi bhavanetti vanaṃ vanatho santhavo sneho apekkhā paṭibandhā āsā āsiṃsanā[^008-19] āsiṃsitattaṃ[^008-20], rūpāsā saddāsā gandhāsā rasāsā[^008-21] phoṭṭhabbāsā lābhāsā dhanāsā puttāsā jīvitāsā, jappā pajappā abhijappā jappanā[^008-22] pajappanā jappitattaṃ, loluppā[^008-23] loluppāyanā loluppāyitattaṃ, mucchañcikatā[^008-24], sādhukamyatā, adhammarāgo visamalobho, nikanti nikāmanā patthanā pihanā sampatthanā, kāmataṇhā bhava-


<!-- PTS Page 009 -->
<a id="page-009"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 009]*

taṇhā vibhavatanhā rūpataṇhā arūpataṇhā nirodhataṇhā rūpataṇhā[^009-1], saddataṇhā gandhataṇhā rasataṇhā phoṭṭhabbataṇhā dhammataṇhā; ogho yogo gantho[^009-2] upādānaṃ āvaranaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ chadanaṃ bandhanaṃ[^009-3] upakkileso anusayo pariyuṭṭhānaṃ latā, vevicchaṃ dukkhamūlaṃ dukkhanidānaṃ[^009-4] dukkhappabhavo, mārapāso mārabaḷisaṃ māravisayo, taṇhānadi tanhājālaṃ taṇhāgaddulaṃ[^009-5] taṇhāsamuddo, abhijjhā, lobho akusalamūlaṃ. Visattikā ti. Ken'; atthena visattikā? Visaṭā[^009-6] ti visattikā, visālā ti visattikā, visaṭā[^009-7] ti visattikā[^009-8], visakkatī[^009-9] ti visattikā, visaṃharatī ti visattikā, visaṃvādikā[^009-10] ti visattikā, visamūlā ti visattikā, visaphalā ti visattikā, visaparibhogā ti visattikā; visālā vā pana sā [^009-11]rūpe taṇhā[^009-11], sadde gandhe rase phoṭṭhabbe kule gaṇe āvāse lābhe yase pasaṃsāya sukhe cīvare piṇḍapāte senāsane gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāre kāmadhātuyā rūpadhātuyā arūpadhātuyā kāmabhave rūpabhave arūpabhave saññābhave asaññābhave nevasaññānāsaññābhave ekavokārabhave catuvokārabhave pañcavokārabhave atīte anāgate paccuppanne diṭṭhasutamutaviññātabbesu dhammesu visaṭā vitthatā ti, visattikā. Loke ti apāyaloke manussaloke devaloke khandhaloke dhātuloke āyatanaloke. Sato ti catūhi kāraṇehi sato, kāye kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānaṃ bhāvento sato, vedanāsu citte[^009-12] dhammesu dhammānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānaṃ bhāvento sato. Aparehi pi catūhi kāraṇehi sato, asatiparivajjanāya sato, satikaraṇīyānañ ca dhammānaṃ katattā sato, satipaṭipakkhānaṃ[^009-13] dhammānaṃ hatattā sato, satinimittānaṃ dhammānaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 010 -->
<a id="page-010"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 010]*

appamuṭṭhattā[^010-1] sato. Aparehi pi catūhi kāraṇehi sato, satiyā samannāgatattā[^010-2] sato, satiyā vasitattā sato, satiyā pāguññatāya[^010-3] sato, satiyā apaccorohaṇatāya sato. Aparehi pi catūhi kāraṇehi sato, satattā[^010-4] sato, santattā sato, samitattā sato, santadhammasamannāgatattā sato. Būddhānussatiyā[^010-5] sato, dhammānussatiyā sato, saṃghānussatiyā sato, sīlānussatiyā sato, cāgānussatiyā sato, devatānussatiyā sato, ānāpāṇasatiyā sato, maraṇasatiyā[^010-6] sato, kāyagatāsatiyā sato, upasamānussatiyā sato. Yā sati anussati patissati sati[^010-7] saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā[^010-8] sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati[^010-9] satisambojjhaṅgo ekāyanamaggo, ayaṃ vuccati sati. Imāya satiyā upeto hoti, samupeto[^010-10] upāgato[^010-11] samupāgato[^010-12] upapanno samupapanno[^010-13] samannāgato[^010-14], so vuccati sato. So 'maṃ visattikaṃ loke sato samativattatī ti yā[^010-15] loke[^010-16] visattikā, imaṃ[^010-15] loke[^010-17] visattikaṃ sato tarati uttarati patarati samatikkamati vītivattatī ti, so 'maṃ visattikaṃ loke sato samativattatī ti. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Yo kāme parivajjeti sappass'; eva padā siro, So 'maṃ visattikaṃ loke sato samativattatī ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,1.4(=769): Khettaṃ vatthuṃ hiraññaṃ vā gavāssaṃ[^010-18] dāsaporisaṃ thiyo[^010-19] bandhū puthū[^010-20] kāme yo naro anugijjhati.  || Nidd_I.1:4 ||


<!-- PTS Page 011 -->
<a id="page-011"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 011]*

Khettaṃ vatthuṃ hiraññaṃ vā ti. Khettan ti sālikhettaṃ vīhikhettaṃ muggakhettaṃ māsakhettaṃ yavakhettaṃ godhūmakhettaṃ[^011-1] tilakhettaṃ. Vatthun ti gharavatthu[^011-2] koṭṭhavatthu[^011-3] purevatthu pacchāvatthu ārāmavatthu vihāravatthu. Hiraññan ti hiraññaṃ[^011-4] vuccati kahāpaṇo[^011-5] ti, khettaṃ vatthuṃ hiraññaṃ vā. Gavāssaṃ dāsaporisan ti. Gavan ti gāvo[^011-6] vuccanti. Assā ti pasukādayo[^011-7] vuccanti. Dāsā ti cattāro dāsā, antojātako dāso, dhanakkitako[^011-8] dāso, sāmaṃ vā dāsavisayaṃ[^011-9] upeti, akāmako[^011-10] vā dāsavisayaṃ[^011-9] upeti. *Āmāya[^011-11] dāsā pi bhavanti h'; eke, dhanena kītā pi bhavanti dāsā, sāmañ ca eke upayanti dāsaṃ, bhayā[^011-12] panuṇṇā pi bhavanti dāsā. Porisan[^011-13] ti tayo purisā, bhaṭakā[^011-14] kammakarā upajīvino ti, gavāssaṃ dāsaporisaṃ. Thiyo bandhū puthū kāme ti. Thiyo ti itthipariggaho vuccati. Bandhū ti cattāro bandhū, ñātibandhavā pi bandhu, gottabandhavā pi bandhu. mantabandhavā pi bandhu, sippabandhavā pi banhdu, Puthū kāme ti bahū kāme, ete puthū kāmā manāpikā rūpā . . . pe . . . manāpikā phoṭṭhabbā ti, thiyo bandhū puthū kāme. Yo naro anugijjhatī ti. Yo ti yo yādiso yathāyutto yathāvihito yathāpakāro yanthānapatto yaṃdhammasamannāgato[^011-15] khattiyo vā brāhmaṇo vā vesso vā suddo vā


<!-- PTS Page 012 -->
<a id="page-012"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 012]*

gahaṭṭho vā pabbajito vā devo vā manusso vā. Naro ti satto naro māṇavo poso puggalo jīvo jagū jantu indagū manujo. Anugijjhatī ti kilesakāmena vatthukāmesu gijjhati anugijjhati paligijjhati palibajjhatī ti, yo[^012-1] naro anugijjhati. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Khettaṃ vatthuṃ hiraññaṃ vā gavāssaṃ dāsaporisaṃ thiyo bandhū puthū kāme yo naro anugijjhati.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,1.5(=770): Abalā naṃ balīyanti, maddante naṃ parissayā, tato naṃ dukkham anveti, nāvaṃ bhinnam ivodakaṃ.  || Nidd_I.1:5 ||

Abalā naṃ balīyantī ti. Abalā ti abalā kilesā dubbalā appabalā appathāmakā hīnā nihīnā parihīnā[^012-2] omakā lāmakā jatukkā[^012-3] parittā. Te kilesā taṃ puggalaṃ sahanti parisahanti abhibhavanti ajjhottharanti pariyādiyanti maddantī ti, evam pi abalā naṃ balīyanti. [^012-4]Athavā abalaṃ[^012-4] puggalaṃ dubbalaṃ appabalaṃ appathāmakaṃ hīnaṃ nihīnaṃ parihīnaṃ[^012-2] omakaṃ lāmakaṃ jatukkaṃ parittaṃ, yassa n'; atthi saddhābalaṃ viriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ paññābalaṃ hiribalaṃ ottappabalaṃ, te kilesā taṃ puggalaṃ sahanti parisahanti abhibhavanti ajjhottharanti[^012-5] pariyādiyanti maddantī ti, evam pi abalā naṃ balīyanti. Maddante naṃ parissayā ti. [^012-6]Parissayā ti[^012-6] dve parissayā, pākaṭaparissayā ca paṭicchannaparissayā ca. Katame pākaṭaparissayā? Sīhā byagghā dīpi-acchataracchā[^012-7] kokā[^012-8] gomahisā[^012-9] hatthī[^012-10] ahi-vicchikā satapadī


<!-- PTS Page 013 -->
<a id="page-013"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 013]*

corā vā assu, māṇavā vā[^013-1] katakammā[^013-2] vā akatakammā vā, cakkhurogo sotarogo ghānarogo jivhārogo kāyarogo sīsarogo kaṇṇarogo mukharogo dantarogo kāso sāso pināso ḍāho[^013-3] jaro kucchirogo mucchā pakkhandikā sūlā[^013-4] visūcikā[^013-5] kuṭṭhaṃ gaṇḍo kilāso soso apamāro daddu kaṇḍu kacchu rakhasā vitacchikā lohitapittaṃ[^013-6] madhumeho aṃsā piḷakā bhagandalā, pittasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā, semhasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā, vātasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā, sannipātikā ābādhā, utupariṇāmajā ābādhā, visamaparihārajā ābādhā, opakkamikā ābādhā, kammavipākajā ābādhā, sītaṃ uṇhaṃ jighacchā pipāsā uccāro passāvo[^013-7] ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasiriṃsapasamphassā[^013-8] iti vā[^013-2], ime vuccanti pākaṭaparissayā. Katame paṭicchannaparissayā? Kāyaduccaritaṃ vacīduccaritaṃ manoduccaritaṃ, kāmachandanīvaraṇaṃ byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ thīnamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ uddhaccakukkuccanīvaraṇaṃ vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ, rāgo doso moho kodho upanāho makkho paḷāso issā macchariyaṃ māyā sāṭheyyaṃ thambho sārambho māno atimāno mado pamādo, sabbe kilesā, sabbe duccaritā, sabbe darathā, sabbe pariḷāhā, sabbe santāpā, sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārā[^013-9]; ime vuccanti paṭicchannaparissayā. Parissayā ti ken'; atthena parissayā? Parisahantī[^013-10] ti parissayā, parihānāya saṃvattantī ti parissayā, tatr'; āsayā ti, parissayā. Kathaṃ parisahantī[^013-10] ti parissayā? Te parissayā taṃ puggaḷaṃ sahanti parisahanti abhibhavanti ajjhottharanti pariyādiyanti maddanti; evaṃ parisahantī ti, parissayā. Kathaṃ parihānāya saṃvattantī ti parissayā? [^013-11]Te parissayā[^013-11] kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ parihānāya[^013-12] antarāyāya saṃvattanti. Katamesaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ? Sammāpaṭipadāya anulomapaṭipadāya apaccanīkapaṭipa-


<!-- PTS Page 014 -->
<a id="page-014"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 014]*

dāya anvatthapaṭipadāya dhammānudhammapaṭipadāya, sīlesu paripūrikāritāya[^014-1], indriyesu guttadvāratāya, bhojane mattaññutāya, jāgariyānuyogassa, satisampajaññassa, catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanānuyogassa, catunnaṃ sammappadhānānaṃ bhāvanānuyogassa, catunnaṃ iddhippādānaṃ bhāvanānuyogassa, pañcannaṃ indriyānaṃ bhāvanānuyogassa, pañcannaṃ balānaṃ bhāvanānuyogassa, sattannaṃ bojjhaṅgānaṃ bhāvanānuyogassa, ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa bhāvanānuyogassa. Imesaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ parihānāya antarāyāya saṃvattanti; evaṃ parihānāya samvattantī ti, parissayā. Kathaṃ tatr'; āsayā ti parissayā? Tatth'; ete pāpakā akusalā dhammā uppajjanti attabhāvasannissayā; yathā bile bilāsayā[^014-2] pāṇā sayanti, dake dakāsayā[^014-3] pāṇā sayanti, vane vanāsayā pāṇā sayanti, rukkhe rukkhāsayā pāṇā sayanti, evam eva tatth'; ete pāpakā akusalā dhammā uppajjanti attabhāvasannissayā[^014-4], evam pi tatr'; āsayā ti, parissayā. Vuttaṃ h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā: *Sāntevāsiko[^014-5] bhikkhave bhikkhu sācariyako[^014-6] dukkhaṃ na phāsu viharati. Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu sāntevāsiko[^014-7] sācariyako[^014-8] dukkhaṃ na phāsu viharati? Idha bhikkhave bhikkhuno cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā uppajjanti[^014-9] pāpakā akusalā dhammā sarasaṃkappā[^014-10] saṃyojanīyā, ty assa anto vasanti anvāvasanti[^014-11] pāpakā akusalā dhammā ti, tasmā sāntevasiko ti vuccati; te naṃ samudācaranti [^014-12]samudācaranti naṃ[^014-12] [^014-13]pāpakā akusalā dhammā ti, tasmā sācariyako[^014-14] ti vuccati. Puna


<!-- PTS Page 015 -->
<a id="page-015"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 015]*

c'; aparaṃ bhikkhave bhikkhuno sotena saddaṃ sutvā, ghānena gandhaṃ ghāyitvā, jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā, kāyena phoṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā[^015-1], manasā dhammaṃ viññāya uppajjanti [^015-2]pāpakā akusalā dhammā sarasaṃkappā[^015-3] saṃyojanīyā, ty assa anto vasanti anvāvasanti pāpakā akusalā dhammā ti, tasmā sāntevāsiko[^015-4] ti vuccati; te naṃ samudācaranti [^015-5]samudācaranti naṃ[^015-5] pāpakā akusalā dhammā ti, tasmā sācariyako[^015-6] ti vuccati. Evaṃ kho bhikkhave bhikkhu sāntevāsiko sācariyako dukkhaṃ na phāsu [^015-7]viharatī ti, evam[^015-7] pi tatr'; āsayā ti, parissayā. Vuttaṃ h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā: *Tayo me bhikkhave antarāmalā antarā-amittā antarāsapattā antarāvadhakā antarāpaccatthikā[^015-8]. Katame tayo? Lobho bhikkhave antarāmalaṃ[^015-9] antarā-amitto antarāsapatto antarāvadhako antarāpaccatthiko. [^015-10]Doso bhikkhave antarāmalaṃ[^015-9] antarā-amitto antarāsapatto antarāvadhako antarāpaccatthiko[^015-10]. Moho bhikkhave antarāmalaṃ[^015-9] antarā-amitto antarāsapatto antarāvadhako antarāpaccatthiko. Ime kho bhikkhave tayo antarāmalā antarā-amittā antarāsapattā antarāvadhakā antarāpaccatthikā ti. Anatthajanano lobho, lobho cittappakopano, bhayaṃ antarato jātaṃ taṃ jano nāvabujjhati. Luddho atthaṃ na jānāti, luddho dhammaṃ na passati; andhaṃ[^015-11] tamaṃ tadā hoti, yaṃ lobho sahate naraṃ. Anatthajanano doso, doso cittappakopano, bhayaṃ antarato jātaṃ taṃ jano nāvabujjhati. Kuddho atthaṃ na jānāti, kuddho dhammaṃ na passati; andhaṃ tamaṃ tadā hoti, yaṃ kodho sahate naraṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 016 -->
<a id="page-016"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 016]*

Anatthajanano moho, moho cittappakopano, bhayaṃ antarato jātaṃ taṃ jano nāvabujjhati. Mūḷho atthaṃ na jānāti, mūḷho dhammaṃ na passati; andhaṃ tamaṃ tadā hoti, yam moho sahate naraṃ, evam pi tatr'; āsayā ti parissayā ti[^016-1]. Vuttaṃ [^016-2]h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā: *Tayo kho mahārāja purisassa dhammā ajjhattam uppajjamānā uppajjanti ahitāya dukkhāya aphāsuvihārāya. Katame tayo? Lobho kho[^016-3] mahārāja purisassa dhammo ajjhattaṃ uppajjamāno uppajjati ahitāya dukkhāya aphāsuvihārāya. Doso kho mahārāja [^016-4]purisassa dhammo ajjhattaṃ uppajjamāno uppajjati ahitāya dukkhāya aphāsuvihārāya[^016-4]. Moho kho mahārāja purisassa dhammo ajjhattaṃ uppajjamāno uppajjati ahitāya dukkhāya aphāsuvihārāya. Ime kho mahārāja tayo purisassa dhammā ajjhattaṃ uppajjamānā uppajjanti ahitāya dukkhāya aphāsuvihārāyā ti[^016-3]. Lobho doso ca moho ca purisaṃ pāpacetasaṃ hiṃsanti attasambhūtā tacasāraṃ va samphalan[^016-5] ti, evam pi tatr'; āsayā ti parissayā. Vuttaṃ pi c'; etaṃ[^016-6] Bhagavatā: *Rāgo ca doso ca itonidānā arati rati lomahaṃso ito jāto[^016-7] ito samuṭṭhāya manovitakkā kumārakā dhaṃkam[^016-8] iv'; ossajjantī[^016-9] ti, evam pi tatr'; āsayā ti, parissayā. Maddante naṃ parissayā ti te parissayā taṃ puggalaṃ sahanti parisahanti abhibhavanti ajjhottharanti pariyādiyanti maddantī ti, maddante naṃ parissayā.


<!-- PTS Page 017 -->
<a id="page-017"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 017]*

Tato naṃ dukkham anvetī ti. [^017-1]Tato ti[^017-1] tato tato parissayato taṃ puggalaṃ dukkhaṃ anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hoti; jātidukkhaṃ anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hoti, jarādukkhaṃ anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hoti, byādhidukkhaṃ anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hoti, maraṇadukkhaṃ anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hoti, sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsadukkhaṃ anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hoti, nerayikaṃ[^017-2] dukkhaṃ, tiracchānayonikaṃ dukkhaṃ, petavisayikaṃ[^017-3] dukkhaṃ anveti anugacchati anvāyikam hoti, mānusakaṃ dukkhaṃ, gabbhe okkantimūlakaṃ[^017-4] dukkhaṃ, gabbhe ṭhitimūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ, gabbhā[^017-5] vuṭṭhānamūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ, jātass' upanibandhikaṃ[^017-6] dukkhaṃ, jātassa parādheyyakaṃ dukkhaṃ, attupakkamadukkhaṃ, parupakkamadukkhaṃ[^017-7] anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hoti; dukkhadukkhaṃ anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hoti, saṃkhāradukkham vipariṇāmadukkhaṃ, cakkhurogo sotarogo ghānarogo jivhārogo kāyarogo sīsarogo kaṇṇarogo mukharogo dantarogo kāso sāso pināso ḍāho[^017-8] jaro kucchirogo mucchā pakkhandikā sūlā visūcikā kuṭṭhaṃ gaṇḍo kilāso soso apamāro daddu kaṇḍu kacchu rakhasā vitacchikā lohitapittaṃ madhumeho aṃsā piḷakā bhagandalā, pittasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā, semhasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā, vātasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā, sannipātikā ābādhā, utupariṇāmajā ābādhā, visamaparihārajā ābādhā, opakkamikā ābādhā, kammavipākajā ābādhā, sitaṃ uṇhaṃ jighacchā pipāsā uccāro passāvo ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasiriṃsapasamphassadukkhaṃ, mātumaraṇadukkhaṃ[^017-9] pitumaraṇadukkhaṃ bhātumaraṇadukkhaṃ bhaginīmaraṇadukkhaṃ puttamaraṇadukkhaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 018 -->
<a id="page-018"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 018]*

dhītumaraṇadukkhaṃ ñātibyasanadukkhaṃ bhogabyasanadukkhaṃ rogabyasanadukkhaṃ sīlabyasanadukkhaṃ diṭṭhibyasanadukkhaṃ anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hotī ti, tato naṃ dukkhaṃ anveti. Nāvaṃ bhinnaṃ ivodakan ti yathā bhinnaṃ nāvaṃ[^018-1] tato tato udakaṃ anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hoti, purato pi udakaṃ anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hoti, pacchato pi, heṭṭhato pi, passato pi udakaṃ anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hoti; evam eva tato tato parissayato taṃ puggalaṃ dukkhaṃ anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hoti, jātidukkhaṃ[^018-2] anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hoti . . . pe . . . diṭṭhibyasanadukkhaṃ anveti anugacchati anvāyikaṃ hotī ti, nāvaṃ bhinnam ivodakaṃ. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Abalā naṃ balīyanti, maddante naṃ parissayā, tato naṃ dukkham anveti, nāvaṃ bhinnam ivodakan ti.

Sn_IV,1.6(=771): Tasmā jantu sadā sato kāmāni parivajjaye, te pahāya tare oghaṃ, nāvaṃ sitvā va pāragū.  || Nidd_I.1:6 ||

Tasmā jantu sadā sato ti. Tasmā ti tasmā taṃkāraṇā taṃhetu tappaccayā taṃnidānā[^018-3] etaṃ ādīnavaṃ sampassamāno kāmesū ti, tasmā. Jantū ti satto naro māṇavo poso puggalo jīvo jagū jantu indagū manujo. Sadā ti sadā sabbadā sabbakālaṃ niccakālaṃ dhuvakālaṃ satataṃ samitaṃ abbocchiṇṇaṃ[^018-4] poṅkhānupoṅkhaṃ[^018-5], udakummikājātaṃ[^018-6] avīci-santatisahitaṃ[^018-7] phusitaṃ[^018-8] purebhattaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 019 -->
<a id="page-019"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 019]*

pacchābhattaṃ purimayāmaṃ majjhimayāmaṃ pacchimayāmaṃ kāḷe juṇhe vasse hemante gimhe, purime vayokhandhe, majjhime vayokhandhe, pacchime vayokhandhe. Sato ti catūhi kāraṇehi sato, kāye kāyānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānaṃ bhāvento sato, vedanāsu citte dhammesu dhammānupassanāsatipaṭṭhānaṃ bhāvento sato; aparehi[^019-1] pi catūhi kāraṇehi sato . . . pe . . . so vuccati sato ti, tasmā jantu sadā sato. Kāmāni parivajjaye ti. Kāmānī[^019-2] ti uddānato dve kāmā, vātthukāmā ca kilesakāmā ca . . . pe . . . ime vuccanti vatthukāmā . . . pe . . . ime vuccanti kilesakāmā. Kāmāni parivajjaye ti dvīhi kāraṇehi kāme parivajjeyya, vikkhambhanato vā samucchedato vā. Kathaṃ vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeyya? Aṭṭhikaṃkalūpamā kāmā appassādatthenā ti passanto vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeyya; maṃsapesūpamā kāmā bahusādhāraṇatthenā ti passanto vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeyya; tiṇukkūpamā kāmā anudahanatthenā ti passanto vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeyya . . . pe . . . nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasamāpattiṃ bhāvento pi[^019-3] vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeyya. Evaṃ vikkhambhanato kāme parivajjeyya . . . pe . . . evaṃ samucchedato kāme parivajjeyyā ti, kāmāni parivajjaye. Te pahāya tare oghan ti. Te ti vatthukāme parijānitvā, kilesakāme pahāya pajahitvā vinoditvā byantikaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamitvā[^019-4], kāmachandanīvaraṇaṃ pahāya pajahitvā vinoditvā byantikaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamitvā, byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ[^019-5] thīnamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ uddhaccakukkuccanīvaraṇaṃ vicikicchānīvaranaṃ pahāya pajahitvā vinoditvā byantikaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamitvā, kāmoghaṃ bhavoghaṃ diṭṭhoghaṃ avijjoghaṃ tareyya uttareyya patareyya samatikkameyya vītivatteyyā ti, te pahāya[^019-6] tare oghaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 020 -->
<a id="page-020"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 020]*

Nāvaṃ sitvā[^020-1] va pāragū ti yathā[^020-2] garukaṃ nāvaṃ bhārikaṃ udakaṃ sitvā osiñcitvā chaḍḍetvā lahukāya nāvāya khippaṃ lahuṃ appakasiren'; eva pāraṃ gaccheyya; evam eva vatthukāme parijānitvā, kilesakāme pahāya pajahitvā vinoditvā byantikaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamitvā, kāmachandanīvaraṇaṃ byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ thīnamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ uddhaccakukkuccanīvaraṇaṃ vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ pahāya pajahitvā vinoditvā byantikaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamitvā, khippaṃ lahuṃ appakasiren'; eva pāraṃ gaccheyya. Pāraṃ vuccati amataṃ nibbānaṃ; yo so sabbasaṃkhārasamatho sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhakkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānaṃ; pāraṃ gaccheyya, pāraṃ adhigaccheyya, pāraṃ phuseyya, pāraṃ sacchikareyya pāragū ti; yo pi pāraṃ gantukāmo so pi pāragū, yo pi pāraṃ gacchati so pi pāragū, yo pi pāraṃ gato so pi pāragū. Vuttaṃ[^020-3] h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā: *Tiṇṇo pāragato[^020-4] thale tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇo ti. [^020-2]Brāhmaṇo ti[^020-2] kho bhikkhave arahato etaṃ adhivacanaṃ; so abhiññāpāragū pariññāpāragū pahānapāragū bhāvanāpāragū sacchikiriyāpāragū samāpattipāragū; abhiññāpāragū sabbadhammānaṃ, pariññāpāragū sabbadukkhānaṃ, pahānapāragū sabbakilesānaṃ, bhāvanāpāragū catunnaṃ ariyamaggānaṃ, sacchikiriyāpāragū nirodhassa, samāpattipāragū sabbasamāpattīnaṃ; so vasippatto pāramippatto ariyasmiṃ sīlasmiṃ, vasippatto pāramippatto ariyasmiṃ samādhismiṃ, vasippatto pāramippatto ariyāya paññāya, vasippatto pāramippatto ariyāya vimuttiyā; so pāragato[^020-4] pārappatto, antagato antappatto, koṭigato koṭippatto, pariyantagato pariyantappatto, vosānagato vosānappatto, tāṇagato tāṇappatto, leṇagato leṇappatto, saraṇagato saraṇappatto, abhayagato abhayappatto, accutagato accutappatto, amatagato amatappatto, nibbānagato nibbānappatto; so vuṭṭhavāso ciṇṇacaraṇo gataddho gatadiso gatakoṭiko pālitabrahmacariyo uttamadiṭṭhippatto bhāvitamaggo


<!-- PTS Page 021 -->
<a id="page-021"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 021]*

pahīnakileso paṭividdhākuppo sacchikatanirodhi; dukkhaṃ tassa pariññātaṃ, samudayo pahīno, maggo bhāvito, nirodho sacchikato, abhiññeyyam abhiññātam, pariññeyyaṃ pariññātaṃ, pahātabbaṃ pahīnaṃ, bhāvetabbaṃ bhāvitaṃ, sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikataṃ; so ukkhittapaligho saṃkiṇṇaparikho[^021-1] abbūḷhesiko niraggaḷo ariyo pannaddhajo pannabhāro visaṃyutto pañcaṅgavippahīno chalaṅgasamannāgato ekārakkho caturāpasseno panuṇṇapaccekasacco[^021-2] samavayasaṭṭhesano[^021-3] anāvilasaṃkappo passaddhakāyasaṃkhāro suvimuttacitto[^021-4] suvimuttapañño kevalī vusitavā uttamapuriso paramapuriso paramapattippatto. So n'; eva ācināti, na apacināti, apacinitvā ṭhito; n'; eva pajahati, na upādiyati, pajahitvā ṭhito; [^021-5]n'; eva visineti[^021-5], na ussineti, visinetvā[^021-6] ṭhito; n'; eva vidhupeti, na sandhupeti[^021-7], vidhupetvā ṭhito; asekkhena[^021-8] sīlakkhandhena samannāgatattā ṭhito, asekkhena samādhikkhandhena, asekkhena paññakkhandhena, asekkhena vimuttikkhandhena, asekkhena vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhena samannāgatattā ṭhito; saccaṃ paṭipādayitvā[^021-9] ṭhito, ejaṃ[^021-10] samatikkamitvā ṭhito, kilesaggiṃ pariyādayitvā[^021-11] ṭhito, aparigamanatāya ṭhito, kataṃ[^021-12] samādāya ṭhito, muttipaṭisevanatāya ṭhito, mettāya pārisuddhiyā ṭhito, karuṇāya muditāya upekkhāya pārisuddhiyā ṭhito, accantapārisuddhiyā[^021-13] ṭhito, akammaññatāya[^021-14] pārisuddhiyā ṭhito, vimuttattā ṭhito, santussitattā ṭhito, khandhapariyante ṭhito, dhātupariyante ṭhito, āyatanapariyante ṭhito, gatipariyante ṭhito, upapattipariyante ṭhito, paṭisandhipariyante ṭhito, bhavapariyante ṭhito, saṃsārapariyante


<!-- PTS Page 022 -->
<a id="page-022"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 022]*

ṭhito, vaṭṭapariyante ṭhito, antimabhave[^022-1] ṭhito, antimasamussaye[^022-2] ṭhito, antimadehadharo arahā. *Tassāyaṃ[^022-3] pacchimako bhavo, carimo 'yaṃ samussayo, jātimaraṇasaṃsāro n'; atthi tassa punabbhavo ti[^022-4], nāvaṃ sitvā va pāragū[^022-5]. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Tasmā jantu sadā sato kāmāni parivajjaye, te pahāya tare oghaṃ, nāvaṃ sitvā va pāragū ti[^022-6].

PAṬHAMO[^022-7] KĀMASUTTANIDDESO NIṬṬHITO.


<!-- PTS Page 023 -->
<a id="page-023"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 023]*

II. DUTIYO[^023-1] GUHAṬṬHAKASUTTANIDDESO[^023-2].

Sn_IV,2.1(=772): Satto guhāyaṃ bahunābhichanno tiṭṭham naro mohanasmiṃ[^023-3] pagālho[^023-4], dūre vivekā hi tathāvidho so, kāmā hi loke na hi suppahāyā.  || Nidd_I.2:1 ||

Satto guhāyaṃ bahunābhichanno[^023-5] ti. Satto ti hi kho vuttaṃ. Api ca guhā tāva vattabbā. Guhā vuccati kāyo: kāyo ti vā guhā ti vā deho ti vā sandeho ti vā nāvā ti vā ratho ti vā dhajo ti vā vammiko ti vā niddan[^023-6] ti vā nagaran ti vā kuṭī ti vā gaṇḍo ti vā kummo[^023-7] ti vā [^023-8]nāgo ti vā[^023-8] kāyass'; etaṃ adhivacanaṃ. Satto guhāyan ti guhāyaṃ satto visatto āsatto[^023-9] laggo laggito[^023-10] palibuddho: yathā bhittikhīle vā nāgadante vā bhaṇḍaṃ[^023-11] sattaṃ visattaṃ āsattaṃ laggaṃ laggitaṃ palibuddhaṃ; evaṃ eva guhāyaṃ satto visatto āsatto laggo laggito palibuddho. Vuttaṃ h'[^023-12] etaṃ Bhavagatā: *Rūpe kho Rādha[^023-13] yo chando, yo rāgo, yā nandi, yā taṇhā, ye upāyupādānā


<!-- PTS Page 024 -->
<a id="page-024"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 024]*

cetaso adhiṭṭhānābhinivesānusayā, [^024-1]tatra satto[^024-1],[^024-2] tatra visatto[^024-2], tasmā[^024-3] satto ti vuccati. [^024-4]Satto ti lagganādhivacanan ti, satto guhāyaṃ. Bahunābhichanno[^024-5] ti bahukehi kilesehi channo[^024-6], rāgena channo, dosena channo, mohena channo, kodhena[^024-7] channo, upanāhena channo, makkhena channo, paḷāsena channo, issāya channo, macchariyena channo, māyāya channo, sāṭheyyena[^024-8] channo, thambhena channo, sārambhena channo, mānena channo, atimānena channo, madena channo, pamādena channo, sabbakilesehi sabbaduccaritehi sabbadarathehi sabbapariḷāhehi sabbasantāpehi sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārehi [^024-9]channo ucchanno[^024-9] āvuṭo nivuṭo ovuṭo[^024-10] pihito paṭicchanno paṭikujjito ti, satto guhāyaṃ bahunābhichanno. Tiṭṭhaṃ naro mohanasmiṃ pagāḷho ti. [^024-11]Tiṭṭhaṃ naro ti[^024-11] tiṭṭhanto naro ratto rāgavaseṇa tiṭṭhati, duṭṭho dosavasena tiṭṭhati, mūḷho mohavasena tiṭṭhati, vinibandho mānavasena tiṭṭhati, parāmaṭṭho diṭṭhivasena tiṭṭhati, vikkhepagato uddhaccavasena tiṭṭhati, aniṭṭhaṅgato vicikicchāvasena tiṭṭhati, thāmagato anusayavasena tiṭṭhatī ti, evam pi tiṭṭhaṃ naro. Vuttaṃ h'; etaṃ Bhavagatā: *Santi bhikkhave cakkhuviññeyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasamhitā rajanīyā; tañ ce bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhosāya tiṭṭhati. Santi bhikkhave sotaviññeyyā saddā,


<!-- PTS Page 025 -->
<a id="page-025"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 025]*

ghānaviññeyyā gandhā, jivhāviññeyyā rasā, kāyaviññeyyā phoṭṭhabbā[^025-1], manoviññeyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā; tañ ce bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhosāya tiṭṭhatī ti, evam pi tiṭṭhaṃ naro. Vuttaṃ h'; etaṃ Bhavagatā: *Rūpupayaṃ[^025-2] vā bhikkhave viññāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhati rūpārammaṇaṃ rūpappatiṭṭhaṃ nandūpasevanaṃ vuddhiṃ[^025-4] virūḷhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjati[^025-5]. Vedanupayaṃ vā bhikkhave, saññupayaṃ [^025-6]vā bhikkhave[^025-6], saṃkhārupayaṃ vā[^025-7] bhikkhave viññāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhati, saṃkhārārammaṇaṃ saṃkhārappatiṭṭhaṃ nandūpasevanaṃ vuddhiṃ virūhiṃ vepullaṃ āpajjatī ti, evam pi tiṭṭhaṃ naro. Vuttaṃ pi[^025-8] h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā:** Kavaḷiṅkāre[^025-9] ce bhikkhave āhāre atthi rāgo, atthi nandi, atthi taṇhā, patiṭṭhitaṃ tattha viññāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ; yattha patiṭṭhitaṃ viññāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ, atthi tattha nāmarūpassāvakkanti; yattha atthi nāmarūpassāvakkanti, atthi tattha saṃkhārānaṃ vuddhi; yattha atthi saṃkhārānaṃ vuddhi atthi tattha āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti; yattha atthi āyatiṃ[^025-10] punabbhavābhinibbatti, atthi tattha āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ; yattha atthi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ, sasokan taṃ bhikkhave sarajaṃ sa-upāyāsan ti vadāmi[^025-11]. Phasse ce bhikkhave āhāre, manosañcetanāya ce bhikkhave āhāre, viññāṇe ce bhikkhave āhāre atthi rāgo, atthi nandi, atthi


<!-- PTS Page 026 -->
<a id="page-026"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 026]*

taṇhā, patiṭṭhitaṃ tattha viññāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ; yattha patiṭṭhitaṃ viññāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ, atthi tattha nāmarūpassāvakkanti; yattha atthi nāmarūpassāvakkanti, atthi tattha saṃkhārānaṃ vuddhi; yattha atthi saṃkhārānaṃ vuddhi, atthi tattha āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti; yattha atthi āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti, atthi tattha āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ[^026-1]; yattha atthi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ[^026-1], sasokan[^026-2] taṃ bhikkhave sarajaṃ[^026-3] sa-upāyāsan[^026-4] ti vadāmī ti, evam pi tiṭṭhaṃ naro. Mohanasmiṃ pagāḷho ti. Mohanā vuccanti pañca kāmaguṇā, cakkhuviññeyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sotaviññeyyā saddā, ghānaviññeyyā gandhā, jivhāviññeyyā rasā, kāyaviññeyyā phoṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā. Kiṃkāraṇā mohanā vuccanti pañca kāmaguṇā? Yebhuyyena devamanussā pañcasu kāmaguṇesu muyhanti sammuyhanti sampamuyhanti, mūḷhā sammūḷhā sampamūḷhā, avijjāya andhikatā āvuṭā nivuṭā ovuṭā[^026-5] pihitā paṭicchannā paṭikujjitā; taṃkāraṇā mohanā vuccanti pañca kāmaguṇā. Mohanasmiṃ pagāḷho ti mohanasmiṃ pagāḷho ogāḷho ajjhogāḷho nimuggo ti, tiṭṭhaṃ naro mohanasmiṃ pagāḷho. Dūre vivekā hi tathāvidho so ti. Vivekā ti tayo vivekā, kāyaviveko cittaviveko upadhiviveko. Katamo kāyaviveko? Idha bhikkhu vivittaṃ senāsanaṃ bhajati araññaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ[^026-6] kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhokāsaṃ palālapuñjaṃ[^026-7], kāyena ca[^026-8] vivitto viharati; so[^026-6] eko gacchati, eko tiṭṭhati, eko nisīdati, eko seyyaṃ kappeti, eko gāmaṃ[^026-9] piṇḍāya pavisati, eko paṭikkamati, eko raho nisīdati, eko caṅkamaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, eko carati viharati iriyati vattati pāleti yapeti yāpeti. Ayaṃ kāyaviveko.


<!-- PTS Page 027 -->
<a id="page-027"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 027]*

Katamo cittaviveko? Paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpannassa nīvaraṇehi cittaṃ vivittaṃ hoti; dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpannassa vitakkavicārehi cittaṃ vivittaṃ hoti; tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpannassa pītiyā cittaṃ vivittaṃ hoti, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpannassa sukhadukkhehi cittaṃ vivittaṃ hoti; ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samāpannassa rūpasaññāya paṭighasaññāya nānattasaññāya cittaṃ vivittaṃ hoti; viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samāpannassa ākāsānañcāyatanasaññāya cittaṃ vivittaṃ hoti; ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samāpannassa viññāṇañcāyatanasaññāya cittaṃ vivittaṃ hoti; nevasaññānāsaññāyatanaṃ samāpannassa ākiñcaññāyatanasaññāya cittaṃ vivittaṃ hoti; sotāpannassa sakkāyadiṭṭhiyā vicikicchāya sīlabbataparāmāsā[^027-1] diṭṭhānusayā vicikicchānusayā tadekaṭṭhehi ca kilesehi cittaṃ vivittaṃ hoti; sakadāgāmissa oḷārikā kāmarāgasaṃyojanā paṭighasaṃyojanā oḷārikā kāmarāgānusayā paṭighānusayā tadekaṭṭhehi ca kilesehi cittaṃ vivittaṃ hoti; anāgāmissa aṇusahagatā kāmarāgasaṃyojanā paṭighasaṃyojanā, aṇusahagatā kāmarāgānusayā paṭighānusayā tadekaṭṭhehi ca kilesehi cittaṃ vivittaṃ hoti; arahato [^027-2]rūparāgā arūparāgā[^027-2] mānā uddhaccā avijjāya[^027-3] mānānusayā bhavarāgānusayā avijjānusayā tadekaṭṭhehi ca kilesehi bahiddhā ca sabbanimittehi cittaṃ vivittaṃ hoti. Ayaṃ cittaviveko. Katamo upadhiviveko? Upadhi vuccanti kilesā ca khandhā ca abhisaṃkhārā ca. Upadhiviveko vuccati amataṃ nibbānaṃ; yo so[^027-4] sabbasaṃkhārasamatho sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhakkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānaṃ. Ayaṃ upadhiviveko. Kāyaviveko ca vūpakaṭṭhakāyānaṃ[^027-5] nekkhammābhiratānaṃ; cittaviveko ca parisuddhacittānaṃ paramavodānappattānaṃ; upadhiviveko ca nirupadhīnaṃ puggalānaṃ visaṃkhāragatānaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 028 -->
<a id="page-028"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 028]*

Dūre vivekā hī ti yo so evaṃ guhāyaṃ satto evaṃ bahukehi kilesehi channo, evaṃ mohanasmiṃ pagāḷho, so kāyavivekā pi dūre, cittavivekā pi dūre, upadhivivekā pi dūre vidūre suvidūre na santike na sāmantā anāsanne vūpakaṭṭhe[^028-1]. Tathāvidho ti tathāvidho[^028-2] tādiso tassaṇṭhito[^028-3] tappakāro tappaṭibhāgo yo so mohanasmiṃ pagāḷho ti, dūre vivekā hi tathāvidho so. Kāmā hi loke na hi suppahāyā ti. Kāmā ti uddānato dve kāmā, vatthukāmā ca kilesakāmā ca. Katame vatthukāmā? Manāpikā rūpā, manāpīkā saddā, manāpikā gandhā, manāpikā rasā, manāpikā phoṭṭhabbā; attharaṇā pāpuraṇā dāsidāsā ajeḷakā kukkuṭasūkarā hatthigavāssavaḷavā khettaṃ vatthu hiraññaṃ suvaṇṇam gāmanigamarājadhāniyo raṭṭhañ ca janapado ca koso ca koṭṭhāgārañ ca, yaṃ kiñci rajanīyavatthu vatthukāmā. Api ca atītā kāmā, anāgatā kāmā paccupapannā kāmā, ajjhattā kāmā, bahiddhā kāmā, ajjhattabahiddhā kāmā, hīnā kāmā, majjhimā kāmā, paṇītā kāmā, āpāyikā kāmā, mānusikā kāmā, dibbā kāmā, paccuppaṭṭhitā kāmā, nimmitā kāmā, paranimmitā kāmā, pariggahitā kāmā, apariggahitā kāmā, mamāyitā kāmā, amamāyitā kāmā, sabbe pi kāmāvacarā dhammā, sabbe pi rūpāvacarā dhammā, sabbe pi arūpāvacarā dhammā, taṇhāvatthukā taṇhārammaṇā kāmanīyatthena rajanīyatthena madanīyatthena kāmā; ime vuccanti vatthukāmā. Katame kilesakāmā? Chando kāmo, rāgo kāmo, chandarāgo kāmo, saṃkappo kāmo, rāgo kāmo, saṃkapparāgo kāmo; yo kāmesu kāmachando kāmarāgo kāmanandi kāmataṇhā kāmasneho kāmapariḷāho kāmamucchā kāmajjhosānaṃ kāmogho kāmayogo kāmupādānaṃ kāmachandanīvaraṇaṃ. *Addasaṃ kāma te mūlaṃ, saṃkappā kāma jāyasi; na taṃ saṃkappayissāmi. evaṃ kāma na hohisi; ime vuccanti kilesakāmā.


<!-- PTS Page 029 -->
<a id="page-029"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 029]*

Loke ti apāyaloke manussaloke devaloke khandhaloke dhātuloke āyatanaloke. Kāmā hi loke na hi suppahāyā ti kāmā hi loke duppahāyā duccajjā[^029-1] duppariccajjā dunnimmadayā[^029-2] dunnivedhayā[^029-3] dubbinivedhayā[^029-4] duttarā duppatarā dussamatikkamā dubbītivattā ti, kāmā hi loke na hi suppāhāyā. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Satto guhāyaṃ bahunābhichanno tiṭṭhaṃ naro mohanasmiṃ pagāḷho, dūre vivekā hi tathāvidho so, kāmā hi loke na hi suppahāya ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,2.2(=773): Icchānidānā bhavasātabaddhā[^029-5] te duppamuñcā, na hi aññamokkhā, pacchā pure vā pi apekkhamānā ime va kāme purime va jappaṃ.  || Nidd_I.2:2 ||

Icchānidānā bhavasātabaddhā ti. Icchā ti vuccati taṇhā, yo rāgo sārāgo anunayo anurodho nandi nandirāgo cittassa sārāgo icchā mucchā ajjhosānaṃ gedho[^029-6] paligedho[^029-7] saṅgo paṅko ejā māyā janikā[^029-8] sañjanikā[^029-9] sañjananī sibbinī jālinī saritā visattikā suttaṃ visaṭā āyūhanī dutiyā paṇidhi bhavanetti vanaṃ vanatho santhavo sneho apekkhā paṭibandho[^029-10], āsā āsiṃsanā āsiṃsitattaṃ rūpāsā saddāsā gandhāsā rasāsā phoṭṭhabbāsā lābhāsā dhanāsā puttāsā jīvitāsā, jappā pajappā abhijappā jappā[^029-11] jappanā jappitattaṃ, loluppā loluppāyanā loluppāyitattaṃ, mucchañcikatā[^029-12], sādhukammaññatā[^029-13], adhammarāgo visama-


<!-- PTS Page 030 -->
<a id="page-030"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 030]*

lobho nikanti nikāmanā patthanā[^030-1] pihanā sampatthanā[^030-2], kāmataṇhā bhavataṇhā vibhavataṇhā, rūpataṇhā arūpataṇhā, nirodhataṇhā, rūpataṇhā[^030-3] saddataṇhā gandhataṇhā rasataṇhā phoṭṭhabbataṇhā dhammataṇhā, ogho yogo gantho[^030-4], upādānaṃ āvaraṇaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ chadanaṃ bandhanaṃ upakkileso anusayo pariyuṭṭhānaṃ latā vevicchaṃ dukkhamūlaṃ dukkhanidānaṃ dukkhappabhavo[^030-5] mārapāso mārabaḷisaṃ māravisayo taṇhānadī taṇhājālaṃ taṇhāgaddulaṃ[^030-6] taṇhāsamuddo abhijjhā, lobho akusalamūlaṃ. Icchānidānā ti icchānidānā[^030-7] icchāhetukā icchāpaccayā icchākāraṇā icchāpabhavā ti, icchānidānā. Bhavasātabaddhā ti. Ekaṃ bhavasātaṃ, sukhā vedanā. Dve bhavasātāni, sukhā ca vedanā iṭṭhañ ca vatthu[^030-8]. Tīṇi bhavasātāni, yobbaññaṃ ārogyaṃ jīvitaṃ. Cattāri bhavasātāni, lābho yaso pasaṃsā sukhaṃ. Pañca bhavasātāni, manāpikā rūpā, manāpikā saddā, manāpikā gandhā, manāpikā rasā, manāpikā phoṭṭhabbā. Cha bhavasātāni, cakkhusampadā sotasampadā ghānasampadā jivhāsampadā kāyasampadā manosampadā. Bhavasātabaddhā ti sukhāya vedanāya[^030-9] baddhā[^030-10], iṭṭhasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ baddhā, yobbaññe baddhā, ārogye[^030-11] baddhā, jīvite baddhā, lābhe baddhā, yase baddhā, pasaṃsāya[^030-12] baddhā, sukhe baddhā, manāpikesu rūpesu baddhā, saddesu gandhesu rasesu manāpikesu phoṭṭhabbesu baddhā, cakkhusampadāya baddhā, [^030-13]sotasampadāya ghānasampadāya jivhāsampadāya kāyasampadāya, manosampadāya[^030-13], baddhā vinibaddhā[^030-14] ābaddhā laggā laggitā palibuddhā ti, icchānidānā bhavasātabaddhā.


<!-- PTS Page 031 -->
<a id="page-031"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 031]*

Te duppamuñcā na hi aññamokkhā ti te vā bhavasātavatthū[^031-1] duppamuñcā, [^031-2]sattā vā etto dummocayā[^031-2]. Kathan te bhavasātavatthū[^031-1] duppamuñcā? Sukhā vedanā dummuñcā[^031-3], iṭṭhaṃ vatthu dummuñcaṃ, yobbaññaṃ dummuñcaṃ, ārogyaṃ dummuñcaṃ, jīvitaṃ dummuñcaṃ, lābho dummuñco, yaso dummuñco, pasaṃsā dummuñcā, sukhaṃ dummuñcaṃ, manāpikā rūpā dummuñcā, manāpikā saddā gandhā rasā phoṭṭhabbā dummuñcā, cakkhusampadā dummuñcā, [^031-4]sotasampadā ghānasampadā jivhāsampadā kāyasampadā manosampadā[^031-4], dummuñcā duppamuñcā dummocayā duppamocayā dunnimmadayā[^031-5] dubbinivedhayā duttarā duppatarā dussamatikkamā dubbītivattā[^031-6]; evan te[^031-7] bhavasātavatthū[^031-8] duppamuñcā. Kathaṃ sattā etto[^031-9] dummocayā? Sukhāya vedanāya sattā dummocayā, iṭṭhasmā vatthusmā dummocayā, yobbaññā dummocayā, ārogyā dummocayā, jīvitā dummocayā, lābhā dummocayā, yasā dummocayā, pasaṃsāya dummocayā, sukhā dummocayā, manāpikehi rūpehi dummocayā, manāpikehi saddehi gandhehi rasehi phoṭṭhabbehi dummocayā, cakkhusampadāya dummocayā, [^031-10]sotasampadāya ghānasampadāya jivhāsampadāya kāyasampadāya manosampadāya[^031-10] dummocayā duppamocayā[^031-11] duddharā dussamuddharā dubbuṭṭhāpanā[^031-12] dussamuṭṭhāpanā[^031-13]; [^031-14]evaṃ sattā etto[^031-9] dummocayā ti, te duppamuñcā.


<!-- PTS Page 032 -->
<a id="page-032"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 032]*

Na hi aññamokkhā ti te attanā palipapalipannā[^032-1] na sakkonti paraṃ palipapalipannaṃ[^032-2] uddharituṃ. Vuttaṃ h'; etaṃ Bhavagatā: *So vata Cunda attanā palipapalipanno[^032-3] paraṃ palipapalipannaṃ[^032-4] uddharissatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjati. So vata Cunda attanā adanto avinīto aparinibbuto paraṃ damessati vinessati parinibbāpessatī ti n'; etaṃ ṭhānaṃ vijjatī ti evaṃ[^032-5], na hi aññamokkhā. Athavā n'; atth'; añño koci mocetā[^032-6]; te yadi mucceyyuṃ[^032-7], sakena thāmena, sakena balena, sakena viriyena, sakena parakkamena sakena purisathāmena, sakena purisabalena, sakena purisaviriyena, sakena purisaparakkamena attanā sammāpaṭipadaṃ anulomapaṭipadam apaccanīkapaṭipadaṃ[^032-8] anvatthapaṭipadaṃ dhammānudhammapaṭipadaṃ paṭipajjamānā[^032-9] mucceyyun ti, evam[^032-9] pi na hi aññamokkhā. Vuttaṃ pi[^032-10] h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā: *Nāhaṃ gamissāmi pamocanāya kathaṃkathiṃ[^032-11] Dhotaka kañci[^032-12] loke, dhammañ ca seṭṭhaṃ[^032-13] abhijānamāno evaṃ tuvaṃ[^032-14] ogham imaṃ taresī ti, evam pi na hi aññamokkhā. Vuttaṃ pi[^032-10] h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā: Attanā pakataṃ[^032-15] pāpaṃ, attanā saṃkilissati, attanā akataṃ pāpaṃ, attanā 'va visujjhati, suddhi asuddhi paccattaṃ, nāñño aññaṃ visodhaye ti, evam pi na hi aññamokkhā.


<!-- PTS Page 033 -->
<a id="page-033"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 033]*

Vuttaṃ pi[^033-1] h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā: *Evam eva kho brāhmaṇa tiṭṭhat'; eva nibbānaṃ, tiṭṭhati nibbānagāmimaggo[^033-2], tiṭṭhām'; ahaṃ samādapetā; atha ca pana mama sāvakā mayā evaṃ ovadiyamānā evaṃ anusāsiyamānā app'; ekacce accantaniṭṭhaṃ nibbānaṃ ārādhenti, ekacce n'; ārādhenti. Ettha kyāhaṃ brāhmaṇa karomi? maggakkhāyī brāhmaṇa tathāgato, maggaṃ buddho ācikkhati, attanā paṭipajjamānā mucceyyun ti. Evam pi na hi aññamokkhā ti, te duppamuñcā na hi aññamokkhā. Pacchā pure vā pi apekkhamānā ti. Pacchā vuccati anāgataṃ, pure vuccati atītaṃ; api ca atītaṃ upādāya anāgatañ ca paccuppannañ ca pacchā, anāgataṃ upādāya atītañ ca paccuppannañ ca pure. Kathaṃ pure apekkhaṃ karoti? Evaṃrūpo ahosiṃ[^033-3] atītaṃ addhānan ti tattha nandiṃ samanvāgameti[^033-4]; evaṃvedano ahosiṃ, [^033-5]evaṃsañño ahosiṃ[^033-5], evaṃsaṃkhāro ahosiṃ, evaṃviññāṇo ahosiṃ atītam addhānan ti tattha nandiṃ samanvāgameti[^033-6]; evam pi pure apekkhaṃ karoti. Athavā, iti me cakkhu ahosi atītam addhānaṃ, iti rūpā ti tattha chandarāgapaṭibaddhaṃ hoti viññāṇaṃ, chandarāgapaṭibaddhattā viññāṇassa, tad abhinandati, tad abhinandanto evam pi pure apekkhaṃ karoti; [^033-7]iti me sotaṃ ahosi atītam addhānaṃ, iti saddā ti; iti me ghānam ahosi atītam addhānaṃ, iti [^033-8]gandhā ti[^033-8]; iti me jivhā ahosi atītam addhānaṃ, iti rasā ti; iti me kāyo ahosi atītam addhānaṃ, iti phoṭṭhabbā ti; iti me mano ahosi atītam addhānaṃ, iti [^033-9]dhammā ti[^033-9] tattha chandarāgapaṭibaddhaṃ hoti viññāṇaṃ; chandarāgapaṭibaddhattā viññāṇassa, tad abhinandati; tad abhinandanto evam pi pure apekkhaṃ karoti.


<!-- PTS Page 034 -->
<a id="page-034"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 034]*

Athavā, yān'[^034-1] assu[^034-2] tāni pubbe mātugāmena saddhiṃ hasitalapitakīḷitāni, tad assādeti, taṃ nikāmeti, tena ca vittiṃ āpajjati; evam pi pure apekkhaṃ karoti. Kathaṃ pacchā apekkhaṃ karoti? Evaṃrūpo siyaṃ[^034-3] anāgatam addhānan ti tattha nandiṃ samanvāgameti[^034-4]; evaṃvedano siyaṃ, evaṃsañño siyaṃ, evaṃsaṃkhāro siyaṃ, evaṃviññāṇo siyaṃ anāgatam addhānan ti tattha nandiṃ samanvāgameti; evam pi pacchā apekkhaṃ karoti. Athavā, iti me cakkhu[^034-5] siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti rūpā ti appaṭiladdhassa paṭilābhāya[^034-6] cittaṃ paṇidahati, cetaso[^034-7] paṇidhānapaccayā, tad abhinandati, tad abhinandanto evam pi pacchā apekkhaṃ karoti; [^034-8]iti me sotaṃ siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti saddā ti; iti me ghānaṃ siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti gandhā ti; iti me jivhā siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti rasā ti; iti me kāyo siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti phoṭṭhabbā ti; iti me mano siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti dhammā ti appaṭiladdhassa paṭilābhāya cittaṃ paṇidahati; cetaso paṇidhānapaccayā[^034-9], tad abhinandati, tad abhinandanto evam pi pacchā apekkhaṃ karoti. Athavā, imināhaṃ sīlena vā vattena[^034-10] vā tapena vā brahmacariyena vā devo vā bhavissāmi[^034-11] devaññataro vā ti appaṭiladdhassa[^034-12] paṭilābhāya cittaṃ paṇidahati; cetaso paṇidhānapaccayā[^034-13], tad abhinandati, tad abhinandanto evam pi pacchā apekkhaṃ karotī ti, pacchā pure vā pi apekkhamānā. Ime va kāme purime va jappan ti. Ime va kāme ti


<!-- PTS Page 035 -->
<a id="page-035"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 035]*

paccuppanne pañca kāmaguṇe icchantā sādiyantā patthayantā[^035-1] pihayantā abhijappantā. Purime va jappan ti atīte pañca kāmaguṇe jappantā pajappantā abhijappantā ti, ime va kāme purime va jappaṃ. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Icchānidānā bhavasātabaddhā te duppamuñcā, na hi aññamokkhā, pacchā pure vā pi apekkhamānā ime va kāme purime va jappan ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,2.3(=774): Kāmesu giddhā pasutā pamūḷhā[^035-2] avadāniyā te visame niviṭṭhā dukkhūpanītā paridevayanti[^035-3]: kiṃ su bhavissāma ito cutāse[^035-4]?  || Nidd_I.2:3 ||

Kāmesu giddhā pasutā pamūḷhā[^035-2] ti. Kāmā ti uddānato[^035-5] dve kāmā, vatthukāmā ca kilesakāmā ca . . . pe . . . ime vuccanti vatthukāmā . . . pe . . . ime vuccanti kilesakāmā; gedho vuccati taṇhā; yo rāgo sārāgo . . . pe . . . abhijjhā lobho akusalamūlaṃ. Kilesakāmena vatthukāmesu rattā giddhā gadhitā mucchitā[^035-6] ajjhopannā[^035-7] laggā laggitā palibuddhā ti, kāmesu giddhā. Pasutā ti ye pi kāme esanti gavesanti pariyesanti, taccaritā tabbahulā taggarukā tanninnā tappoṇā[^035-8] tappabbhārā [^035-9]tadadhimuttā tadādhipateyyā[^035-9], te pi kāmapasutā. [^035-10]Ye pi taṇhāvasena rūpe esanti gavesanti pariyesanti, sadde, gandhe, rase, phoṭṭhabbe [^035-11]esanti gavesanti[^035-11] pariyesanti, taccaritā tabbahulā taggarukā tanninnā tappoṇā[^035-8] tappabbhārā tadadhimuttā tadādhipateyyā, te pi kāmapasutā. Ye pi taṇhāvasena rūpe paṭilabhanti, sadde


<!-- PTS Page 036 -->
<a id="page-036"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 036]*

gandhe rase phoṭṭhabbe [^036-1]paṭilabhanti, taccaritā tabbahulā taggarukā tanninnā tappoṇā tappabbhārā tadadhimuttā[^036-1] tadādhipateyyā, te pi kāmappasutā. Ye pi taṇhāvasena rūpe paribhuñjanti, sadde gandhe rase phoṭṭhabbe paribhuñjanti[^036-2], taccaritā tabbahulā taggarukā tanninnā tappoṇā tappabbhāra tadadhimuttā tadādhipateyyā, te pi kāmappasutā. Yathā kalahakārako kalahappasuto[^036-3], kammakārako kammappasuto, gocare caranto gocarappasuto[^036-4], jhāyī jhānappasuto; evaṃ eva [^036-5]ye pi kāme esanti gavesanti pariyesanti, taccaritā tabbahulā taggarukā tanninnā tappoṇā tappabbhārā tadadhimuttā[^036-5] tadādhipateyyā, te pi kāmappasutā. Ye pi taṇhāvasena rūpe esanti gavesanti pariyesanti, sadde gandhe rase phoṭṭhabbe [^036-6]esanti gavesanti[^036-6] pariyesanti, taccaritā tabbahulā taggarukā tanninnā tappoṇā tappabbhārā tadadhimuttā tadādhipateyya, te pi kāmappasutā. Ye pi taṇhāvasena rūpe paṭilabhanti, sadde gandhe rase phoṭṭhabbe paṭilabhanti, taccaritā tabbahulā taggarukā tanninnā tappoṇā tappabbhārā tadadhimuttā tadādhipateyyā, te pi kāmappasutā. Ye pi taṇhāvasena rūpe paribhuñjanti, sadde gandhe rase phoṭṭhabbe paribhuñjanti, taccaritā tabbahulā taggarukā tanninnā tappoṇā tappabbhārā tadadhimuttā tadādhipateyyā, te pi kāmappasutā. Pamūḷhā ti yebhuyyena devamanussā pañcasu kāmaguṇesu muyhanti saṃmuyhanti sampamuyhanti, mūḷhā[^036-7] sammūḷhā[^036-8] sampamūḷhā, avijjāya andhikatā[^036-9] āvuṭā nivuṭā ovuṭā pihitā paṭicchannā paṭikujjitā ti, kāmesu giddhā pasutā pamūḷhā. Avadāniyā te visame niviṭṭhā[^036-10] ti. Avadāniyā ti avaṃ gacchantī ti pi avadāniyā. Maccharino pi vuccanti avadāniyā. Buddhānaṃ buddhasāvakānaṃ[^036-11] vacanaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 037 -->
<a id="page-037"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 037]*

byappathaṃ desanaṃ anusiṭṭhiṃ n'; ādiyantī ti[^037-1], avadāniyā[^037-2]. Kathaṃ avaṃ gacchantī ti avadāniyā? [^037-3]Avaṃ gacchantī ti[^037-3] nirayaṃ gacchanti, tiracchānayoniṃ gacchanti, pittivisayaṃ gacchantī[^037-4] ti[^037-5] evaṃ avaṃ gacchantī ti, avadāniyā. Kathaṃ maccharino vuccanti avadāniyā? Pañca macchariyāni, āvāsamacchariyaṃ kulamacchariyaṃ lābhamacchariyaṃ vaṇṇamacchariyaṃ dhammamacchariyaṃ; yaṃ evarūpaṃ macchariyaṃ maccharāyanā maccharāyitattaṃ vevicchaṃ kadariyaṃ kaṭukañcakatā aggahitattaṃ[^037-6] cittassa, idaṃ vuccati macchariyaṃ. Api ca khandhamacchariyaṃ pi macchariyaṃ, dhātumacchariyam pi macchariyaṃ[^037-7], āyatanamacchariyam pi macchariyaṃ gāho, idaṃ vuccati macchariyaṃ. Iminā macchariyena avadaññutāya samannāgatā[^037-8] janā pamattā; evaṃ maccharino vuccanti avadāniyā. Kathaṃ buddhānaṃ buddhasāvakānaṃ[^037-9] vacanaṃ byappathaṃ desanaṃ anusiṭṭhim[^037-10] n'; ādiyantī ti avadāniyā? Buddhānaṃ buddhasāvakānaṃ vacanaṃ byappathaṃ desanaṃ anusiṭṭhiṃ n'; ādiyanti, na sussusanti, na sotaṃ odahanti, na aññācittaṃ upaṭṭhapenti, anassavā avacanakarā paṭilomavuttino aññen'; eva mukhaṃ karonti, evaṃ buddhānaṃ buddhasāvakānaṃ vacanaṃ byappathaṃ desanaṃ anusiṭṭhiṃ n'; ādiyantī ti, avadāniyā[^037-11]. Avadāniyā[^037-12] te visame niviṭṭhā ti. Visame [^037-13]ti visame[^037-13] kāyakamme niviṭṭhā, visame vacīkamme niviṭṭhā, visame manokamme niviṭṭhā, visame pāṇātipāte niviṭṭhā, visame adinnādāne niviṭṭhā, visame kāmesu micchācāre niviṭṭhā, visame musāvāde niviṭṭhā, visamāya pisuṇāya vācāya


<!-- PTS Page 038 -->
<a id="page-038"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 038]*

niviṭṭhā[^038-1], visamāya pharusāya vācāya niviṭṭhā[^038-1], visame samphappalāpe niviṭṭhā[^038-1], visamāya abhijjhāya niviṭṭhā, visame byāpāde niviṭṭhā[^038-1], visamāya micchādiṭṭhiyā niviṭṭhā, visamesu saṃkhāresu niviṭṭhā, visamesu pañcasu[^038-2] kāmaguṇesu niviṭṭhā, visamesu pañcasu nīvaraṇesu niviṭṭhā, [^038-3]visamāya cetanāya, visamāya patthanāya, visamāya paṇidhiyā[^038-3] niviṭṭhā[^038-4] patiṭṭhitā allīnā upagatā ajjhositā adhimuttā laggā laggitā palibuddhā ti, avadāniyā te visame niviṭṭhā. Dukkhūpanītā paridevayantī ti. Dukkhūpanītā ti dukkhūpanītā[^038-1] dukkhappattā dukkhasampattā dukkhupagatā mārappattā mārasampattā mārupagatā maraṇappattā maraṇasampattā[^038-5] maraṇupagatā ti[^038-1], [^038-6]dukkhūpanītā. Paridevayantī ti lapanti lālapanti socanti kilamanti paridevanti, urattāḷiṃ kandanti, sammohaṃ āpajjantī ti, dukkhūpanītā paridevayanti. Kiṃ su bhavissāma ito cutāse[^038-7]? ti ito cutā kiṃ su[^038-5] bhavissāma? nerayikā bhavissāma, tiracchānayonikā bhavissāma, pittivisayikā bhavissāma, [^038-8]manussā bhavissāma[^038-8], devā kiṃ[^038-1] bhavissāma? rūpī bhavissāma, arūpī bhavissāma, saññī bhavissāma, asaññī bhavissāma, nevasaññīnāsaññī bhavissāma? bhavissāma nu kho mayaṃ anāgatam addhānaṃ? [^038-9]nanu kho bhavissāma anāgatam addhānaṃ? kiṃ nu kho bhavissāma anāgatam addhānam?[^038-9] kathaṃ nu kho bhavissāma anagātam addhānaṃ? kiṃ hutvā bhavissāma [^038-10]nu kho[^038-10] mayaṃ anāgatam addhānan? ti saṃsayapakkhannā[^038-11] vimatipakkhannā[^038-12] dveḷhakajātā lapanti lālapanti socanti kilamanti paridevanti, urattāḷiṃ kandanti, sammohaṃ āpajjantī ti, kiṃ su bhavissāma ito cutāse? Ten'; āha Bhagavā:


<!-- PTS Page 039 -->
<a id="page-039"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 039]*

Kāmesu giddhā pasutā pamūḷhā avadāniyā te visame niviṭṭhā dukkhūpanītā paridevayanti: kiṃ su bhavissāma ito cutāse? ti.
