Sn_IV,2.4(=775): Tasmā hi sikkhetha idh'; eva jantu; yaṃ kiñci jaññā visaman ti loke, na tassa hetu visamaṃ careyya, appaṃ h'; idaṃ jīvitam āhu dhīrā[^039-1].  || Nidd_I.2:4 ||

Tasmā hi sikkhetha idh'; eva jantū ti. Tasmā ti tasmā taṃkāraṇā taṃhetu tappaccayā tannidānā etaṃ ādīnavaṃ sampassamāno kāmesū ti, tasmā. Sikkhethā ti. Tisso sikkhā, adhisīlasikkhā adhicittasikkhā adhipaññāsikkhā. Katamā adhisīlasikkhā? Idha bhikkhu sīlavā hoti, pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno; aṇumattesu vajjesu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu. Khuddako[^039-2] sīlakkhandho, mahanto sīlakkhandho, sīlaṃ patiṭṭhā ādicaraṇaṃ saṃyamo saṃvaro, mukhaṃ[^039-3] pamukham[^039-4] kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā. Ayaṃ[^039-5] adhisīlasikkhā. Katamā adhicittasikkhā? Idha bhikkhu vivicc'; eva kāmehi, vivicca akusalehi dhammehi, savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. Vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ saṃpasādanaṃ[^039-6], cetaso ekodibhāvaṃ, avitakkaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ[^039-7] jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. Pītiyā ca virāgā upekkhako ca viharati sato[^039-2] sampajāno, sukhañ ca ka, paññāya pajānanto sikkheyya, abhiññeyyaṃ abhijānanto sikkheyya, pariññeyyaṃ parijānanto sikkheyya, pahātabbaṃ pajahanto sikkheyya, bhāvetabbaṃ bhāvento sikkheyya, sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikaronto sikkheyya ācareyya[^039-6] samācareyya samādāya vatteyya. Idhā ti imissā diṭṭhiyā, imissā khantiyā, imissā ruciyā, imasmiṃ ādāye, imasmiṃ dhamme, imasmiṃ[^039-7] vinaye, imasmiṃ dhammavinaye, imasmiṃ pāvacane, imasmiṃ brahmacariye, imasmiṃ satthusāsane, imasmiṃ attabhāve, imasmiṃ manussaloke. [^039-8]Tena vuccati idhā ti.[^039-8] Jantū ti satto naro . . . pe . . . manujo ti, tasmā hi sikkhetha[^039-9] idh'; eva jantu. Yaṃ kiñci jaññā visaman ti loke ti. Yaṃ kiñcī ti sabbena sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ asesaṃ nissesaṃ[^039-10]


<!-- PTS Page 041 -->
<a id="page-041"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 041]*

pariyādāyavacanaṃ[^041-1] etaṃ yaṃ kiñcī ti. [^041-2]Jaññā visaman tī ti[^041-2], visamaṃ kāyakammaṃ visaman ti jāneyya, visamaṃ vacīkammaṃ visaman ti jāneyya[^041-3], visamaṃ manokammaṃ visaman ti jāneyya, visamaṃ pāṇātipātaṃ visamo ti jāneyya, visamaṃ adinnādānaṃ visaman[^041-4] ti jāneyya, visamaṃ kāmesu micchācāraṃ visamo ti jāneyya, visamaṃ musāvādaṃ visamo ti jāneyya, visamaṃ pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ visamā ti jāneyya, visamaṃ pharusaṃ[^041-5] vācaṃ visamā ti jāneyya, visamaṃ samphappalāpaṃ visamo ti jāneyya, visamaṃ abhijjhaṃ visamā ti jāneyya, visamaṃ byāpādaṃ visamo ti jāneyya, visamaṃ micchādiṭṭhiṃ[^041-6] visamā ti jāneyya, visame saṃkhāre visamā ti jāneyya, visame pañca kāmaguṇe visamā ti jāneyya, visame pañca nīvaraṇe visamā ti jāneyya, [^041-7]visamaṃ cetanaṃ visamā ti jāneyya, visamaṃ paṭṭhanam visamā ti jāneyya, visamaṃ paṇidhiṃ visamā ti jāneyya[^041-7] ājāneyya[^041-8] vijāneyya paṭijāneyya[^041-9] paṭivijjheyya. Loke ti apāyaloke . . . pe . . . āyatanaloke ti, yaṃ kiñci jaññā visaman ti loke. Na tassa hetu visamaṃ careyyā ti visamassa kāyakammassa hetu visamaṃ na careyya,[^041-10] visamassa vacīkammassa hetu visamaṃ na careyya[^041-10], visamassa manokammassa hetu visamaṃ na careyya, visamassa pāṇātipātassa hetu visamaṃ na careyya, visamassa adinnādānassa hetu visamaṃ na careyya, visamassa kāmesu micchācārassa hetu visamaṃ na careyya, visamassa musāvādassa hetu visamaṃ na careyya[^041-11], visamāya pisuṇāya vācāya hetu visamaṃ na careyya, visamāya pharusāya vācāya hetu visamaṃ na careyya, visamassa samphappalāpassa hetu visamaṃ na careyya, visamāya abhijjhāya hetu[^041-12] visamaṃ na careyya, visamassa byāpādassa hetu visamaṃ na careyya, visamāya micchādiṭṭhiyā hetu visamaṃ na careyya, visamānaṃ saṃkhārānaṃ hetu visame[^041-13] na careyya[^041-11],


<!-- PTS Page 042 -->
<a id="page-042"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 042]*

visamānaṃ pañcannaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ hetu visame[^042-1] na careyya, visamānaṃ pañcannaṃ nīvaraṇānaṃ hetu visame[^042-1] na careyya, visamāya cetanāya hetu[^042-2], visamāya patthanāya hetu[^042-2], visamāya paṇidhiyā hetu visamaṃ na careyya, na ācareyya, na samācareyya, na samādāya vatteyyā ti, na tassa hetu visamaṃ careyya. Appaṃ h'; idaṃ jīvitam āhu dhīrā ti. Jīvitan ti āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ. Api ca dvīhi kāraṇehi appakaṃ jīvitaṃ[^042-3]: ṭhitiparittatāya vā appakaṃ jīvitaṃ, sarasaparittatāya[^042-4] vā appakaṃ jīvitaṃ. Kathaṃ ṭhitiparittatāya appakaṃ jīvitaṃ? Atīte cittakkhaṇe jīvittha, na jīvati, na jīvissati. Anāgate cittakkhaṇe jīvissati, na jīvati, na jīvittha. Paccuppanne cittakkhaṇe jīvati, na jīvittha, na jīvissati. *Jīvitaṃ attabhāvo ca sukhadukkhā ca kevalā ekacittasamāyuttā, lahuso vattati kkhaṇo[^042-5]. Cullāsīti[^042-6] sahassāni kappā tiṭṭhanti ye marū, na tv eva te pi jīvanti dvīhi cittehi[^042-7] samāhitā. Ye niruddhā marantassa tiṭṭhamānassa vā idha, sabb'; eva sadisā khandhā, gatā appaṭisandhikā[^042-8]. Anantarā ca ye bhaṅgā[^042-9] ye ca bhaṅgā[^042-9] anāgatā, tadantare niruddhānaṃ[^042-10] vesammaṃ[^042-11] n'; atthi lakkhaṇe. Anibbattena na jāto, paccuppannena jīvati, cittabbhaṅgamato[^042-12] loko, paññatti paramatthiyā. Yathā ninnā pavattanti, chandena pariṇāmitā acchinnavārā[^042-13] vattanti saḷāyatanapaccayā.


<!-- PTS Page 043 -->
<a id="page-043"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 043]*

Anidhānagatā bhaṅgā[^043-1], puñjo n'; atthi anāgate, nibbattā yeva tiṭṭhanti āragge sāsapūpamā. Nibbattānañ ca dhammānaṃ bhaṅgo[^043-2] nesaṃ purekkhato[^043-3], palokadhammā[^043-4] tiṭṭhanti purāṇehi amissitā. Adassanato āyanti bhaṅgā, gacchanti dassanaṃ, vijjuppādo va ākāse uppajjanti vayanti[^043-5] cā ti. Evaṃ ṭhitiparittatāya appakaṃ jīvitaṃ. Kathaṃ sarasaparittatāyā[^043-6] appakaṃ jīvitam? Assāsupanibaddhaṃ jīvitaṃ, [^043-7]passāsupanibaddhaṃ jīvitaṃ[^043-7] assāsappassāsupanibaddhaṃ[^043-8] jīvitaṃ, mahābhūtupanibaddhaṃ jīvitaṃ, [^043-9]usmūpanibaddhaṃ jīvitam[^043-9], [^043-10]kavaḷiṅkārāhārupanibaddhaṃ jīvitaṃ[^043-10], viññāṇupanibaddhaṃ jīvitaṃ. Mūlam pi imesaṃ[^043-11] dubbalaṃ; pubbahetū pi imesaṃ dubbalā[^043-12]; ye pi[^043-13] paccayā te pi dubbalā; ye[^043-14] pi pabhavikā[^043-15] te pi dubbalā; sahabhū[^043-16] pi imesaṃ dubbalā; sampayogā pi imesaṃ dubbalā; sahajā pi imesaṃ dubbalā; yā pi payojikā[^043-17] sā pi dubbalā. Aññamaññaṃ niccadubbalā[^043-18] ime[^043-19]. Aññamaññaṃ anavaṭṭhitā[^043-20] ime. Aññamaññaṃ paripāṭayanti[^043-21] ime. Aññamaññassa hi n'; atthi tāyitā. Na cā pi ṭhapenti aññamaññ'; ime. Yo pi nibbattako so na vijjati. Na ca kena ci koci hāyati. Bhaṅgabyā[^043-22] ca imehi sabbaso. Purimehi pabhāvitā ime, ye pi pabhāvitā[^043-23] te pure matā. Purimā pi ca pacchimā


<!-- PTS Page 044 -->
<a id="page-044"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 044]*

pi ca aññamaññaṃ na [^044-1]kadāci addasaṃsū[^044-1] ti. Evaṃ sarasaparittatāya appakaṃ jīvitaṃ. Api ca cātummahārājikānaṃ devānaṃ jīvitaṃ upādāya manussānaṃ appakaṃ jīvitaṃ, parittakaṃ jīvitaṃ, thokaṃ[^044-2] jīvitaṃ, khaṇikaṃ jīvitaṃ, lahukaṃ jīvitaṃ, ittaraṃ[^044-3] jīvitaṃ, anaddhanīyaṃ[^044-4] jīvitaṃ, na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ jīvitaṃ; tāvatiṃsānaṃ devānaṃ, yāmānaṃ devānaṃ, tusitānaṃ devānaṃ, nimmānaratīnaṃ devānaṃ, parinimmitavasavattīnaṃ devānaṃ, brahmakāyikānaṃ devānaṃ jīvitaṃ [^044-5]upādāya manussānaṃ appakaṃ jīvitaṃ, parittakaṃ jīvitaṃ, thokaṃ[^044-6] jīvitaṃ, khaṇikaṃ jīvitaṃ, lahukaṃ jīvitaṃ, ittaraṃ jīvitaṃ, anaddhanīyaṃ jīvitaṃ, na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ jīvitaṃ. Vuttaṃ h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā: *Appam idaṃ bhikkhave manussānaṃ āyu, gamanīyo samparāyo, mantāya[^044-7] phoṭṭhabbaṃ, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ, caritabbaṃ brahmacariyaṃ. N'; atthi jātassa amaraṇaṃ. Yo hi[^044-8] bhikkhave ciraṃ jīvati so vassasataṃ appaṃ vā bhiyyo. Appam āyu manussānaṃ, hīḷeyya[^044-9] naṃ suporiso; careyy'[^044-10] ādittasīso va, n'; atthi maccussa n'; āgamo. Accayanti ahorattā, jīvitaṃ uparujjhati, āyu jīyati maccānaṃ kunnadīnaṃ va ūdakan[^044-11] ti. Appaṃ h'; idaṃ jīvitam āhu dhīrā ti. Dhīrā[^044-12] ti dhitimā ti dhīrā; dhitisampannā ti dhīrā; [^044-13]dhikkatapāpā ti dhīrā. Dhi vuccati[^044-13] paññā; yā paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo


<!-- PTS Page 045 -->
<a id="page-045"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 045]*

dhammavicayo[^045-1] sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccuppalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā[^045-2] cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassantā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññā-āloko pañña-obhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ[^045-3] amoho dhammavicayo, sammādiṭṭhi; tāya paññāya samannāgatattā[^045-4] dhīrā. Api ca khandhadhīrā dhātudhīrā āyatanadhīrā paṭiccasamuppādadhīrā satipaṭṭhānadhīrā sammappadhānadhīrā iddhipādadhīrā[^045-5] indriyadhīrā baladhīrā bojjhaṅgadhīrā maggadhīrā phaladhīrā nibbānadhīrā. Te dhīrā evaṃ āhaṃsu manussānaṃ appakaṃ jīvitaṃ, parittakaṃ jīvitaṃ, thokaṃ jīvitaṃ, khaṇikaṃ jīvitaṃ, lahukaṃ jīvitaṃ, ittaraṃ jīvitaṃ, anaddhanīyaṃ jīvitaṃ, na ciraṭṭhitikaṃ jīvitan ti, evaṃ āhaṃsu, evaṃ kathenti, evaṃ bhaṇanti, evaṃ dīpayanti, evaṃ voharantī ti, appaṃ[^045-6] h'; idaṃ jīvitam āhu dhīrā. Ten āha Bhagavā:

Tasmā hi sikkhetha idh'; eva jantu; yaṃ kiñci jaññā visaman ti loke, na tassa hetū visamaṃ careyya, appaṃ h'; idaṃ jīvitaṃ āhu dhīrā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,2.5(=776): Passāmi loke pariphandamānaṃ pajaṃ imaṃ taṇhagataṃ bhavesu, hīnā narā maccumukhe lapanti avītataṇhāse bhavābhavesu.  || Nidd_I.2:5 ||

Passāmi loke pariphandamānan ti. Passāmī ti maṃsacakkhunā pi passāmi, dibbacakkhunā pi passāmi, paññācakkhunā pi passāmi, buddhacakkhunā pi passāmi, samantacakkhunā pi passāmi, dakkhāmi olokemi nijjhāyāmi upaparikkhāmi. Loke ti apāyaloke manussaloke devaloke


<!-- PTS Page 046 -->
<a id="page-046"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 046]*

khandhaloke dhātuloke āyatanaloke. Pariphandamānan ti taṇhāphandanāya phandamānaṃ, diṭṭhiphandanāya phandamānaṃ, kilesaphandanāya phandamānaṃ, payogaphandanāya phandamānaṃ, vipākaphandanāya phandamānaṃ, duccaritaphandanāya phandamānaṃ, rattaṃ[^046-1] rāgena phandamānaṃ, duṭṭhaṃ dosena phandamānaṃ, mūḷhaṃ mohena phandamānaṃ, vinibandhaṃ mānena phandamānaṃ, parāmaṭṭhaṃ diṭṭhiyā phandamānaṃ, vikkhepagataṃ uddhaccena phandamānaṃ, aniṭṭhaṅgataṃ vicikicchāya phandamānaṃ, thāmagataṃ anusayehi phandamānaṃ, lābhena phandamānaṃ, alābhena phandamānaṃ, yasena phandamānaṃ, ayasena phandamānaṃ, pasaṃsāya phandamānaṃ, nindāya phandamānaṃ, sukhena phandamānaṃ, dukkhena phandamānaṃ, jātiyā phandamānaṃ, jarāya phandamānaṃ, byādhinā[^046-2] phandamānaṃ, maraṇena phandamānaṃ, sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsehi phandamānaṃ, nerayikena dukkhena phandamānaṃ, tiracchānayonikena dukkhena phandamānaṃ, pittivisayikena[^046-3] dukkhena phandamānaṃ, mānusikena[^046-4] dukkhena phandamānam, gabbhokkantimūlakena dukkhena phandamānaṃ, gabbhe[^046-5] ṭhitimūlakena dukkhena phandamānaṃ, gabbhavuṭṭhānamūlakena dukkhena phandamānaṃ, jātass'; upanibandhikena dukkhena phandamānaṃ, jātassa parādheyyakena dukkhena phandamānaṃ, attupakkamena dukkhena phandamānaṃ, parupakkamena dukkhena phandamānaṃ, dukkhadukkhena phandamānaṃ, saṃkhāradukkhena phandamanaṃ, vipariṇāmadukkhena phandamānaṃ, cakkhurogena dukkhena phadamānaṃ, sotarogena dukkhena phandamānaṃ,


<!-- PTS Page 047 -->
<a id="page-047"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 047]*

ghānarogena dukkhena, jivhārogena dukkhena[^047-1], kāyarogena[^047-2] dukkhena[^047-1], sīsarogena dukkhena[^047-1], kaṇṇarogena[^047-3], mukharogena, dantarogena, kāsena, sāsena, pināsena, ḍahena[^047-4], jareṇa, kucchirogena, mucchāya, pakkhandikāya, sulāya[^047-5], visūcikāya, kuṭṭhena, gaṇḍena, kilāsena, sosena, apamāreṇa, dadduyā, kaṇḍuyā, kacchuyā, rakhasāya[^047-6], vitacchikāya, lohitena, pittena[^047-7], madhumehena, aṃsāya piḷakāya, bhagaṇḍalena, pittasamuṭṭhānena ābādhena, semhasamuṭṭhānena ābādhena, vātasamuṭṭhānena ābādhena, sannipātikena ābādhena, utupariṇāmajena ābāḍhena, visamaparihārajena ābādhena, opakkamikena ābādhena, kammavipākajena ābādhena, sītena, uṇhena, jighacchāya, pipāsāya, uccārena, passāvena[^047-8], ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasiriṃsapasamphassena[^047-9] dukkhena, mātumaraṇena dukkhena, pitumaraṇena dukkhena, bhātumaraṇena dukkhena, bhaginimaraṇena dukkhena, puttamaraṇena dukkhena, dhītumaraṇena dukkhena, ñātibyasanena[^047-10], bhogabyasanena[^047-11], rogabyasanena, sīlabyasanena, diṭṭhibyasanena, dukkhena phandamānaṃ samphandamānam vipphandamānam vedhamānam pavedhamānam sampavedhamānam passāmi dakkhāmi olokemi nijjhāyāmi upaparikkhāmī ti, passāmi loke pariphandamānaṃ. Pajaṃ imaṃ[^047-12] taṇhagataṃ[^047-13] bhavesū ti. Pajā ti sattādhivacanaṃ. Taṇhā ti rūpataṇhā saddataṇhā gandhataṇhā rasataṇhā phoṭṭhabbataṇhā dhammataṇhā. Taṇhagatan ti taṇhāgataṃ taṇhānugataṃ taṇhānusaṭaṃ[^047-14] [^047-15]taṇhāya pannaṃ[^047-15] taṇhāya pātitaṃ[^047-16] abhibhūtaṃ pariyādiṇṇacit-


<!-- PTS Page 048 -->
<a id="page-048"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 048]*

taṃ. Bhavesū ti kāmabhave rūpabhave arūpabhave ti, pajaṃ imaṃ taṇhagataṃ bhavesu. Hīnā[^048-1] narā maccumukhe lapantī ti. Hīnā narā ti hīnena[^048-2] kāyakammena samannāgatā [^048-3]ti hīnā[^048-3]; hīnena vacīkammena samannāgatā [^048-3]ti hīnā[^048-3]; hīnena manokammena samannāgatā ti hīnā[^048-4]; hīnena pāṇātipātena samannāgatā ti hīnā[^048-4]; hīnena adinnādānena, hīnena kāmesu micchācārena, hīnena musāvādena, hīnāya pisuṇāya vācāya, hīnāya pharusāya vācāya, hīnena samphappalāpena, hīnāya abhijjhāya, hīnena byāpādena, hīnāya micchādiṭṭhiyā, hīnehi saṃkhārehi, hīnehi pañcahi kāmaguṇehi, hīnehi pañcahi nīvaraṇehi, hīnāya cetanāya, hīnāya patthanāya[^048-5], hīnāya paṇidhiyā samannāgatā[^048-6] ti hīnā [^048-7]nihīnā [^048-8]omakā lāmakā jatukkā[^048-9] parittā ti, hīnā narā. Maccumukhe lapantī ti maccumukhe māramukhe maraṇamukhe maccuppattā[^048-10] maccusampattā maccupāgatā mārappattā mārasampattā mārupāgatā maraṇappattā maraṇasampattā maraṇupāgatā lapanti lālapanti socanti kilamanti paridevanti urattāḷiṃ kandanti sammohaṃ āpajjantī ti, hīnā narā maccumukhe lapanti. Avītataṇhāse bhavābhavesū ti [^048-11]taṇhā ti[^048-11] rūpataṇhā . . . pe . . . dhammataṇhā. Bhavābhavesū ti bhavābhave kammabhave, punabbhave, kāmabhave kammabhave, kāmabhave punabbhave[^048-12], rūpabhave kammabhave, rūpabhave punabbhave, arūpabhave kammabhave, arūpabhave punabbhave, [^048-13]punappunaṃ bhave, punappunaṃ gatiyā[^048-13], punappunaṃ[^048-14]


<!-- PTS Page 049 -->
<a id="page-049"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 049]*

upapattiyā, punappunaṃ[^049-1] paṭisandhiyā, punappunaṃ[^049-2] attabhāvābhinibbattiyā. Avītataṇhā ti[^049-3] avigatataṇhā acattataṇhā avantataṇhā amuttataṇhā appahīnataṇhā appaṭinissaṭṭhataṇhā ti, avītataṇhāse bhavābhavesu. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Passāmi loke pariphandamānaṃ pajaṃ imaṃ taṇhagataṃ bhavesu, hīnā narā maccumukhe lapanti avītataṇhāse bhavābhavesū ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,2.6(=777): Mamāyite passatha phandamāne macche va appodake[^049-4] khīṇasote, etam pi disvā amamo careyya bhavesu āsattim akubbamāno.  || Nidd_I.2:6 ||

Mamāyite passatha phandamāne ti. Mamattā ti dve mamattā: taṇhāmamattañ ca diṭṭhimamattañ ca. Katamaṃ taṇhāmamattaṃ? Yāvatā taṇhāsaṃkhātena sīmakataṃ mariyādikataṃ[^049-3] odhikataṃ pariyantikataṃ[^049-5] pariggahitaṃ mamāyitaṃ: idaṃ mamaṃ, etaṃ mamaṃ, ettakaṃ mamaṃ, ettāvatā mamaṃ, mama rūpā saddā gandhā rasā phoṭṭhabbā, attharaṇā, pāpuraṇā, dāsidāsā ajeḷakā kukkuṭasūkarā hatthigavāssavaḷavā khettam vatthu hiraññaṃ suvaṇṇaṃ gāmanigamarājadhāniyo[^049-6] raṭṭhañ ca janapado ca koso ca koṭṭhāgārañ ca; kevalam pi mahāpaṭhaviṃ taṇhāvasena mamāyati, yāvatā[^049-7] aṭṭhasatataṇhāvicaritaṃ[^049-8]; idaṃ taṇhāmamattaṃ. Katamam diṭṭhimamattaṃ? Vīsativatthukā sakkāyadiṭṭhi, dasavatthukā micchādiṭṭhi, dasavatthukā antaggāhikā[^049-9] diṭṭhi; yā evarūpā diṭṭhi, diṭṭhigataṃ diṭṭhi-


<!-- PTS Page 050 -->
<a id="page-050"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 050]*

gahaṇaṃ diṭṭhikantāro diṭṭhivisūkāyikaṃ diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ[^050-1] diṭṭhisaṃyojanaṃ gāho paṭiggāho[^050-2] abhiniveso parāmāso kummaggo micchāpatho micchattaṃ titthāyatanaṃ[^050-3], vipariyesagāho, viparittagāho[^050-4], vippallāsagāho, micchāgāho, ayāthāvakasmiṃ yāthāvakan ti gāho, yāvatā dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni; idaṃ diṭṭhimamattaṃ. Mamāyite passatha phandamāne ti mamāyitavatthuacchedasaṃkino[^050-5] pi phandanti, acchijjante[^050-6] [^050-7]pi phandanti, acchinne pi[^050-7] phandanti, {mamāyitavatthuvipariṇāmasampino}[^050-8] pi phandanti, vipariṇāmante pi phandanti, vipariṇate pi phandanti paphandanti[^050-9] samphandanti vipphandanti vedhanti[^050-10] pavedhanti[^050-11] sampavedhanti: evaṃ phandamāne paphandamāne[^050-12] samphandamāne vipphandamāne vedhamāne pavedhamāne sampavedhamāne passatha dakkhatha oloketha nijjhāyatha upaparikkhathā ti, mamāyite passatha phandamāne. Macche va appodake khīṇasote ti: yathā macchā appodake parittodake udakapariyādāne kākehi vā kulalehi vā balākehi vā paripātiyamānā ukkhipiyamānā khajjamānā phandanti paphandanti[^050-11] samphandanti vipphandanti vedhanti pavedhanti sampavedhanti; evaṃ eva pajā mamāyitavatthuacchedasaṃkino pi phandanti, acchijjante pi phandanti, acchinne pi phandanti, mamāyitavatthuvipariṇāmasaṃkino pi phandanti, vipariṇāmante pi phandanti, vipariṇate pi phandanti paphandanti[^050-11] samphandanti vipphandanti vedhanti pavedhanti sampavedhantī ti, macche va appodake khīṇasote. Etam pi disvā amamo careyyā ti etaṃ ādīnavaṃ disvā passitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā


<!-- PTS Page 051 -->
<a id="page-051"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 051]*

mamattesū ti, etam pi disvā amamo careyya. Mamattā ti dve mamattā: taṇhāmamattañ ca diṭṭhimamattañ ca . . . pe . . . idaṃ taṇhāmamattaṃ . . . pe . . . idaṃ diṭṭhimamattaṃ. Taṇhāmamattam pahāya[^051-1], diṭṭhimamattaṃ paṭinissajjitvā, cakkhuṃ amamāyanto, sotaṃ amamāyanto, ghānaṃ amamāyanto, jivhaṃ amamāyanto, kāyaṃ amamāyanto, manaṃ amamāyanto, rūpe sadde gandhe rase phoṭṭhabbe kulaṃ gaṇaṃ āvāsaṃ lābhaṃ yasaṃ pasaṃsaṃ sukhaṃ cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātaṃ senāsanaṃ gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāraṃ kāmadhātuṃ rūpadhātuṃ arūpadhātuṃ kāmabhavaṃ rūpabhavaṃ arūpabhavaṃ saññābhavaṃ asaññābhavaṃ nevasaññānāsaññābhavaṃ ekavokārabhavaṃ catuvokārabhavaṃ pañcavokārabhavaṃ atītaṃ anāgataṃ paccuppannaṃ diṭṭhasutamutaviññātabbe dhamme amamāyanto aggaṇhanto aparāmasanto anabhinivisanto, careyya vihareyya iriyeyya vatteyya pāleyya yapeyya yāpeyyā ti, etam[^051-2] pi disvā amamo careyya. Bhavesu āsattim akubbamāno ti. Bhavesū ti kāmabhave rūpabhave arūpabhave. Āsatti vuccati taṇhā; yo rāgo sārāgo . . . pe . . . abhijjhā lobho akusalamūlaṃ. Bhavesu āsattim akubbamāno ti bhavesu āsattiṃ akubbamāno, chandaṃ pemaṃ rāgaṃ khantiṃ akubbamāno ajanayamāno asañjanayamāno anibbattayamāno anabhinibbattayamāno ti, bhavesu āsattim akubbamāno. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Mamāyite passatha phandamāne macche va appodake khīṇasote, etam[^051-3] pi disvā amamo careyya bhavesu āsattim akubbamāno ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,2.7(=778): ubhosu antesu vineyya chandaṃ phassaṃ pariññāya anānugiddho, yad attagarahī, tad akubbamāno na limpatī[^051-4] diṭṭhasutesu dhīro.  || Nidd_I.2:7 ||


<!-- PTS Page 052 -->
<a id="page-052"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 052]*

Ubhosu antesu vineyya chandan ti. Antā ti phasso eko anto, phassasamudayo dutiyo anto. Atīto eko anto, anāgato dutiyo anto. Sukhā vedanā eko anto, dukkhā vedanā dutiyo anto. Nāmaṃ eko anto, rūpaṃ dutiyo anto. Cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni eko anto, cha bāhirāni āyatanāni dutiyo anto. Sakkāyo eko anto, sakkāyasamudayo dutiyo anto ti[^052-1]. [^052-2]Chando ti[^052-2] yo kāmesu kāmacchando, kāmarāgo kāmanandi kāmataṇhā kāmasneho kāmapariḷāho kāmamucchā kāmajjhosānaṃ kāmogho kāmayogo kāmupādānaṃ kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ. [^052-3]Ubhosu antesu vineyya chandan ti[^052-3] ubhosu antesu chandaṃ vineyya paṭivineyya pajaheyya vinodeyya byantīkareyya anabhāvaṃ gameyyā ti, ubhosu antesu vineyya chandaṃ. Phassaṃ pariññāya anānugiddho ti. Phasso ti cakkhusamphasso, sotasamphasso, ghānasamphasso jivhāsamphasso, kāyasamphasso, manosamphasso, adhivacanasamphasso, paṭighasamphasso, sukhavedanīyo samphasso, dukkhavedanīyo samphasso, adukkha-m-asukhavedanīyo samphasso, kusalo phasso, akusalo phasso, abyākato[^052-4] phasso, kāmāvacaro phasso, rūpāvacaro phasso, arūpāvacaro phasso, suññato phasso, animitto phasso, appaṇihito phasso, lokiyo phasso, lokuttaro phasso, atīto phasso, anāgato phasso, paccuppanno phasso; yo evarūpo phasso phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ[^052-5]; ayaṃ vuccati phasso. Phassaṃ pariññāyā ti phassaṃ tīhi pariññāhi parijānitvā ñātapariññāya tīraṇapariññāya[^052-6] pahānapariññāya. Katamā ñātapariññā? Phassaṃ jānāti: ayaṃ cakkhusamphasso, ayaṃ sotasamphasso, ayaṃ ghānasamphasso, ayaṃ jivhāsamphasso, ayaṃ kāyasamphasso, ayaṃ manosamphasso, ayaṃ adhivacanasamphasso, ayaṃ paṭighasamphasso, ayaṃ sukhavedanīyo phasso, ayaṃ dukkhavedanīyo phasso, ayaṃ adukkha-m-asukhavedanīyo phasso, ayaṃ kusalo phasso, ayaṃ akusalo phasso, ayaṃ abyākato phasso, ayaṃ kāmāvacaro phasso, ayaṃ rūpāvacaro phasso,


<!-- PTS Page 053 -->
<a id="page-053"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 053]*

ayaṃ arūpāvacaro phasso, ayaṃ suññato phasso, ayaṃ animitto phasso, ayaṃ appaṇihito phasso, ayaṃ lokiyo phasso, ayaṃ lokuttaro phasso, ayaṃ atīto phasso, ayaṃ anāgato phasso, ayaṃ paccuppanno phasso ti jānāti passati; ayaṃ ñātapariññā. Katamā tīraṇapariññā? Evaṃ ñātaṃ[^053-1] katvā phassaṃ tīreti, aniccato dukkhato rogato gaṇḍato sallato[^053-2] aghato ābādhato parato[^053-3] palokato ītito upaddavato bhayato upasaggato calato pabhaṅguto addhuvato atāṇato aleṇato asaraṇato rittato[^053-4] tucchato suññato anattato ādīnavato vipariṇāmadhammato asārakato[^053-5] aghamūlato vadhakato vibhavato sāsavato saṃkhatato mārāmisato jātidhammato jarādhammato byādhidhammato maraṇadhammato sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsadhammato saṃkilesikadhammato[^053-6] samudayato atthaṅgamato assādato ādīnavato nissaraṇato tīreti; ayaṃ tīraṇapariññā. Katamā pahānapariññā? Evaṃ tīrayitvā phasse chandarāgaṃ pajahati vinodeti byantīkaroti[^053-7] anabhāvaṃ gameti. Vuttaṃ h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā: *Yo bhikkhave phassesu chandarāgo, taṃ pajahatha; evaṃ so phasso pahīno bhavissati ucchinnamūlo tālāvatthukato anabhāvaṅgato[^053-8] āyatiṃ anuppādadhammo ti; ayaṃ pahānapariññā. Phassaṃ pariññāyā ti phassaṃ imāhi tīhi pariññāhi parijānitvā. Anānugiddho ti. Gedho vuccati taṇhā. Yo rāgo sārāgo . . . pe . . . abhijjhā lobho akusalamūlaṃ; yass'; eso[^053-9] gedho pahīno samucchinno vūpasanto paṭipassaddho abhabbuppattiko ñāṇagginā daḍḍho, so vuccati agiddho. So rūpe agiddho, sadde agiddho, gandhe agiddho, rase agiddho, phoṭṭhabbe agiddho, kule gaṇe āvāse lābhe yase pasaṃsāya sukhe cīvare piṇḍapāte senāsane gilānapaccaya-


<!-- PTS Page 054 -->
<a id="page-054"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 054]*

bhesajjaparikkhāre, kāmadhātuyā rūpadhātuyā arūpadhātuyā, kāmabhave rūpabhave arūpabhave saññābhave asaññābhave nevasaññānāsaññābhave ekavokārabhave catuvokārabhave pañcavokārabhave, atīte anāgate paccuppanne, diṭṭhasutamutaviññātabbesu dhammesu agiddho agadhito amucchito anajjhopanno[^054-1] vītagedho vigatagedho cattagedho vantagedho[^054-2] muttagedho[^054-3] pahīnagedho paṭinissaṭṭhagedho vītarāgo vigatarāgo cattarāgo vantarāgo muttarāgo pahīnarāgo paṭinissaṭṭharāgo nicchāto nibbuto sītibhūto {sukhapaṭisaṃvedī} brahmabhūtena attanā viharatī ti, phassaṃ pariññāya anānugiddho. Yad attagarahī tad akubbamāno ti. Yadan ti yaṃ. [^054-4]Attagarahī ti[^054-4] dvīhi kāraṇehi attānaṃ garahati, katattā ca akatattā ca. Kathaṃ katattā ca akatattā ca attānaṃ garahati? Kataṃ me kāyaduccaritaṃ, akataṃ me kāyasucaritan ti attānaṃ garahati; kataṃ me vacīduccaritaṃ, akataṃ me vacīsucaritan ti attānaṃ garahati; kataṃ me manoduccaritaṃ, akataṃ me manosucaritan ti attānaṃ garahati; kato me pāṇātipāto, akatā me pāṇātipātā veramaṇī ti attānaṃ garahati; kataṃ me adinnādānaṃ, akatā me adinnādānā veramaṇī ti attānaṃ garahati; kato me kāmesu micchācāro, akatā me kāmesu micchācārā veramaṇī ti attānaṃ garahati; kato me musāvādo, akatā me musāvādā veramaṇī ti attānaṃ garahati; katā me pisuṇā vācā, akatā me pisuṇāya vācāya veramaṇī ti attānaṃ garahati; katā me pharusā vācā, akatā me pharusāya vācāya veramaṇī ti attānaṃ garahati; kato me samphappalāpo, akatā me samphappalāpā veramaṇī ti attānaṃ garahati; katā me abhijjhā, akatā me anabhijjhā ti attānaṃ garahati; kato me byāpādo, akato me abyābhijjhā ti attānaṃ garahati; katā me micchādiṭṭhi, akatā me sammādiṭṭhī ti attānaṃ garahati; evaṃ katattā ca akatattā ca attānaṃ garahati. Athavā, sīlesu 'mhi na paripūrakārī[^054-5] ti attānaṃ garahati;


<!-- PTS Page 055 -->
<a id="page-055"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 055]*

indriyesu 'mhi aguttadvāro ti attānaṃ garahati; bhojane [^055-1]amattaññu 'mhī ti[^055-1] attānaṃ garahati; jāgariyaṃ [^055-2]ananuyutto 'mhī ti[^055-2] attānaṃ garahati; na[^055-3] satisampajaññena [^055-4]samannāgato 'mhī ti[^055-4] attānaṃ garahati; abhāvitā me cattāro satipaṭṭhānā ti attānaṃ garahati; abhāvitā me cattāro sammappadhānā ti attānaṃ garahati; abhāvitā me cattāro iddhipādā ti attānaṃ garahati; abhāvitāni me pañc'; indriyānī ti attānaṃ garahati; abhāvitāni me pañca balānī ti attānaṃ garahati; abhāvitā me satta bojjhaṅgā ti attānaṃ garahati; abhāvito me ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo ti attānaṃ garahati; dukkhaṃ me apariññātan ti attānaṃ garahati; samudayo me appahīno ti attānaṃ garahati; maggo me abhāvito ti attānaṃ garahati; nirodho me asacchikato ti attānaṃ garahati; evaṃ katattā ca akatattā ca attānaṃ garahati. Evaṃ attagarahitaṃ kammaṃ akubbamāno [^055-5]ajanayamāno asañjanayamāno[^055-5] anibbattayamāno anabhinibbattayamāno ti, yad attagarahī tad akubbamāno. Na limpatī diṭṭhasutesu dhīro ti. Lepā[^055-6] ti dve lepā: taṇhālepo ca diṭṭhilepo ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhālepo . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhilepo. Dhīro ti dhīro paṇḍito paññavā buddhimā ñāṇī vibhāvī medhāvī. Dhīro taṇhālepaṃ pahāya, diṭṭhilepaṃ paṭinissajjitvā, diṭṭhe na limpati, sute na limpati, mute na limpati, viññāte na limpati na saṃlimpati[^055-7] na upalimpati, alitto asaṃlitto[^055-8] anupalitto nikkhanto nissaṭṭho vippamutto visaṃyutto vimariyādikatena[^055-9] cetasā viharatī ti, na limpati diṭṭhasutesu dhīro[^055-10]. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Ubhosu antesu vineyya chandaṃ phassaṃ pariññāya anānugiddho, yad attagarahī, tad akubbamāno na limpatī diṭṭhasutesu dhīro[^055-10] ti.

<!-- PTS Page 056 -->
<a id="page-056"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 056]*

Sn_IV,2.8(=779): Saññaṃ pariññā vitareyya oghaṃ pariggahesu muni nopalitto abbūḷha-sallo caram[^056-1] appamatto nāsiṃsatī lokam imaṃ parañ ca.  || Nidd_I.2:8 ||

Saññaṃ pariññā vitareyya oghan ti. Saññā ti kāmasaññā byāpādasaññā vihiṃsāsaññā, [^056-2]nekkhammasaññā abyāpādasaññā avihiṃsāsaññā[^056-2], rūpasaññā saddasaññā gandhasaññā rasasaññā phoṭṭhabbasaññā dhammasaññā; yā evarūpā saññā sañjānanā sañjānitattaṃ ayaṃ vuccati saññā. Saññaṃ pariññā ti saññaṃ tīhi pariññāhi parijānitvā ñātapariññāya tīraṇapariññāya pahānapariññāya. Katamā ñātapariññā? Saññaṃ jānāti: ayaṃ kāmasaññā, ayaṃ byāpādasaññā, ayaṃ vihiṃsāsaññā, ayaṃ nekkhammasaññā, ayaṃ abyāpādasaññā, ayaṃ avihiṃsāsaññā, ayaṃ rūpasaññā, ayaṃ saddasaññā, ayaṃ gandhasaññā, ayaṃ rasasaññā, ayaṃ phoṭṭhabbasaññā, ayaṃ dhammasaññā ti jānāti passati; ayaṃ ñātapariññā. Katamā tīraṇapariññā? Evaṃ ñātaṃ katvā saññaṃ tīreti, aniccato dukkhato rogato gaṇḍato sallato aghato ābādhato parato palokato ītito upaddavato bhayato upasaggato calato pabhaṅguto . . . pe . . . samudayato aṭṭhaṅgamato assādato ādīnavato nissaraṇato tīreti; ayaṃ tīraṇapariññā. Katamā pahānapariññā? [^056-3] Evaṃ tīretvā[^056-4] saññāya chandarāgaṃ pajahati vinodeti byantīkaroti[^056-5] anabhāvaṃ gameti. Vuttaṃ[^056-6] h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā: *Yo bhikkhave saññāya[^056-7] chandarāgo, taṃ pajahatha; evaṃ sā saññā pahīnā bhavissati ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṅgatā[^056-8] āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā ti; ayaṃ pahānapariññā. Saññaṃ pariññā ti saññaṃ imāhi tīhi pariññāhi parijānitvā.


<!-- PTS Page 057 -->
<a id="page-057"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 057]*

Vitareyya oghan ti kāmoghaṃ bhavoghaṃ diṭṭhoghaṃ avijjoghaṃ vitareyya[^057-1] uttareyya patareyya samatikkameyya vītivatteyyā ti, saññaṃ pariññā vitareyya oghaṃ. Pariggahesu muni nopalitto ti. Pariggahā ti dve pariggahā, taṇhāpariggaho ca diṭṭhipariggaho ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhāpariggaho . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhipariggaho. Munī ti. Monaṃ vuccati ñāṇaṃ, yā paññā pajānanā . . . pe . . . amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi; tena ñāṇena samannāgato muni monappatto ti. Tīṇi moneyyāni, kāyamoneyyaṃ vacīmoneyyaṃ manomoneyyaṃ. Katamaṃ kāyamoneyyaṃ? Tividhānaṃ[^057-2] kāyaduccaritānaṃ pahānaṃ, kāyamoneyyaṃ. Tividhaṃ kāyasucaritaṃ, kāyamoneyyaṃ. Kāyārammaṇaṃ[^057-3] ñāṇaṃ, kāyamoneyyaṃ. Kāyapariññā, kāyamoneyyaṃ. Pariññāsahagato maggo, kāyamoneyyaṃ. Kāye chandarāgassa pahānaṃ, kāyamoneyyaṃ. Kāyasaṃkhāranirodho catutthajjhānasamāpatti[^057-4], kāyamoneyyaṃ; idaṃ kāyamoneyyaṃ. Katamaṃ vacīmoneyyaṃ? Catubbidhānaṃ[^057-5] vacīduccaritānaṃ pahānaṃ, vacīmoneyyaṃ. Catubbidhaṃ vacīsucaritaṃ, vacīmoneyyaṃ. Vācārammaṇaṃ[^057-6] ñāṇaṃ, vacīmoneyyaṃ. Vācāpariññā, vacīmoneyyaṃ. Pariññāsahagato maggo, vacīmoneyyaṃ. Vācāya chandarāgassa pahānaṃ, vacīmoneyyaṃ. Vacīsaṃkhāranirodho dutiyajjhānasamāpatti[^057-7], vacīmoneyyaṃ; idaṃ vacīmoneyyaṃ. Katamaṃ manomoneyyaṃ? Tividhānaṃ[^057-8] manoduccaritānaṃ pahānaṃ, manomoneyyaṃ. Tividhaṃ manosucaritaṃ, manomoneyyaṃ. Cittārammaṇaṃ[^057-9] ñāṇaṃ, manomoneyyaṃ. Cittapariññā, manomoneyyaṃ. Pariññāsahagato maggo, manomoneyyam. Cittacchandarāgassa[^057-10] pahānaṃ, manomoneyyaṃ. Cittasaṃkhāranirodho saññāvedayita-


<!-- PTS Page 058 -->
<a id="page-058"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 058]*

nirodhasamāpatti, manomoneyyaṃ; idaṃ manomoneyyaṃ. *Kāyamuniṃ[^058-1] vācāmuniṃ manomunim anāsavaṃ muniṃ moneyyasampannaṃ[^058-2] āhu sabbapahāyinaṃ. Kāyamuniṃ vācāmuniṃ manomunim anāsavaṃ muniṃ moneyyasampannaṃ āhu ninhātapāpakan ti[^058-3]. Imehi tīhi[^058-4] moneyyehi dhammehi samannāgatā cha munayo[^058-5], āgāramunayo[^058-6] anāgāramunayo sekhamunayo asekhamunayo paccekamunayo munimunayo[^058-7]. Katame āgāramunayo? Ye te āgārikā diṭṭhapadā viññātasāsanā ime āgāramunayo. Katame anāgāramunayo? Ye te pabbajitā diṭṭhapadā viññātasāsanā, ime anāgāramunayo. Satta sekhā sekhamunayo. Arahanto asekhamunayo. Paccekabuddhā paccekamunayo. Munimunayo vuccanti tathāgatā arahanto sammāsambuddhā. Na monena muni hoti mūḷharūpo aviddasu, yo ca tulaṃ va paggayha varam ādāya paṇḍito. Pāpāni parivajjeti, sa muni, tena so muni; yo munāti ubho loke, muni tena pavuccati. Asatañ ca satañ ca ñatvā dhammaṃ ajjhattaṃ bahiddhā ca sabbaloke devamanussehi pūjito, yo so saṅgajālam aticca so muni. Lepā ti dve lepā, taṇhālepo ca diṭṭhilepo ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ tanhālepo . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhilepo. Muni taṇhālepaṃ pahāya diṭṭhilepaṃ paṭinissajjitvā parig-


<!-- PTS Page 059 -->
<a id="page-059"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 059]*

gahesu na limpati [^059-1]na saṃlimpati[^059-1] na upalimpati, alitto[^059-2] asaṃlitto anupalitto nikkhanto nissaṭṭho vippamutto visaṃyutto vimariyādikatena[^059-3] cetasā viharatī ti, pariggahesu muni nopalitto. Abbūḷhasallo caraṃ appamatto ti. Sallan ti satta sallāni, rāgasallaṃ dosasallaṃ mohasallaṃ mānasallaṃ diṭṭhisallaṃ sokasallaṃ kathaṃkathāsallaṃ[^059-4]. Yass'; etāni[^059-5] sallāni[^059-3] pahīnāni[^059-7] samucchinnāni vūpasantāni paṭipassaddhāni[^059-8] abhabbuppattikāni ñāṇagginā daḍḍhāni, so vuccati abbūḷhasallo abbūḷhitasallo[^059-9] pahatasallo[^059-10] uddhaṭasallo samuddhaṭasallo uppāṭitasallo samuppāṭitasallo cattasallo vantasallo muttasallo pahīnasallo paṭinissaṭṭhasallo, nicchāto nibbuto sītibhūto {sukhapaṭisaṃvedī} brahmabhūtena attanā viharatī ti abbūḷhasallo. Caran ti caranto viharanto iriyanto vattento[^059-11] pālento yapento yāpento. Appamatto ti sakkaccakārī[^059-12] sātaccakārī[^059-13] aṭṭhitakārī[^059-14] anolīnavuttiko anikkhittacchando anikkhittadhuro kusalesu dhammesu. Kathāhaṃ aparipūraṃ vā sīlakkhandhaṃ paripūreyyaṃ, paripūraṃ vā sīlakkhandhaṃ tattha tattha paññāya anuggaṇheyyan? ti, yo tattha chando ca vāyāmo ca ussāho ca ussoḷhī ca appaṭivāṇī ca satisampajaññañ[^059-15] ca ātappaṃ padhānaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ anuyogo appamādo kusalesu dhammesu. [^059-16]Kathāhaṃ aparipūraṃ vā samādhikkhandhaṃ paripūreyyaṃ paripūraṃ vā samādhikkhandhaṃ tattha tattha paññāya anuggaṇheyyan? ti . . . pe . . . kusalesu dhammesu. Kathāhaṃ aparipūraṃ vā


<!-- PTS Page 060 -->
<a id="page-060"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 060]*

paññākkhandhaṃ vimuttikkhandhaṃ[^060-1] vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaṃ paripūreyyaṃ paripūraṃ vā vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaṃ tattha tattha paññāya anuggaṇheyyan? ti, yo tattha chando ca vāyāmo ca ussāho ca ussoḷhī ca appaṭivānī ca satisampajaññañ[^060-2] ca ātappaṃ padhānaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ anuyogo appamādo kusalesu dhammesu[^060-3]. Kathāhaṃ apariññātaṃ vā dukkhaṃ parijāneyyaṃ, appahīne vā kilese pajaheyyaṃ, abhāvitaṃ vā maggaṃ bhāveyyaṃ, asacchikataṃ vā nirodhaṃ sacchikareyyan? ti, yo tattha chando ca vāyāmo ca ussāho ca ussoḷhī ca appaṭivāṇī ca satisampajaññañ[^060-2] ca ātappaṃ padhānaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ anuyogo appamādo kusalesu dhammesū ti, abbūḷhasallo caraṃ appamatto. Nāsiṃsati[^060-4] lokam imaṃ parañ cā ti imaṃ lokaṃ nāsiṃsati sakattabhāvaṃ, paraṃ[^060-5] lokaṃ nāsiṃsati parattabhāvaṃ. Imaṃ lokaṃ nāsiṃsati sakarūpavedanāsaññāsaṃkhāraviññāṇaṃ, paraṃ lokaṃ nāsiṃsati pararūpavedanāsaññāsaṃkhāraviññāṇaṃ[^060-6]. [^060-7]Imaṃ lokaṃ nāsiṃsati cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni[^060-7], paraṃ lokaṃ nāsiṃsati cha bāhirāni āyatānani. Imaṃ lokaṃ nāsiṃsati manussalokaṃ, paraṃ[^060-8] lokaṃ nāsiṃsati devalokaṃ. Imaṃ lokaṃ nāsiṃsati kāmadhātum, paraṃ lokaṃ nāsiṃsati rūpadhātuṃ arūpadhātuṃ. Imaṃ lokaṃ nāsiṃsati kāmadhātuṃ rūpadhātuṃ, paraṃ[^060-8] lokaṃ nāsiṃsati arūpadhātuṃ. Punāgatiṃ[^060-9] vā upapattiṃ[^060-10] vā paṭisandhiṃ vā bhavaṃ vā saṃsāraṃ vā vaṭṭaṃ vā nāsiṃsati na icchati na sādiyati na pattheti na piheti nābhijappatī ti, nāsiṃsati lokaṃ imaṃ parañ ca. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:


<!-- PTS Page 061 -->
<a id="page-061"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 061]*

Saññaṃ pariññā vitareyya oghaṃ pariggahesu muni nopalitto abbūḷhasallo caraṃ appamatto nāsiṃsati lokaṃ imaṃ parañ cā ti[^061-1].

DUTIYO[^061-2] GUHAṬṬHAKASUTTANIDDESO[^061-3] NIṬṬHITO[^061-4].


<!-- PTS Page 062 -->
<a id="page-062"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 062]*

III. TATIYO[^062-1] DUṬṬHAṬṬHAKASUTTANIDDESO[^062-2]. Sn_IV,3.1(=780): Vadanti ve duṭṭhamanā pi eke, aññe[^062-3] pi ve saccamanā vadanti, vādañ ca jātaṃ muni no upeti, tasmā munī n'; atthi khilo kuhiñci.  || Nidd_I.3:1 ||

Vadanti ve duṭṭhamanā pi eke ti. Te titthiyā duṭṭhamanā paduṭṭhamanā viruddhamanā paṭiviruddhamanā āhatamanā paccāhatamanā āghāṭitamanā paccāghāṭitamanā vadanti upavadanti Bhagavantañ ca bhikkhusaṃghañ ca abhūtenā ti, vadanti ve duṭṭhamanā pi eke. Aññe[^062-3] pi ve saccamanā vadantī ti. Ye tesaṃ titthiyānaṃ saddahantā okappentā adhimuccantā saccamanā saccasaññino bhūtamanā bhūtasaññino tathamanā tathasaññino yāthāvamanā yāthāvasaññino aviparittamanā[^062-4] aviparittasaññino vadanti upavadanti Bhagavantañ ca bhikkhusaṃghañ ca abhūtenā ti, aññe pi ve saccamanā vadanti. Vādañ ca jātaṃ muni no upetī ti. So vādo jāto hoti sañjāto nibbatto abhinibbatto pātubhūto, parato ghoso akkoso upavādo Bhagavato ca bhikkhusaṃghassa ca abhūtenā ti, vādañ ca jātaṃ muni no upetī ti. Munī* ti. Monaṃ vuccati ñāṇaṃ, yā paññā pajānanā . . . pe[^062-1] . . . amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi; tena


<!-- PTS Page 063 -->
<a id="page-063"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 063]*

ñāṇena samannāgato muni monappatto . . . pe . . . saṅgajālam aticca so munī ti[^063-1]. Yo vādaṃ upeti so dvīhi kāraṇehi vādaṃ upeti. Kārako kārakatāya vādaṃ upeti; atha vā vuccamāno upavadiyamāno kuppati[^063-2] byāpajjati patiṭṭhiyati, kopañ ca dosañ ca appaccayañ ca pātukaroti akārako 'mhī ti [^063-3]yo vādaṃ upeti so[^063-3] imehi dvīhi kāraṇehi vādaṃ upeti. Muni dvīhi kāraṇehi vādaṃ na upeti. Akārako akārakatāya[^063-4] vādaṃ na upeti; athavā vuccamāno upavadiyamāno na kuppati na byāpajjati na patiṭṭhiyati na kopañ ca dosañ ca appaccayañ ca pātukaroti akārako 'mhī ti muni imehi dvīhi kāraṇehi vādaṃ na upeti na upagacchati na gaṇhāti na parāmasati na abhinivisatī ti, vādañ ca jātaṃ muni no upeti. Tasmā munī n'; atthi khilo kuhiñcī ti. Tasmā ti tasmā taṃkāraṇā taṃhetu tappaccayā taṃnidānā munino ahatacittatā[^063-5] khilajātatā[^063-6] pi n'; atthi; pañca pi cetokhilā n' atthi; tayo pi khilā n'; atthi; rāgakhilo dosakhilo mohakhilo n'; atthi na santi na saṃvijjati n'; upalabbhati, pahīno samucchinno vūpasanto paṭipassaddho abhabbuppattiko ñāṇagginā daḍḍho. Kuhiñcī ti kuhiñci kimhici katthaci ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā ajjhattabahiddhā vā ti, tasmā munī n'; atthi khilo kuhiñci. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Vadanti ve duṭṭhamanā pi eke, aññe[^063-7] pi ve[^063-8] saccamanā vadanti, vādañ ca jātaṃ muni no upeti, tasma munī n'; atthi khilo kuhiñcī ti.

Sn_IV,3.2(=781): Sakaṃ hi diṭṭhiṃ katham accayeyya chandānunīto ruciyā niviṭṭho? sayaṃ samattāni pakubbamāno[^063-9], yathā hi jāneyya, tathā vadeyya.  || Nidd_I.3:2 ||


<!-- PTS Page 064 -->
<a id="page-064"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 064]*

Sakaṃ hi diṭṭhiṃ katham accayeyyā? ti. Ye te titthiyā Sundariṃ* paribbājikaṃ hantvā[^064-1], samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ avaṇṇaṃ pakāsayitvā, evaṃ etaṃ lābhaṃ yasaṃ sakkārasammānaṃ paccāharissāmā ti evaṃdiṭṭhikā[^064-2] evaṃkhantikā evaṃrucikā evaṃladdhikā evamajjhasayā evamadhippāyā, te nāsakkhiṃsu sakaṃ diṭṭhiṃ sakaṃ khantiṃ sakaṃ ruciṃ sakaṃ laddhiṃ sakaṃ ajjhāsayaṃ sakaṃ adhippāyaṃ atikkamituṃ. Atha kho sv eva ayaso te[^064-3] paccāgato ti, evam pi sakaṃ hi diṭṭhiṃ katham accayeyya? Athavā sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti yo so evaṃvādo, so sakaṃ diṭṭhiṃ sakaṃ khantiṃ sakaṃ ruciṃ sakaṃ laddhiṃ sakaṃ ajjhāsayaṃ sakaṃ adhippāyaṃ kathaṃ accayeyya atikkameyya samatikkameyya vītivatteyya? Taṃ kissa hetu? Tassa sā diṭṭhi tathā samattā samādiṇṇā gahitā parāmaṭṭhā abhiniviṭṭhā ajjhositā adhimuttā ti, evam pi sakam hi diṭṭhiṃ katham accayeyya?[^064-4] Asassato loko, antavā loko, anantavā loko, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ, aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīraṃ, hoti tathāgato param maraṇā, na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā, hoti ca na ca hoti tathāgato param maraṇā, n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti yo so evaṃvādo, so sakaṃ diṭṭhiṃ sakaṃ khantiṃ sakaṃ ruciṃ sakaṃ laddhiṃ sakaṃ ajjhāsayaṃ sakaṃ adhippāyaṃ kathaṃ accayeyya atikkameyya samatikkameyya vītivatteyya? Taṃ kissa hetu? Tassa sā diṭṭhi tathā samattā samādiṇṇā gahitā parāmaṭṭhā abhiniviṭṭhā ajjhositā adhimuttā ti, evam pi sakaṃ hi diṭṭhiṃ katham accayeyya? Chandānunīto ruciyā niviṭṭho ti. Chandānunīto ti sakāya diṭṭhiyā sakāya khantiyā sakāya ruciyā sakāya laddhiyā yāyati niyyati vuyhati saṃhariyati[^064-5]. Yathā hatthiyānena


<!-- PTS Page 065 -->
<a id="page-065"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 065]*

vā rathayānena vā assayānena vā goyānena vā ajayānena vā meṇḍakayānena[^065-1] vā oṭṭhayānena vā kharayānena vā yāyati niyyati vuyhati saṃhariyati[^065-2]; evam eva sakāya diṭṭhiyā sakāya khantiyā sakāya ruciyā sakāya laddhiyā yāyati niyyati vuyhati saṃhariyatī[^065-2] ti, chandānunīto. Ruciyā niviṭṭho ti sakāya diṭṭhiyā sakāya khantiyā sakāya ruciyā sakāya laddhiyā niviṭṭho patiṭṭhito allīno upāgato ajjhosito adhimutto ti, chandānunīto ruciyā niviṭṭho. Sayaṃ samattāni pakubbamāno[^065-3] ti sayaṃ samattaṃ karoti, paripuṇṇaṃ karoti, anomaṃ karoti, aggaṃ seṭṭhaṃ viseṭṭhaṃ[^065-4] pāmokkhaṃ[^065-5] uttamaṃ pavaraṃ karoti; ayaṃ satthā sabbaññū ti sayaṃ samattaṃ karoti, paripuṇṇaṃ karoti, anomaṃ karoti, aggaṃ seṭṭhaṃ viseṭṭhaṃ pāmokkhaṃ uttamaṃ pavaraṃ karoti; ayaṃ dhammo svākkhāto[^065-6], ayaṃ gaṇo supaṭipanno, ayaṃ diṭṭhi bhaddikā, ayaṃ paṭipadā supaññattā, ayaṃ maggo niyyāniko[^065-7] ti sayaṃ samattaṃ karoti, paripuṇṇaṃ karoti, anomaṃ karoti, aggaṃ seṭṭhaṃ viseṭṭhaṃ pāmokkhaṃ uttamaṃ pavaraṃ karoti janeti sañjaneti[^065-8] nibbatteti abhinibbattetī[^065-9] ti, sayaṃ samattāni pakubbamāno. Yathā hi jāneyya tathā vadeyyā ti yathā jāneyya tathā vadeyya katheyya bhaṇeyya dīpeyya[^065-10] vohareyya. Sassato loko, idaṃ eva saccaṃ, moghaṃ aññan ti yathā jāneyya tathā vadeyya katheyya bhaneyya dīpeyya vohareyya. Asassato loko . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato paraṃ maraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti yathā jāneyya tathā vadeyya katheyya bhaṇeyya dīpeyya[^065-10] vohareyyā ti, yathā hi jāneyya tathā vadeyya. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:


<!-- PTS Page 066 -->
<a id="page-066"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 066]*

Sakaṃ hi ditthiṃ kathaṃ accayeyya chandānunīto ruciyā niviṭṭho? sayaṃ samattāni pakubbamāno, yathā hi jāneyya tathā vadeyyā ti.

Sn_IV,3.3(=782): Yo attano sīlavatāni jantu anānupuṭṭho ca[^066-1] paresa[^066-2] pāvā[^066-3], anariyadhammaṃ kusalā tam āhu, yo ātumānaṃ sayam eva pāvā[^066-4].  || Nidd_I.3:3 ||

Yo attano sīlavatāni jantū ti. Yo ti yo yādiso yathāyutto yathāvihito yathāpakāro yanthānappatto[^066-5] yaṃdhammasamannāgato khattiyo vā brāhmaṇo vā vesso vā suddo vā gahaṭṭho vā pabbajito vā devo vā manusso vā. Sīlavatānī[^066-6] ti. Atthi sīlañ c'; eva vattañ[^066-7] ca, atthi vattaṃ[^066-7] na sīlaṃ. Katamaṃ sīlañ c'; eva vattañ ca? Idha bhikkhu sīlavā hoti, pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno, aṇumattesu vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu; yo tattha saṃyamo saṃvaro avītikkamo, idaṃ sīlaṃ; yaṃ samādānaṃ, taṃ vattaṃ[^066-7]; saṃvaratthena sīlaṃ, samādānatthena vattaṃ[^066-7]; idaṃ vuccati sīlañ c'; eva vattañ ca. Katamaṃ vattaṃ[^066-7] na sīlaṃ? Aṭṭha dhūtaṅgāni, āraññikaṅgaṃ[^066-8] piṇḍapātikaṅgaṃ paṃsukūlikaṅgaṃ tecīvarikaṅgaṃ sapadānacārikaṅgaṃ khalupacchābhattikaṅgaṃ nesajjikaṅgaṃ yathāsanthatikaṅgaṃ; idaṃ vuccati[^066-9] vattaṃ na[^066-9] sīlaṃ. Viriyasamādānam pi vuccati vattaṃ[^066-7] na sīlaṃ: Kāmaṃ taco ca nhārū ca aṭṭhi ca avasussatu, sarīre upasussatu maṃsalohitaṃ, yan taṃ purisathāmena purisabalena purisaviriyena purisaparakkamena pattab-


<!-- PTS Page 067 -->
<a id="page-067"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 067]*

baṃ, na taṃ apāpuṇitvā viriyassa saṇṭhānaṃ bhavissatī ti cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; evarūpaṃ viriyasamādānaṃ vuccati[^067-1] vattaṃ[^067-2] na sīlaṃ. [^067-3]Na tāvāhaṃ imaṃ pallaṅkaṃ bhindissāmi yāva me anupādāya āsavehi cittaṃ vimuccissatī ti cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; evarūpam pi viriyasamādānaṃ vuccati vattaṃ na sīlaṃ[^067-3]. [^067-4]*Nāssissaṃ, na pivissāmi, vihārato na nikkhamiṃ, na pi passaṃ nipātessaṃ taṇhāsalle anūhate ti cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; evarūpam pi viriyasamādānaṃ vuccati vattaṃ na sīlaṃ[^067-4]. Na tāvāhaṃ imamhā āsanā vuṭṭhahissāmi, caṅkamā orohissāmi, vihārā nikkhamissāmi, aḍḍhayogā nikkhamissāmi, pāsādā nikkhamissāmi, hammiyā nikkhamissāmi[^067-5], guhāya nikkhamissāmi leṇā nikkhamissāmi, kuṭiyā nikkhamissāmi, kūṭāgārā nikkhamissāmi, aṭṭā[^067-6] nikkhamissāmi, māḷā nikkhamissāmi, uddhaṇhā[^067-7] nikkhamissāmi, upaṭṭhānasālāya nikkhamissāmi, maṇḍapā nikkhamissāmi, rukkhamūlā nikkhamissāmi yāva me anupādāya[^067-8] āsavehi cittaṃ vimuccissatī ti cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; evarūpam pi viriyasamādānaṃ vuccati vattaṃ na sīlaṃ. Imasmiñ ñeva pubbaṇhasamayaṃ ariyadhammaṃ āharissāmi samāharissāmi adhigacchissāmi phusayissāmi[^067-9] sacchikarissāmi ti cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; evarūpam pi viriyasamādānaṃ vuccati vattaṃ na sīlaṃ. Imasmiñ ñeva majjhantikasamayaṃ sāyaṇhasamayam purebhattaṃ pacchābhattaṃ purimayāmaṃ[^067-10] majjhimayāmaṃ pacchimayāmaṃ kāḷe juṇhe vasse hemante gimhe, purime vayokhandhe, majjhime vayokhandhe, pacchime vayokhandhe ariyadhammaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 068 -->
<a id="page-068"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 068]*

āharissāmi samāharissāmi adhigacchissāmi phusayissāmi[^068-1] sacchikarissāmī ti cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; evarūpam pi viriyasamādānaṃ vuccati vattaṃ na sīlaṃ. Jantū ti satto naro māṇavo poso puggalo jīvo jagū[^068-2] jantu indagū manujo ti, yo attano sīlavatāni jantu. Anānupuṭṭho ca[^068-3] paresa pāvā[^068-4] ti. Paresan ti paresaṃ khattiyānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ vessānaṃ suddānaṃ gahaṭṭhānaṃ pabbajitānaṃ devānaṃ manussānaṃ. Anānupuṭṭho ti apuṭṭho apucchito anāyācito[^068-5] anajjhesito appasadito. Pāvā ti[^068-6] attano sīlaṃ vā vattaṃ vā sīlavattaṃ vā pāvadati. Aham asmi sīlasampanno ti vā, vattasampanno ti vā, sīlavattasampanno[^068-7] ti vā, jātiyā vā gottena vā kolaputtikena vā vaṇṇapokkharatāya vā dhanena vā ajjhenena vā kammāyatanena vā sippāyatanena vā vijjaṭṭhānena vā sutena vā paṭibhāṇena vā aññataraññatarena vā vatthunā, uccākulā pabbajito ti vā, mahākulā pabbajito ti vā, mahābhogakulā pabbajito ti vā, uḷārabhogakulā pabbajito ti vā, ñāto yasassī sagahaṭṭhapabbajitānan[^068-8] ti vā, lābhi 'mhi cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānan ti vā, suttantiko ti vā, vinayadharo ti vā, dhammakathiko ti va, āraññiko ti vā, piṇḍapātiko ti vā, paṃsukūliko ti vā, tecīvariko ti vā, sapadānacāriko ti vā, khalupacchābhattiko ti vā, nesajjiko ti va, yathāsanthatiko ti vā, paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā, dutiyassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā, tatiyassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā, catutthassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā, ākāsānañcāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā, viññāṇañcāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā, ākiñcaññāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā, nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā pāvadati katheti bhaṇati dīpayati voharatī, ti anānupuṭṭho ca paresa[^068-9] pāvā[^068-10].


<!-- PTS Page 069 -->
<a id="page-069"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 069]*

Anariyadhammaṃ kusalā tam āhū ti. Kusalā ti ye te khandhakusalā dhātukusalā āyatanakusalā paṭiccasamuppādakusalā satipaṭṭhānakusalā sammappadhānakusalā iddhippādakusalā indriyakusalā balakusalā bojjhaṅgakusalā maggakusalā phalakusalā nibbānakusalā, [^069-1]te kusalā[^069-1] evam āhaṃsu: Anariyānaṃ eso dhammo, n'; eso dhammo ariyānaṃ; bālānaṃ eso dhammo, n'; eso dhammo paṇḍitānaṃ; asappurisānaṃ eso dhammo, n'; eso dhammo sappurisānan ti evam āhaṃsu evaṃ kathenti evaṃ bhaṇanti[^069-2] evaṃ dīpayanti evaṃ voharantī ti, anariyadhammaṃ kusalā tam āhu. Yo ātumānaṃ sayam eva pāvā[^069-3] ti. Ātumā vuccati attā. Sayam eva pāvā ti sayam eva attānaṃ {pāvadati}[^069-4]: aham asmi sīlasampanno ti vā, vattasampanno ti vā, sīlavattasampanno ti vā, jātiyā vā gottena vā kolaputtikena vā vaṇṇapokkharatāya [^069-5]vā dhanena[^069-5] vā ajjhenena[^069-6] vā kammāyatanena vā sippāyatanena vā vijjaṭṭhānena vā sutena vā paṭibhāṇena[^069-7] vā aññataraññatarena vā vatthunā, uccākulā pabbajito ti vā, mahākulā pabbajito ti vā, mahābhogakulā pabbajito ti vā, uḷārabhogakulā[^069-8] pabbajito ti vā, ñāto yasassī sagahaṭṭhapabbajitānan[^069-9] ti vā, lābhi 'mhi cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānan ti vā, suttantiko ti vā, vinayadharo ti vā, dhammakathiko ti vā, āraññiko ti vā, piṇḍapātiko[^069-10] ti vā, paṃsukūliko ti vā, tecīvariko ti vā, sapadānacāriko ti vā, khalupacchābhattiko ti vā, nesajjiko ti vā, yathāsanthatiko ti vā, paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā, dutiyassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā, tatiyassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā, catutthassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā, ākāsānañcāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā, viññāṇañcāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā, ākiñcaññāya-


<!-- PTS Page 070 -->
<a id="page-070"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 070]*

tanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā, nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā pāvadati katheti bhaṇati dīpayati voharatī ti, yo ātumānaṃ sayam eva pāvā[^070-1]. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Yo attano sīlavatāni jantu anānupuṭṭho ca paresa pāvā[^070-2], anariyadhammaṃ kusalā tam āhu, yo ātumānaṃ sayam eva pāvā[^070-3] ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,3.4(=783): Santo ca bhikkhu abhinibbutatto iti 'han ti sīlesu akatthamāno[^070-4] tam ariyadhammaṃ kusalā vadanti, yass'; ussadā n'; atthi kuhiñci loke.  || Nidd_I.3:4 ||

Santo ca bhikkhu abhinibbutatto ti. Santo ti rāgassa santattā[^070-5] santo, dosassa santattā[^070-5] santo, mohassa santattā[^070-5] santo, kodhassa upanāhassa makkhassa paḷāsassa issāya macchariyassa māyāya sāṭheyyassa thambhassa sārambhassa mānassa atimānassa madassa pamādassa sabbakilesānaṃ sabbaduccaritānam sabbadarathānaṃ sabbapariḷāhānaṃ sabbasantāpānaṃ sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārānaṃ santattā samitattā vūpasamitattā[^070-6] vijjhātattā nibbutattā vigatattā paṭipassaddhattā santo vūpasanto[^070-7] nibbuto paṭipassaddho ti, santo[^070-8]. Bhikkhū ti sattannaṃ dhammānaṃ bhinnattā bhikkhu; sakkāyadiṭṭhi bhinnā hoti, vicikicchā bhinnā hoti, sīlabbataparāmāso[^070-9] bhinno hoti, rāgo bhinno hoti, doso bhinno hoti, moho bhinno hoti, māno bhinno hoti; bhinn'; assa honti pāpakā akusalā dhammā saṃkilesikā ponobbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇīyā.


<!-- PTS Page 071 -->
<a id="page-071"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 071]*

*Pajjena katena attanā, Sabhiyā ti Bhagavā, parinibbānagato vitiṇṇakaṃkho vibhavañ[^071-1] ca bhavañ ca vippahāya vusitavā khīṇapunabbhavo ti[^071-2]. Santo ca bhikkhu abhinibbutatto ti. Rāgassa nibbāpitattā[^071-3] dosassa nibbāpitattā[^071-3] mohassa nibbāpitattā abhinibbutatto, kodhassa upanāhassa makkhassa paḷāsassa issāya macchariyassa māyāya sāṭheyyassa thambhassa sārambhassa mānassa atimānassa madassa pamādassa sabbakilesānaṃ sabbaduccaritānaṃ sabbadarathānaṃ sabbapariḷāhānaṃ sabbasantāpānaṃ sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārānaṃ nibbāpitattā abhinibbutatto ti, santo ca bhikkhu abhinibbutatto. [^071-4]Iti 'han ti[^071-4] sīlesu akatthamāno ti. Iti 'han ti padasandhi padasaṃsaggo padapāripūri akkharasamavāyo byañjanasiliṭṭhatā padānupubbatā-m-etaṃ, iti 'han ti. Sīlesu akatthamāno ti. idh'; ekacco katthī hoti vikatthī; so katthati vikatthati[^071-5] aham asmi sīlasampanno ti vā vattasampanno ti vā sīlavattasampanno ti vā, jātiyā vā gottena vā kolaputtikena vā vaṇṇapokkharatāya vā . . . pe . . . n'; evasaññānāsaññāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā katthati vikatthati. Evaṃ na katthati na vikatthati, katthanā[^071-6] ārato virato paṭivirato nikkhanto nissaṭṭho vippamutto visaṃyutto vimariyādikatena cetasā viharati ti, iti 'han ti sīlesu akatthamāno. Tam ariyadhammaṃ kusalā vadantī ti. Kusalā ti ye te khandhakusalā[^071-7] dhātukusalā āyatanakusalā paṭiccasamup-


<!-- PTS Page 072 -->
<a id="page-072"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 072]*

pādakusalā satipaṭṭhānakusalā sammappadhānakusalā iddhippādakusalā indriyakusalā balakusalā bojjhaṅgakusalā maggakusalā[^072-1] phalakusalā nibbānakusalā, [^072-2]te kusalā[^072-2] evaṃ vadanti. Ariyānaṃ eso dhammo, n'; eso dhammo anariyānaṃ; paṇḍitānaṃ eso dhammo, n'; eso dhammo bālānaṃ; sappurisānaṃ eso dhammo, n'; eso dhammo asappurisānan ti, evaṃ vadanti evaṃ[^072-3] kathenti evaṃ bhaṇanti evaṃ dīpayanti evaṃ voharantī ti, tam ariyadhammaṃ kusalā vadanti. Yass'; ussadā n'; atthi kuhiñci loke ti. Yassā ti arahato khīṇāsavassa. Ussadā ti satt'; ussadā, rāgussado dosussado mohussado mānussado diṭṭhussado kilesussado kammussado. Yass'[^072-4] ime ussadā n'; atthi na santi na saṃvijjanti[^072-5] n'; upalabbhanti, pahīnā samucchinnā vūpasantā paṭipassaddhā[^072-6] abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā[^072-7]. Kuhiñcī ti kuhiñci kimhici katthaci ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā ajjhattabahiddhā vā. Loke ti apāyaloke manussaloke devaloke khandhaloke dhātuloke āyatanaloke ti, yass' ussadā n'; atthi kuhiñci loke. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Santo ca bhikkhu abhinibbutatto iti 'han ti sīlesu akatthamāno tam ariyadhammaṃ kusalā vadanti, yass'; ussadā n'; atthi kuhiñci loke ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,3.5(=784): Pakappitā saṃkhatā yassa dhammā purakkhatā[^072-8] santi avīvadātā[^072-9], yad attani passati ānisaṃsaṃ, tan[^072-10] nissito kuppa-paṭicca-santiṃ.  || Nidd_I.3:5 ||

Pakappitā saṃkhatā yassa dhammā ti. Pakappanā ti dve pakappanā, taṇhāpakappanā ca diṭṭhipakappanā ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhāpakappanā . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhipakappanā. Saṃkhatā ti saṃkhatā visaṃkhatā abhi-


<!-- PTS Page 073 -->
<a id="page-073"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 073]*

saṃkhatā saṇṭhapitā ti pi saṃkhatā; athavā aniccā saṃkhatā paṭiccasamuppannā khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā nirodhadhammā ti pi saṃkhatā. Yassā ti diṭṭhigatikassa. Dhammā vuccanti dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatānī ti, pakappitā saṃkhatā yassa dhammā. Purakkhatā[^073-1] santi avīvadātā[^073-2] ti. Purekkhārā ti dve purekkhārā, taṇhāpurekkhāro ca diṭṭhipurekkhāro ca . . . . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhāpurekkhāro . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhipurekkhāro. Tassa taṇhāpurekkhāro appahīno, diṭṭhipurekkhāro appaṭinissaṭṭho, tassa taṇhāpurekkhārassa appahīnattā, tassa[^073-3] diṭṭhipurekkhārassa appaṭinissaṭṭhattā, so taṇhaṃ vā diṭṭhiṃ vā purato katvā carati, taṇhādhajo taṇhāketu taṇhādhipateyyo diṭṭhidhajo diṭṭhiketu diṭṭhādhipateyyo taṇhāya vā diṭṭhiyā vā parivārito caratī ti, purakkhatā. Santī ti santi[^073-3] saṃvijjanti upalabbhanti[^073-4]. Avīvadātā[^073-5] ti avīvadātā[^073-5] avodātā aparisuddhā saṃkiliṭṭhā saṃkilesikā ti, purekkhatā santi avīvadātā[^073-5] Yad attani passati ānisaṃsan ti. Yad attanī ti yaṃ attani. Attā vuccati diṭṭhigataṃ; attano diṭṭhiyā dve ānisaṃse passati, {diṭṭhadhammikañ} ca ānisaṃsaṃ samparāyikañ ca ānisaṃsaṃ. Katamo[^073-6] diṭṭhiyā {diṭṭhadhammiko} ānisaṃso? Yaṃdiṭṭhiko satthā hoti, taṃdiṭṭhikā sāvakā honti; taṃdiṭṭhikaṃ satthāraṃ sāvakā sakkaronti garukaronti[^073-7] mānenti pūjenti apacitiṃ[^073-8] karonti; labhanti ca tatonidānaṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāraṃ. Ayaṃ diṭṭhiyā diṭṭhadhammiko ānisaṃso. Katamo diṭṭhiyā samparāyiko ānisaṃso? Ayaṃ diṭṭhi alaṃ nāgattāya[^073-9] vā supaṇṇattāya vā yakkhattāya vā asurattāya vā gandhabbattāya vā mahārājattāya vā indattāya vā brahmattāya vā devattāya vā. Ayaṃ diṭṭhi[^073-10]


<!-- PTS Page 074 -->
<a id="page-074"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 074]*

alaṃ suddhiyā visuddhiyā parisuddhiyā[^074-1] muttiyā vimuttiyā parimuttiyā; imāya diṭṭhiyā sujjhanti visujjhanti[^074-2] parisujjhanti muccanti vimuccanti parimuccanti, imāya diṭṭhiyā sujjhissāmi visujjhissāmi parisujjhissāmi muccissāmi[^074-3] vimuccissāmi parimuccissāmī ti āyatiṃ phalapāṭikaṃkhī[^074-4] hoti. Ayaṃ diṭṭhiyā samparāyiko ānisaṃso. Attano diṭṭhiyā ime dve ānisaṃse passati dakkhati oloketi nijjhāyati upaparikkhatī ti, yad attani passati ānisaṃsaṃ. Tan[^074-5] nissito kuppa-paticca-santin[^074-6] ti. Tisso santiyo, accantasanti tadaṅgasanti sammatisanti[^074-7]. Katamā accantasanti? Accantasanti vuccati amataṃ nibbānaṃ; yo so sabbasaṃkhārasamatho sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhakkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānaṃ; ayaṃ accantasanti. Katamā tadaṅgasanti? Paṭhamaṃ[^074-8] jhānaṃ samāpannassa nīvaraṇā santā honti, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpannassa vitakkavicārā santā honti, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpannassa pīti santā hoti[^074-9], catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpannassa sukhadukkhā[^074-10] santā honti, ākāsānañcāyatanaṃ samāpannassa rūpasaññā paṭighasaññā nānattasaññā santā honti, viññāṇañcāyatanaṃ samāpannassa ākāsānañcāyatanasaññā santā hoti, ākiñcaññāyatanaṃ samāpannassa viññāṇañcāyatanasaññā santā hoti[^074-9], n'; evasaññā-nāsaññāyatanaṃ samāpannassa ākiñcāññāyatanasaññā santā hoti; ayaṃ tadaṅgasanti. Katamā sammatisanti? Sammatisanti vuccanti dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatā[^074-11] diṭṭhisantiyo; api ca sammatisanti imasmiṃ atthe[^074-12] adhippetā santatī[^074-13] ti.


<!-- PTS Page 075 -->
<a id="page-075"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 075]*

Tan[^075-1] nissito[^075-2] kuppa-paṭicca-santin ti[^075-3] kuppasantiṃ pakuppasantim[^075-4] eritasantiṃ[^075-5] sameritasantiṃ[^075-6] calitasantiṃ[^075-6] ghaṭitasantiṃ[^075-7] kappitasantiṃ pakappitasantiṃ[^075-6] aniccaṃ saṃkhataṃ paṭiccasamuppannaṃ khayadhammaṃ vayadhammaṃ virāgadhammaṃ nirodhadhammaṃ santiṃ nissito[^075-8] āsito[^075-9] allīno upāgato ajjhosito adhimutto ti, tan nissito kuppa-paṭicca-santiṃ. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Pakappitā saṃkhatā yassa dhammā purakkhatā santi avīvadātā, yad attani passati ānisaṃsaṃ, tan nissito kuppa-paṭicca-santin[^075-10] ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,3.6(=785): Diṭṭhīnivesā na[^075-11] hi svātivattā dhammesu niccheyya samuggahītaṃ, tasmā naro tesu nivesanesu nidassatī[^075-12] ādiyati-cca dhammaṃ.  || Nidd_I.3:6 ||

Diṭṭhīnivesā na hi svātivattā ti. [^075-13]Diṭṭhīnivesā ti[^075-13] sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti abhinivesapparāmāso diṭṭhinivesanaṃ; asassato loko, antavā loko, anantavā loko, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ, aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīraṃ, hoti tathāgato parammaraṇā[^075-14], na hoti tathāgato parammaraṇā, hoti ca na ca hoti tathāgato parammaraṇā, n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato parammaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti abhinivesapparāmāso diṭṭhinivesanan[^075-15] ti, diṭṭhinivesā. Na hi


<!-- PTS Page 076 -->
<a id="page-076"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 076]*

svātivatā ti durativattā[^076-1] duttarā duppatarā dussamatikkamā dubbītivattā ti, diṭṭhīnivesā na hi svātivattā. Dhammesu niccheyya samuggahītan ti. Dhammesū ti dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatesu. Niccheyyā ti nicchinitvā vinicchinitvā vicinitvā pavicinitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā. [^076-2]Samuggahītan ti nivesanesu[^076-2] odhisaggāho[^076-3] vilaggāho varaggāho[^076-4] koṭṭhāsaggāho uccayaggāho samuccayaggāho: idaṃ saccaṃ tacchaṃ tathaṃ bhūtaṃ yāthāvaṃ aviparittan ti gahitaṃ parāmaṭṭhaṃ abhiniviṭṭhaṃ ajjhositaṃ adhimuttan ti, dhammesu niccheyya samuggahītaṃ. Tasmā naro tesu nivesanesū ti. Tasmā ti tasmā taṃkāraṇā taṃhetu taṃpaccayā[^076-5] taṃnidānā[^076-6]. Naro ti satto naro māṇavo poso puggalo jīvo jagū jantu indagū manujo. Tesu nivesanesū ti tesu diṭṭhinivesanesū ti, tasmā naro tesu nivesanesu. Nidassatī ādiyati-cca[^076-7] dhamman ti. Nidassatī[^076-8] ti dvīhi kāraṇehi nidassati, paravicchindanāya vā nidassati, anabhisambhuṇanto vā nidassati. Kathaṃ paravicchindanāya nidassati? Paro vicchindati: So satthā na sabbaññū, dhammo na svākkhāto, gaṇo na suppaṭipanno, diṭṭhi na bhaddikā, paṭipadā na suppaññattā, maggo na niyyāniko; n'; atth'; ettha suddhi vā visuddhi vā parisuddhi vā mutti vā vimutti vā parimutti vā; [^076-9]na tattha sujjhanti[^076-9] vā visujjhanti vā parisujjhanti vā muccanti vā vimuccanti vā parimuccanti vā; hīnā nihīnā omakā lāmakā jatukkā[^076-10] parittā ti. Evaṃ paro vicchindati; evaṃ vicchindiyamāno satthāraṃ nidas-


<!-- PTS Page 077 -->
<a id="page-077"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 077]*

sati, dhammakkhānaṃ nidassati, gaṇaṃ nidassati, diṭṭhiṃ nidassati, paṭipadaṃ nidassati, maggaṃ nidassati. Evaṃ paravicchindanāya nidassati. Kathaṃ anabhisambhuṇanto nidassati? Sīlaṃ anabhisambhuṇanto sīlaṃ nidassati, vattaṃ anabhisambhuṇanto vattaṃ nidassati, sīlavattaṃ anabhisambhuṇanto sīlavattaṃ nidassati. Evaṃ anabhisambhuṇanto nidassati. Ādiyati-cca[^077-1] dhamman ti. Satthāraṃ gaṇhāti, dhammakkhānaṃ gaṇhāti, gaṇaṃ gaṇhāti, diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti, paṭipadaṃ gaṇhāti, maggaṃ gaṇhāti, [^077-2]phalaṃ gaṇhāti[^077-2] parāmasati abhinivisatī ti, nidassatī ādiyati-cca dhammaṃ. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Diṭṭhīnivesā[^077-3] na hi svātivattā dhammesu niccheyya samuggahītaṃ, tasmā naro tesu nivesanesu nidassatī ādiyati-cca dhamman ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,3.7(=786): Dhonassa hi n'; atthi kuhiñci loke pakappitā diṭṭhi bhavābhavesu, māyañ ca mānañ ca pahāya dhono sa kena gaccheyya? Anūpayo so.  || Nidd_I.3:7 ||

Dhonassa hi n'; atthi kuhiñci loke pakappitā diṭṭhi bhavābhavesū ti. Dhono[^077-4] ti. Dhonā vuccati paññā, yā paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo[^077-5] dhammavicayo sallakhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā[^077-5] paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhavyā[^077-6] cintā upaparikkhā bhūri medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajjaññaṃ patodo[^077-7] paññā {paññindri-} yam paññābalaṃ paññāsaṭṭhaṃ[^077-8] paññāpāsādo[^077-9] paññāāloko paññā-obhāso[^077-10] paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi. Kiṃkāraṇā dhonā vuccati paññā? Tāya paññāya[^077-11]


<!-- PTS Page 078 -->
<a id="page-078"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 078]*

kāyaduccaritaṃ dhutañ ca [^078-1]dhotañ ca[^078-1] sandhotañ ca [^078-1]niddhotañ ca[^078-1], vacīduccaritaṃ [^078-2]dhutañ ca dhotañ ca sandhotañ ca niddhotañ ca[^078-2], manoduccaritaṃ dhutañ ca dhotañ ca sandhotañ ca niddhotañ ca, rāgo dhuto ca dhoto ca sandhoto ca niddhoto ca, doso moho kodho upanāho makkho paḷāso[^078-3] issā macchariyaṃ māyā sāṭheyyaṃ thambho sārambho māno atimāno mado pamādo, sabbe kilesā, sabbe duccaritā, sabbe darathā, sabbe pariḷāhā, sabbe santāpā, sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārā dhutā ca dhotā ca sandhotā ca niddhotā ca; taṃ-kāraṇā dhonā vuccati paññā. Athavā sammādiṭṭhiyā micchādiṭṭhi dhutā ca dhotā ca sandhotā ca niddhotā ca, sammāsaṃkappena micchāsaṃkappo dhuto ca dhoto ca sandhoto ca niddhoto ca, sammāvācāya micchāvācā dhutā ca, sammākammantena[^078-4] micchākammanto dhuto ca, sammā-ājīvena micchāājīvo dhuto ca, sammāvāyāmena micchāvāyāmo dhuto ca, sammāsatiyā micchāsati dhutā ca, sammāsamādhinā micchāsamādhi dhuto ca, sammāñāṇena[^078-5] micchāñāṇaṃ dhutañ ca, sammāvimuttiyā micchāvimutti dhutā ca dhotā cā sandhotā ca niddhotā ca. Athavā ariyena aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena sabbe kilesā, sabbe duccaritā, sabbe darathā, sabbe pariḷāhā, sabbe santāpā, sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārā dhutā ca dhotā ca sandhotā ca niddhotā ca. [^078-6]Arahā imehi[^078-6] dhoneyyehi dhammehi upeto samupeto upagato samupagato upapanno samupapanno samannāgato; tasmā arahā dhono. So dhutarāgo dhutapāpo dhutakileso dhutapariḷāho ti dhono. Kuhiñcī ti kuhiñci kimhici katthaci ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā ajjhattabahiddhā vā. Loke ti apāyaloke . . . pe . . . āyatanaloke. Pakappanā ti dve pakappanā,


<!-- PTS Page 079 -->
<a id="page-079"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 079]*

taṇhāpakappanā ca diṭṭhipakappanā ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhāpakappanā . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhipakappanā. Bhavābhavesu ti bhavābhave, kammabhave, punabbhave, kāmabhave kammabhave, kāmabhave punabbhave, rūpabhave kammabhave, rūpabhave punabbhave, arūpabhave kammabhave, arūpabhave punabbhave, punappunaṃ[^079-1] bhave[^079-2] [^079-3]punappunaṃ gatiyā[^079-3] punappunaṃ upapattiyā punappunaṃ paṭisandhiyā[^079-4] punappunaṃ attabhāvābhinibbattiyā. Dhonassa hi n'; atthi kuhiñci loke pakappitā diṭṭhi bhavābhavesū ti; [^079-5]dhonassa kuhiñci loke bhavābhavesu ca[^079-6] kappitā[^079-5] pakappitā abhisaṃkhatā saṇṭhapitā diṭṭhi n'; atthi na santi na saṃvijjanti n'; upalabbhanti, pahīnā samucchinnā vūpasantā paṭipassaddhā abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā ti, dhonassa hi n'; atthi kuhiñci loke pakappitā diṭṭhi bhavābhavesu. Māyañ ca mānañ ca pahāya dhono ti. Māyā vuccati vañcanikā cariyā. Idh'; ekacco kāyena duccaritaṃ caritvā, vacasā[^079-7] duccaritaṃ caritvā, manasā duccaritaṃ caritvā, tassa paṭicchādanahetu pāpikaṃ icchaṃ paṇidahati: Mā maṃ jaññā[^079-8] ti icchati, mā maṃ jaññā[^079-8] ti saṃkappeti, mā maṃ jaññā[^079-8] ti vācaṃ bhāsati, mā maṃ jaññā[^079-8] ti kāyena parakkamati. Yā evarūpā māyā māyāvitā accasarā[^079-9] vañcañā nikati nikiraṇā[^079-10] pariharaṇā kuhanā[^079-11] parikuhaṇā chādanā paricchādanā anuttānikammaṃ[^079-12] anāvikammaṃ[^079-12] vocchādanā pāpakiriyā; ayaṃ vuccati māyā. Māno ti. Ekavidhena māno, yā cittassa uṇṇati. Duvidhena māno: attukkaṃsanamāno paravambhanamāno. Tividhena māno: seyyo 'ham asmī ti māno, sadiso 'ham


<!-- PTS Page 080 -->
<a id="page-080"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 080]*

asmī ti māno, hīno 'ham asmī ti māno. Catubbidhena māno: lābhena mānaṃ janeti, yasena[^080-1] mānaṃ janeti, pasaṃsāya mānaṃ janeti, sukhena mānaṃ janeti. Pañcavidhena māno: lābhi 'mhi manāpikānaṃ rūpānan ti mānaṃ janeti, lābhi 'mhi manāpikānaṃ saddānaṃ gandhānaṃ rasānaṃ phoṭṭhabbānan ti mānaṃ janeti. Chabbidhena māno: cakkhusampadāya mānaṃ janeti, sotasampadayā[^080-2] ghānasampadāya jivhāsampadāya kāyasampadāya manosampadāya mānaṃ janeti. Sattavidhena māno: māno atimāno mānātimāno omāno adhimāno asmimāno micchāmāno. Aṭṭhavidhena māno: lābhena mānaṃ janeti, alābhena omānaṃ janeti, yasena mānaṃ janeti, ayasena omānaṃ janeti, pasaṃsāya mānaṃ janeti, [^080-3]nindāya omānaṃ janeti[^080-3], sukhena mānaṃ janeti, dukkhena omānaṃ janeti. Navavidhena māno: seyyassa seyyo 'ham asmī ti māno, [^080-4]seyyassa sadiso 'ham asmī ti māno, seyyassa hīno 'ham asmī ti māno[^080-4], sadisassa[^080-5] seyyo 'ham asmī ti māno, sadisassa sadiso 'ham asmī ti māno, sadisassa hīno 'ham asmī ti māno, hīnassa seyyo 'ham asmī ti māno, hīnassa sadiso 'ham asmī ti māno, hīnassa hīno 'ham asmī ti māno. Dasavidhena māno: idh'; ekacco mānaṃ janeti jātiyā vā gottena vā kolaputtikena vā vaṇṇapokkharatāya vā dhanena vā ajjhenena vā kammāyatanena vā sippāyatanena vā vijjaṭṭhānena vā sutena vā paṭibhāṇena vā aññataraññatarena vā vatthunā. Yo evarūpo māno maññanā maññitattaṃ uṇṇati uṇṇamo dhajo saṃpaggāho ketukamyatā cittassa, ayaṃ vuccati māno. Māyañ ca mānañ ca pahāya dhono ti[^080-6] māyañ ca mānañ ca pahāya pajahitvā vinoditvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃgametvā ti, māyañ ca mānañ ca pahāya dhono. Sa[^080-7] kena gaccheyya anūpayo so ti. Upayo[^080-8] ti dve upayā, taṇhūpayo ca diṭṭhūpayo ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhūpayo . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhūpayo. Tassa taṇhūpayo


<!-- PTS Page 081 -->
<a id="page-081"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 081]*

pahīno, diṭṭhūpayo paṭinissaṭṭho; taṇhūpayassa pahīnattā, diṭṭhūpayassa paṭinissaṭṭhattā anūpayo so[^081-1] kena rāgena gaccheyya, kena dosena gaccheyya, kena mohena gaccheyya, kena mānena gaccheyya, kāya diṭṭhiyā gaccheyya, kena uddhaccena gaccheyya kāya vicikicchāya gaccheyya, kehi anusayehi[^081-2] gaccheyya ratto ti vā duṭṭho ti vā mūḷho ti vā vinibandho ti vā parāmaṭṭho ti vā vikkhepagato ti vā aniṭṭhaṅgato[^081-3] ti vā thāmagato ti vā? Te abhisaṃkhārā pahīnā, abhisaṃkhārānaṃ pahīnattā gatiyā[^081-4] kena gaccheyya nerayiko ti vā tiracchānayoniko ti vā pittivisayiko[^081-5] ti vā manusso ti vā devo ti vā rūpī ti vā arūpī ti vā saññī ti vā asaññī ti vā nevasaññī-nāsaññī ti vā? So hetu n'; atthi paccayo n'; atthi kāraṇaṃ n'; atthi yena gaccheyyā ti, sa[^081-6] kena[^081-7] gaccheyya? Anūpayo so. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Dhonassa hi n'; atthi kuhiñci loke pakappitā diṭṭhi bhavābhavesu; māyañ ca mānañ ca pahāya dhono, sa[^081-8] kena[^081-9] gaccheyya? Anūpayo so ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,3.8(=787): Upayo hi dhammesu upeti vādaṃ, anūpayaṃ kena kathaṃ vadeyya? attaṃ nirattaṃ na hi tassa atthi, adhosi so diṭṭhim idh'; eva sabbaṃ.  || Nidd_I.3:8 ||

Upayo hi dhammesu upeti vādan ti. Upayo ti dve upayā, taṇhūpayo ca diṭṭhūpayo ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhūpayo . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhūpayo. Tassa taṇhūpayo appahīno, diṭṭhūpayo appaṭinissaṭṭho, taṇhūpayassa appahīnattā, diṭṭhūpayassa appaṭinissaṭṭhattā dhammesu vādaṃ upeti, ratto ti vā duṭṭho ti vā mūḷho ti vā vinibandho ti vā parāmaṭṭho ti vā vikkhepagato ti vā aniṭ-


<!-- PTS Page 082 -->
<a id="page-082"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 082]*

ṭhaṅgato[^082-1] ti vā thāmagato ti vā; te abhisaṃkhārā appahīnā, abhisaṃkhārānaṃ appahīnattā gatiyā vādaṃ upeti, nerayiko ti vā tiracchānayoniko ti vā pittivisayiko ti vā manusso ti vā devo ti vā rūpī ti vā arūpī ti vā saññī ti vā asaññī ti vā nevasaññī-nāsaññī ti vā vādaṃ upeti upagacchati gaṇhāti parāmasati abhinivisatī ti, upayo hi dhammesu upeti vādaṃ. Anūpayaṃ kena kathaṃ vadeyyā? ti. Upayo ti dve upayā, taṇhūpayo ca diṭṭhūpayo ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhūpayo . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhūpayo. Tassa taṇhūpayo pahīno, diṭṭhūpayo paṭinissaṭṭho, taṇhūpayassa pahīnattā, diṭṭhūpayassa paṭinissaṭṭhattā anūpayaṃ[^082-2] kena[^082-3] rāgena vadeyya, kena dosena vadeyya, kena mohena vadeyya, kena mānena vadeyya, kāya diṭṭhiyā vadeyya, kena uddhaccena vadeyya, kāya vicikicchāya vadeyya, kehi anusayehi vadeyya ratto ti vā duṭṭho ti vā mūḷho ti vā vinibandho ti vā parāmaṭṭho ti vā vikkhepagato ti vā aniṭṭhaṅgato[^082-4] ti vā thāmagato ti vā? Te abhisaṃkhārā pahīnā, abhisaṃkhārānaṃ pahīnattā gatiyā kena vadeyya nerayiko ti vā . . . pe . . . nevasaññī nāsaññī ti vā? So hetu n'; atthi, paccayo n'; atthi, kāraṇaṃ n' atthi yena vadeyya katheyya bhaṇeyya dīpayeyya vohareyyā ti, anūpayaṃ kena kathaṃ vadeyya? Attaṃ nirattaṃ na hi tassa atthī ti. Attā ti sassatadiṭṭhi[^082-5] n'; atthi, nirattā ti ucchedadiṭṭhi n'; atthi, attā ti gahitaṃ n'; atthi, nirattā ti muñcitabbaṃ n'; atthi; yass' atthi gahitaṃ tass'; atthi muñcitabbaṃ, yass'; atthi muñcitabbaṃ tass'; atthi gahitaṃ; gahaṇamuñcanaṃ[^082-6] samatikkanto[^082-7] arahā vuddhiparihāniṃ vītivatto. So vuṭṭhavāso ciṇṇacaraṇo . . . pe[^082-8] . . . n'; atthī tassa punabbhavo ti, attaṃ nirattaṃ na hi tassa atthi.


<!-- PTS Page 083 -->
<a id="page-083"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 083]*

Adhosi so diṭṭhim idh'; eva sabban ti. Tassa dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni pahīnāni samucchinnāni vūpasantāni paṭipassaddhāni abhabbuppattikāni ñāṇagginā daḍḍhāni. So sabbaṃ[^083-1] diṭṭhigataṃ idh'; eva adhosi dhuni niddhuni[^083-2] pajahi vinodesi byanti-akāsi anabhāvaṃ gamesī ti, adhosi so diṭṭhim idh'; eva sabbaṃ. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Upayo hi dhammesu upeti vādaṃ, anupayaṃ kena kathaṃ vadeyya? attaṃ nirattaṃ na hi tassa atthi, adhosi so diṭṭhim idh'; eva sabban ti.

TATIYO[^083-3] DUṬṬHAṬṬHAKASUTTANIDDESO[^083-4] NIṬṬHITO[^083-5].


<!-- PTS Page 084 -->
<a id="page-084"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 084]*

CATUTTHO[^084-1] SUDDHAṬṬHAKASUTTANIDDESO[^084-2].

Sn_IV,4.1(=788): Passāmi suddhaṃ paramaṃ arogaṃ[^084-3], diṭṭhena saṃsuddhi narassa hoti, evābhijānaṃ[^084-4] paraman ti ñatvā suddhānupassī ti[^084-5] pacceti ñāṇaṃ.  || Nidd_I.4:1 ||

Passāmi suddhaṃ paramaṃ arogan ti. Passāmi [^084-6]suddhan ti passāmi[^084-6] suddhaṃ, dakkhāmi suddhaṃ, olokemi suddhaṃ, nijjhāyāmi suddhaṃ, upaparikkhāmi suddhaṃ. Paramaṃ arogan ti paramaṃ ārogyappattaṃ[^084-7] khemappattaṃ[^084-8] tāṇappattaṃ[^084-9] leṇappattaṃ[^084-9] saraṇappattaṃ parāyanappattaṃ abhayappattaṃ accutappattaṃ amatappattaṃ nibbānappattan ti, passāmi suddhaṃ paramaṃ arogaṃ. Diṭṭhena saṃsuddhi narassa hotī ti cakkhuviññāṇena[^084-10] rūpadassanena narassa suddhi visuddhi parisuddhi mutti vimutti parimutti hoti, naro sujjhati visujjhati parisujjhati muccati vimuccati parimuccatī ti, diṭṭhena saṃsuddhi narassa hoti. Evābhijānaṃ paraman ti ñatvā ti evaṃ abhijānanto ājānanto vijānanto paṭivijānanto pativijjhanto, idaṃ paramaṃ aggaṃ seṭṭhaṃ viseṭṭhaṃ pāmokkhaṃ uttamaṃ pavaran[^084-11] ti ñatvā jānitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā ti, evābhijānaṃ paraman ti ñatvā.


<!-- PTS Page 085 -->
<a id="page-085"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 085]*

Suddhānupassī ti pacceti ñāṇan ti. [^085-1]Yo suddhaṃ passati so suddhānupassī. Pacceti ñāṇan ti cakkhuviññāṇena[^085-2] rūpadassanaṃ[^085-3] ñāṇan ti pacceti, maggo ti pacceti, patho ti pacceti, niyyānan ti paccetī ti, suddhānupassī ti pacceti ñāṇaṃ. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Passāmi suddhaṃ paramaṃ arogaṃ, diṭṭhena saṃsuddhi narassa hoti, evābhijānaṃ paraman ti ñātvā, suddhānupassī ti pacceti ñāṇan ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,4.2(=789): Diṭṭhena ce suddhi narassa hoti, ñāṇena vā so pajahāti dukkhaṃ, aññena so sujjhati sopadhīko, diṭṭhī hi naṃ pāva[^085-4] tathā vadānaṃ.  || Nidd_I.4:2 ||

Diṭṭhena ce suddhi narassa hotī ti cakkhuviññāṇena[^085-5]* rūpadassanena ce narassa suddhi visuddhi parisuddhi mutti vimutti parimutti hoti, naro sujjhati visujjhati parisujjhati muccati vimuccati parimuccatī ti, diṭṭhena ce suddhi narassa hoti. Ñāṇena vā so pajahāti dukkhan ti cakkhuviññāṇena[^085-5] rūpadassanena ce naro jātidukkhaṃ pajahati, jarādukkhaṃ pajahati, byādhidukkhaṃ pajahati, maraṇadukkhaṃ pajahati, sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsadukkhaṃ pajahatī ti, ñāṇena vā so pajahāti dukkhaṃ. Aññena so sujjhati sopadhīko ti aññena asuddhimaggena micchāpaṭipadāya aniyyānapathena[^085-6] aññatra satipaṭṭhānehi aññatra sammappadhānehi aññatra iddhippādehi[^085-7] aññatra indriyehi aññatra balehi aññatra bojjhaṅgehi aññatra ariyaṭṭhaṅgikamaggena[^085-8] naro sujjhati visujjhati


<!-- PTS Page 086 -->
<a id="page-086"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 086]*

parisujjhati muccati vimuccati parimuccati. Sopadhīko ti sarāgo sadoso samiho samāno sataṇho sadiṭṭhi sakileso sa-upādāno ti, aññena so sujjhati sopadhīko. Diṭṭhī hi naṃ pāva[^086-1] tathā vadānan ti sā va diṭṭhi taṃ puggalaṃ {pāvadati}[^086-2]; iti vā yaṃ puggalo micchādiṭṭhiko viparītadassano ti[^086-3]. Tathā vadānan ti tathā vadantaṃ kathentaṃ bhaṇantaṃ[^086-4] dīpayantaṃ voharantaṃ: sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti tathā vadantaṃ[^086-5] kathentaṃ bhaṇantaṃ dīpayantaṃ voharantaṃ asassato[^086-6] loko, antavā loko, anantavā loko, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ, aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīraṃ, hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā, na hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā, hoti ca na ca hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā, n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti tathā vadantaṃ[^086-5] kathentaṃ bhaṇantaṃ dīpayantaṃ voharantan ti, diṭṭhī hi naṃ pāva[^086-1] tathā vadānaṃ. Ten' āha Bhagavā:

Diṭṭhena ce suddhi narassa hoti, ñāṇena vā so pajahāti dukkhaṃ, aññena so sujjhati sopadhīko, diṭṭhī hi naṃ pāva[^086-1] tathā vadānan ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,4.3(=790): Na brāhmaṇo aññato suddhim āha diṭṭhe sute sīlavate mute vā, puññe ca pāpe ca anūpalitto, attañjaho na-y-idha pakubbamāno[^086-7].  || Nidd_I.4:3 ||

Na brāhmaṇo aññato suddhim āha diṭṭhe sute sīlavate mute vā ti. Nā ti paṭikkhepo. Brāhmaṇo ti sattannaṃ dhammānaṃ bāhitattā brāhmaṇo; sakkāyadiṭṭhi bāhitā hoti, vicikicchā bāhitā hoti, sīlabbataparāmāso[^086-8] bāhito


<!-- PTS Page 087 -->
<a id="page-087"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 087]*

hoti, rāgo bāhito hoti, doso bāhito hoti, moho bāhito hoti, māno bāhito hoti, bāhit'; assa honti pāpakā akusalā dhammā saṅkilesikā ponobbhavikā sadarā[^087-1] dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇīyā: *Bāhetvā sabbapāpakāni Sabhiyā ti Bhagavā vimalo sādhusamāhito ṭhitatto saṃsāram aticca kevalī so, anissito[^087-2] tādi pavuccate[^087-3] sa brahmā. Na brāhmaṇo aññato suddhim āhā ti brāhmaṇo aññena asuddhimaggena micchāpaṭipadāya aniyyānapathena aññatra satipaṭṭhānehi aññatra sammappadhānehi aññatra iddhippādehi aññatra indriyehi aññatra balehi aññatra bojjhaṅgehi aññatra ariyaṭṭhaṅgikamaggena[^087-4], suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ n' āha na katheti na bhaṇati na dīpayati na voharatī ti, na brāhmaṇo aññato suddhim āha. Diṭṭhe sute sīlavate mute vā ti. Sant'; eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā diṭṭhasuddhikā; te ekaccānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanaṃ maṅgalaṃ paccenti; ekaccānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanaṃ amaṅgalaṃ paccenti. Katamesaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanaṃ maṅgalaṃ paccenti? Te kālato vuṭṭhahitvā abhimaṅgalagatāni rūpāṇi passanti: vātasakuṇaṃ[^087-5] passanti, pussaveḷuvalaṭṭhiṃ[^087-6] passanti, gabbhinitthiṃ passanti, kumārikaṃ[^087-7] khandhe āropetvā gacchantaṃ passanti, puṇṇaghaṭaṃ passanti, rohitamacchaṃ passanti, ājaññaṃ passanti, ājaññarathaṃ passanti,


<!-- PTS Page 088 -->
<a id="page-088"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 088]*

usabhaṃ passanti, gokapilaṃ passanti; evarūpānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanaṃ maṅgalaṃ paccenti. Katamesaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanaṃ amaṅgalaṃ paccenti? Palālapuñjaṃ passanti, takkaghaṭaṃ passanti, rittaghaṭaṃ passanti, naṭaṃ passanti, naggasamaṇaṃ[^088-1] passanti, kharaṃ passanti, kharayānaṃ passanti, ekayuttayānaṃ passanti, kāṇaṃ passanti, kuṇiṃ passanti, khañjaṃ passanti, pakkhahataṃ[^088-2] passanti, jiṇṇakaṃ passanti, byādhikaṃ passanti, mataṃ[^088-3] passanti; evarūpānaṃ rūpānaṃ dassanaṃ amaṅgalaṃ paccenti. Ime te samaṇabrāhmaṇā diṭṭhasuddhikā, te diṭṭhena suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ paccenti. Sant'; eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sutasuddhikā; te ekaccānaṃ saddānaṃ savanaṃ maṅgalaṃ paccenti; ekaccānaṃ saddānaṃ savanaṃ amaṅgalaṃ paccenti. Katamesaṃ saddānaṃ savanaṃ maṅgalaṃ paccenti? Te kālato vuṭṭhahitvā abhimaṅgalagatāni[^088-4] saddāni suṇanti: vaḍḍhā ti vā vaḍḍhamānā ti vā puṇṇā ti vā pussā[^088-5] ti vā assokā[^088-6] ti vā sumanā ti vā sunakkhattā ti vā sumaṅgalā ti vā sirī ti vā sirivaḍḍhā ti vā; evarūpānaṃ saddānaṃ savanaṃ maṅgalaṃ paccenti. Katamesaṃ saddānaṃ savanaṃ amaṅgalaṃ paccenti? Kāṇo ti vā kuṇī ti vā khañjo ti vā pakkhahato[^088-7] ti vā jiṇṇako ti vā byādhiko ti vā mato ti vā chinnan ti vā bhinnan ti vā daḍḍhan ti vā naṭṭhan ti vā n'; atthī[^088-8] ti vā; evarūpānaṃ saddānaṃ savanaṃ amaṅgalaṃ paccenti. Ime te samaṇabrāhmaṇā sutasuddhikā te sutena suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ paccenti. Sant'; eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sīlasuddhikā; te sīlamattena saṃyamamattena saṃvaramattena avītikkamamattena


<!-- PTS Page 089 -->
<a id="page-089"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 089]*

suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ paccenti. Samaṇo[^089-1] Muṇḍikāputto evam āha: Catūhi kho ahaṃ thapati[^089-2] dhammehi samannāgataṃ purisapuggalaṃ paññāpemi sampannakusalaṃ paramakusalaṃ uttamapattippattaṃ samaṇaṃ ayojjhaṃ[^089-3]. Katamehi catūhi? Idha thapati[^089-4] na kāyena pāpakammaṃ karoti, na pāpikaṃ[^089-5] vācaṃ bhāsati, na pāpakaṃ saṃkappaṃ[^089-6] saṃkappeti, na pāpakaṃ ājīvaṃ ājīvati. Imehi kho ahaṃ thapati[^089-4] catūhi dhammehi samannāgataṃ purisapuggalaṃ paññāpemi sampannakusalaṃ paramakusalaṃ uttamapattippattaṃ samaṇaṃ ayojjhaṃ. Evaṃ eva sant'; eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sīlasuddhikā; te sīlamattena saṃyamamattena saṃvaramattena avītikkamamattena suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ paccenti. Sant'; eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā vattasuddhikā[^089-7]. Te hatthivattikā[^089-8] vā honti, assavattikā vā honti, govattikā vā honti, kukkuravattikā vā honti, kākavattikā vā honti, Vāsudevavattikā vā honti, Baladevavattikā vā honti, Puṇṇabhaddavattikā vā honti, Maṇibhaddavattikā vā honti, aggivattikā vā honti, nāgavattikā vā honti, supaṇṇavattikā vā honti, yakkhavattikā vā honti, asuravattikā vā honti, gandhabbavattikā vā honti, mahārājavattikā vā honti, candavattikā vā honti, suriyavattikā vā honti, Indavattikā vā honti, Brahmavattikā vā honti, devavattikā vā honti, disavattikā vā honti. Ime te samaṇabrāhmaṇā vattasuddhikā; tena vattena suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ paccenti. Sant'; eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā mutavisuddhikā. Te kālato vuṭṭhahitvā[^089-9] paṭhaviṃ āmasanti, haritaṃ āmasanti,


<!-- PTS Page 090 -->
<a id="page-090"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 090]*

gomayaṃ āmasanti, kacchapaṃ[^090-1] āmasanti, jālaṃ[^090-2] akkamanti[^090-3], tilavāhaṃ[^090-4] āmasanti, pussatilaṃ[^090-5] khādanti, pussatelaṃ makkhenti, pussadantakaṭṭhaṃ khādanti, pussamattikāya nhāyanti, pussasāṭakaṃ[^090-6] nivāsenti, pussaveṭṭhanaṃ[^090-7] veṭṭhanti. Ime te samaṇabrāhmaṇā mutasuddhikā; tena mutena suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ paccenti. Na brāhmaṇo aññato suddhim āha diṭṭhe sute sīlavate mute vā ti brāhmaṇo diṭṭhasuddhiyā pi suddhiṃ n'; āha, sutasuddhiyā pi suddhiṃ n'; āha, sīlasuddhiyā pi suddhiṃ n'; āha, vattasuddhiyā pi suddhiṃ n'; āha, mutasuddhiyā pi suddhiṃ n'; āha na katheti na bhaṇati na dīpayati na voharatī ti, na brāhmaṇo aññato suddhim āha diṭṭhe sute sīlavatre mute vā. Puññe ca pāpe ca anūpalitto ti. Puññaṃ vuccati yaṃ kiñci tedhātukaṃ[^090-8] kusalābhisaṃkhāraṃ; apuññaṃ vuccati sabbaṃ akusalaṃ. Yato puññābhisaṃkhāro ca apuññābhisaṃkhāro ca āneñjābhisaṃkhāro ca pahīnā honti ucchinnamūlā tālavatthukatā anabhāvaṅgatā[^090-9] āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, ettāvatā[^090-10] puññe ca pāpe ca na limpati [^090-11]na saṃlimpati[^090-11] na upalimpati, alitto asaṃlitto anūpalitto nikkhanto nissaṭṭho[^090-12] vippamutto visaṃyutto vimariyādikatena[^090-13] cetasā viharatī ti, puññe ca pāpe ca anūpalitto. Attañjaho na-y-idha pakubbamāno ti. Attañjaho ti attadiṭṭhijaho; attañjaho ti gāhajaho[^090-14]; attañjaho ti taṇhāvasena diṭṭhivasena gahitaṃ parāmaṭṭhaṃ abhiniviṭṭhaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 091 -->
<a id="page-091"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 091]*

ajjhositaṃ adhimuttaṃ, [^091-1]sabbaṃ taṃ[^091-1] cattaṃ hoti vantaṃ muttaṃ pahīnaṃ paṭinissaṭṭhaṃ. Na-y-idha pakubbamāno[^091-2] ti puññābhisaṃkhāraṃ vā apuññābhisaṃkhāraṃ vā āneñjābhisaṃkhāraṃ vā akubbamāno[^091-3] ajanayamāno asañjanayamāno anibbattayamāno anabhinibbattayamāno ti, attañjaho na-y-idha pakubbamāno. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Na brāhmaṇo aññato suddhim āha diṭṭhe sute sīlavate mute vā, puññe ca pāpe ca anūpalitto, attañjaho na-y-idha pakubbamāno ti.
