<!-- PTS Page 142 -->
<a id="page-142"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 142]*

Sn_IV,7.2(=815): Methunam anuyuttassa Metteyyā ti Bhagavā mussat'; evāpi sāsanaṃ, micchā ca paṭipajjati, etaṃ tasmim anāriyaṃ.  || Nidd_I.7:2 ||

Methunam anuyuttassā ti. Methunadhammo nāma yo so asaddhammo gāmadhammo vasaladhammo duṭṭhullo odakantiko rahasso dvayadvayasamāpatti[^142-1]. Kiṃkāraṇā vuccati methunadhammo? Ubhinnaṃ rattānaṃ sārattānaṃ avassutānaṃ pariyuṭṭhitānaṃ pariyādiṇṇacittānaṃ ubhinnaṃ sadisānaṃ dhammo ti taṃkāraṇā vuccati methunadhammo. Yathā ubho kalahakārakā methunakā ti vuccanti, ubho bhaṇḍanakārakā methunakā ti vuccanti, ubho bhassakārakā methunakā ti vuccanti, ubho vivādakārakā methunakā ti vuccanti, ubho adhikaraṇakārakā methunakā ti vuccanti, ubho vādino methunakā ti vuccanti, ubho sallāpakā methunakā ti vuccanti; evam eva ubhinnaṃ rattānaṃ sārattānam avassutānam pariyuṭṭhitānam pariyādiṇṇacittānam ubhinnaṃ sadisānaṃ dhammo ti taṃkāraṇā vuccati methunadhammo. Methunam anuyuttassā ti methunadhamme yuttassa payuttassa āyuttassa samāyuttassa taccaritassa tabbahulassa taggarukassa tanninnassa tapponassa tappabbhārassa tadadhimuttassa tadādhipateyyassā ti, methunam anuyuttassa. Metteyyā ti Bhagavā taṃ theraṃ gottena ālapati. Bhagavā ti gāravādhivacanaṃ; api ca bhaggarāgo ti Bhagavā, bhaggadoso ti Bhagavā, bhaggamoho ti Bhagavā, bhaggadiṭṭhī ti Bhagavā, bhaggakaṇṭako[^142-2] ti Bhagavā, bhaggakileso ti Bhagavā; bhajī vibhajī paṭibhajī[^142-3] dhammaratanan ti Bhagavā; bhavānaṃ antakaro ti Bhagavā; bhāvitakāyo ti bhāvitasīlo bhāvitacitto bhāvitapañño ti Bhagavā; bhajī vā Bhagavā araññavanapatthāni[^142-4] pantāni senāsanāni appasaddāni appanigghosāni vijanavātāni manussarāhaseyyakāni[^142-5] paṭisallānasārūpānī ti Bhagavā; bhāgī vā


<!-- PTS Page 143 -->
<a id="page-143"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 143]*

Bhagavā cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānan ti Bhagavā; bhāgī vā Bhagavā attharasassa dhammarasassa vimuttirasassa adhisīlassa adhicittassa adhipaññāyā ti Bhagavā; bhāgī vā Bhagavā catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ catunnaṃ appamaññānaṃ catunnaṃ arūpasamāpattīnan ti Bhagavā; bhāgī vā Bhagavā aṭṭhannaṃ vimokkhānam aṭṭhannaṃ abhibhāyatanānaṃ navannaṃ anupubbavihārasamāpattīnan ti Bhagavā; bhāgī vā Bhagavā dasannaṃ saññābhāvanānaṃ dasannaṃ kasiṇasamāpattīnam ānāpānasatisamāpattiyā[^143-1] asubhasamāpattiyā ti Bhagavā; bhāgī vā Bhagavā catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ catunnaṃ sammappadhānānaṃ catunnam iddhippādānaṃ pañcannaṃ indriyānaṃ pañcannaṃ balānaṃ sattannaṃ bojjhaṅgānam ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassā ti Bhagavā; bhāgī vā Bhagavā dasannaṃ tathāgatabalānaṃ catunnaṃ vesārajjānaṃ catunnaṃ paṭisambhidānaṃ channaṃ abhiññānaṃ[^143-2] channaṃ buddhadhammānan ti Bhagavā; Bhagavā ti n'; etaṃ nāmaṃ mātarā kataṃ, na pitarā kataṃ, na bhātarā kataṃ, na bhaginiyā kataṃ, na mittāmaccehi kataṃ, na ñātisālohitehi kataṃ, na samaṇabrāhmaṇehi kataṃ, na devatāhi kataṃ; vimokkhantikam etaṃ Buddhānaṃ Bhagavantānaṃ bodhityā mūle saha sabbaññutaññāṇassa paṭilābhā sacchikā paññatti yadidaṃ Bhagavā ti, Metteyyā ti Bhagavā. Mussat'; evāpi sāsanan ti. Dvīhi kāraṇehi sāsanaṃ mussati: pariyattisāsanam pi mussati, paṭipattisāsanam pi mussati. Katamaṃ pariyattisāsanaṃ? Yaṃ tassa pariyāpuṭaṃ suttaṃ geyyaṃ veyyākaraṇaṃ gāthā udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vedallaṃ, idaṃ pariyattisāsanaṃ; [^143-3]taṃ pi mussati parimussati[^143-3] paribāhiro hotī ti, evam pi mussat'; evāpi sāsanaṃ. Katamaṃ paṭipattisāsanaṃ? Sammāpaṭipadā, anulo-


<!-- PTS Page 144 -->
<a id="page-144"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 144]*

mapaṭipadā, apaccanīkapaṭipadā, anvatthapaṭipadā, dhammānudhammapaṭipadā, [^144-1]kusalesu paripūrikāritā[^144-1], indriyesu guttadvāratā, bhojane mattaññutā, jāgariyānuyogo, satisampajaññaṃ, cattāro satipaṭṭhānā, cattāro sammappadhānā, cattāro iddhippādā, pañc'; indriyāni, pañca balāni, satta bojjhaṅgā, ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo; idaṃ paṭipattisāsanaṃ; [^144-2]taṃ pi mussati parimussati[^144-2], paribāhiro hotī ti, evam pi mussat'; evāpi sāsanaṃ. Micchā ca paṭipajjatī ti. Pāṇam pi hanati, adinnam pi ādiyati, sandhim pi chindati, nillopam pi harati, ekāgārikam pi karoti, paripanthe pi tiṭṭhati, paradāram pi gacchati, musā pi bhaṇatī ti, micchā ca paṭipajjati. Etaṃ tasmiṃ anāriyan ti etaṃ tasmiṃ puggale anariyadhammo bāladhammo mūḷhadhammo aññāṇadhammo amarāvikkhepadhammo yadidaṃ micchā paṭipadā ti, etaṃ tasmiṃ anāriyaṃ. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Methunam anuyuttassa Metteyyā ti Bhagavā mussat'; evāpi sāsanaṃ, micchā ca paṭipajjati, etaṃ tasmiṃ anāriyan ti.

Sn_IV,7.3(=816): Eko pubbe caritvāna methunaṃ yo nisevati, yānaṃ bhantaṃ va taṃ loke hīnam āhu puthujjanaṃ.  || Nidd_I.7:3 ||

Eko pubbe caritvānā ti. Dvīhi kāraṇehi eko pubbe caritvāna, pabbajjāsaṃkhātena vā gaṇāvavassaggatthena vā. Kathaṃ pabbajjāsaṃkhātena eko pubbe caritvāna? Sabbaṃ gharāvāsapalibodhaṃ chinditvā, puttadārapalibodhaṃ chinditvā, ñātipalibodhaṃ chinditvā, mittāmaccapalibodhaṃ chinditvā, sannidhipalibodhaṃ chinditvā, kesamassuṃ ohāretvā, kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādetvā, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā, ākiñcanabhāvaṃ upagantvā,


<!-- PTS Page 145 -->
<a id="page-145"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 145]*

eko carati viharati iriyati vattati pāleti yapeti yāpeti; evaṃ pabbajjāsaṃkhātena, eko pubbe caritvāna. Kathaṃ gaṇāvavassaggatthena eko pubbe caritvāna? So evaṃ pabbajito samāno eko araññavanapatthāni pantāni senāsanāni paṭisevati appasaddāni appanigghosāni vijanavātāni manussarāhaseyyakāni paṭisallānasāruppāni[^145-1]. So eko gacchati, eko tiṭṭhati, eko nisīdati; eko seyyaṃ kappeti, eko gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisati, eko paṭikkamati, eko raho nisīdati, eko caṅkamaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, eko carati, eko viharati iriyati vattati pāleti yapeti yāpeti; evaṃ gaṇāvavassaggatthena, eko pubbe caritvāna. Methunaṃ yo nisevatī ti. Methunadhammo nāma yo so asaddhammo . . . pe . . . taṃkāraṇā vuccati methunadhammo. Methunaṃ yo nisevatī ti so aparena samayena Buddhaṃ dhammaṃ saṃghaṃ sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattitvā methunadhammaṃ sevati nisevati saṃsevati paṭisevatī ti, methunaṃ yo nisevati. Yānaṃ bhantaṃ va taṃ loke ti. Yānan ti hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ goyānaṃ ajayānaṃ meṇḍakayānaṃ[^145-2] oṭṭhayānaṃ kharayānaṃ bhantaṃ adantaṃ akāritam avinītam uppathaṃ gaṇhāti, visamaṃ khāṇum pi pāsāṇam pi abhiruhati, [^145-3]yānam pi[^145-3] ārohakam pi bhañjati, papāte pi papatati. Yathā taṃ bhantaṃ yānaṃ adantaṃ akāritaṃ avinītaṃ uppathaṃ gaṇhāti, evam eva so vibbhantako bhantayānapaṭibhāgo uppathaṃ gaṇhāti: micchādiṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti . . . pe . . . micchāsamādhiṃ gaṇhāti. Yathā taṃ bhantaṃ yānaṃ adantaṃ akāritaṃ avinītaṃ visamaṃ khāṇum pi pāsāṇam pi abhiruhati, evam eva so vibbhantako bhantayānapaṭibhāgo visamaṃ kāyakammaṃ abhiruhati, visamaṃ vacīkammaṃ abhiruhati, visamaṃ manokammaṃ abhiruhati, visamaṃ pāṇātipātaṃ abhiruhati, visamaṃ adinnādānaṃ abhiruhati, visamaṃ kāmesu micchācāraṃ abhiruhati, visamaṃ musāvādaṃ abhiruhati, visamaṃ pisuṇaṃ[^145-4] vācaṃ abhiruhati, visamaṃ pharusaṃ[^145-5] vācaṃ abhiruhati, visamaṃ samphappalāpaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 146 -->
<a id="page-146"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 146]*

abhiruhati, visamaṃ abhijjhaṃ abhiruhati, visamaṃ byāpādaṃ abhiruhati, visamaṃ micchādiṭṭhiṃ abhiruhati, visame saṃkhāre abhiruhati, visame pañca kāmaguṇe abhiruhati, visame nīvaraṇe abhiruhati. Yathā taṃ bhantaṃ yānaṃ adantaṃ akāritaṃ avinītaṃ [^146-1]yānaṃ pi[^146-1] ārohakam[^146-2] pi bhañjati, evam eva so vibbhantako bhantayānapaṭibhāgo niraye attānaṃ bhañjati, tiracchānayoniyā[^146-3] attānaṃ bhañjati, pittivisaye attānaṃ bhañjati, manussaloke attānaṃ bhañjati, devaloke attānaṃ bhañjati. Yathā taṃ bhantaṃ yānam adantaṃ akāritaṃ avinītaṃ papāte pi papatati, evam eva so vibbhantako bhantayānapaṭibhāgo jātipapātamhi[^146-4] pi papatati, jarāpapātamhi[^146-4] pi papatati, byādhipapātamhi[^146-4] pi papatati, maraṇapapātamhi[^146-4] pi papatati, sokaparidevadukkhadomanassupāyāsapapātamhi[^146-4] pi papatati. Loke ti apāyaloke . . . pe . . . manussaloke ti, yānaṃ bhantaṃ va taṃ loke. Hīnam āhu puthujjanan ti. Puthujjanā ti ken'; atthena puthujjanā? Puthu kilese janentī ti puthujjanā, puthu avihatasakkāyadiṭṭhikā ti puthujjanā, puthu satthārānaṃ mukhullokikā[^146-5] ti puthujjanā, puthu sabbagatīhi āvuṭā[^146-6] ti puthujjanā, puthu nānābhisaṃkhārehi abhisaṃkharontī ti puthujjanā, puthu nānā-oghehi vuyhantī ti puthujjanā, puthu nānāsantāpehi santappantī ti puthujjanā, puthu nānāpariḷāhehi paridayhantī[^146-7] ti puthujjanā, puthu pañcasu[^146-8] kāmaguṇesu rattā giddhā gadhitā mucchitā ajjhosānā laggā laggitā palibuddhā ti puthujjanā, puthu pañcahi nīvaraṇehi āvuṭā[^146-9] nivuṭā ophuṭā[^146-10] pihitā paṭicchannā paṭikujjitā ti puthujjanā. Hīnam āhu puthujjanan ti puthujjanaṃ hīnaṃ nihīnaṃ omakaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 147 -->
<a id="page-147"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 147]*

lāmakaṃ jatukkaṃ[^147-1] parittan ti evam[^147-2] āhu[^147-2] evam āhaṃsu evaṃ kathenti evaṃ bhaṇanti evaṃ dīpayanti evaṃ voharantī ti, hīnam āhu puthujjanaṃ. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Eko pubbe caritvāna methunaṃ yo nisevati, yānaṃ bhantaṃ va taṃ loke hīnam āhu puthujjanan ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,7.4(=817): Yaso kittī ca yā pubbe hāyat'; evāpi tassa sā, etam pi disvā sikkhetha methunaṃ vippahātave.  || Nidd_I.7:4 ||

Yaso kittī ca yā pubbe hāyat'; evāpi tassa sā ti. Katamo yaso? Katamā[^147-3] kitti[^147-3]? Idh'; ekacco pubbe samaṇabhāve sakkato hoti[^147-4] garukato[^147-5] mānito pūjito apacito lābhī cīvara{piṇḍapāta}senāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ; ayaṃ yaso. Katamā kitti? Idh'; ekacco pubbe samaṇabhāve kittivaṇṇabhato[^147-6] hoti paṇḍito viyatto medhāvī bahussuto cittakathī kalyāṇapaṭibhāṇo suttantiko ti vā vinayadharo ti vā dhammakathiko ti vā āraññiko ti vā piṇḍapātiko ti vā paṃsukūliko ti vā, tecīvariko ti vā sapadānacāriko[^147-7] ti vā khalupacchābhattiko ti vā nesajjiko ti vā yathāsanthatiko ti vā paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā dutiyassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā tatiyassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā catutthassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā ākāsānañcāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā viññāṇañcāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā ākiñcaññāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā; ayaṃ kitti. Yaso kittī ca yā pubbe hāyat'; evāpi tassa sā ti. Tassa aparena samayena Buddhaṃ dhammaṃ saṃghaṃ sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattassa[^147-8] so ca yaso sā ca kitti hāyati


<!-- PTS Page 148 -->
<a id="page-148"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 148]*

parihāyati paridhaṃsati paripatati antaradhāyati vippalujjatī ti, yaso kittī ca yā pubbe hāyat'; evāpi tassa sā. Etam pi disvā sikkhetha [^148-1]methunaṃ vippahātave ti. Etan ti pubbe samaṇabhāve yaso kitti ca, aparabhāge Buddhaṃ dhammaṃ saṃghaṃ sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattassa ayaso ca akitti ca[^148-1]; etaṃ sampattivipattiṃ disvā[^148-2] passitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā ti, etam pi disvā. Sikkhethā ti tisso sikkhā, adhisīlasikkhā adhicittasikkhā adhipaññāsikkhā. Katamā adhisīlasikkhā? Idha bhikkhu sīlavā hoti, pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocarasampanno, aṇumattesu vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu; khuddako sīlakkhandho, mahanto sīlakkhandho, sīlaṃ patiṭṭhā ādicaraṇaṃ saṃyamo saṃvaro mukhaṃ pamukhaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā. Ayaṃ adhisīlasikkhā. Katamā adhicittasikkā? Idha bhikkhu vivicc'; eva kāmehi, vivicca akusalehi dhammehi, savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ[^148-3] jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, [^148-4]vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ cetaso ekodibhāvaṃ avitakkaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ[^148-4] dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati. Ayaṃ adhicittasikkhā. Katamā adhipaññāsikkhā? Idha bhikkhu paññavā hoti, udayatthagāminiyā paññāya samannāgato ariyāya nibbedhikāya sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā; so idaṃ dukkhan ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayo ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ dukkhanirodho ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ime āsavā ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ āsavasamudayo ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ āsavanirodho ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ āsavanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti. Ayaṃ adhipaññāsikkhā.


<!-- PTS Page 149 -->
<a id="page-149"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 149]*

Methunadhammo nāma yo so asaddhammo . . . pe . . . taṃkāraṇā vuccati methunadhammo. Etam pi disvā sikkhetha methunaṃ vippahātave ti methunadhammassa[^149-1] pahānāya vūpasamāya paṭinissaggāya paṭipassaddhiyā[^149-2] adhisīlam pi sikkheyya, adhicittam pi sikkheyya, adhipaññam pi sikkheyya. Imā[^149-3] tisso sikkhā[^149-4] āvajjento[^149-5] sikkheyya, jānanto sikkheyya, passanto sikkheyya, paccavekkhanto sikkheyya, cittaṃ adhiṭṭhahanto sikkheyya, saddhāya[^149-6] adhimuccanto sikkheyya, viriyaṃ paggaṇhanto sikkheyya, satiṃ upaṭṭhapento sikkheyya, cittaṃ samādahanto sikkheyya, paññāya pajānanto sikkheyya abhiññeyyaṃ abhijānanto sikkheyya, pariññeyyaṃ parijānanto sikkheyya, pahātabbaṃ pajahanto sikkheyya, bhāvetabbaṃ bhāvento sikkheyya, sacchikātabbaṃ sacchikaronto sikkheyya ācareyya samācareyya samādāya vatteyyā ti, etam pi disvā sikkhetha methunaṃ vippahātave[^149-7]. Ten 'āha Bhagavā.

Yaso kittī ca yā pubbe hāyat'; evāpi tassa sā, etam pi disvā sikkhetha methunaṃ vippahātave ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,7.5(=818): Saṃkappehi pareto so kapano viya jhāyati, sutvā[^149-8] paresaṃ nigghosaṃ maṅku hoti tathāvidho.  || Nidd_I.7:5 ||

Saṃkappehi pareto so kapaṇo viya jhāyatī ti. Kāmasaṃkappena byāpādasaṃkappena vihiṃsāsaṃkappena diṭṭhisaṃkappena phuṭṭho[^149-9] pareto samohito samannāgato pihito[^149-3] kapaṇo viya mando viya momūho viya jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhāyati avajjhāyati[^149-10]. Yathā ulūko rukkhasākhāyaṃ mūsikaṃ magayamāno[^149-11] jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhāyati avajjhāyati[^149-10]; yathā koṭṭhu[^149-12] nadītīre macche magayamāno[^149-11] jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhayāti avajjhāyati[^149-10];


<!-- PTS Page 150 -->
<a id="page-150"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 150]*

yathā vilāro[^150-1] sandhisamalasapaṅkatīre[^150-2] mūsikaṃ magayamāno[^150-3] jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhāyati avajjhāyati[^150-4]; yathā gadrabho vahacchinno sandhisamalasapaṅkatīre[^150-2] jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhāyati avajjhāyati; evam eva so vibbhantako kāmasaṃkappena byāpādasaṃkappena vihiṃsāsaṃkappena[^150-5] diṭṭhisaṃkappena phuṭṭho[^150-6] pareto samohito samannāgato pihito[^150-7] kapaṇo viya mando viya momūho viya jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhāyati avajjhāyātī[^150-4] ti, saṃkappehi pareto so kapaṇo viya jhāyati. Sutvā paresaṃ nigghosaṃ maṅku hoti tathāvidho ti. Paresan ti upajjhāyakā[^150-8] vā ācariyakā[^150-9] vā samānupajjhāyakā vā samānācariyakā vā mittā vā sandiṭṭhā vā sambhattā vā sahāyā vā codenti: Tassa te kho[^150-10] āvuso alābhā, tassa te dulladdhaṃ; yaṃ tvaṃ evarūpaṃ uḷāraṃ satthāraṃ labhitvā, evaṃ svākkhāte dhammavinaye pabbajitvā, evarūpaṃ ariyagaṇaṃ labhitvā, hīnassa methunadhammassa kāraṇā Buddhaṃ dhammaṃ saṃghaṃ sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvatto 'si[^150-11]; saddhā pi nāma te nāhosi kusalesu dhammesu, hiri pi nāma te nāhosi kusalesu dhammesu, ottappam pi nāma te nāhosi kusalesu dhammesu, viriyam pi nāma te nāhosi kusalesu dhammesu, sati pi nāma te nāhosi kusalesu dhammesu, paññā pi nāma te nāhosi kusalesu dhammesū ti tesaṃ vacanaṃ byappathaṃ[^150-12] desanaṃ anusāsanaṃ[^150-7] anusiṭṭhiṃ sutvā suṇitvā uggahetvā[^150-13] upadhārayitvā upalakkhayitvā maṅku hoti, pīḷito ghaṭṭito[^150-14] byatthito[^150-15] domanassito hoti. Tathā-


<!-- PTS Page 151 -->
<a id="page-151"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 151]*

vidho ti tathāvidho tādiso tassaṇṭhito[^151-1] tappakāro tappaṭibhāgo, yo so vibbhantako ti, sutvā paresaṃ nigghosaṃ maṅku hoti tathāvidho. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Saṃkappehi pareto so kapaṇo viya jhāyati, sutvā paresaṃ nigghosaṃ maṅku hoti tathāvidho ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,7.6(=819): Atha satthāni kurute paravādehi codito, esa khv-assa mahāgedho, mosavajjaṃ pagāhati[^151-2].  || Nidd_I.7:6 ||

Atha satthāni kurute paravādehi codito ti. Athā ti padasandhi padasaṃsaggo padapāripūri akkharasamavāyo byañjanasiliṭṭhatā padānupubbatā-m-etaṃ athā ti. Satthānī ti tīṇi satthāni, kāyasatthaṃ vacīsatthaṃ manosatthaṃ; tividhaṃ kāyaduccaritaṃ kāyasatthaṃ, catubbidhaṃ vacīduccaritaṃ vacīsatthaṃ, tividhaṃ manoduccaritaṃ manosatthaṃ. Paravādehi codito ti upajjhāyakehi[^151-3] vā ācariyakehi[^151-4] vā samānupajjhāyakehi[^151-5] vā samānācariyakehi vā mittehi vā sandiṭṭhehi vā sambhatte i vā sahāyehi vā codito, sampajānamusā bhāsati: Abhirato ahaṃ bhante ahosiṃ pabbajjāya, mātā me posetabbā, tena 'mhi vibbhanto ti bhaṇati; pitā me posetabbo tena 'mhi vibbhanto ti bhaṇati; bhātā me posetabbo, bhaginī me posetabbā, putto me posetabbo, dhītā me posetabbā, mittā me posetabbā, amaccā me posetabbā, ñātakā me posetabbā, ñātisālohitā[^151-6] me posetabbā, tena 'mhi vibbhanto ti bhaṇati vacīsatthaṃ karoti saṃkaroti[^151-7] janeti sañjaneti nibbatteti abhinibbattetī ti, atha satthāni kurute paravādehi codito. Esa khv[^151-8] -assa[^151-9] mahāgedho ti. Eso kho[^151-8] assa[^151-9] mahā-


<!-- PTS Page 152 -->
<a id="page-152"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 152]*

gedho mahāvanaṃ mahāgahaṇaṃ mahākantāro mahāvisamo mahākuṭilo mahāpaṅko mahāpalipo[^152-1] mahāpalibodho mahābandhanaṃ yadidaṃ sampajānamusāvādo ti, esa khv-assa mahāgedho. Mosavajjaṃ pagāhatī[^152-2] ti. Mosavajjaṃ vuccati musāvādo. Idh'; ekacco sabhaggato vā parisaggato vā ñātimajjhagato[^152-3] vā pūgamajjhagato vā rājakulamajjhagato vā abhinīto sakkhipuṭṭho: [^152-4]ehi bho[^152-4] purisa yaṃ jānāsi taṃ vadehī ti. So ajānaṃ vā āha[^152-5] jānāmī ti, jānaṃ vā āha na jānāmī ti, apassaṃ vā āha passāmī ti, passaṃ vā āha na passāmī ti, iti attahetu vā parahetu vā āmisakiñcikkhahetu vā sampajānamusā bhāsati; idaṃ vuccati mosavajjam. Api ca tīh'; ākārehi musāvādo hoti; pubb'; ev'; assa hoti musā bhaṇissan ti, bhaṇantassa hoti musā bhaṇāmī ti, bhaṇitassa hoti musā mayā bhaṇitan ti; imehi tīh'; ākārehi musāvādo hoti. Api ca catūh'; ākārehi[^152-6] pañcah'; ākārehi chah'; ākārehi sattah'; ākarehi aṭṭhah'; ākārehi musāvādo hoti: pubb'; ev'; assa hoti musā bhaṇissan ti, bhaṇantassa hoti musā bhaṇāmī ti, bhaṇitassa hoti musā mayā bhaṇitan ti, vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ, vinidhāya khantim, vinidhāya ruciṃ, vinidhāya saññaṃ, vinidhāya bhāvaṃ, imehi aṭṭhah'; ākārehi musāvādo hoti. Mosavajjaṃ pagāhatī[^152-7] ti mosavajjaṃ pagāhati[^152-7] ogāhati ajjhogāhati pavisatī ti, mosavajjaṃ pagāhati[^152-7]. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:


<!-- PTS Page 153 -->
<a id="page-153"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 153]*

Atha satthāni kurute paravādehi codito, esa khv-assa mahāgedho, mosavajjaṃ pagāhatī ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,7.7(=820): Paṇḍito ti samaññāto ekacariyaṃ adhiṭṭhito, athāpi[^153-1] methune[^153-2] yutto mando va parikissati.  || Nidd_I.7:7 ||

Paṇḍito ti samaññāto ti idh'; ekacco pubbe samaṇabhāve kittivaṇṇabhato[^153-3] hoti paṇḍito viyatto medhāvī bahussuto cittakathī kalyāṇapatibhāṇo suttantiko ti vā vinayadharo ti vā dhammakathiko ti vā . . . pe . . . nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā evaṃ ñāto hoti [^153-4]saññāto samaññāto ti[^153-4], paṇḍito ti samaññāto. Ekacariyaṃ adhiṭṭhito ti dvīhi kāraṇehi ekacariyaṃ adhiṭṭhito pabbajjāsaṃkhātena vā gaṇāvavassaggatthena vā. Kathaṃ[^153-5] pabbajjāsaṃkhātena ekacariyaṃ adhiṭṭhito? Sabbaṃ gharāvāsapalibodhaṃ chinditvā . . . pe . . . evaṃ pabbajjāsaṃkhātena ekacariyaṃ adhiṭṭhito. Kathaṃ gaṇāvavassaggatthena ekacariyaṃ adhiṭṭhito? So evaṃ pabbajito samāno eko araññavanapatthāni pantāni . . . pe . . . evaṃ gaṇāvavassaggatthena ekacariyaṃ adhiṭṭhito ti, ekacariyaṃ adhiṭṭhito. Athāpi[^153-6] methune yutto ti. Methunadhammo nāma yo so asaddhammo[^153-7] . . . pe . . . taṃkāraṇā vuccati methunadhammo. Athāpi[^153-6] methune yutto ti so aparena samayena Buddhaṃ dhammaṃ saṃghaṃ sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattitvā[^153-8] methunadhamme yutto payutto[^153-9] ayutto samāyutto ti, athāpi[^153-6] methune yutto. Mando va parikissatī[^153-10] ti kapaṇo viya momūho[^153-11] viya


<!-- PTS Page 154 -->
<a id="page-154"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 154]*

kissati[^154-1] parikissati[^154-2] parikilissati[^154-3], pāṇam pi hanati, adinnam pi ādiyati, sandhim pi chindati, nillopam pi harati, ekāgārikam pi karoti, paripanthe pi tiṭṭhati, paradāram pi gacchati, musā pi bhaṇati, evam pi kissati parikissati parikilissati. Tam enaṃ rājāno gahetvā vividhā kammakāraṇā kārenti[^154-4]: kasāhi pi tāḷenti, vettehi pi tāḷenti, aḍḍhadaṇḍakehi pi tāḷenti, hattham pi chindanti, pādam pi chindanti, hatthapādam pi chindanti, kaṇṇam pi chindanti, nāsam pi chindanti, kaṇṇanāsam pi chindanti, vilaṅgathālikam[^154-5] pi karonti, saṃkhamuṇḍikam pi karonti, rāhumukham pi karonti, jotimālikam pi karonti, hatthapajjotikam pi karonti, erakavattikam pi karonti, cīrakavāsikam[^154-6] pi karonti, eṇeyyakam pi karonti, baḷisamaṃsikam pi karonti, kahāpaṇakam[^154-7] pi karonti, khārāpatacchikam[^154-8] pi[^154-9] karonti, palighaparivattikam pi karonti, palālapīṭhakam[^154-10] pi karonti, tattena pi telena osiñcanti, sunakhehi pi khādāpenti, jīvantam pi sūle uttāsenti[^154-11], asinā pi sīsaṃ chindanti; evam pi kissati parikissati parikilissati[^154-3]. Athavā kāmataṇhāya abhibhūto pariyādiṇṇacitto bhoge pariyesanto nāvāya mahāsamuddaṃ pakkhandati, sītassa purakkhato, uṇhassa purakkhato, ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasiriṃsapasamphassehi [^154-12]rissamāno khuppipāsāya pīḷiyamāno[^154-12] Gumbaṃ[^154-13] gacchati, Takkolaṃ gacchati, Takkasilaṃ gacchati, Kālamukhaṃ gacchati, Maraṇapāraṃ[^154-14] gacchati, Vesuṅgaṃ gacchati, Verāpathaṃ gacchati, Javaṃ gacchati, Tamaliṃ[^154-15] gacchati, Vaṅgam[^154-16] gacchati, Eḷavaddanaṃ[^154-17] gacchati,


<!-- PTS Page 155 -->
<a id="page-155"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 155]*

Suvaṇṇakūṭaṃ gacchati, Suvaṇṇabhūmiṃ gacchati, Tambapaṇṇiṃ gacchati, Suppāraṃ[^155-1] gacchati, Bharukacchaṃ[^155-2] gacchati, Suraṭṭhaṃ[^155-3] gacchati, [^155-4]Aṅgaṇekaṃ gacchati[^155-4], Gaṅgaṇaṃ[^155-5] gacchati, Paramagaṅgaṇaṃ[^155-6] gacchati, Yonaṃ[^155-7] gacchati, Paramayonaṃ[^155-8] gacchati, [^155-9]Allasandaṃ gacchati[^155-9], [^155-10]Marukantāraṃ gacchati, Jaṇṇupathaṃ gacchati, Ajapathaṃ gacchati, Meṇḍapathaṃ gacchati, Saṅkupathaṃ gacchati, Chattapathaṃ gacchati, Vaṃsapathaṃ[^155-11] gacchati, Sakuṇapathaṃ[^155-12] gacchati, Mūsikapathaṃ gacchati, Daripathaṃ gacchati, Vettādhāraṃ[^155-13] gacchati; evam pi kissati parikissati parikilissati. Pariyesanto[^155-14] na labhati[^155-15], alābhamūlakam pi dukkhadomanassaṃ[^155-16] {paṭisaṃvedeti}; evam pi kissati[^155-17] parikissati[^155-18] parikilissati. Pariyesanto[^155-19] labhati[^155-15], laddhā ca[^155-20] ārakkhamūlakam pi dukkhadomanassaṃ[^155-16] {paṭisaṃvedeti}: Kin ti me bhoge n'; eva rājāno hareyyuṃ, na corā hareyyuṃ, na aggi ḍaheyya[^155-21], [^155-22]na udakaṃ vaheyya[^155-23] [^155-22], na appiyā dāyādā hareyyun ti; tassa evaṃ rakkhato[^155-24] gopayato te[^155-25] bhogā vippalujjanti, so[^155-25] vippayogamūlakam pi dukkhadomanassaṃ[^155-16] {paṭisaṃvedeti}; evam pi kissati parikissati parikilissatī


<!-- PTS Page 156 -->
<a id="page-156"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 156]*

ti, athāpi methune yutto mando va parikissati. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Paṇḍito ti samaññāto ekacariyaṃ adhiṭṭhito, athāpi methune yutto mando va parikissatī ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,7.8(=821): Etam ādīnavaṃ ñatvā muni pubbāpare idha, ekacariyaṃ daḷhaṃ kayirā[^156-1], na nisevetha methunaṃ.  || Nidd_I.7:8 ||

Etam ādīnavaṃ ñatvā muni pubbāpare idhā ti. Etan ti pubbe samaṇabhāve yaso ca kitti ca, aparabhāge Buddhaṃ dhammaṃ saṃghaṃ sikkhaṃ paccakkhāya hīnāyāvattassa ayaso ca akitti ca, etaṃ sampattivipattiṃ[^156-2] ñatvā jānitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā[^156-3] vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā. Munī ti. Monaṃ vuccati[^156-4] ñāṇaṃ, yā paññā pajānanā . . . pe . . . saṅgajālam aticca so muni. Idhā ti imissā diṭṭhiyā, imissā khantiyā, imissā ruciyā, imasmiṃ ādāye, imasmiṃ dhamme, imasmiṃ vinaye, imasmiṃ dhammavinaye, imasmiṃ pāvacane, imasmiṃ brahmacariye, imasmiṃ satthusāsane, imasmiṃ attabhāve, imasmiṃ manussaloke ti, etam ādīnavaṃ ñatvā muni pubbāpare idha. Ekacariyaṃ daḷhaṃ kayirā[^156-5] ti dvīhi kāraṇehi ekacariyaṃ daḷhaṃ kareyya, pabbajjāsaṃkhātena vā gaṇāvavassaggatthena vā. Kathaṃ pabbajjāsaṃkhātena ekacariyaṃ daḷhaṃ kareyya? Sabbaṃ gharāvāsapalibodhaṃ chinditvā, puttadārapalibodhaṃ chinditvā, ñātipalibodhaṃ chinditvā, mittāmaccapalibodhaṃ chinditvā, sannidhipalibodhaṃ chinditvā, kesamassuṃ ohāretvā, kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādetvā, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā, akiñcanabhāvaṃ[^156-6] upagantvā, eko careyya vihareyya iriyeyya vatteyya


<!-- PTS Page 157 -->
<a id="page-157"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 157]*

pāleyya yapeyya yāpeyya; evaṃ pabbajjāsaṃkhātena ekacariyaṃ daḷhaṃ kareyya. Kathaṃ gaṇāvavassaggatthena ekacariyaṃ daḷhaṃ kareyya? So evaṃ pabbajito[^157-1] samāno eko araññavanapatthāni[^157-2] pantāni senāsanāni patiseveyya appasaddāni appanigghosāni vijanavātāni manussarāhaseyyakāni paṭisallānasāruppāni[^157-3]; so eko gaccheyya, eko tiṭṭheyya, eko nisīdeyya, eko seyyaṃ kappeyya, eko gāmaṃ piṇḍāya paviseyya, eko paṭikkameyya, eko raho nisīdeyya, eko caṅkamaṃ adhiṭṭheyya, eko careyya vihareyya iriyeyya vatteyya pāleyya yapeyya yāpeyya; evaṃ gaṇāvavassaggatthena ekacariyaṃ daḷhaṃ kareyyā ti. Ekacariyaṃ daḷhaṃ kareyya, thiraṃ[^157-4] kareyya, daḷhasamādāno[^157-5] assa avaṭṭhitasamādāno[^157-6] kusalesu dhammesū ti, ekacariyaṃ daḷhaṃ kayirā[^157-7]. [^157-8]Na nisevetha methunan ti. Methunadhammo nāma yo so asaddhammo gāmadhammo . . . pe . . . taṃkāraṇā[^157-9] vuccati methunadhammo. Methunadhammaṃ[^157-10] na seveyya, na niseveyya, na[^157-11] saṃseveyya, na paṭiseveyya, na careyya, [^157-12]na samācareyya[^157-12], na samādāya vatteyā ti[^157-8], na nisevetha methunam. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Etam ādīnavaṃ ñatvā muni pubbāpare idha, ekacariyaṃ daḷhaṃ kayirā, na nisevetha methunan ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,7.9(=822): Vivekañ ñeva sikkhetha, etad ariyānam uttamaṃ; tena settho na maññetha, sa ve nibbānasantike.  || Nidd_I.7:9 ||

Vivekañ ñeva sikkhethā ti. Viveko ti tayo vivekā, kāyaviveko cittaviveko upadhiviveko.


<!-- PTS Page 158 -->
<a id="page-158"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 158]*

Katamo kāyaviveko? . . . pe . . . ayaṃ upadhiviveko. Kāyaviveko ca vūpakaṭṭhakāyānaṃ[^158-1] nekkhammābhiratānaṃ; cittaviveko ca parisuddhacittānaṃ paramavodānapattānaṃ[^158-2]; upadhiviveko ca nirupadhīnaṃ puggalānaṃ visaṃkhāragatānaṃ. Sikkhā ti tisso sikkhā, adhisīlasikkhā adhicittasikkhā adhipaññāsikkhā . . . pe . . . ayaṃ adhipaññāsikkhā. Vivekañ ñeva sikkhethā ti vivekañ ñeva sikkheyya ācareyya samācareyya samādāya vatteyyā ti, vivekañ ñeva sikkhetha. Etad ariyānam uttaman ti. Ariyā vuccanti Buddhā ca buddhasāvakā ca paccekabuddhā ca. Ariyānaṃ etaṃ aggaṃ seṭṭhaṃ viseṭṭhaṃ[^158-3] pāmokkhaṃ uttamaṃ pavaraṃ, yadidaṃ vivekacariyā ti, etad ariyānam uttamaṃ. Tena[^158-4] seṭṭho na maññethā ti [^158-5]tāya vivekacariyāya[^158-5] uṇṇatiṃ na kareyya, uṇṇamaṃ na[^158-6] kareyya, mānaṃ na kareyya[^158-7], thambhaṃ na kareyya, na[^158-6] tena mānaṃ janeyya, [^158-8]bandhaṃ na kareyya[^158-8], na[^158-6] tena thaddho assa patthaddho[^158-9] paggahitasiro ti, tena seṭṭho na maññetha. Sa ve nibbānasantike[^158-10] ti so nibbānassa[^158-10] santike sāmantā āsanne avidūre upakaṭṭhe ti, sa ve nibbānasantike. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Vivekañ ñeva sikkhetha, etad ariyānam uttamaṃ; tena[^158-4] seṭṭho na maññetha, sa ve nibbānasantike ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,7.10(=823): Rittassa munino carato kāmesu anapekkhino oghatiṇṇassa pihayanti kāmesu gadhitā[^158-11] pajā[^158-12].  || Nidd_I.7:10 ||

Rittassa munino carato ti. Rittassā[^158-13] ti[^158-13] rittassa vivit-


<!-- PTS Page 159 -->
<a id="page-159"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 159]*

tassa pavivittassa, kāyaduccaritena rittassa vivittassa pavivittassa, vacīduccaritena manoduccaritena rāgena dosena mohena kodhena upanāhena makkhena paḷāsena issāya macchariyena māyāya sāṭheyyena thambhena sārambhena mānena atimānena madena pamādena sabbakilesehi sabbaduccaritehi sabbadarathehi sabbapariḷāhehi sabbasantāpehi sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārehi rittassa vivittassa pavivittassa[^159-1]. Munino ti monaṃ vuccati ñāṇaṃ,[^159-2]yā paññā pajānanā[^159-2] . . . pe . . . saṅgajālam aticca so muni[^159-3]. Carato ti carato viharato iriyato vattato[^159-4] pālayato yapayato yāpayato ti, rittassa munino carato. Kāmesu anapekkhino ti. Kāmā ti uddānato[^159-5] dve kāmā, vatthukāmā ca kilesakāmā ca . . . pe . . . ime vuccanti vatthukāmā . . . pe . . . ime vuccanti kilesakāmā. Vatthukāme parijānitvā[^159-6], kilesakāme pahāya pajahitvā vinoditvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamitvā, kāmesu anapekkhavā[^159-7], cattakāmo vantakāmo muttakāmo pahīnakāmo paṭinissaṭṭhakāmo vītarāgo[^159-8] vantarāgo muttarāgo pahīnarāgo patinissaṭṭharāgo nicchāto[^159-9] nibbuto sītibhūto {sukhapaṭisaṃvedī} brahmabhūtena attanā viharatī ti, kāmesu anapekkhino. Oghatiṇṇassa pihayanti kāmesu gadhitā pajā ti. Pajā ti sattādhivacanaṃ. Pajā kāmesu rattā giddhā gadhitā mucchitā ajjhopannā laggā laggitā palibuddhā, tā[^159-10] kāmoghaṃ[^159-11] tiṇṇassa, [^159-12]bhavoghaṃ tiṇṇassa[^159-12], diṭṭhoghaṃ tiṇṇassa, avijjoghaṃ tiṇṇassa, sabbasaṃkhārapathaṃ tiṇṇassa uttiṇṇassa[^159-13] nitiṇṇassa[^159-14] atikkantassa samatikkantassa vītivattassa pāragatassa[^159-15] pārappattassa[^159-16]


<!-- PTS Page 160 -->
<a id="page-160"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 160]*

[^160-1]antagatassa antappattassa[^160-1] koṭigatassa koṭippattassa[^160-2] [^160-3]pariyantagatassa pariyantappattassa[^160-3] vosānagatassa vosānappattassa tāṇagatassa tāṇappattassa leṇagatassa leṇappattassa saraṇagatassa saraṇappattassa abhayagatassa abhayappattassa accutagatassa accutappattassa[^160-4] amatagatassa amatappattassa nibbānagatassa nibbānappattassa icchanti sādiyanti patthayanti pihayanti abhijappanti. Yathā iṇāyikā ānaṇyaṃ patthenti pihayanti, yathā ābādhikā ārogyam patthenti pihayanti, yathā bandhanabandhā[^160-5] bandhanamokkhaṃ patthenti pihayanti, yathā dāsā bhujissaṃ[^160-6] patthenti pihayanti, yathā [^160-7]kantāraddhānaṃ pakkhannā[^160-7] khemantabhūmiṃ patthenti pihayanti; evam eva pajā kāmesu rattā giddhā gadhitā mucchitā ajjhopannā[^160-8] laggā laggitā palibuddhā, tā[^160-9] kāmoghatiṇṇassa bhavoghatiṇṇassa . . . pe . . . nibbānagatassa nibbānappattassa icchanti sādiyanti patthayanti pihayanti abhijappantī ti, oghatiṇṇassa pihayanti kāmesu gadhitā pajā[^160-10]. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Rittassa munino carato kāmesu anapekkhino oghatiṇṇassa pihayanti kāmesu gadhitā pajā ti.

[^160-11]SATTAMO TISSAMETTEYYASUTTANIDDESO NITTHITO[^160-11].


<!-- PTS Page 161 -->
<a id="page-161"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 161]*

AṬṬHAMO PASŪRASUTTANIDDESO

Sn_IV,[^161-8].[^161-1](=824): Idh'; eva suddhiṃ[^161-1] iti vādiyanti[^161-2], nāññesu dhammesu visuddhim āhu, yaṃ nissitā, tattha subhā[^161-3] vadānā paccekasaccesu puthū niviṭṭhā.  || Nidd_I.8:1 ||

Idh'; eva suddhiṃ[^161-4] iti vādiyantī ti. [^161-5]Idh'; eva[^161-5] suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ vadanti kathenti[^161-6] bhaṇanti[^161-7] dīpayanti voharanti: sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharanti, asassato[^161-8] loko, antavā loko, anantavā loko, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ, aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīraṃ, hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā, na hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā, hoti ca na ca hoti tathāgato {paraṃmaraṇā}, n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato {paraṃmaraṇā}, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti suddhiṃ[^161-9] visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, idh'; eva suddhiṃ iti vādiyanti. Nāññesu dhammesu visuddhim āhū ti. Attano satthāraṃ dhammakkhānaṃ gaṇaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭipadaṃ maggaṃ ṭhapetvā sabbe paravāde khipanti ukkhipanti parikkhipanti: so satthā na sabbaññū, dhammo na svākkhāto[^161-10], gaṇo na supaṭipanno[^161-11], diṭṭhi na bhaddikā[^161-12], paṭipadā na


<!-- PTS Page 162 -->
<a id="page-162"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 162]*

supaññattā, maggo na niyyāniko; n'; atth'; ettha suddhi vā visuddhi vā parisuddhi vā mutti vā vimutti vā parimutti vā; [^162-1]n'; atth'; ettha[^162-1] sujjhanti vā visujjhanti vā parisujjhanti vā muccanti vā vimuccanti vā parimuccanti vā hīnā nihīnā omakā lāmakā jatukkā parittā ti evam āhaṃsu evaṃ kathenti evaṃ bhaṇanti evaṃ dīpayanti evaṃ voharantī ti, nāññesu dhammesu visuddhim āhu. Yaṃ nissitā tattha subhā vadānā ti. [^162-2]Yaṃ nissitā ti[^162-2]. Yaṃ satthāraṃ dhammakkhānaṃ gaṇaṃ diṭṭhim paṭipadaṃ maggaṃ nissitā sannissitā[^162-3] allīnā upagatā ajjhositā adhimuttā[^162-4]. Tatthā ti sakāya diṭṭhiyā sakāya khantiyā sakāya ruciyā sakāya laddhiyā. Subhā vadānā ti subhavādā[^162-5] sobhaṇavādā paṇḍitavādā dhīravādā[^162-6] ñāṇavādā[^162-7] hetuvādā lakkhaṇavādā kāraṇavādā ṭhānavādā sakāya laddhiyā ti, yaṃ nissitā tattha subhā vadānā. Paccekasaccesu puthū niviṭṭhā ti. Puthū samaṇabrāhmaṇā puthupaccekasaccesu niviṭṭhā patiṭṭhitā allīnā upagatā ajjhositā adhimuttā: sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti niviṭṭhā patiṭṭhitā allīnā upagatā ajjhositā adhimuttā, assassato loko . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato {paraṃmaraṇā}, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti niviṭṭhā patiṭṭhitā allīnā upagatā ajjhositā adhimuttā ti, paccekasaccesu puthū niviṭṭhā. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Idh'; eva suddhiṃ iti vādiyanti, nāññesu dhammesu visuddhim āhu, yaṃ nissitā, tattha subhā vadānā paccekasaccesu puthū niviṭṭhā ti.


<!-- PTS Page 163 -->
<a id="page-163"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 163]*

Sn_IV,8.2(=825): Te vādakāmā[^163-1] parisaṃ vigayha bālaṃ dahanti mithu aññamaññaṃ, vadanti te aññasitā kathojjaṃ pasaṃsakāmā kusalā vadānā.  || Nidd_I.8:2 ||

Te vādakāmā parisaṃ vigayhā ti. Te vādakāmā [^163-2]ti te vādakāmā[^163-2] vādatthikā vādādhippāyā vādapurekkhārā vādapariyesanaṃ carantā. Parisaṃ vigayhā ti khattiyaparisaṃ brāhmaṇaparisaṃ gahapatiparisaṃ samaṇaparisaṃ[^163-3] vigayha ogayha ajjhogahetvā pavisitvā ti[^163-3], te vādakāmā[^163-1] parisaṃ vigayha. Bālaṃ dahanti mithu aññamaññan ti. Mithū ti dve janā dve kalahakārakā dve bhaṇḍanakārakā dve bhassakārakā dve vivādakārakā dve adhikaraṇakārakā dve vādino dve sallāpakā, te aññamaññaṃ bālato hīnato nihīnato omakato lāmakato jatukkato parittato dahanti[^163-4] passanti dakkhanti[^163-5] olokenti nigghāyanti[^163-6] upaparikkhantī ti, bālaṃ dahanti mithu aññamaññaṃ. Vadanti te aññasitā[^163-7] kathojjan ti. Aññaṃ satthāraṃ dhammakkhānaṃ gaṇaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭipadaṃ maggaṃ nissitā sannissitā[^163-8] allīnā upagatā ajjhositā adhimuttā. Kathojjaṃ vuccati kalaho bhaṇḍanaṃ viggaho[^163-9] vivādo medhagaṃ; athavā kathojjan ti anojavantī [^163-10]sā kathā[^163-10]. Kathojjaṃ vadanti, kalahaṃ vadanti, bhaṇḍanaṃ vadanti, viggahaṃ vadanti, vivādaṃ vadanti, medhagaṃ vadanti[^163-11] bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, vadanti te aññasitā[^163-12] kathojjaṃ. Pasaṃsakāmā kusalā vadānā[^163-13] ti. Pasaṃsakāmā ti pasaṃsakāmā[^163-14] pasaṃsatthikā pasaṃsādhippāyā pasaṃsapurekkhārā pasaṃsapariyesanaṃ carantā[^163-15]. Kusalā vadānā


<!-- PTS Page 164 -->
<a id="page-164"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 164]*

ti kusalavādā paṇḍitavādā dhīravādā[^164-1] ñāṇavādā[^164-2] hetuvādā lakkhaṇavādā kāraṇavādā ṭhānavādā[^164-3] sakāya laddhiyā ti, pasaṃsakāmā kusalā vadānā. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Te vādakāmā parisaṃ vigayha bālaṃ dahanti mithu aññamaññaṃ, vadanti te aññasitā kathojjaṃ pasaṃsakāmā kusalā vadānā ti.

Sn_IV,8.3(=826): Yutto kathāyaṃ parisāya majjhe pasaṃsam icchaṃ vinighāti hoti, apāhatasmiṃ pana maṅku hoti, nindāya so kuppati randhamesī.  || Nidd_I.8:3 ||

Yutto kathāyaṃ parisāya majjhe ti. Khattiyaparisāya vā brāhmaṇaparisāya vā gahapatiparisāya vā samaṇaparisāya vā majjhe attano kathāyaṃ yutto payutto āyutto samāyutto sampayutto kathetun ti, yutto kathāyaṃ parisāya majjhe. Pasaṃsam icchaṃ vinighāti hotī ti. [^164-4]Pasaṃsam icchan ti[^164-4] pasaṃsaṃ thomanaṃ [^164-5]kittiṃ vaṇṇahāriyaṃ[^164-5] icchanto sādiyanto patthayanto pihayanto abhijappanto. Vinighāti hotī ti pubb'; eva sallāpā kathaṃkathī vinighāti hoti: Jayo nu kho me bhavissati, parājayo nu kho me bhavissati? kathaṃ niggahaṃ karissāmi? kathaṃ patikammaṃ[^164-6] karissāmi? kathaṃ visesaṃ karissāmi? kathaṃ paṭivisesaṃ karissāmi? kathaṃ āvedhiyaṃ[^164-7] karissāmi? kathaṃ nibbedhiyaṃ[^164-8] karissāmi? kathaṃ chedaṃ karissāmi? kathaṃ maṇḍalaṃ karissāmī? ti. Evaṃ pubb'; eva sallāpā kathaṃkathī vinighāti hotī ti, pasaṃsam icchaṃ vinighāti hoti. Apāhatasmiṃ pana maṅku hotī ti. Ye[^164-9] te pañhavīmaṃ-


<!-- PTS Page 165 -->
<a id="page-165"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 165]*

sakā[^165-1] pārisajjā pāsanikā[^165-2] te apaharanti, atthāpagataṃ bhaṇitan ti atthato apaharanti, byañjanāpagataṃ bhaṇitan ti byañjanato apaharanti, atthabyañjanāpagataṃ bhaṇitan ti atthabyañjanato apaharanti; attho te dunnīto, byañjanan te duropitaṃ, atthabyañjanan te dunnītaṃ duropitaṃ, niggaho te akato, patikamman[^165-3] te dukkaṭaṃ, viseso te akato, paṭiviseso te dukkaṭo, āvedhiyā[^165-4] te akatā, nibbedhiyā te dukkaṭā, chedo te akato, maṇḍalan [^165-5]te dukkaṭaṃ dukkathitaṃ[^165-5] dubbhaṇitaṃ dullapitaṃ duruttaṃ dubbhāsitan ti apaharanti. Apāhatasmiṃ pana maṅku hotī ti apāhatasmiṃ maṅku hoti, pīlito ghaṭṭito byatthito[^165-6] domanassito hotī ti, apāhatasmiṃ pana maṅku hoti. Nindāya so kuppati randhamesī ti. [^165-7]Nindāyā ti[^165-7] nindāya garahāya akittiyā avaṇṇahārikāya. [^165-8]Kuppatī ti[^165-8] kuppati byāpajjati patitthīyati kopañ ca dosañ ca apaccayañ ca pātukarotī ti nindāya so kuppati. [^165-7]Randhamesī ti[^165-7] randhamesī[^165-9] virandhamesī aparandhamesī[^165-9] khalitamesī gaḷitamesī[^165-9] vivaramesī[^165-10] ti, nindāya so kuppati randhamesī ti. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Yutto kathāyaṃ parisāya majjhe pasaṃsam icchaṃ vinighāti hoti, apāhatasmiṃ pana maṅku hoti, nindāya so kuppati randhamesī ti.

##### *[PTS Page 166]*

Sn_IV,8.4(=827): Yam assa vādaṃ parihīnam āhu apāhataṃ [^166-1]pañhavimaṃsakā ye[^166-1], paridevatī socati hīnavādo, upaccagā man ti anutthunāti[^166-2].  || Nidd_I.8:4 ||

Yam assa vādaṃ parihīnam āhū ti. Yaṃ tassa vādaṃ hīnaṃ nihīnam parihīnaṃ parihāpitaṃ na paripūritaṃ evam āhaṃsu evaṃ kathenti evaṃ bhaṇanti evaṃ dīpayanti evaṃ voharantī ti, yam assa vādaṃ parihīnam āhu. Apāhataṃ pañhavimaṃsakā ye ti. Ye te pañhavīmaṃsakā[^166-3] pārisajjā pāsanikā te apaharanti, atthāpagataṃ bhaṇitan ti atthato apaharanti, byañjanāpagataṃ bhaṇitan ti byañjanato apaharanti, atthabyañjanāpagataṃ bhaṇitan ti atthabyañjanato apaharanti, attho te dunnīto, byañjanaṃ te duropitaṃ, atthabyañjanaṃ te dunnītaṃ duropitaṃ, niggaho te akato, patikamman te dukkaṭaṃ, viseso te akato, paṭiviseso te dukkaṭo, āvedhiyā te akatā, nibbedhiyā te dukkaṭā, chedo te akato, maṇḍalaṃ te dukkaṭaṃ dukkathitaṃ dubbhaṇitaṃ dullapitaṃ duruttaṃ dubbhāsitan ti apaharantī ti, apāhataṃ pañhavimaṃsakā ye. Paridevati socati hīnavādo ti. Paridevatī ti aññaṃ mayā āvajjitaṃ, aññaṃ cintitaṃ, aññaṃ upadhāritaṃ, aññaṃ upalakkhitaṃ; so mahāpakkho mahāpariso mahāparivāro parisā cāyaṃ vaggā na samaggā, samaggāya[^166-4] [^166-5]hotu kathāsallāpo[^166-5], puna bhañjissāmī ti [^166-6]yo evarūpo[^166-6] vācāpalāpo vippalāpo lālappo[^166-7] lālappāyanā lālappāyitattan ti, paridevati. Socatī ti tassa jayo ti socati, mayhaṃ parājayo ti socati, tassa lābho ti socati, mayhaṃ alābho ti socati, tassa yaso ti socati, mayhaṃ ayaso ti socati, tassa pasaṃsā ti socati, mayhaṃ nindā ti socati, tassa sukhan ti socati, mayhaṃ dukkhan ti socati; so sakkato garukato mānito pūjito apacito lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ, aham asmi asakkato agarukato


<!-- PTS Page 167 -->
<a id="page-167"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 167]*

amānito apūjito anapacito[^167-1] na lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānan ti socati kilamati paridevati urattāḷiṃ kandati sammohaṃ āpajjatī ti paridevati socati. Hīnavādo ti hīnavādo nihīnavādo parihīnavādo parihāpitavādo na paripūrivādo ti, paridevati socati hīnavādo. Upaccagā man ti anutthunātī[^167-2] ti. So maṃ vādena vādaṃ accagā upaccagā atikkanto samatikkanto vītivatto ti evam pi upaccagā man ti; athavā vādena vādaṃ abhibhavitvā ajjhottharitvā pariyāditvā madditvā[^167-3] carati viharati iriyati vattati pāleti yapeti yāpetī ti, evam pi upaccagā man ti. Anutthunā[^167-4] vuccati vācāpalāpo vippalāpo lālappo[^167-5] lālappāyanā lālappāyitattan ti, upaccagā man ti anutthunāti. Ten āha Bhagavā:

Yam assa vādaṃ parihīnam āhu apāhataṃ pañhavimaṃsakā ye, paridevati socati hīnavādo, upaccagā man ti anutthunātī[^167-6] ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,8.5(=828): Ete vivādā samaṇesu jātā, etesu ugghātinighāti hoti, etam pi disvā virame kathojjaṃ, na[^167-7] h'; aññad-atth'; atthi pasaṃsalābhā.  || Nidd_I.8:5 ||

Ete vivādā samaṇesu jātā ti. Samaṇā ti [^167-8]ye keci[^167-8] ito bahiddhā paribbājupagatā[^167-9] paribbājakasamāpannā[^167-9]. Ete diṭṭhikalahā diṭṭhibhaṇḍanā diṭṭhiviggahā diṭṭhivivādā diṭṭhimedhagā samaṇesu jātā sañjātā nibbattā abhinibbattā[^167-10] pātubhūtā ti, ete vivādā samaṇesu jātā. Etesu ugghātinighāti hotī ti. Jayaparājayo hoti, lābhālābho hoti, yasāyaso hoti, nindāpasaṃsā[^167-11] hoti, sukhaduk-


<!-- PTS Page 168 -->
<a id="page-168"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 168]*

khaṃ hoti, somanassadomanassaṃ hoti, iṭṭhāniṭṭhaṃ hoti, [^168-1]anunayapatighaṃ hoti, [^168-2]ugghātinighāti hoti[^168-2], anurodhavirodho hoti[^168-1]; jayena cittam ugghātitaṃ hoti, parājayena cittaṃ nighātitaṃ[^168-3] hoti, lābhena cittaṃ ugghātitaṃ hoti, alābhena cittaṃ nighātitaṃ hoti, yasena cittaṃ ugghātitaṃ hoti, ayasena cittaṃ nighātitaṃ hoti, pasaṃsāya cittaṃ ugghātitaṃ hoti, nindāya cittaṃ nighātitaṃ hoti, sukhena cittaṃ ugghātitaṃ hoti, dukkhena cittaṃ nighātitaṃ hoti, somanassena cittaṃ ugghātitaṃ hoti, domanassena cittaṃ nighātitaṃ hoti, uṇṇatiyā cittaṃ ugghātitaṃ hoti, oṇatiyā cittaṃ nighātitaṃ hotī ti, etesu ugghātinighāti[^168-4] hoti. Etam pi disvā virame kathojjan ti. [^168-5]Etaṃ pi disvā ti[^168-5] etam ādīnavaṃ disvā passitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā diṭṭhikalahesu diṭṭhibhaṇḍanesu diṭṭhiviggahesu diṭṭhivivādesu diṭṭhimedhagesū ti. Etam[^168-6] pi disvā virame kathojjan ti. Kathojjaṃ vuccati kalaho bhaṇḍanaṃ viggaho vivādo medhagaṃ[^168-7]; athavā kathojjan ti anojavantī sā[^168-8] kathā. Kathojjaṃ na kareyya, kalahaṃ na kareyya, bhaṇḍanaṃ na kareyya, viggahaṃ na kareyya, vivādaṃ na kareyya, medhagaṃ na kareyya, kalahabhaṇḍanaviggahavivādamedhagaṃ pajaheyya vinodeyya byantīkareyya anabhāvaṃ gameyya, kalahabhaṇḍanaviggahavivādamedhagā ārato assa, virato paṭivirato nikkhanto nissaṭṭho[^168-9] vippayutto visaṃyutto vimariyādikatena[^168-10] cetasā vihareyyā ti, etam pi disvā virame kathojjam. Na[^168-11] h'; aññad-atth'; atthi pasaṃsalābhā ti. Pasaṃsalābhā[^168-12] añño attho n'; atthi attattho vā parattho vā ubhayattho vā, diṭṭhadhammiko vā attho samparāyiko vā attho, uttāno vā


<!-- PTS Page 169 -->
<a id="page-169"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 169]*

attho gambhīro vā attho, gūḷho[^169-1] va attho paṭicchanno vā attho, neyyo vā attho nīto vā attho, anavajjo vā attho, nikkileso vā attho, vodāno va attho, paramattho vā attho [^169-2]n'; atthi na saṃvijjati n'; upalabbhatī[^169-2] ti, na h'; aññad-atth' atthi pasaṃsalābhā. Ten'; aha Bhagavā:

Ete vivādā samaṇesu jātā, etesu ugghātinighāti hoti, etam pi disvā virame kathojjaṃ, na[^169-3] h'; aññad-atth'; atthi pasaṃsalābhā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,8.6(=829): Pasaṃsito vā pana tattha hoti akkhāya vādaṃ parisāya majjhe, so hassati[^169-4] uṇṇamati-cca tena pappuyya[^169-5] tam atthaṃ yathā mano ahu.  || Nidd_I.8:6 ||

Pasaṃsito vā pana tattha hotī ti. Tatthā ti sakāya diṭṭhiyā sakāya khantiyā sakāya ruciyā sakāya laddhiyā. Pasaṃsito thomito kittito vaṇṇito hotī ti, pasaṃsito vā pana tattha hoti. Akkhāya vādaṃ parisāya majjhe ti. Khattiyaparisāya vā brāhmaṇaparisāya vā gahapatiparisāya vā samaṇaparisāya vā majjhe attano vādaṃ akkhāya ācikkhitvā anuvādaṃ akkhāya ācikkhitvā thambhayitvā brūhayitvā dīpayitvā jotayitvā voharitvā pariggaṇhitvā ti, akkhāya vādaṃ parisāya majjhe. So hassati uṇṇamati-cca tenā ti. So tena jayatthena tuṭṭho hoti haṭṭho pahaṭṭho attamano paripuṇṇasaṃkappo; athavā dantavidaṃsakaṃ hasamāno ti, so hassati. Uṇṇamati-cca tenā ti so tena jayatthena uṇṇato hoti


<!-- PTS Page 170 -->
<a id="page-170"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 170]*

uṇṇamo dhajo sampaggāho ketukamyatā cittassā ti, so hassati uṇṇamati-cca tena. Pappuyya tam atthaṃ yathāmano ahū[^170-1] ti. [^170-2]Taṃ jayattham[^170-2] pappuyya pāpuṇitvā adhigantvā vinditvā paṭilabhitvā yathāmano ahū ti yathāmano ahu, yathācitto ahu, yathāsaṃkappo ahu, yathāviññāṇo[^170-3] ahū ti, pappuyya tam atthaṃ yathāmano ahu. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Pasaṃsito vā pana tattha hoti akkhāya vādaṃ parisāya majjhe, so hassati uṇṇamati-cca tena pappuyya tam atthaṃ yathāmano ahū ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,8.7(=830): Yā unnatī, sā 'ssa vighātabhūmi, mānātimānaṃ vadate[^170-4] pan'; eso, etam pi disvā na vivādayetha, na hi tena suddhiṃ kusalā vadanti.  || Nidd_I.8:7 ||

Yā uṇṇatī sā 'ssa vighātabhūmī ti. Yā uṇṇatī uṇṇamo dhajo sampaggāho ketukamyatā [^170-5]cittassa sā tassa vighātabhūmi[^170-5] ugghātabhūmi pīlanabhūmi ghaṭṭanabhūmi upaddavabhūmi upasaggabhūmī ti, yā uṇṇatī sā 'ssa vighātabhūmi. Mānātimānaṃ vadate[^170-6] pan'; eso ti. So puggalo mānañ ca vadati[^170-6] atimānañ ca vadatī[^170-6] ti, mānātimānaṃ vadate[^170-6] pan'; eso. Etam pi disvā na vivādayethā[^170-7] ti. Etaṃ ādīnavaṃ disvā passitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā, diṭṭhikalahesu diṭṭhibhaṇḍanesu diṭṭhiviggahesu diṭṭhivivādesu diṭṭhimedhagesū[^170-8] ti, etam pi disvā. Na


<!-- PTS Page 171 -->
<a id="page-171"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 171]*

vivādayethā[^171-1] ti na kalahaṃ kareyya, na bhaṇḍanaṃ kareyya, na viggahaṃ kareyya, na vivādaṃ kareyya, na medhagaṃ kareyya, kalahabhaṇḍanaviggahavivādamedhagaṃ pajaheyya vinodeyya byantīkareyya anabhāvaṃ gameyya, kalahabhaṇḍanaviggahavivādamedhagā ārato assa, virato paṭivirato nikkhanto patinissaṭṭho[^171-2] vippamutto[^171-3] visaṃyutto vimariyādikatena cetasā vihareyyā ti, etam pi disvā na vivādayetha[^171-4]. Na hi tena suddhiṃ kusalā vadantī ti. Kusalā ti ye te khandhakusalā dhātukusalā āyatanakusalā paticcasamuppādakusalā satipaṭṭhānakusalā [^171-5]sammappadhānakusalā iddhippādakusalā[^171-5] indriyakusalā balakusalā bojjhaṅgakusalā maggakusalā phalakusalā nibbānakusalā. Te kusalā diṭṭhikalahena diṭṭhibhaṇḍanena diṭṭhiviggahena diṭṭhivivādena diṭṭhimedhagena suddhiṃ[^171-6] visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ na vadanti na kathenti na[^171-7] bhaṇanti[^171-7] na dīpayanti na voharantī ti, na hi tena suddhiṃ kusalā vadanti. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Yā uṇṇatī, sā 'ssa vighātabhūmi, mānātimānaṃ vadate pan'; eso, etam pi disvā na vivādayetha, na hi tena suddhiṃ kusalā vadantī ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,8.8(=831): Sūro yathā rājakhadāya puṭṭho abhigajjam eti paṭisūram icchaṃ, yen'; eva so tena palehi sūra[^171-8], pubb'; eva n'; atthī yadidaṃ yudhāya.  || Nidd_I.8:8 ||

Sūro yathā rājakhadāya puṭṭho ti. Sūro ti sūro vīro vikkanto abhīrū[^171-9] achambhī anutrāsī apalāyī. Rājakhadāya puṭṭho ti rājakhādanīyena rājabhojanīyena puṭṭho


<!-- PTS Page 172 -->
<a id="page-172"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 172]*

posito āpādito paṭipādito[^172-1] vaḍḍhito ti, sūro yathā rājakhadāya puṭṭho. Abhigajjam eti paṭisūram icchan ti, So gajjanto uggajjanto abhigajjanto eti upeti upagacchati paṭisūraṃ paṭipūrisaṃ paṭisattuṃ paṭimallaṃ icchanto sādiyanto patthayanto pihayanto abhijappanto ti, abhigajjam eti paṭisūram icchaṃ. Yen'; eva so tena palehi sūrā[^172-2] ti. Yen'; eva so diṭṭhigatiko [^172-3]tena palehi[^172-3]: ten'; eva vaja[^172-4], tena gaccha, tena abhikkama[^172-5]; so tuyhaṃ paṭisūro paṭipūriso paṭisattu paṭimallo ti, yen'; eva so tena palehi sūra[^172-6]. Pubb'; eva n'; atthī yadidaṃ yudhāyā ti. Pubb'; eva bodhiyā mūle ye[^172-7] paṭisenikarā kilesā paṭilomakarā paṭikaṇṭakakarā[^172-8] paṭipakkhakarā, te n'; atthi na santi na saṃvijjanti n'; upalabbhanti, pahīnā samucchinnā vūpasantā paṭipassaddhā abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā. Yadidaṃ yudhāyā ti yadidaṃ yuddhatthāya kalahatthāya[^172-9] bhaṇḍanatthāya viggahatthāya vivādatthāya medhagatthāyā ti'; pubb'; eva n'; atthī yadidaṃ yudhāya. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Sūro yathā rājakhadāya puṭṭho abhigajjam eti paṭisūram icchaṃ, yen'; eva so tena palehi sūra, pubb'; eva n'; atthī yadidaṃ yudhāyā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,8.9(=832): Ye diṭṭhim uggayha vivādiyanti[^172-10], idam eva saccan ti ca vādiyanti, te tvaṃ vadassū[^172-11] na hi te 'dha atthi vādamhi jāte paṭisenikattā[^172-12].  || Nidd_I.8:9 ||


<!-- PTS Page 173 -->
<a id="page-173"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 173]*

Ye diṭṭhim uggayha vivādiyantī[^173-1] ti. Ye [^173-2]dvāsaṭṭhiyā diṭṭhigatānaṃ[^173-2] aññataraññataraṃ diṭṭhigataṃ gahetvā [^173-3]uggahitvā gaṇhitvā[^173-3] parāmasitvā abhinivisitvā vivādiyanti[^173-1], kalahaṃ karonti, bhaṇḍanaṃ karonti, viggahaṃ karonti, vivādaṃ karonti, medhagaṃ karonti: na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi, ahaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāmi[^173-4]; kiṃ tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānissasi? micchāpaṭipanno tvam asi, aham asmi sammāpaṭipanno; sahitam me, asahitan te; pure vacanīyaṃ, pacchā avaca; pacchā vacanīyaṃ, pure avaca; adhiciṇṇan te viparāvattaṃ, āropito te vādo, niggahito si, cara, vādappamokkhāya nibbedhehi[^173-5] vā sace pahosī ti, ye diṭṭhim uggayha vivādiyanti. Idam eva saccan ti ca vādiyantī ti. Sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti vādiyanti kathenti[^173-6] bhananti[^173-7] dīpayanti voharanti. Asassato[^173-8] loko . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti vādiyanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, idam eva saccan ti ca vādiyanti. Te tvaṃ vadassū, na hi te 'dha[^173-9] atthi vādamhi jāte paṭisenikattā ti. Te tvaṃ diṭṭhigatike vadassū [^173-10]vādena vādaṃ[^173-10] niggahena niggahaṃ, paṭikkammena paṭikkammaṃ, visesena visesam, paṭivisesena paṭivisesaṃ, āvedhiyāya āvedhiyaṃ, nibbedhiyāya nibbedhiyaṃ, chedena chedaṃ, maṇḍalena maṇḍalaṃ; te tuyhaṃ paṭisūrā paṭipurisā paṭisattū paṭimallā ti te tvaṃ vadassu. Na hi te 'dha[^173-11] atthi vādamhi jāte paṭisenikattā ti: vāde jāte sañjāte nibbatte abhinibbatte pātubhūte ye[^173-12] paṭisenikattā[^173-13] paṭi-


<!-- PTS Page 174 -->
<a id="page-174"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 174]*

lomakattā[^174-1] paṭikaṇṭakakattā[^174-2] paṭipakkhakattā[^174-3] kalahaṃ kareyyuṃ, bhaṇḍanaṃ kareyyuṃ, viggahaṃ kareyyuṃ, vivādaṃ kareyyuṃ, medhagaṃ kareyyuṃ, te n'; atthi na santi na saṃvijjanti n'; upalabbhanti, pahīnā samucchinnā vūpasantā paṭipassaddhā abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā ti, te tvaṃ vadassū na hi te 'dha atthi vādamhi jāte paṭisenikattā[^174-4]. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Ye diṭṭhim uggayha vivādiyanti, idam eva saccan ti ca vādiyanti, te tvaṃ vadassu na hi te 'dha[^174-5] atthi vādamhi jāte paṭisenikattā[^174-6] ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,8.10(=833): Visenikatvā pana ye caranti diṭṭhīhi diṭṭhiṃ avirujjhamānā, tesu tvaṃ [^174-7]kiṃ labhetho[^174-7] Pasūra, yesīdha n'; atthī param uggahītaṃ.  || Nidd_I.8:10 ||

Visenikatvā pana ye carantī ti. Senā vuccati mārasenā; kāyaduccaritaṃ mārasenā, vacīduccaritaṃ mārasenā, manoduccaritaṃ mārasenā, rāgo[^174-8] mārasenā, doso mārasenā, moho mārasenā, kodho upanāho makkho paḷāso issā macchariyaṃ māyā sāṭheyyaṃ thambho sārambho māno atimāno mado pamādo [^174-9]sabbe kilesā sabbe duccaritā[^174-9] sabbe darathā sabbe pariḷāhā sabbe santāpā sabbākusalabhisaṃkhārā mārasenā. Vuttaṃ h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā: *Kāmā te pathamā senā, dutiyārati vuccati . . . pe . . . jetvā ca labhate sukhan ti. Yato catūhi ariyamaggehi sabbā ca mārasenā sabbe ca paṭisenikarā kilesā jitā [^174-10]ca parājitā ca[^174-10] bhaggā vippa-


<!-- PTS Page 175 -->
<a id="page-175"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 175]*

luggā[^175-1] parammukhā; tena vuccanti[^175-2] visenikatvā[^175-3] ye ti arahanto khīṇāsavā. Carantī ti caranti viharanti iriyanti vattenti pālenti yapenti yāpentī ti, visenikatvā pana ye caranti. Diṭṭhīhi diṭṭhiṃ avirujjhamānā ti. Yesaṃ[^175-4] dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni pahīnāni samucchinnāni vūpasantāni paṭipassaddhāni abhabbuppattikāni ñāṇagginā daḍḍhāni; te diṭṭhīhi diṭṭhiṃ avirujjhamānā[^175-5] aghaṭṭiyamānā[^175-6] appaṭihaññamānā appaṭihatamānā ti, diṭṭhīhi diṭṭhiṃ avirujjhamānā. Tesu tvaṃ [^175-7]kiṃ labhetho[^175-7] Pasūrā[^175-8] ti; tesu arahantesu khīṇāsavesu kiṃ labhetho paṭisūra[^175-9] paṭipurisa paṭisattu paṭimallā ti, tesu tvaṃ kiṃ labhetho Pasūra[^175-10]. Yesīdha n'; atthī param uggahītan ti yesaṃ arahantānaṃ khīṇāsavānaṃ idaṃ paramaṃ aggaṃ seṭṭhaṃ viseṭṭhaṃ pāmokkhaṃ uttamaṃ pavaran ti gahitaṃ parāmaṭṭhaṃ[^175-11] ajjhositaṃ adhimuttaṃ n'; atthi na saṃvijjati[^175-12] n'; upalambhati[^175-12]; pahīnaṃ samucchinnaṃ vūpasantaṃ paṭipassaddhaṃ abhabbuppattikaṃ ñāṇagginā daḍḍhan ti, yesīdha n'; atthī param uggahītaṃ. Ten'; aha Bhagavā:

Visenikatvā pana ye caranti diṭṭhīhi diṭṭhiṃ avirujjhamānā, tesu tvaṃ kiṃ labhetho Pasūra, yesīdha n'; atthī param uggahītan ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,8.11(=834): Atha tvaṃ pavitakkam[^175-13] āgamā manasā diṭṭhigatāni cintayanto, dhonena yugaṃ[^175-14] samāgamā, na hi tvaṃ sakkhasi sampayātave[^175-15].  || Nidd_I.8:11 ||


<!-- PTS Page 176 -->
<a id="page-176"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 176]*

Atha tvaṃ pavitakkam āgamā ti. Athā ti padasandhi padasaṃsaggo padapāripūri akkharasamavāyo byañjanasiliṭṭhatā padānupubbatā-m-etaṃ, athā ti. Pavitakkam āgamā ti takkento[^176-1] vitakkento[^176-1] saṃkappento[^176-1]: Jayo nu kho me bhavissati, parājayo nu kho me bhavissati? kathaṃ niggahaṃ karissāmi? kathaṃ paṭikammaṃ karissāmi? [^176-2]kathaṃ visesaṃ karissāmi[^176-2] kathaṃ paṭivisesaṃ karissāmi? kathaṃ āvedhiyaṃ karissāmi? kathaṃ nibbedhiyaṃ karissāmi? kathaṃ chedaṃ karissāmi? kathaṃ maṇḍalaṃ karissāmī? ti evaṃ takkento vitakkento saṃkappento āgato 'si upāgato 'si sampatto 'si mayā saddhiṃ samāgato 'sī ti, atha tvaṃ pavitakkam āgamā. Manasā diṭṭhigatāni cintayanto ti. Mano[^176-3] ti yaṃ cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho, tajjā manoviññāṇadhātu. Cittena diṭṭhigatāni[^176-4] cintento vicintento; sassato loko ti vā, asassato loko ti vā . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā ti vā ti, manasā diṭṭhigatāni cintayanto. Dhonena yugaṃ samāgamā, na hi tvaṃ sakkhasi sampayātave ti. Dhonā[^176-5] vuccati paññā; yā paññā pajānanā . . . pe . . . amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi. Kiṃkāraṇā dhonā vuccati paññā? Tāya paññāya kayaduccaritaṃ dhutañ ca dhotañ ca sandhotañ ca niddhotañ ca . . . pe . . . sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārā dhutā ca dhotā ca sandhotā ca niddhotā ca. Athavā sammādiṭṭhiyā micchadiṭṭhi[^176-6] [^176-7]dhutā ca dhotā ca sandhotā ca niddhotā ca[^176-7] . . . pe . . . sammāvimuttiyā micchāvimutti dhutā ca dhotā ca sandhotā ca niddhotā ca; athavā ariyena aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena sabbe akusalā[^176-8], sabbe duccaritā, sabbe darathā, sabbe pariḷāhā, sabbe santāpā, sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārā dhutā ca dhotā ca sandhotā ca niddhotā ca. Bhagavā imehi dhoneyyehi dhammehi upeto samu-


<!-- PTS Page 177 -->
<a id="page-177"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 177]*

peto upagato samupagato upapanno samupapanno[^177-1] samannāgato; tasmā Bhagavā dhono. So dhutarāgo dhutapāpo dhutakileso dhutapariḷāho ti [^177-2]dhoṇo ti[^177-2]. Dhonena yugaṃ samāgamā na hi tvaṃ sakkhasi sampayātave ti. Pasūro paribbājako na-ppaṭibalo dhonena Buddhena Bhagavatā saddhiṃ yugaṃ samāgamā[^177-3] samāgantvā yugaggāhaṃ gaṇhituṃ[^177-4] sākacchetuṃ sallapituṃ sākacchaṃ samāpajjituṃ. Taṃ kissa hetu? Pasūro paribbājako hīno nihīno omako lāmako jatukko[^177-5] paritto. So hi Bhagavā aggo ca seṭṭho ca viseṭṭho ca pāmokkho ca pavaro ca. Yathā saso na-ppaṭibalo mattena mātaṅgena saddhiṃ yugaṃ samāgamā samāgantvā yugaggāhaṃ gaṇhituṃ; yathā koṭṭhako na-ppaṭibalo sīhena migaraññā saddhiṃ yugaṃ samāgamā samāgantvā yugaggāhaṃ gaṇhituṃ; yathā vacchako taruṇako dhenupako na-ppaṭibalo usabhena balakkakunā[^177-6] saddhiṃ yugaṃ samāgamā samāgantvā yugaggāhaṃ gaṇhituṃ; yathā dhaṃko na-ppaṭibalo garuḷena venateyyena saddhiṃ yugaṃ samāgamā samāgantvā yugaggāhaṃ gaṇhituṃ; yathā caṇḍālo na-ppaṭibalo raññā cakkavattinā saddhiṃ yugaṃ samāgamā samāgantvā yugaggāhaṃ gaṇhituṃ; yathā paṃsupisācako na-ppaṭibalo Indena devaraññā saddhiṃ yugaṃ samāgamā samāgantvā yugaggāhaṃ gaṇhituṃ; evam eva Pasūro paribbājako na-ppaṭibalo dhonena Buddhena Bhagavatā saddhiṃ yugaṃ samāgamā samāgantvā yugaggāhaṃ gaṇhituṃ sākacchetuṃ sallapituṃ sākacchaṃ samāpajjituṃ. Taṃ kissa hetu? Pasūro paribbājako hīnapañño nihīnapañño omakapañño lāmakapañño jatukkapañño parittapañño. So hi Bhagavā mahāpañño puthupañño hāsapañño javanapañño[^177-7] tikkhapañño nibbedhikapañño[^177-8] paññāppabhedakusalo pabhinnañāṇo adhigatapaṭisambhido catuvesārajjappatto dasabaladhārī purisāsabho purisasīho purisanāgo


<!-- PTS Page 178 -->
<a id="page-178"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 178]*

purisājañño purisadhorayho anantañāṇo anantatejo anantayaso aḍḍho[^178-1] mahaddhano dhanavā netā[^178-2] vinetā anunetā saññāpetā[^178-3] nijjhāpetā pekkhatā[^178-4] pasāretā[^178-5]. So hi Bhagavā anuppannassa maggassa uppādetā, asañjātassa maggassa sañjānetā, anakkhātassa maggassa akkhātā, maggaññū maggavidū maggakovido; maggānugā ca [^178-6]pan' assa etarahi[^178-6] sāvakā viharanti pacchā samannāgatā. So hi Bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ passati, cakkhubhūto ñāṇabhūto dhammabhūto brahmabhūto vattā pavattā, atthassa ninnetā, amatassa dātā, dhammasāmi[^178-7] tathāgato. N'; atthi tassa Bhagavato aññātaṃ[^178-8] adiṭṭhaṃ aviditaṃ asacchikataṃ aphusitaṃ[^178-9] paññāya; atītaṃ anāgataṃ paccuppannaṃ upādāya sabbe dhammā sabbākārena Buddhassa Bhagavato ñāṇamukhe āpāthaṃ āgacchanti; yaṃ kiñci neyyaṃ[^178-10] nāma atthi[^178-11] jānitabbaṃ, attattho vā parattho vā ubhayattho vā, diṭṭhadhammiko vā attho samparāyiko vā attho, uttāno vā attho gambhīro vā attho, gūḷho[^178-12] vā attho paṭicchanno vā attho, neyyo vā attho nīto vā attho, anavajjo vā attho, nikkileso vā attho, vodāno vā attho, paramattho vā attho[^178-13], sabban taṃ anto buddhañāṇe parivattati. [^178-14]Sabbaṃ kāyakammaṃ[^178-14] Buddhassa Bhagavato ñāṇānuparivattati[^178-15], sabbaṃ vacīkammaṃ ñāṇānuparivattati, sabbaṃ manokammaṃ ñāṇānuparivattati. Atīte Buddhassa Bhagavato appaṭihataṃ ñāṇaṃ, anāgate Buddhassa Bhagavato appaṭihataṃ ñāṇaṃ, paccuppanne Buddhassa Bhagavato appaṭihataṃ ñāṇaṃ. Yāvatakaṃ neyyaṃ tāvatakaṃ ñāṇaṃ, yāvatakaṃ ñāṇaṃ tāvatakaṃ neyyaṃ; neyyapariyantikaṃ ñāṇaṃ, ñāṇapariyantikaṃ neyyaṃ. Neyyaṃ atikkamitvā ñāṇaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 179 -->
<a id="page-179"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 179]*

na-pparivattati[^179-1], ñāṇaṃ atikkamitvā neyyapatho n'; atthi, aññamaññapariyantaṭṭhāyino te dhammā. Yathā dvinnaṃ samuggapaṭalānaṃ sammāphusitānaṃ[^179-2] heṭṭhimaṃ samuggapaṭalaṃ uparimaṃ nātivattati, uparimaṃ samuggapaṭalaṃ heṭṭhimaṃ nātivattati, aññamaññapariyantaṭṭhāyino; evam eva Buddhassa Bhagavato neyyañ ca ñāṇañ ca aññamaññapariyantaṭṭhāyino: yāvatakaṃ neyyaṃ tāvatakaṃ ñāṇaṃ, yāvatakaṃ ñānaṃ tāvatakaṃ neyyaṃ; neyyapariyantikaṃ ñānaṃ, ñānapariyantikaṃ neyyaṃ, neyyaṃ atikkamitvā ñāṇaṃ na-pparivattati[^179-3], ñāṇaṃ atikkamitvā neyyapatho n'; atthi; aññamaññapariyantaṭṭhāyino te dhammā. Sabbadhammesu Buddhassa Bhagavato ñāṇaṃ pavattati, sabbe dhammā Buddhassa Bhagavato āvajjanapaṭibaddhā ākaṃkhapaṭibaddhā manasikārapaṭibaddhā cittuppādapaṭibaddhā, sabbasattesu Buddhassa Bhagavato ñāṇaṃ pavattati; sabbesaṃ[^179-4] sattānaṃ Bhagavā[^179-5] āsayaṃ[^179-6] jānāti anusayaṃ jānāti caritaṃ jānāti adhimuttiṃ jānāti, apparajakkhe mahārajakkhe tikkhindriye mudindriye svākāre dvākāre suviññāpaye duviññāpaye bhabbābhabbe satte pajānāti. Sadevako loko samārako[^179-7] sabrahmako[^179-7] sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī[^179-8] pajā sadevamanussā anto buddhañāṇe parivattati. Yathā ye keci macchakacchapā antamaso timitimiṅgalaṃ upādāya anto mahāsamudde[^179-9] parivattanti; evam eva sadevako loko samārako[^179-7] sabrahmako[^179-7] sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī[^179-8] pajā[^179-10] sadevamanussā anto buddhañāṇe parivattati. Yathā ye keci pakkhī antamaso garuḷaṃ venateyyaṃ upādāya[^179-11] ākāsassa padese parivattanti[^179-12], evam eva ye pi te Sāriputtasamāpaññāya[^179-13] te[^179-14] pi


<!-- PTS Page 180 -->
<a id="page-180"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 180]*

Buddhañāṇassa padese parivattanti. Buddhañāṇaṃ devamanussānaṃ paññaṃ pharitvā abhibhavitvā[^180-1] tiṭṭhati[^180-2]. Ye pi te khattiyapaṇḍitā brāhmaṇapaṇḍitā gahapatipaṇḍitā samaṇapaṇḍitā nipuṇā kataparappavādā vālavedhirūpā vobhindantā[^180-3] maññe caranti paññāgatena diṭṭhigatāni, te pañhe abhisaṃkharitvā abhisaṃkharitvā tathāgataṃ upasaṃkamitvā pucchanti [^180-4]gūḷhāni ca paṭicchannāni ca[^180-4], kathitā [^180-5]ca visajjitā ca te pañhā Bhagavatā[^180-5] honti niddiṭṭhakāraṇā[^180-6] upakkhittakā[^180-7] ca. Te[^180-8] Bhagavato sampajjanti. Atha kho Bhagavā[^180-9] tattha atirocati[^180-10] yadidam paññāyā ti, dhonena yugaṃ [^180-11]samāgamā na[^180-11] hi tvaṃ sakkhasi sampayātave. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Atha tvaṃ pavitakkam[^180-12] āgamā manasā diṭṭhigatāni cintayanto, dhonena yugaṃ samāgamā, na hi tvaṃ sakkhasi sampayātave ti.

AṬṬHAMO[^180-13] PASŪRASUTTANIDDESO[^180-14] NIṬṬHITO[^180-15].

##### *[PTS Page 181]*

Sn_IV,9.1(=835): Disvāna Taṇhaṃ Aratiñ ca[^181-3] Rāgaṃ[^181-4] nāhosi chando api methunasmiṃ; kim'; ev'; idaṃ muttakarīsapuṇṇaṃ, pādā pi naṃ samphusituṃ na icche.  || Nidd_I.9:1 ||

Disvāna Taṇhaṃ Aratiñ ca[^181-3] Rāgaṃ[^181-4] nāhosi chando api methunasmin ti Taṇhañ ca [^181-5]Aratiñ ca Rāgañ ca māradhītaro disvā passitvā[^181-5], methunadhamme chando vā rāgo vā pemaṃ vā nāhosī ti, disvāna Taṇhaṃ Aratiñ ca Rāgaṃ nāhosi chando api methunasmiṃ. Kim'; ev'; idaṃ muttakarīsapuṇṇaṃ pādā pi naṃ samphusituṃ na icche ti kim'; ev'; idaṃ sarīram[^181-6] muttapuṇṇaṃ karīsapuṇṇam semhapuṇṇaṃ rudhirapuṇṇaṃ[^181-7] aṭṭhisaṃghāṭaṃ nhārusambandhaṃ[^181-8] rudhiramaṃsāvalepanaṃ cammāvanaddhaṃ[^181-9] chaviyā paṭicchannaṃ chiddāvachiddaṃ ugghariṃ[^181-10] magghariṃ[^181-11] kimisaṃghanisevitaṃ[^181-12] nānākalimalaparipūraṃ pādena akkamituṃ na iccheyyaṃ[^181-13]. Kuto pana saṃvāso vā samāgamo vā ti, kim ev'


<!-- PTS Page 182 -->
<a id="page-182"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 182]*

idaṃ muttakarīsapuṇṇaṃ pādā pi naṃ samphusituṃ na icche. [^182-1]Anacchariyañ[^182-2] c'; etaṃ manusso[^182-3] yaṃ[^182-4] dibbe kāme patthayanto mānussake kāme na iccheyya, mānussake vā kāme patthayanto dibbe kāme na iccheyya; yaṃ tvaṃ ubho pi na icchasi, na sādiyasi na patthesi na pihesi nābhijappasi, kin te dassanaṃ, katamāya tvaṃ[^182-5] diṭṭhiyā samannāgato? ti pucchatī[^182-6] ti[^182-1]. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Disvāna Taṇhaṃ Aratiñ ca Rāgaṃ nāhosi chando api methunasmiṃ; kim'; ev'; idaṃ muttakarīsapuṇṇaṃ, pādā pi naṃ samphusituṃ na icche ti.

Sn_IV,9.2(=836): Etādisañ ce ratanaṃ na icchasi[^182-7] nāriṃ narindehi bahūhi patthitaṃ, diṭṭhigataṃ sīlavatānujīvitaṃ[^182-8] bhavūpapattiñ ca vadesi kīdisaṃ.  || Nidd_I.9:2 ||

Idaṃ vadāmī ti na tassa hoti Māgandiyā ti Bhagavā dhammesu[^182-9] niccheyya samuggahītaṃ, passañ[^182-10] ca diṭṭhīsu anuggahāya ajjhattasantiṃ pacinaṃ adassaṃ.  || Nidd_I.9:3 ||

Idaṃ vadāmī ti na tassa hotī ti. Idaṃ vadāmī ti idaṃ vadāmi, etaṃ vadāmi, ettakaṃ vadāmi, ettāvatā vadāmi, [^182-11]idam diṭṭhigataṃ vadāmi[^182-11], sassato loko ti vā . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā ti vā ti[^182-12]. Na tassa hotī ti na mayhaṃ[^182-13] [^182-14]hoti ettāvatā vadāmī ti na tassa[^182-14] hotī ti, idaṃ vadāmī ti na tassa hoti.


<!-- PTS Page 183 -->
<a id="page-183"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 183]*

Māgandiyā ti Bhagavā taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ nāmena ālapati. Bhagavā ti gāravādhivacanaṃ[^183-1] . . . pe . . . sacchikā paññatti; yadidaṃ Bhagavā ti, Māgandiyā ti Bhagavā. Dhammesu[^183-2] niccheyya samuggahītan ti. Dhammesu[^183-3] dvāsaṭṭhiyā diṭṭhigatesu. Niccheyyā ti nicchinitvā[^183-4] vinicchinitvā[^183-5] vicinitvā[^183-6] pavicinitvā[^183-6] tulayitvā tīrayitvā[^183-7] vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā, odhiggāho[^183-8] vilaggāho varaggāho[^183-9] koṭṭhāsaggāho uccayaggāho samuccayaggāho: idaṃ saccaṃ tacchaṃ tathaṃ[^183-6] bhūtaṃ yāthāvaṃ aviparittan ti gahitaṃ parāmaṭṭhaṃ abhiniviṭṭhaṃ ajjhositaṃ adhimuttaṃ n' atthi[^183-10] na saṃvijjati n'; upalabbhati; pahīnaṃ samucchinnaṃ vūpasantaṃ paṭipassaddhaṃ abhabbuppattikaṃ ñāṇagginā daḍḍhan ti[^183-11], dhammesu niccheyya samuggahītaṃ. Passañ[^183-12] ca diṭṭhīsu anuggahāyā ti diṭṭhīsu ādīnavaṃ passanto diṭṭhiyo na gaṇhāmi na parāmasāmi na[^183-13] abhinivisāmi. Athavā na gaṇhitabbā na parāmasitabbā na[^183-13] abhinivisitabbā ti[^183-6], evam pi passañ[^183-12] ca diṭṭhīsu anuggahāya. Athavā sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ mogham aññan ti diṭṭhigatam etaṃ, diṭṭhigahaṇaṃ diṭṭhikantāro[^183-14] diṭṭhivisūkāyikaṃ diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ diṭṭhisaṃyojanaṃ sadukkhaṃ savighātaṃ sa-upāyāsaṃ[^183-15] sapariḷāhaṃ, na nibbidhāya[^183-16], na virāgāya, na nirodhāya, na upasamāya, na abhiññāya, na sambodhāya, na nibbānāya saṃvattatī ti diṭṭhīsu ādīnavaṃ passanto, diṭṭhiyo na gaṇhāmi, na parāmasāmi, na abhinivisāmi. Athavā na gaṇhitabbā na parāmasitabbā, [^183-17]na abhinivisitabbā[^183-17] ti, evam pi passañ[^183-12] ca diṭṭhīsu anuggahāya.


<!-- PTS Page 184 -->
<a id="page-184"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 184]*

Asassato[^184-1] loko, antavā loko, anantavā loko, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ, aññaṃ jīvaṃ aññaṃ sarīraṃ, hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā, na hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā, hoti ca na ca hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā, n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti diṭṭhigatam etaṃ diṭṭhigahaṇaṃ diṭṭhikantāro diṭṭhivisūkāyikaṃ[^184-2] diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ diṭṭhisaṃyojanaṃ sadukkhaṃ savighātaṃ sa-upāyāsaṃ sapariḷāhaṃ, na nibbidhāya[^184-3], na virāgāya, na nirodhāya, na upasamāya, na abhiññāya, na sambodhāya, na nibbānāya saṃvattatī ti diṭṭhīsu[^184-4] ādīnavaṃ passanto, diṭṭhiyo na gaṇhāmi, na parāmasāmi, na abhinivisāmi. Athavā na gaṇhitabbā, na parāmasitabbā, na abhinivisitabbā ti, evam pi passañ[^184-5] ca diṭṭhīsu anuggahāya. Athavā imā diṭṭhiyo evaṃgahitā evaṃ-parāmaṭṭhā evaṃ-gatikā[^184-6] bhavissanti[^184-7] evaṃabhisamparāyā ti diṭṭhīsu ādīnavaṃ passanto, diṭṭhiyo na gaṇhāmi, na parāmasāmi, na abhinivisāmi. Athavā na gaṇhitabbā, na parāmasitabbā, na abhinivisitabbā ti, evam pi passañ[^184-5] ca diṭṭhīsu anuggahāya. [^184-8]Athavā imā diṭṭhiyo nirayasaṃvattanikā[^184-9] [^184-8]tiracchānayonisaṃvattanikā pittivisayasaṃvattanikā ti diṭṭhisu ādīnavaṃ passanto diṭṭhiyo na gaṇhāmi, na parāmasāmi, nābhinivisāmi. Athavā na gaṇhitabbā, na parāmasitabbā, nābhinivisitabbā ti, evam pi passañ[^184-10] ca diṭṭhīsu anuggahāya. Athavā imā diṭṭhiyo aniccā saṃkhatā paṭiccasamuppannā khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā nirodhadhammā ti diṭṭhīsu ādīnavaṃ passanto diṭṭhiyo na gaṇhāmi, na parāmasāmi, nābhinivisāmi. Athavā na gaṇhitabbā, na parāmasitabbā, nābhinivisitabbā ti[^184-11], evam pi[^184-11] passañ[^184-10] ca diṭṭhīsu anuggahāya.


<!-- PTS Page 185 -->
<a id="page-185"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 185]*

Ajjhattasantiṃ[^185-1] pacinaṃ adassan ti [^185-2]ajjhattaṃ santiṃ[^185-2], ajjhattaṃ rāgassa santiṃ, dosassa santiṃ, mohassa santiṃ, kodhassa upanāhassa makkhassa paḷāsassa issāya macchariyassa māyāya sāṭheyyassa[^185-3] thambhassa sārambhassa mānassa atimānassa madassa[^185-4] pamādassa sabbakilesānaṃ sabbaduccaritānaṃ sabbadarathānaṃ sabbapariḷāhānaṃ sabbasantāpānaṃ sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārānaṃ santiṃ[^185-5] vūpasantiṃ nibbutiṃ paṭipassaddhiṃ. Pacinan[^185-6] ti pacinanto vicinanto[^185-7] pavicinanto[^185-8] tulayanto tīrayanto vibhāvayanto vibhūtaṃ karonto. Sabbe saṃkhārā aniccā ti pacinanto[^185-9] vicinanto pavicinanto[^185-7] tulayanto tīrayanto vibhāvayanto vibhūtaṃ karonto. Sabbe saṃkhārā dukkhā ti, sabbe dhammā anattā ti[^185-10] . . . pe . . . yaṃ kiñci samudayadhammaṃ sabban taṃ nirodhadhamman ti pacinanto vicinanto pavicinanto[^185-7] tulayanto tīrayanto vibhāvayanto vibhūtaṃ karonto. Adassan ti adassaṃ adakkhiṃ[^185-11] passiṃ[^185-12] paṭivijjhin[^185-13] ti, ajjhattasantiṃ pacinaṃ adassaṃ[^185-14]. Ten āha Bhagavā:

Idaṃ vadāmī ti na tassa hoti Māgandiyā ti Bhagavā dhammesu[^185-15] niccheyya samuggahītaṃ, passañ[^185-16] ca diṭṭhīsu anuggahāya ajjhattasantiṃ pacinaṃ adassan ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,9.4(=838): Vinicchayā yāni pakappitāni, iti Māgandiyo te ve[^185-17] munī brūsi anuggahāya, ajjhattasantī ti yam etam atthaṃ kathaṃ nu dhīrehi paveditan taṃ.  || Nidd_I.9:4 ||


<!-- PTS Page 186 -->
<a id="page-186"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 186]*

Vinicchayā yāni pakappitānī ti. Vinicchayā[^186-1] vuccanti dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni diṭṭhivinicchayā[^186-2]. Pakappitānī ti kappitā[^186-3] abhisaṃkhatā saṇṭhapitā ti pi pakappitā. Athavā aniccā saṃkhatā paticcasamuppannā khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā[^186-4] nirodhadhammā vipariṇāmadhammā ti pi pakappitā ti, vinicchayā yāni pakappitāni. Iti Māgandiyo ti. Itī ti padasandhi[^186-5] . . . pe . . . padānupubbatā-m-etam, itī ti[^186-6]. Te ve munī brūsi anuggahāya ajjhattasantī ti [^186-7]yam etam atthan ti[^186-7]. Te ve ti dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni. Munī ti monaṃ vuccati ñāṇaṃ . . . pe . . . saṅgajālam aticca so munī ti. Anuggahāyā ti. Diṭṭhīsu ādīnavaṃ passanto diṭṭhiyo na gaṇhāmi, na parāmasāmi, nābhinivisāmī[^186-8] ti paggaṇhāsi[^186-9]. Ajjhattasantī ti ca bhaṇasi. Yam etam atthan ti yaṃ paramatthan ti, te[^186-2] ve[^186-2] munī brūsi anuggahāya, ajjhattasantī[^186-10] ti yam etam atthaṃ. Kathaṃ nu dhīrehi paveditan tan ti. Kathan [^186-11]nū ti[^186-11] saṃsayapucchā[^186-12] vimatipucchā dveḷhakapucchā anekaṃsapucchā[^186-13]. Evaṃ nu kho,[^186-14]na nu kho, kin nu kho[^186-14], kathaṃ nu kho ti, kathaṃ nu[^186-8]. Dhīrehī ti[^186-15] paṇḍitehi paññavantehi buddhimantehi ñāṇīhi vibhāvīhi medhāvīhi. Paveditan ti veditaṃ paveditaṃ[^186-16] ācikkhitaṃ desitaṃ paññapitaṃ paṭṭhapitaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 187 -->
<a id="page-187"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 187]*

vivaritaṃ[^187-1] vibhajitaṃ[^187-2] uttānīkataṃ pakāsitan ti, kathaṃ nu dhīrehi paveditan taṃ. Ten āha Bhagavā[^187-3]:

Vinicchayā yāni pakappitāni, iti Māgandiyo te ve munī brūsi anuggahāya, ajjhattasantī ti yam etam atthaṃ kathaṃ nu dhīrehi [^187-4]paveditan taṃ[^187-4]?

_________________________________

Sn_IV,9.5(=839): Na diṭṭhiyā na sutiyā na ñānena, Māgandiyā ti Bhagavā sīlabbatenā pi na suddhim āha adiṭṭhiyā assutiyā[^187-5] añāṇā[^187-6] asīlatā abbatā[^187-7] no pi tena, ete ca nissajja anuggahāya santo anissāya bhavaṃ na jappe[^187-8].  || Nidd_I.9:5 ||

Na diṭṭhiyā na sutiyā na ñāṇenā ti. Diṭṭhena pi suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ n'; āha, na kathesi, na bhaṇasi, na dīpayasi, na voharasi. Sutena pi suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ n'; āha, na kathesi, na bhaṇasi, na dīpayasi, na voharasi. [^187-9]Diṭṭhasutena pi suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ n'; āha, na kathesi, na bhaṇasi, na dīpayasi, na voharasi[^187-9]. Ñāṇena pi suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ n'; āha, na kathesi, na bhaṇasi[^187-10], na dīpayasi, na voharasī ti, na diṭṭhiyā na sutiyā na ñāṇena. Māgandiyā ti Bhagavā taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ nāmena ālapati. Bhagavā ti gāravādhivacanaṃ . . . pe . . . sacchikā paññatti, yadidaṃ Bhagavā ti, Māgandiyā ti Bhagavā.


<!-- PTS Page 188 -->
<a id="page-188"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 188]*

Sīlabbatenā pi na suddhim āhā ti sīlena pi suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ n' āha, na kathesi, na bhaṇasi[^188-1], na dīpayasi [^188-2]na voharasi[^188-2]. Vattena[^188-3] pi suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ n'; āha, na kathesi, na bhaṇasi, na dīpayasi, na voharasi. Sīlabbatenā[^188-4] pi suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ n'; āha, na kathesi, na bhaṇasi, na dīpayasi, na voharasī ti, sīlabbatenā pi na suddhiṃ āha. Adiṭṭhiyā assutiyā[^188-5] añāṇā[^188-6] asīlatā abbatā[^188-7] no pi tenā ti. Diṭṭhi pi icchitabbā, dasavatthukā sammādiṭṭhi: atthi dinnaṃ, atthi yiṭṭhaṃ, atthi hutaṃ, atthi sukaṭadukkaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipāko, atthi ayaṃ loko, atthi paro loko, atthi mātā, atthi pitā, atthi sattā opapātikā, atthi loke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā[^188-8] sammāpaṭipannā, ye imañ ca[^188-9] lokaṃ parañ ca lokaṃ sayaṃ[^188-10] abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentī ti. Savanam pi icchitabbaṃ: parato ghoso, suttaṃ geyyaṃ veyyākaraṇaṃ gāthā udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ[^188-11] jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ[^188-12] vedallaṃ. Ñāṇam pi icchitabbaṃ: kammassakataṃ[^188-13] ñāṇaṃ saccānulomikaṃ[^188-14] ñāṇaṃ abhiññā ñāṇaṃ samāpattiñāṇaṃ[^188-15]. Sīlam pi icchitabbaṃ: pātimokkhasaṃvaro[^188-16]. Vattam pi icchitabbaṃ: aṭṭha dhutaṅgāni, āraññikaṅgaṃ {piṇḍapātikaṅgaṃ} paṃsukūlikaṅgaṃ tecīvarikaṅgaṃ sapadānacārikaṅgaṃ khalupacchābhattikaṅgaṃ nesajjikaṅgaṃ yathāsanthatikaṅgaṃ. Adiṭṭhiyā assutiyā[^188-17] añāṇā[^188-18] asīlatā abbatā[^188-7] no pi tenā


<!-- PTS Page 189 -->
<a id="page-189"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 189]*

ti na pi [^189-1]sammādiṭṭhimattena, na pi[^189-1] [^189-2]savanamattena, na pi[^189-2] ñāṇamattena, na[^189-3] pi[^189-3] sīlamattena, na[^189-3] pi[^189-3] vattamattena ajjhattasantipatto hoti, na pi vinā etehi dhammehi ajjhattasantiṃ pāpuṇāti; api ca sambhārā ime dhammā honti[^189-4] ajjhattasantiṃ pāpuṇituṃ adhigantuṃ phusituṃ[^189-5] sacchikātun ti, adiṭṭhiyā assutiyā[^189-6] añāṇā[^189-7] asīlatā abbatā no pi tena. Ete ca nissajja[^189-8] anuggahāyā ti ete[^189-9] kaṇhapakkhikānaṃ[^189-10] dhammānaṃ samugghātato pahānaṃ icchitabbaṃ. Tedhātukesu kusalesu[^189-3] dhammesu atammayatā[^189-11] icchitabbā. Yato kaṇhapakkhikā[^189-12] dhammā samugghātapahānena pahīnā honti ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃgatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, tedhātukesu ca kusalesu dhammesu atammayatā[^189-13] hoti. Ettāvatā pi na gaṇhāti, na parāmasati, nābhinivisati. Athavā na[^189-3] gaṇhitabbā, na parāmasitabbā, nābhinivisitabbā ti, evam pi ete ca[^189-14] nissajja anuggahāya. Yato[^189-15] taṇhā ca diṭṭhi ca[^189-16] māno ca pahīnā honti, ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃgatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā[^189-17], ettāvatā pi na gaṇhāti, na parāmasati, nābhinivisatī ti, evam pi ete ca nissajja anuggahāya. Yato puññābhisaṃkhāro ca apuññābhisaṃkhāro ca [^189-18]āṇeñjābhisaṃkhāro ca[^189-18] pahīnā honti, ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṅgatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, ettāvatā pi na gaṇhāti, na parāmasati, nābhinivisatī ti, evam pi ete ca nissajja anuggahāya. Santo anissāya bhavaṃ na jappe ti. Santo ti rāgassa santattā[^189-19] santo, dosassa santattā santo, mohassa santattā


<!-- PTS Page 190 -->
<a id="page-190"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 190]*

santo, kodhassa upanāhassa makkhassa paḷāsassa issāya macchariyassa māyāya sāṭheyyassa thambhassa sārambhassa mānassa atimānassa madassa pamādassa sabbakilesānaṃ sabbaduccaritānaṃ sabbadarathānaṃ sabbapariḷāhānaṃ sabbasantāpānaṃ sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārānaṃ santattā samitattā vūpasamitattā vijjhātattā nibbutattā vigatattā paṭipassaddhattā santo[^190-1] vūpasanto nibbuto paṭipassaddho ti, santo. Anissāyā ti dve nissayā, taṇhānissayo ca diṭṭhinissayo ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhānissayo . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhinissayo. Taṇhānissayaṃ pahāya, diṭṭhinissayaṃ paṭinissajjitvā, cakkhuṃ anissāya, sotaṃ anissāya, ghānaṃ anissāya, jivhaṃ anissāya, kāyaṃ anissāya, manaṃ anissāya, rūpe sadde gandhe rase phoṭṭhabbe kulaṃ gaṇam āvāsaṃ lābhaṃ yasaṃ pasaṃsaṃ sukhaṃ cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātaṃ senāsanaṃ gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāraṃ kāmadhātuṃ rūpadhātuṃ arūpadhātuṃ kāmabhavaṃ rūpabhavaṃ arūpabhavaṃ saññābhavaṃ asaññābhavaṃ nevasaññānāsaññābhavaṃ ekavokārabhavaṃ catuvokārabhavaṃ pañcavokārabhavaṃ atītaṃ anāgataṃ paccuppannaṃ diṭṭhasutamutaviññātabbe dhamme[^190-2] anissāya agaṇhitvā aparāmasitvā abhinivisitvā ti, santo anissāya. Bhavaṃ na jappe ti kāmabhavaṃ na jappeyya, rūpabhavaṃ na jappeyya, arūpabhavaṃ na jappeyya, [^190-3]na pajappeyya[^190-3], na abhijappeyyā ti, santo anissāya bhavaṃ na jappe. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Na diṭṭhiyā na sutiyā na ñāṇena, Māgandiyā ti Bhagavā sīlabbatenā pi na suddhim āha adiṭṭhiyā assutiyā[^190-4] añāṇā[^190-5] asīlatā abbatā no pi tena, ete[^190-6] ca nissajja anuggahāya santo anissāya bhavaṃ na jappe ti.
