##### *[PTS Page 191]*

Sn_IV,9.6(=840): No ce kira diṭṭhiyā na sutiyā na ñāṇena iti Māgandiyo sīlabbatenā pi [^191-1]na suddhim[^191-1] āha adiṭṭhiyā assutiyā[^191-2] añāṇā[^191-3] asīlatā abbatā[^191-4] no pi tena, maññām'[^191-5] ahaṃ momuham eva dhammam, diṭṭhiyā eke paccenti suddhiṃ.  || Nidd_I.9:6 ||

No ce kira diṭṭhiyā na sutiyā na ñāṇenā ti diṭṭhiyā pi suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttim [^191-6]n'; āha[^191-6], na kathesi, na bhaṇasi, na dīpayasi, na voharasi[^191-7]. Sutena pi suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhim muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ [^191-8]n'; āha, na kathesi, na bhaṇasi, na dīpayasi, na voharasi[^191-8]. Diṭṭhasutena pi suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ [^191-6]muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ n'; āha, na kathesi, na bhaṇasi, na dīpayasi, na voharasi[^191-6]. Ñāṇena pi suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhim muttiṃ vimuttim parimuttiṃ n'; āha, na kathesi, na bhaṇasi, na dīpayasi, na voharasī ti, no ce kira diṭṭhiyā na sutiyā na ñāṇena. Iti Māgandiyo ti. Itī ti padasandhi . . . pe . . .[^191-8] anupubbatā-m-etaṃ, itī ti[^191-8]. Māgandiyo ti tassa brāhmaṇassa nāman [^191-9]ti, iti Māgandiyo[^191-9]. Sīlabbatenā pi na suddhim āhā ti sīlena pi suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ[^191-10]; vattena[^191-11] pi suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parissuddhiṃ[^191-10] sīlabbatenā pi suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ n'; āha, na kathesi, na bhaṇasi, na dīpayasi, na voharasī ti, sīlabbatenā pi na suddhim āha.


<!-- PTS Page 192 -->
<a id="page-192"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 192]*

Adiṭṭhiyā assutiyā añāṇā asīlatā abbatā[^192-1] no pi tenā ti diṭṭhi pi icchitabbā ti evaṃ bhaṇasi, savanam pi icchitabban ti evaṃ bhaṇasi, ñāṇam pi icchitabban ti evaṃ bhaṇasi, [^192-2]sīlam pi icchitabban ti evaṃ bhaṇasi, vattam pi icchitabban ti evaṃ bhaṇasi[^192-2]. Na sakkosi ekaṃsena anujānituṃ, na sakkosi ekaṃsena paṭikkhipitun ti, adiṭṭhiyā assutiyā añāṇā asīlatā abbatā no pi tena. [^192-3]Maññām'; ahaṃ[^192-3] momuham eva dhamman ti momūhadhammo ayaṃ tuyhaṃ bāladhammo[^192-4] añāṇadhammo[^192-5] amarāvikkhepadhammo[^192-6] ti evaṃ [^192-7]maññāmi, evaṃ[^192-7] jānāmi, evaṃ ājānāmi,[^192-8] evaṃ paṭivijjhāmī[^192-9] ti, maññām' ahaṃ momuham eva dhammaṃ. Diṭṭhiyā eke paccenti suddhin ti diṭṭhiyā eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ paccenti: sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti, diṭṭhiyā eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ paccenti; [^192-10]asassato loko . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti diṭṭhiyā eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ paccentī ti[^192-10], diṭṭhiyā[^192-11] eke paccenti suddhiṃ. Ten'; āha so brāhmaṇo:

No ce kira diṭṭhiyā na sutiyā [^192-12]na ñāṇena[^192-12] iti Māgandiyo sīlabbatenā pi na suddhim āha diṭṭhiyā assutiyā añāṇā asīlatā abbatā no pi tena, maññām'; ahaṃ momuham eva dhammaṃ, diṭṭhiyā eke paccenti suddhin ti.

_________________________________


<!-- PTS Page 193 -->
<a id="page-193"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 193]*

Sn_IV,9.7(=841): Diṭṭhīsu[^193-1] nissāya anupucchamāno Māgandiyā ti Bhagavā samuggahītesu pamoham[^193-2] āgā ito ca nāddakkhi aṇum pi saññaṃ, tasmā tuvaṃ momuhato dahāsi.  || Nidd_I.9:7 ||

Diṭṭhīsu nissāya anupucchamāno ti. Māgandiyo brāhmaṇo diṭṭhiṃ nissāya diṭṭhiṃ pucchati, lagganaṃ nissāya laggaṇaṃ pucchati, bandhanaṃ nissāya bandhanaṃ pucchati, palibodhaṃ nissāya palibodhaṃ pucchati. Anupucchamāno ti punappunaṃ pucchatī ti, diṭṭhīsu nissāya anupucchamāno. Māgandiyā ti Bhagavā taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ nāmena ālapati Bhagavā ti gāravādhivacanaṃ . . . pe . . . sacchikā paññatti yadidaṃ Bhagavā ti, Māgandiyā ti Bhagavā. Samuggahītesu pamoham[^193-3] āgā ti yā[^193-4] diṭṭhi tayā gahiṭā parāmaṭṭhā abhiniviṭṭhā ajjhositā adhimuttā; tay'[^193-5] eva tvaṃ diṭṭhiyā mūlho 'si, pamūḷho[^193-6] sammūḷho[^193-7], mohaṃ āgato 'si, pamohaṃ āgato 'si, sammohaṃ āgato 'si, andhakāraṃ pakkhanto 'sī ti, samuggahītesu pamoham[^193-8] āgā. Ito ca nāddakkhi aṇum pi saññan ti. Ito ajjhattasantito vā paṭipattito[^193-9] vā dhammadesanato vā, yuttasaññaṃ [^193-10]vā pattasaññaṃ[^193-10] vā[^193-11] lakkhaṇasaññaṃ vā kāraṇasaññaṃ vā ṭhānasaññaṃ vā [^193-12]na paṭilabhasi[^193-12]; kuto ñāṇan? ti, evam pi ito ca nāddakkhi aṇum pi saññaṃ. Athavā aniccaṃ vā aniccasaññānulomaṃ vā, dukkhaṃ vā[^193-6] dukkhasaññānulomaṃ vā, anattaṃ vā anattasaññānulomaṃ vā, saññuppādamattaṃ vā saññānimittaṃ[^193-13] vā na[^193-6] paṭila-


<!-- PTS Page 194 -->
<a id="page-194"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 194]*

bhasi, kuto ñāṇan? ti, evam pi ito ca nāddakkhi aṇum pi saññaṃ. Tasmā tuvaṃ momuhato dahāsī ti. Tasmā ti tasmā taṃkāraṇā taṃhetu taṃpaccayā taṃnidānā. Momūhadhammato bāladhammato mūlhadhammato añāṇadhammato amarāvikkhepadhammato dahāsi passasi dakkhasi olokesi nijjhāyasi upaparikkhasī ti, tasmā tuvaṃ momuhato dahāsi. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Diṭṭhīsu nissāya anupucchamāno Māgandiyā ti Bhagavā samuggahītesu pamoham āgā ito ca nāddakkhi aṇum pi saññaṃ, tasmā tuvaṃ momuhato dahāsī ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,9.8(=842): Samo visesī uda vā nihīno yo maññatī, so vivadetha tena, tīsu vidhāsu avikampamāno[^194-1] samo visesī ti na tassa hoti.  || Nidd_I.9:8 ||

Samo visesī uda vā nihīno yo maññatī so vivadetha tenā ti. Sadiso 'ham asmī ti vā, seyyo 'ham asmī ti vā, nihīno[^194-2] 'ham asmī ti vā yo maññati, so tena mānena, tāya diṭṭhiyā, tena[^194-3] vā puggalena, kalahaṃ kareyya, bhaṇḍanaṃ kareyya, viggahaṃ kareyya, vivādaṃ kareyya, medhagaṃ kareyya: Na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi, ahaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāmi; kiṃ tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānissasi? micchāpaṭipanno tvam asi, aham asmi sammāpaṭipanno, sahitam[^194-4] me, asahitan[^194-5] te, pure vacanīyaṃ pacchā avaca, pacchā vacanīyaṃ pure avaca, adhiciṇṇan te viparāvattaṃ, āropito te vādo, niggahito [^194-6]tvam asi[^194-6]; cara vādappamokkhāya, nibbedhehi vā sace pahosī ti, samo visesī uda vā nihīno yo maññatī, so vivadetha tena.


<!-- PTS Page 195 -->
<a id="page-195"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 195]*

Tīsu vidhāsu avikampamāno[^195-1] samo visesī ti na tassa hotī ti. Yass'; etā tisso vidhā pahīnā samucchinnā vūpasantā paṭipassaddhā abhabbuppattikā, ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā, so tīsu vidhāsu na kampati[^195-2], na vikampati[^195-3]; avikampamānassa[^195-4] puggalassa sadiso 'ham asmī ti vā, seyyo 'ham asmī ti vā, nihīno[^195-5] 'ham asmī ti vā, na tassa hotī ti, na mayhaṃ hotī ti, tīsu vidhāsu avikampamāno samo visesī ti na tassa hoti. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Samo visesī uda vā nihīno yo maññatī, so vivadetha tena, tīsu vidhāsu avikampamāno[^195-6] samo visesī ti na tassa hotī ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,9.9(=843): Saccan ti so brāhmaṇo kiṃ vadeyya? musā ti vā so vivadetha kena? yasmiṃ samaṃ visamaṃ vā pi n' atthi, sa kena vādaṃ paṭisaṃyujeyya?  || Nidd_I.9:9 ||

Saccan ti so brāhmaṇo kiṃ vadeyyā? ti. Brāhmaṇo ti sattannaṃ dhammānaṃ bāhitattā brāhmaṇo . . . pe . . . anissito[^195-7] tādi pavuccati[^195-8] [^195-9]sa brahmā ti[^195-9]. Saccan ti so brāhmaṇo kiṃ vadeyyā? ti. Sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, moghamaññan ti brāhmaṇo kiṃ vadeyya, kiṃ katheyya, kiṃ bhaṇeyya, kiṃ dīpayeyya[^195-10], kiṃ vohareyya, asassato loko . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti brāhmaṇo kiṃ vadeyya, kiṃ katheyya, kiṃ bhaṇeyya, kiṃ dīpayeyya, kiṃ vohareyyā? ti[^195-11], saccan ti so brāhmaṇo kiṃ vadeyya? Musā ti vā so vivadetha kenā? ti. Brāhmaṇo mayhaṃ va saccaṃ, tuyhaṃ musā ti kena mānena, kāya diṭṭhiyā, kena vā puggalena kalahaṃ kareyya, bhaṇḍanaṃ kareyya,


<!-- PTS Page 196 -->
<a id="page-196"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 196]*

viggahaṃ kareyya, vivādaṃ kareyya, medhagaṃ[^196-1] kareyya: na[^196-2] tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi . . . pe . . . nibbedhehi[^196-3] vā sace pahosī? ti, musā ti vā so vivadetha kena? Yasmiṃ samaṃ visamaṃ vā pi n'; atthī ti. Yasmin[^196-2] ti[^196-2] yasmiṃ puggale[^196-4] arahante khīṇāsave. Sadiso 'ham asmī ti māno n'; atthi, seyyo 'ham asmī ti atimāno n'; atthi, hīno 'ham asmī ti omāno n'; atthi[^196-5], na saṃvijjati, n' upalabbhati, pahīnaṃ samucchinnaṃ vūpasantaṃ paṭipassaddhaṃ abhabbuppattikaṃ ñāṇagginā daḍḍhan ti, yasmiṃ samaṃ visamaṃ vā pi n'; atthi. Sa kena vādaṃ paṭisaṃyujeyyā? ti so kena mānena kāya diṭṭhiyā, kena vā puggalena vādaṃ paṭisaṃyujjeyya[^196-6] paṭiphareyya[^196-7], kalahaṃ kareyya, bhaṇḍanaṃ kareyya, viggahaṃ kareyya, vivādaṃ kareyya, medhagaṃ kareyya: na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi . . . pe . . . nibbedhehi vā sace pahosī? ti, sa kena vādaṃ paṭisaṃyujeyya[^196-8]? Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Saccan ti so brāhmaṇo kiṃ vadeyya? musā ti vā so vivadetha kena? yasmiṃ samaṃ visamaṃ vā pi n'; atthi, sa kena vādaṃ paṭisaṃyujeyyā? ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,9.10(=844): Okam pahāya aniketasārī gāme[^196-9] akubbaṃ muni santhavānī[^196-10] kāmehi ritto apurekkharāno[^196-11] kathaṃ na[^196-12] viggayha janena kayirā?  || Nidd_I.9:10 ||


<!-- PTS Page 197 -->
<a id="page-197"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 197]*

*Atha kho Hālindakāni[^197-1] gahapati yen'; āyasmā Mahākaccāno ten'; upasaṃkami; upasaṃkamitvā āyasmantaṃ Mahākaccānaṃ abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi; ekam antaṃ nisinno kho Hālindakāni gahapati āyasmantaṃ Mahākaccānaṃ etad avoca: Vuttam idaṃ bhante Kaccāna[^197-2] Bhagavatā Aṭṭhakavaggike[^197-3] Māgandiyapañhe[^197-4]: Okaṃ pahāya aniketasārī gāme[^197-5] akubbaṃ muni santhavānī[^197-6] kāmehi ritto, apurekkharāno kathaṃ na[^197-7] viggayha janena kayirā[^197-8] ti. Imassa nu kho bhante Kaccāna[^197-9] Bhagavatā saṃkhittena bhāsitassa kathaṃ [^197-10]attho vitthārena daṭṭhabbo[^197-10]? ti. -- Rūpadhātu kho gahapati viññāṇassa oko, rūpadhāturāgavinibandhañ[^197-11] ca pana viññāṇaṃ okasārī[^197-12] ti vuccati[^197-13]. Vedanādhātu kho gahapati, saññādhātu kho gahapati, saṃkhāradhātu kho gahapati viññāṇassa oko, saṃkhāradhāturāgavinibandhañ[^197-14] ca pana viññāṇaṃ okasarī ti vuccati; evaṃ kho gahapati okasārī hoti. Kathañ ca gahapati anokasārī[^197-15] hoti? Rūpadhātuyā kho gahapati yo chando, yo rāgo, yā nandi, yā taṇhā, ye upāyupādānā[^197-16] cetaso adhiṭṭhānābhinivesānusayā, te tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃ[^197-17]


<!-- PTS Page 198 -->
<a id="page-198"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 198]*

gatā āyatiṃ[^198-1] anuppādadhammā; tasmā tathāgato anokasārī ti vuccati. Vedanādhātuyā kho gahapati, saññādhātuyā kho gahapati, saṃkhāradhātuyā kho gahapati, viññāṇadhātuyā kho gahapati yo chando, yo rāgo, yā nandi, yā taṇhā, ye upāyupādānā cetaso adhiṭṭhānābhinivesānusayā, te tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃ gatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā; tasmā tathāgato anokasārī ti vuccati; evaṃ kho gahapati anokasārī hoti. Kathañ ca gahapati niketasārī[^198-2] hoti? Rūpanimittaniketasāravinibandhaṃ[^198-3] kho gahapati niketasārī[^198-4] ti vuccati. Saddanimittagandhanimittarasanimittaphoṭṭhabbanimittadhammanimittaniketasāravinibandhaṃ[^198-5] kho gahapati niketasārī ti vuccati; evaṃ kho gahapati niketasārī hoti. Kathañ ca gahapati aniketasārī[^198-6] hoti? Rūpanimittaniketasāravinibandhā[^198-7] kho gahapati tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃ gatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā; tasmā tathāgato aniketasārī ti vuccati. Saddanimittagandhanimittarasanimittaphoṭṭhabbanimittadhammanimittaniketasāravinibandhā[^198-8] kho gahapati tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthugatā anabhāvaṃ gatā āyatiṃ[^198-9] anuppādadhammā; tasmā tathāgato aniketasārī ti vuccati; evaṃ kho gahapati aniketasārī hoti. Kathañ ca gahapati gāme[^198-10] santhavajāto[^198-11] hoti?


<!-- PTS Page 199 -->
<a id="page-199"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 199]*

Idha gahapati ekacco[^199-1] gihīhi[^199-2] saṃsaṭṭho viharati, sahanandī sahasokī, sukhitesu sukhito, dukkhitesu dukkhito, uppannesu kiccakaraṇīyesu attanā voyogaṃ āpajjati. Evaṃ kho gahapati gāme santhavajāto hoti. Kathañ ca gahapati gāme na santhavajāto hoti? Idha gahapati[^199-3] bhikkhu gihīhi[^199-4] asaṃsaṭṭho[^199-5] viharati, na sahanandī, na sahasokī, na sukhitesu sukkhito, na dukkhitesu dukkhito, uppannesu kiccakaraṇīyesu na attanā voyogaṃ āpajjati. Evaṃ kho gahapati gāme na santhavajāto hoti. Kathañ ca gahapati kāmehi[^199-6] aritto[^199-7] hoti? Idha gahapati ekacco[^199-8] kāmesu avītarāgo[^199-9] hoti avītachando[^199-10] avītapemo[^199-11] avītapipāso[^199-12] avītapariḷāho[^199-13] avītataṇho[^199-13], evaṃ kho gahapati kāmehi aritto[^199-14] hoti. Kathañ ca gahapati kāmehi ritto[^199-15] hoti? Idha gahapati[^199-16] bhikkhu kāmesu vītarāgo hoti vītachando[^199-17] [^199-18] vītapemo[^199-17] vītapipāso[^199-17] vītapariḷāho[^199-17] vītataṇho[^199-17]; evaṃ kho gahapati kāmehi ritto hoti. Kathañ ca gahapati purekkharāno[^199-19] hoti? Idha gahapati ekaccassa[^199-20] evaṃ hoti: evaṃrūpo siyaṃ[^199-21] anāgatam addhānan ti, tattha nandiṃ samanvāgameti[^199-22]; evaṃvedano siyaṃ, evaṃsañño siyaṃ, evaṃsaṃkhāro siyaṃ, evaṃviññāṇo siyaṃ anāgatam addhānan ti, tattha nandiṃ samanvāgameti[^199-22]; evaṃ kho gahapati purekkharāno[^199-23] hoti.


<!-- PTS Page 200 -->
<a id="page-200"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 200]*

Kathañ ca gahapati apurekkharāno[^200-1] hoti? Idha gahapati ekaccassa[^200-2] evaṃ hoti: evaṃrūpo siyaṃ anāgatam addhānan ti[^200-3], [^200-4]tattha nandiṃ[^200-4] na[^200-5] samanvāgameti[^200-5], evaṃvedano siyaṃ, evaṃsañño siyaṃ, evaṃsaṃkhāro siyaṃ, evaṃviññāṇo siyaṃ anāgatam addhānan ti, tattha[^200-7] nandiṃ[^200-8] na[^200-9] samanvāgameti[^200-6]; evaṃ kho gahapati apurekkharāno hoti. Kathañ ca gahapati kathaṃ viggayha janena kattā hoti? Idha gahapati ekacco evarūpiṃ[^200-10] kathaṃ kattā hoti: na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi . . . pe[^200-11] . . . nibbedhehi vā sace pahosī ti; evaṃ kho gahapati kathaṃ viggayha janena kattā hoti. Kathañ ca gahapati kathaṃ na viggayha janena kattā hoti? Idha gahapati ekacco na evarūpiṃ kathaṃ kattā hotī: na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi . . . pe . . . nibbedhehi vā sace pahosī ti; evaṃ kho gahapati kathaṃ na viggayha janena kattā hoti. Iti kho gahapati yan taṃ vuttaṃ Bhagavatā Aṭṭhakavaggike Māgaṇḍikapañhe: Okam pahāya aniketasārī gāme akubbaṃ muni santhavānī[^200-12] kāmehi ritto[^200-13] apurekkharāno kathaṃ na viggayha janena kayirā[^200-14] ti.


<!-- PTS Page 201 -->
<a id="page-201"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 201]*

Imassa kho gahapati Bhagavatā saṃkhittena bhāsitassa evaṃ vitthārena attho[^201-1] daṭṭhabbo[^201-2]. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Okam pahāya aniketasārī gāme akubbaṃ muni santhavānī kāmehi ritto apurekkharāno kathaṃ [^201-3]na viggayha[^201-3] janena kayirā[^201-4] ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,9.11(=845): Yehi vivitto vicareyya loke, na tāni uggayha vadeyya[^201-5] nāgo: elambujaṃ[^201-6] kaṇṭakavārijaṃ[^201-7] yathā jalena paṅkena c'; anūpalittaṃ, evaṃ munī santivado[^201-8] agiddho kāme ca loke ca anūpalitto.  || Nidd_I.9:11 ||

Yehi vivitto vicareyya loke ti. Yehī ti yehi[^201-9] diṭṭhigatehi. Vivitto ti kāyaduccaritena vitto[^201-10] vivitto[^201-11] pavivitto, vacīduccaritena manoduccaritena rāgena . . . pe[^201-12] . . . sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārehi[^201-13] vitto[^201-14] vivitto pavivitto[^201-12]. Vicareyyā ti careyya vihareyya iriyeyya vatteyya pāleyya yapeyya yāpeyya. Loke ti manussaloke ti, yehi vivitto vicareyya loke. Na tāni uggayha vadeyya nāgo ti. Nāgo ti āguṃ na karotī ti nāgo; na gacchatī ti nāgo, nāgacchatī ti nāgo. Kathaṃ āguṃ na karotī ti nāgo? Āgu vuccanti pāpakā akusalā dhammā saṃkilesikā ponobbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇīyā.


<!-- PTS Page 202 -->
<a id="page-202"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 202]*

*Āguṃ na karotī ti kiñci loke, Sabhiyā ti Bhagavā, sabbasaṃyoge[^202-1] visajja bandhanāni sabbattha na sajjati vimutto, nāgo tādi vuccate[^202-2] tathattā[^202-3] ti[^202-4]; evaṃ āguṃ na karotī ti, nāgo[^202-5]. Kathaṃ na gacchatī ti nāgo[^202-6]? Na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dosāgatiṃ gacchati, na mohāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati; na rāgavasena gacchati, na dosavasena gacchati, na mohavasena gacchati, na mānavasena gacchati, na diṭṭhivasena gacchati, na uddhaccavasena gacchati, na vicikicchāvasena gacchati, na anusayavasena gacchati, na vaggehi dhammehi yāyati niyyati vuyhati saṃhariyati; evaṃ na gacchatī ti nāgo. Kathaṃ nāgacchatī ti nāgo? Sotāpattimaggena ye kilesā pahīnā, te kilese na puneti, na pacceti, na paccāgacchati; sakadāgāmimaggena anāgāmimaggena arahattamaggena ye kilesā pahīnā, te kilese na puneti, na pacceti, na paccāgacchati; evaṃ nāgacchatī ti nāgo. Na tāni uggayha vadeyya nāgo ti. Nāgo na tāni diṭṭhigatāni[^202-5] gahetvā uggahetvā gaṇhitvā parāmasitvā abhinivisitvā vadeyya katheyya bhaṇeyya dīpayeyya vohareyya[^202-7]: sassato loko[^202-8] asassato loko[^202-8] . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti vadeyya katheyya bhaṇeyya dīpayeyya vohareyyā ti, na tāni uggayha vadeyya nāgo. Elambujaṃ kaṇṭakavārijaṃ[^202-9] yathā jalena paṅkena c' anūpalittan ti. Elaṃ vuccati udakaṃ. [^202-10]Ambu vuccati udakaṃ[^202-10]. Ambujaṃ vuccati padumaṃ. Kaṇṭako[^202-11] vuc-


<!-- PTS Page 203 -->
<a id="page-203"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 203]*

cati kharadaṇḍo[^203-1]. Vāri vuccati udakaṃ. Vārijaṃ[^203-2] vuccati padumaṃ vārijaṃ[^203-3] vārisambhavaṃ[^203-4]. Jalaṃ vuccati udakaṃ. Paṅko vuccati kaddamo. Yathā padumaṃ vārijaṃ[^203-5] vārisambhavaṃ jalena ca paṅkena ca na limpati, [^203-6]na saṃlimpati[^203-6], [^203-7]n'; upalimpati[^203-7], alittaṃ asaṃlittaṃ anupalittan ti, elambujaṃ kaṇṭakavārijaṃ yathā jalena paṅkena c'; anūpalittaṃ. Evaṃ munī santivado agiddho kāme ca loke ca anūpalitto ti. Evan ti opammasampaṭipādanā[^203-8]. Munī ti monaṃ vuccati ñāṇaṃ . . . pe . . . saṅgajālam aticca so muni. Santivado ti santivādo muni tāṇavādo leṇavādo saraṇavādo[^203-9] accutavādo amatavādo nibbānavādo ti, evaṃ muni santivado. Agiddho ti. Gedho vuccati taṇhā; yo rāgo sārāgo . . . pe . . . abhijjhā lobho akusalamūlaṃ. [^203-10]Yassa so[^203-10] gedho pahīno samucchinno vūpasanto paṭipassaddho abhabbuppattiko ñāṇagginā daḍḍho, so vuccati agiddho. So rūpe agiddho, sadde gandhe rase phoṭṭhabbe kule[^203-11] gaṇe āvāse lābhe yase pasaṃsāya sukhe cīvare piṇḍapāte senāsane gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāre kāmadhātuyā rūpadhātuyā arūpadhātuyā kāmabhave rūpabhave arūpabhave saññābhave asaññābhave nevasaññānāsaññābhave[^203-12] ekavokārabhave catuvokārabhave pañcavokārabhave atīte anāgate paccuppanne diṭṭhasutamutaviññātabbesu dhammesu agiddho agadhito amucchito[^203-13] anajjhopanno[^203-14] vītagedho[^203-15] cattagedho vantagedho muttagedho[^203-16] pahīnagedho paṭinissaṭṭhagedho vītarāgo[^203-17] cattarāgo vantarāgo mutta-


<!-- PTS Page 204 -->
<a id="page-204"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 204]*

rāgo[^204-1] pahīnarāgo paṭinissaṭṭharāgo[^204-2] nicchāto[^204-3] nibbuto[^204-4] sītibhūto sukhapaṭisaṃvedī brahmabhūtena attanā viharatī ti, evaṃ munī santivado agiddho. Kāme ca loke ca anūpalitto[^204-5] ti. Kāmā ti udānato dve kāmā, vatthukāmā ca kilesa kāmā ca . . . pe . . . ime vuccanti[^204-6] vatthukāmā . . . pe . . . ime vuccanti[^204-6] kilesakāmā. Loke ti apāyaloke manussaloke devaloke khandhaloke dhātuloke āyatanaloke. Lepo[^204-7] ti dve lepā, taṇhālepo ca diṭṭhilepo ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhālepo . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhilepo. Muni taṇhālepaṃ pahāya, diṭṭhilepam paṭinissajjitvā, kāme ca loke ca na limpati, na saṃlimpati[^204-8], n'[^204-9] upalimpati, alitto asaṃlitto anupalitto nikkhanto nissaṭṭho[^204-10] vippamutto visaṃyutto vimariyādikatena cetasā viharatī ti, evaṃ munī santivado[^204-11] agiddho kāme ca loke ca anūpalitto. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Yehi vivitto careyya[^204-12] loke, na tāni uggayha vadeyya nāgo: elambujaṃ kaṇṭakavārijam yathā jalena paṅkena c'; anūpalittaṃ, evaṃ munī santivado agiddho kāme ca loke ca anūpalitto ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,9.12(=846): Na vedagū diṭṭhiyā na mutiya sa mānam eti, na hi tammayo[^204-13] so, na kammunā[^204-14] no pi sutena neyyo anūpanīto sa[^204-15] nivesanesu.  || Nidd_I.9:12 ||

Na vedayū diṭṭhiyā na mutiyā sa mānam etī ti. Nā ti paṭikkhepo. Vedagū ti. Vedo[^204-16] vuccati catūsu maggesu


<!-- PTS Page 205 -->
<a id="page-205"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 205]*

ñāṇaṃ, paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo vīmaṃsā vipassanā sammādiṭṭhi. Tehi vedehi jātijarāmaraṇassa antagato [^205-1]antappatto koṭigato koṭippatto pariyantagato pariyantappatto vosānagato vosānappatto tāṇagato tāṇappatto leṇagato leṇappatto saraṇagato saraṇappatto abhayagato abhayappatto accutagato accutappatto [^205-2]amatagato[^205-1] amatappatto nibbānagato nibbānappatto. Vedānaṃ vā antaṃ gato ti vedagū, [^205-1]vedehi vā antagato ti vedagū[^205-1], sattannaṃ vā dhammānaṃ viditattā vedagū, sakkāyadiṭṭhi viditā hoti, vicikicchā viditā hoti, sīlabbataparāmāso[^205-3] vidito hoti,[^205-1]rāgo vidito hoti, doso vidito hoti, moho vidito hoti[^205-1], māno vidito hoti, vidit'; assa honti pāpakā akusalā dhammā saṃkilesikā ponobbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇīyā[^205-4]. *Vedāni viceyya[^205-5] kevalāni Sabhiyā ti Bhagavā samaṇānaṃ [^205-6]yāni p'; atthi[^205-6] brāhmaṇānaṃ sabbavedanāsu vītarāgo sabbaṃ vedam aticca vedagū so ti[^205-7]. Na diṭṭhiyā ti. Tassa dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni pahīnāni samucchinnāni vūpasantāni paṭipassaddhāni[^205-8] abhabbuppattikāni ñāṇagginā daḍḍhāni; so diṭṭhiyā na yāyati, na niyyati, na vuyhati, na saṃhariyati[^205-9]; na pi taṃ diṭṭhigataṃ sārato pacceti, na[^205-7] paccāgacchatī ti, na vedagū diṭṭhiyā. Na mutiyā [^205-10]sa mānam etī[^205-10] ti[^205-11]. Mutarūpena[^205-12] vā paraghosena vā[^205-13] mahājanasammutiyā vā na[^205-11] mānaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 206 -->
<a id="page-206"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 206]*

eti[^206-1], na upeti, na upagacchati, na gaṇhāti, na parāmasati, nābhinivisatī ti, na vedagū diṭṭhiyā na mutiyā sa mānam eti. Na hi tammayo so ti na[^206-2] taṇhāvasena diṭṭhivasena tammayo hoti tapparamo[^206-3] tapparāyano[^206-3]. Yato taṇhā ca diṭṭhi ca māno ca[^206-4] pahīnā[^206-5] honti[^206-6] ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃ gatā[^206-7] āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, ettāvatā na tammayo hoti, na tapparamo[^206-8], na tapparāyano ti[^206-9], na hi tammayo so. Na kammunā no pi sutena neyyo ti. Na kammunā[^206-10] ti puññābhisaṃkhārena vā apuññābhisaṃkhārena vā āṇeñjābhisaṃkhārena vā na yāyati, na niyyati[^206-11], na vuyhati, na saṃhariyatī[^206-12] ti, na kammunā. No pi sutena neyyo ti sutasuddhiyā vā paraghosena vā mahājanasammutiyā vā na yāyati, na niyyati[^206-13], [^206-14]na vuyhati[^206-14], na saṃhariyatī[^206-12] ti, na kammunā no pi sutena neyyo. Anūpanīto[^206-15] sa[^206-16] nivesanesū ti. Upayo[^206-17] ti dve upayā, taṇhupayo ca diṭṭhupayo ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhupayo . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhupayo. Tassa taṇhupayo pahīno, diṭṭhupayo paṭinissaṭṭho, taṇhupayassa pahīnattā, diṭṭhupayassa paṭinissaṭṭhattā, so nivesanesu anupanīto anupalitto anupagato anajjhosito anadhimutto nikkhanto nissaṭṭho[^206-18] vippamutto visaṃyutto vimariyādikatena cetasā viharatī ti, anūpanīto sa nivesanesu.


<!-- PTS Page 207 -->
<a id="page-207"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 207]*

Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Na vedagū diṭṭhiyā na mutiyā sa mānam eti, na hi tammayo so, na kammunā no pi sutena neyyo anūpanīto sa nivesanesū ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,9.13(=847): Saññāvirattassa na santi ganthā[^207-1], paññāvimuttassa na santi mohā: saññañ[^207-2] ca diṭṭhiñ ca ye aggahesuṃ[^207-3], te ghaṭṭayantā[^207-4] vicaranti[^207-5] loke.  || Nidd_I.9:13 ||

Saññāvirattassa na santi ganthā ti yo samathapubbaṅgamaṃ ariyamaggaṃ bhāveti, tassa ādito upādāya ganthā vikkhambhitā honti; arahattappatte[^207-6], arahato ganthā ca mohā ca nīvaraṇā ca kāmasaññā byāpādasaññā vihiṃsāsaññā diṭṭhisaññā ca[^207-7] pahīnā honti, ucchinnamūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃ gatā[^207-8] āyatiṃ[^207-9] anuppādadhammā ti, saññāvirattassa na santi ganthā. Paññāvimuttassa[^207-10] na santi mohā ti yo vipassanāpubbaṅgamaṃ ariyamaggaṃ bhāveti, tassa ādito upādāya mohā vikkhambhitā honti; arahattappatte[^207-6], arahato mohā ca ganthā ca nīvaraṇā ca kāmasaññā byāpādasaññā vihiṃsāsaññā diṭṭhisaññā ca[^207-11] pahīnā honti, ucchinnamūlātālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃ gatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā ti, paññāvimuttassa[^207-10] na santi mohā. Saññañ ca diṭṭhiñ ca ye aggahesuṃ te ghaṭṭayantā[^207-12] vicaranti[^207-13] loke ti ye saññaṃ gaṇhanti, kāmasaññaṃ byāpādasaññaṃ vihiṃsāsaññaṃ, te saññāvasena ghaṭṭenti saṃghaṭṭenti[^207-7]. Rājāno pi rājūhi vivadanti, khattiyā pi


<!-- PTS Page 208 -->
<a id="page-208"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 208]*

khattiyehi vivadanti; [^208-1]brāhmaṇā pi brāhmaṇehi vivadanti[^208-1]; gahapatī pi gahapatīhi vivadanti; mātā pi puttena vivadati; putto pi mātarā vivadati; pitā pi puttena vivadati; putto pi pitarā vivadati; bhātā pi bhātarā vivadati; [^208-2]bhaginī pi bhaginiyā vivadati[^208-2]; bhātā pi bhaginiyā vivadati; bhaginī pi bhātarā vivadati; sahāyo pi sahāyena vivadati. Te tattha kalahaviggahavivādāpannā aññamaññaṃ pāṇīhi pi upakkamanti, leḍḍūhi[^208-3] pi upakkamanti[^208-4], satthehi pi upakkamanti[^208-5]. Te[^208-6] tattha maraṇam pi nigacchanti, maraṇamattam pi dukkhaṃ. Ye diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhanti: sassato loko ti vā . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato paraṃmaraṇā ti vā, te[^208-7] diṭṭhivasena ghaṭṭenti saṃghaṭṭenti[^208-8]. Satthārato[^208-9] satthāraṃ ghaṭṭenti; dhammakkhānato dhammakkhānaṃ ghaṭṭenti; gaṇato[^208-10] gaṇaṃ ghaṭṭenti, diṭṭhiyā diṭṭhiṃ ghaṭṭenti, paṭipadāya paṭipadaṃ ghaṭṭenti, maggato maggaṃ ghaṭṭenti. Athavā te[^208-6] vivadanti[^208-11], kalahaṃ karonti, bhaṇḍanaṃ karonti, viggahaṃ karonti, vivādaṃ karonti, medhagaṃ karonti: na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi . . . pe[^208-12] . . . nibbedhehi vā sace pahosī ti. Tesaṃ abhisaṃkhārā appahīnā; abhisaṃkhārānaṃ appahīnatta, gatiyā[^208-13] ghaṭṭenti, niraye ghaṭṭenti, tiracchānayoniyā ghaṭṭenti, pittivisaye[^208-14] ghaṭṭenti, manussaloke ghaṭṭenti, devaloke ghaṭṭenti, gatiyā gatiṃ upapattiyā upapattiṃ paṭisandhiyā paṭisandhiṃ bhavena bhavaṃ[^208-15], saṃsārena saṃsāraṃ [^208-2]vaṭṭena vaṭṭaṃ[^208-2] ghaṭṭenti[^208-16], ghaṭṭentā caranti viharanti[^208-17] iriyanti[^208-18] vattenti pālenti [^208-1]yapenti yāpenti[^208-1]. Loke ti apāyaloke [^208-19]manussaloke devaloke khandhaloke dhātuloke[^208-19] āyatanaloke ti, saññañ[^208-20]


<!-- PTS Page 209 -->
<a id="page-209"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 209]*

ca diṭṭhiñ ca ye aggahesuṃ te ghaṭṭayantā[^209-1] vicaranti loke. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Saññāvirattassa[^209-2] [^209-3]na santi[^209-3] ganthā, paññāvimuttassa[^209-4] na santi mohā: saññañ ca diṭṭhiñ ca ye aggahesuṃ, te ghaṭṭayantā[^209-1] vicaranti loke ti.

NAVAMO[^209-5] MĀGANDIYASUTTANIDDESO NIṬṬHITO[^209-6].

##### *[PTS Page 210]*

DASAMO[^210-1] PURĀBHEDASUTTANIDDESO[^210-2].

Sn_IV,10.1(=848): Kathaṃdassī kathaṃsīlo upasanto ti vuccati, tam me Gotama pabrūhi pucchito uttamaṃ naraṃ.  || Nidd_I.10:1 ||

Kathaṃdassī kathaṃsīlo upasanto ti vuccatī ti. [^210-3]Kathaṃdassī ti[^210-3] kīdisena[^210-4] dassanena samannāgato, kiṃsaṇṭhitena[^210-5] kiṃpakārena kiṃpaṭibhāgenā[^210-6] ti, kathaṃdassī. Kathaṃsīlo ti kīdisena[^210-7] sīlena samannāgato, kiṃsaṇṭhitena[^210-5] kiṃpakārena kiṃpaṭibhāgenā ti, kathaṃdassī kathaṃsīlo. Upasanto ti vuccatī ti santo[^210-8] vūpasanto[^210-9] nibbuto paṭipassaddho ti vuccati[^210-9] pavuccati kathiyati[^210-10] bhaṇiyati[^210-9] dīpayati vohariyati[^210-11]. Kathaṃdassī ti[^210-9] [^210-12]adhipaññaṃ pucchati[^210-12]. Kathaṃsīlo ti adhisīlaṃ pucchati. Upasanto ti adhicittaṃ pucchatī[^210-13] ti, kathaṃdassī kathaṃsīlo upasanto ti vuccati. Tam me Gotama pabruhī ti. Tan ti yaṃ pucchāmi, yaṃ yācāmi, yaṃ ajjhesāmi[^210-14], yaṃ pasādemi. Gotamā ti so nimmito Buddhaṃ Bhagavantaṃ gottena ālapati.


<!-- PTS Page 211 -->
<a id="page-211"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 211]*

Pabrūhī ti brūhi ācikkha desehi paññāpehi paṭṭhapehi vivara[^211-1] vibhaja[^211-2] uttānīkarohi pakāsehī ti, taṃ me Gotama pabrūhi. Pucchito uttamaṃ naran ti. Pucchito ti puṭṭho pucchito yācito ajjhesito pasādito. Uttamaṃ naran ti aggaṃ seṭṭhaṃ viseṭṭhaṃ pāmokkhaṃ[^211-3] uttamaṃ pavaraṃ[^211-4] naran ti, pucchito uttamaṃ naraṃ. Ten'; āha so nimmito:

Kathaṃdassī kathaṃsīlo upasanto ti vuccati, tam me Gotama pabrūhi pucchito uttamaṃ naran ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,10.2(=849): Vītataṇho purā bhedā ti[^211-5] Bhagavā pubbam antam anissito vemajjhe n'; upasaṃkheyyo, tassa n' atthi purekkhataṃ[^211-6].  || Nidd_I.10:2 ||

Vītataṇho purā bhedā[^211-7] ti [^211-8]Bhagavā ti[^211-8] purā kāyassa bhedā[^211-7], purā attabhāvassa bhedā[^211-7], purā kaḷevarassa nikkhepā[^211-9], purā jīvitindriyassa upacchedā, vītataṇho vigatataṇho cattataṇho vantataṇho muttataṇho pahīnataṇho paṭinissaṭṭhataṇho vītarāgo vigatarāgo cattarāgo[^211-10] vantarāgo[^211-11] pahīnarāgo paṭinissaṭṭharāgo nicchāto[^211-12] nibbuto sītibhūto sukhapaṭisaṃvedī brahmabhūtena attanā [^211-13]viharatī ti[^211-13]. Bhagavā ti gāravādhivacanaṃ. Api ca bhaggarāgo ti Bhagavā, bhaggadoso ti Bhagavā, bhaggamoho ti Bhagavā, bhaggadiṭṭhī ti Bhagavā, bhaggakaṇṭako[^211-14] ti Bhagavā, bhaggakileso ti Bhagavā, bhaji vibhaji paṭivibhaji[^211-15] dhammaratanan ti Bhagavā, bhavānaṃ antakaro ti Bhagavā, bhāvitakāyo [^211-8]ti Bhagavā[^211-8], bhāvitasīlo bhāvitacitto bhāvitapañño [^211-16]ti Bhagavā[^211-16]. Bhaji vā Bhagavā


<!-- PTS Page 212 -->
<a id="page-212"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 212]*

araññavanapatthāni[^212-1] pantāni senāsanāni appasaddāni appanigghosāni vijanavātāni manussarāhaseyyakāni paṭisallānasārūpānī[^212-2] ti Bhagavā. Bhāgī vā Bhagavā cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānan ti Bhagavā. Bhāgī vā Bhagavā attharasassa dhammarasassa vimuttirasassa adhisīlassa adhicittassa adhipaññāyā ti Bhagavā. Bhāgī vā Bhagavā catunnaṃ jhānānaṃ catunnaṃ appamaññānaṃ catunnaṃ arūpasamāpattīnan ti Bhagavā. Bhāgī vā Bhagavā aṭṭhannaṃ vimokkhānaṃ aṭṭhannaṃ abhibhāyatanānaṃ navannaṃ anupubbavihārasamāpattīnan ti Bhagavā. Bhāgī vā Bhagavā dasannaṃ saññābhāvanānaṃ[^212-3] dasannaṃ kasiṇasamāpattīnaṃ ānāpānasatisamādhissa [^212-4]asubhasamāpattiyā ti[^212-4] Bhagavā. Bhāgī vā Bhagavā catunnaṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ catunnaṃ sammappadhānānaṃ catunnaṃ iddhippādānaṃ pañcannaṃ indriyānaṃ pañcannaṃ balānaṃ sattannaṃ bojjhaṅgānaṃ ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassā ti Bhagavā. Bhāgī vā Bhagavā dasannaṃ tathāgatabalānaṃ catunnaṃ vesārajjānaṃ catunnaṃ paṭisambhidānaṃ channaṃ abhiññānaṃ channaṃ buddhadhammānan ti Bhagavā. Bhagavā ti n' etaṃ nāmaṃ mātarā kataṃ, na pitarā katam, na bhātarā kataṃ, na bhaginiyā kataṃ, na mittāmaccehi kataṃ, na ñātisālohitehi kataṃ, na samaṇabrāhmaṇehi kataṃ, na devatāhi kataṃ, vimokkhantikam etaṃ Buddhānaṃ Bhagavantānaṃ bodhiyā mūle saha sabbaññutañāṇassa paṭilābhā sacchikā paññatti, yadidaṃ Bhagavā ti, vītataṇho purā bhedā ti Bhagavā. Pubbam antam anissito ti pubbanto[^212-5] vuccati atīto addhā; atītaṃ addhānaṃ ārabbha taṇhā pahīnā hoti, diṭṭhi paṭinissaṭṭhā, taṇhāya pahīnattā diṭṭhiyā paṭinissaṭṭhattā, evam pi [^212-6]pubbaṃ antam[^212-6] anissito. Athavā evaṃrūpo ahosiṃ atītam addhānan ti, tattha nandiṃ na samanvāgameti[^212-7]. Evaṃvedano ahosiṃ, evaṃsañño ahosiṃ,


<!-- PTS Page 213 -->
<a id="page-213"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 213]*

evaṃsaṃkhāro ahosiṃ, evaṃviññāno ahosiṃ atītam addhānan ti, tattha[^213-1] nandiṃ na samanvāgameti[^213-2], evam pi [^213-3]pubbaṃ antam[^213-3] anissito. Athavā iti me cakkhu[^213-4] ahosi atītam addhānaṃ, iti rūpā ti, tattha na[^213-5] chandarāgapaṭibaddhaṃ[^213-6] hoti viññāṇaṃ; na chandarāgapaṭibaddhattā viññāṇassa na tad abhinandati, na tad abhinandanto[^213-6], evam pi pubbaṃ antam anissito. Iti me sotaṃ ahosi atītam addhāṇaṃ, iti saddā ti, iti me ghānaṃ ahosi atītam addhānaṃ, iti[^213-7] gandhā ti, iti me jivhā ahosi atītam addhānaṃ, iti[^213-7] rasā ti, iti me kāyo ahosi atītam addhānaṃ, iti phoṭṭhabbā ti, iti me mano ahosi atītam addhānaṃ, iti dhammā ti, tattha na[^213-5] chandarāgapaṭibaddhaṃ hoti viññāṇaṃ, na chandarāgapaṭibaddhattā viññāṇassa, na tad abhinandati, na tadabhinandanto, evam pi pubbaṃ antam anissito. Athavā yān'[^213-8] assu tāni pubbe mātugāmena saddhiṃ hasitalapitakathitakīḷitāni[^213-9] na tad assādeti, na taṃ nikāmeti, na ca tena pavittiṃ[^213-10] āpajjati, evam pi pubbam antam anissito. Vemajjhe n'; upasaṃkheyyo ti vemajjhe[^213-11] vuccati paccuppanno addhā; paccuppannaṃ addhānaṃ ārabbha taṇhā pahīnā hoti, diṭṭhi paṭinissaṭṭhā, taṇhāya pahīnattā diṭṭhiyā paṭinissaṭṭhattā ratto ti n'; upasaṃkheyyo, [^213-12]duṭṭho ti n'; upasaṃkheyyo[^213-12], mūḷho ti n'; upasaṃkheyyo, vinibandho ti n'; upasaṃkheyyo, parāmaṭṭho ti n'; upasaṃkheyyo, [^213-13]vikkhepagato ti n'; upasaṃkheyyo, aniṭṭhaṅgato[^213-14] ti n'; upasaṃkheyyo, thāmagato ti n'; upasaṃkheyyo; te abhisaṃkhārā pahīnā, abhisaṃkhārānaṃ pahīnattā gatiyā n'; upasaṃkheyyo[^213-13], nerayiko ti vā, tiracchānayoniko ti vā, pittivisayiko ti vā, manusso[^213-15] ti vā, devo ti vā,


<!-- PTS Page 214 -->
<a id="page-214"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 214]*

rūpī ti vā, arūpī ti vā, saññī ti vā, asaññī ti vā, nevasaññī[^214-1] nāsaññī ti vā. So hetu n'; atthi, paccayo n'; atthi, kāraṇaṃ n'; atthi, yena saṃkhaṃ[^214-2] gaccheyyā ti, vemajjhe n' upasaṃkheyyo. Tassa n'; atthi purekkhatan ti. Tassā ti arahato khīṇāsavassa. Purekkhāro[^214-3] ti dve purekkhārā, taṇhāpurekkhāro ca diṭṭhipurekkhāro ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhāpurekkhāro . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhipurekkhāro. Tassa[^214-4] taṇhāpurekkhāro pahīno, diṭṭhipurekkhāro paṭinissaṭṭho, taṇhāpurekkhārassa pahīnattā diṭṭhipurekkhārassa paṭinissaṭṭhattā, na taṇhaṃ vā diṭṭhiṃ vā purato[^214-5] katvā carati, na taṇhādhajo, na taṇhāketu, na taṇhādhipateyyo, na diṭṭhidhajo, na diṭṭhiketu, na diṭṭhādhipateyyo, na taṇhāya vā diṭṭhiyā vā parivārito caratī ti, evam pi tassa n'; atthi purekkhataṃ. Athavā evaṃrūpo siyaṃ anāgatam addhānan ti, tattha nandiṃ na samanvāgameti[^214-6], evaṃvedano siyaṃ, evaṃsañño siyaṃ, evaṃsaṃkhāro siyaṃ, evaṃviññāṇo siyaṃ anāgatam addhānan ti, tattha nandiṃ na samanvāgameti[^214-6], evam pi tassa n'; atthi purekkhataṃ. Athavā iti me cakkhu siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti rūpā ti appaṭiladdhassa paṭilābhāya cittaṃ na paṇidahati, cetaso appaṇidhānapaccayā[^214-7] na tad abhinandati, na tad abhinandanto[^214-8], evam pi tassa n'; atthi purekkhataṃ. Iti me sotaṃ siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti saddā[^214-9] ti; iti me ghānaṃ siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti gandhā ti; iti me jivhā siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti rasā ti; iti me kāyo siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti[^214-10] phoṭṭhabbā ti; iti me mano siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti[^214-10] dhammā ti appaṭiladdhassa paṭilābhāya cittaṃ na paṇidahati, cetaso appaṇidhānapaccayā[^214-11] na tad abhinandati, na tad abhinandanto, evam pi tassa n'; atthi purekkhataṃ. Athavā


<!-- PTS Page 215 -->
<a id="page-215"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 215]*

imināhaṃ[^215-1] sīlena [^215-2]vā vattena[^215-2] vā tapena vā brahmacariyena vā devo vā bhavissāmi devaññataro vā ti appaṭiladdhassa paṭilābhāya cittaṃ na paṇidahati, cetaso appaṇidhānapaccayā na tad abhinandati, na tadabhinandanto, evam pi tassa n'; atthi purekkhataṃ. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Vītataṇho purā bhedā ti Bhagavā pubbam antaṃ anissito vemajjhe n'; upasaṃkheyyo, tassa n'; atthi purekkhatan ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,10.3(=850): Akkodhano asantāsī, avikatthī[^215-3] akukkuco[^215-4], mantābhāṇī anuddhato, sa ve vācāyato[^215-5] munī.  || Nidd_I.10:3 ||

Akkodhano asantāsī ti. Akkodhano ti hi[^215-6] kho vuttaṃ, api ca kodho tāva vattabbo[^215-7]. Dasah'; ākārehi kodho jāyati: anattham me carī[^215-8] ti kodho jāyati, anattham me caratī ti kodho jāyati, anattham me carissatī[^215-9] ti kodho jāyati, piyassa me manāpassa anatthaṃ acarī ti, anatthaṃ caratī ti, anatthaṃ carissatī ti [^215-10]kodho jāyati[^215-10], appiyassa me amanāpassa atthaṃ acarī ti, atthaṃ caratī ti, appiyassa me amanāpassa atthaṃ carissatī ti kodho jāyati, aṭṭhāne vā pana kodho jāyati. Yo evarūpo cittassa āghāto, [^215-11]paṭighāto paṭighaṃ paṭinirodho[^215-12] [^215-11] kopo pakopo sampakopo doso padoso sampadoso cittassa, byāpatti manopadoso kodho kujjhanā kujjhitattaṃ doso dussanā dussitattaṃ byāpatti byāpajjanā byāpajjitattaṃ virodho paṭivirodho [^215-13]caṇḍikkaṃ assuropo[^215-13], anattamanatā cittassa; ayaṃ vuccati kodho.


<!-- PTS Page 216 -->
<a id="page-216"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 216]*

Api ca kodhassa adhimattaparittatā veditabbā. Atthi kañci[^216-1] kālaṃ kodho cittāvilakaraṇamatto hoti, na ca tāva mukhakulānavikulāno[^216-2] hoti. Atthi kañci[^216-3] kālaṃ[^216-4] kodho mukhakulānavikulānamatto hoti, na ca tāva hanusañcopano hoti. Atthi kañci kālaṃ kodho hanusañcopanamatto hoti, na ca tāva pharusavācaniccharāṇo[^216-5] hoti. Atthi kañci kālaṃ kodho pharusavācanicchāraṇamatto[^216-5] hoti, na ca tāva disāvidisaṃ anuvilokano hoti. Atthi kañci kālaṃ kodho[^216-6] disāvidisaṃ anuvilokanamatto[^216-6] hoti, na ca tāva daṇḍasatthaparāmasano hoti. Atthi kañci kālaṃ kodho daṇḍasatthaparāmasanamatto hoti, na ca tāva daṇḍasattha-abbhukkiraṇo[^216-7] hoti. Atthi kañci kālaṃ kodho daṇḍasattha-abbhukkiraṇamatto[^216-8] hoti, na ca tāva daṇḍasattha-abhinipātano[^216-7] hoti. Atthi kañci[^216-9] kālaṃ kodho daṇḍasattha-abhinipātanamatto[^216-10] hoti, na ca tāva chindavicchindakaraṇo[^216-11] hoti. Atthi kañci kālaṃ kodho chindavichindakaraṇamatto[^216-11] hoti, na ca tāva sambhañjanaparibhañjano[^216-12] hoti. Atthi kañci kālaṃ kodho sambhañjanaparibhañjanamatto [^216-12]hoti, [^216-13]na ca tāva aṅgamaṅgāpakaḍḍhano[^216-14] hoti. Atthi kañci kālaṃ kodho {aṅgamaṅgāpakaḍḍhanamatto} hoti, na ca tāva jīvitapanāsano[^216-15] hoti. Atthi kañci kālaṃ kodho jīvitapanāsanamatto[^216-16] hoti, na ca tāva sabbacāgaparicāgasaṇṭhito[^216-17] hoti. Yato kodho parapuggalaṃ ghātetvā attānaṃ ghāteti; ettāvatā kodho[^216-13] paramussadagato paramavepullapatto[^216-18] hoti. [^216-19]Yass'; eso[^216-19] kodho pahīno samucchinno vūpasanto


<!-- PTS Page 217 -->
<a id="page-217"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 217]*

paṭipassaddho abhabbuppattiko ñāṇagginā daḍḍho, so vuccati akkodhano. Kodhassa pahīnattā akkodhano, kodhavatthussa pariññātattā akkodhano, kodhahetussa ucchinnattā[^217-1] akkodhano ti, akkodhano. Asantāsī ti. Idh'; ekacco tāsī hoti uttāsī parittāsī. So tasati uttasati paritasati[^217-2] bhāyati santāsaṃ āpajjati: kulaṃ vā na labhāmi, gaṇaṃ vā na labhāmi, āvāsaṃ vā na labhāmi, lābhaṃ vā na labhāmi, yasaṃ vā na labhāmi, pasaṃsaṃ vā na labhāmi, sukhaṃ vā na labhāmi, cīvaraṃ vā na labhāmi, piṇḍapātaṃ vā na labhāmi, senāsanaṃ vā na labhāmi[^217-3], gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāraṃ vā na labhāmi, gilānupaṭṭhākaṃ[^217-4] vā na labhāmi, appaññāto 'mhī ti[^217-5]. Idha bhikkhu asantāsī hoti anuttāsī aparittāsī. So na tasati, na uttasati, na paritasati, na bhāyati, na santāsaṃ[^217-6] āpajjati: kulaṃ vā na labhāmi, gaṇaṃ vā na labhāmi, āvāsaṃ vā na labhāmi, lābhaṃ vā na labhāmi, yasaṃ vā na labhāmi, pasaṃsaṃ vā na labhāmi, sukhaṃ vā na labhāmi, cīvaraṃ vā na labhāmi, piṇḍapātaṃ vā na labhāmi, senāsanaṃ vā na labhāmi, gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāraṃ vā na labhāmi, gilānupaṭṭhākaṃ vā na labhāmi, appaññāto 'mhī ti, na tasati, na[^217-7] uttasati, na[^217-7] parittasati, na[^217-7] bhāyati, na[^217-7] santāsaṃ āpajjatī ti, akkodhano asantāsī. Avikatthī akukkuco ti. Idh'; ekacco katthī hoti vikatthī[^217-8]. So katthati vikatthati: aham asmi sīlasampanno ti vā[^217-9], sīlabbatasampanno ti vā, jātiyā vā gottena vā kolaputtikena[^217-10] vā vaṇṇapokkharatāya vā dhanena vā ajjhenena vā kammāyatanena vā sippāyatanena vā vijjaṭṭhānena vā sutena vā paṭibhāṇena vā aññataraññatarena vā vatthunā,


<!-- PTS Page 218 -->
<a id="page-218"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 218]*

uccākulā pabbajito ti vā, mahākulā pabbajito ti vā, mahābhogakulā pabbajito ti vā, uḷārabhogakulā pabbajito ti vā, ñāto yasassī gahaṭṭhapabbajitānan ti vā, lābhi 'mhi cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānan ti vā, suttantiko ti vā, vinayadharo ti vā, dhammakathiko ti vā, āraññiko ti vā, piṇḍapātiko ti vā, paṃsukūliko ti vā, tecīvariko ti vā, sapadānacāriko ti vā, khalupacchābhattiko ti vā, nesajjiko ti vā, yathāsanthatiko ti vā, [^218-1]paṭhamassa jhānassa[^218-1] lābhī ti vā, dutiyassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā, tatiyassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā, catutthassa jhānassa lābhī ti vā, ākāsānañcāyatanasamāpattiyā viññāṇañcāyatanasamāpattiyā ākiñcaññāyatanasamāpattiyā nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī ti vā katthati vikatthati; evaṃ na katthati na vikatthati, katthanā[^218-2] ārato virato paṭivirato nikkhanto nissaṭṭho[^218-3] vippamutto visaṃyutto vimariyādikatena[^218-4] cetasā viharatī ti, avikatthī. Akukkuco ti. Kukkuccan ti hatthakukkuccam pi kukkuccaṃ, pādakukkuccam pi kukkuccaṃ, hatthapādakukkuccam pi kukkuccaṃ, akappiye kappiyasaññitā, kappiye akappiyasaññitā, vikāle kālasaññitā avajje vajjasaññitā, vajje avajjasaññitā; yaṃ evarūpaṃ kukkuccaṃ, kukkuccāyanā, kukkuccāyitattaṃ, cetaso vippaṭisāro, manovilekho; idaṃ vuccati kukkuccaṃ. Api ca dvīhi kāraṇehi uppajjati kukkuccaṃ, cetaso vippaṭisāro manovilekho, katattā ca akatattā ca. Kathaṃ katattā ca akatattā ca uppajjati kukkuccaṃ cetaso vippaṭisāro manovilekho? Kataṃ me kāyaduccaritaṃ, akataṃ me kāyasucaritan ti uppajjati kukkuccaṃ cetaso vippaṭisāro manovilekho. Kataṃ me vacīduccaritaṃ, akataṃ me vacīsucaritan[^218-5] ti uppajjati kukkuccaṃ cetaso vippaṭisāro manovilekho[^218-5]. Kataṃ me manoduccaritaṃ, akataṃ me manosucaritan ti uppajjati kukkuccaṃ cetaso vippaṭisāro manovilekho. Kato me pāṇātipāto, akatā me pāṇātipātā veramaṇī ti uppajjati kukkuccaṃ cetaso vippaṭisāro manovilekho. Kataṃ me


<!-- PTS Page 219 -->
<a id="page-219"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 219]*

adinnādānaṃ, akatā me adinnādānā veramaṇī ti uppajjati kukkuccaṃ cetaso vippaṭisāro manovilekho. Kato me kāmesu micchācāro, akatā me kāmesu micchācārā veramanī ti uppajjati kukkuccaṃ cetaso vippaṭisāro manovilekho. Kato me musāvādo, akatā me musāvādā veramaṇī ti. Katā me pisuṇā vācā, akatā me [^219-1]pisuṇāya vācāya[^219-1] veramaṇī ti. Katā me pharusā vācā, akatā me [^219-2]pharusāya vācāya[^219-2] veramaṇī ti. Kato me samphappalāpo, akatā me samphappalāpā veramaṇī ti. Katā me abhijjhā, akatā me anabhijjhā ti. Kato me byāpādo, akato me abyāpādo ti. Katā me micchādiṭṭhi, akatā me sammādiṭṭhī ti uppajjati kukkuccaṃ cetaso vippaṭisāro manovilekho; evaṃ katattā[^219-3] ca akatattā[^219-4] ca uppajjati kukkuccaṃ cetaso vippatisāro manovilekho. Athavā sīlesu 'mhi na paripūrakārī ti uppajjati kukkuccaṃ cetaso vippaṭisāro manovilekho. Indriyesu 'mhi aguttadvāro ti. Bhojane amattaññū 'mhī ti. Jāgariyaṃ ananuyutto 'mhī ti. Na satisampajaññena samannāgato 'mhī ti. Abhāvitā me cattāro satipaṭṭhānā ti. Abhāvitā me cattāro sammappadhānā ti. Abhāvitā me cattāro iddhippādā ti. Abhāvitāni me pañc'; indriyānī ti. Abhāvitāni me pañca balānī ti. Abhāvitā me satta bojjhaṅgā ti. Abhāvito me ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo ti. Dukkhaṃ me apariññātan ti. Samudayo me appahīno ti[^219-5]. Maggo me abhāvito ti. Nirodho me asacchikato ti uppajjati kukkuccaṃ cetaso vippatisāro[^219-6] manovilekho. Yass'; etaṃ kukkuccaṃ pahīnaṃ samucchinnaṃ vūpasantaṃ paṭipassaddhaṃ abhabbuppattikaṃ ñāṇagginā daḍḍhaṃ, so vuccati akukkucco ti, avikatthī akukkuco. Mantābhāṇī anuddhato ti mantā vuccati paññā; yā paññā pajānanā . . . pe . . . amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi. Mantāya pariggahetvā vācaṃ bhāsati, bahum pi[^219-7] kathento, bahum pi[^219-7] bhaṇanto, bahum pi[^219-7] dīpayanto, bahum pi[^219-7] voharanto, dukkaṭaṃ[^219-8] dukkathitaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 220 -->
<a id="page-220"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 220]*

dubbhāsitaṃ[^220-1] dubbhaṇitaṃ dullapitaṃ duruttaṃ[^220-2] vācaṃ na bhāsatī ti, mantābhāṇī. Anuddhato ti. Tattha katamaṃ uddhaccaṃ? Yaṃ cittassa uddhaccaṃ avūpasamo, cetaso vikkhepo, bhantattaṃ cittassa, idaṃ vuccati uddhaccaṃ. Yass'; etaṃ uddhaccaṃ pahīnaṃ samucchinnaṃ vūpasantaṃ paṭipassaddhaṃ abhabbuppattikaṃ ñāṇagginā daḍḍhaṃ, so vuccati anuddhato ti, mantābhāṇī anuddhato. Sa ve vācāyato munī ti. Idha bhikkhu musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭivirato hoti saccavādī saccasandho theto paccayiko avisaṃvādako lokassa. Pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti; ito sutvā na amutra akkhātā imesaṃ bhedāya, amutra vā sutvā na imesaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhedāya; iti bhinnānaṃ vā sandhātā, sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā, samaggārāmo samaggarato samagganandī samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭivirato hoti; yā sā vācā nelā kaṇṇasukhā pemanīyā[^220-3] hadayaṅgamā porī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā, tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti. Samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti, kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī nidānavatiṃ[^220-4] vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti, kālena sāpadesaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ. Catūhi vacīsucaritehi samannāgato[^220-5] catudosāgataṃ vācaṃ bhāsati, dvattiṃsāya[^220-6] tiracchānakathāya ārato[^220-7] virato paṭivirato nikkhanto paṭinissaṭṭho[^220-8] vippamutto[^220-9] visaṃyutto vimariyādikatena[^220-10] cetasā viharati. Dasavatthūni[^220-11] katheti, seyyathīdaṃ appicchakathaṃ katheti, santuṭṭhikathaṃ[^220-12] pavivekakatham[^220-13] asaṃsaggakathaṃ viriyārambhakathaṃ[^220-14] sīlakathaṃ samādhikathaṃ paññākathaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 221 -->
<a id="page-221"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 221]*

vimuttikathaṃ vimuttiñāṇadassanakathaṃ satipaṭṭhānakathaṃ sammappadhānakathaṃ iddhippādakathaṃ indriyakathaṃ balakathaṃ bojjhaṅgakathaṃ maggakathaṃ phalakatham nibbānakathaṃ katheti. Vācāyato ti yatto paṭiyatto gutto gopito[^221-1] rakkhito saṃvuto[^221-2] vūpasanto. Munī ti monaṃ vuccati ñāṇaṃ[^221-3] . . . pe . . . saṅgajālam aticca so munī* ti, sa ve vācāyato muni. Ten āha Bhagavā:

Akkodhano asantāsī, avikatthī akukkuco, mantābhāṇī anuddhato, sa ve vācāyato munī ti.

Sn_IV,10.4(=851): Nirāsattī anāgate atītaṃ nānusocati, vivekadassī phassesu diṭṭhīsu ca na niyyati.  || Nidd_I.10:4 ||

Nirāsattī anāgate ti āsatti vuccati taṇhā; yo rāgo sārāgo . . . pe . . . abhijjhā lobho akusalamūlaṃ. Yass'; esā āsatti taṇhā pahīnā samucchinnā vūpasantā paṭipassaddhā abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā ti, evam pi nirāsattī anāgate[^221-4]. Athavā evaṃrūpo siyaṃ anāgatam addhānan ti, tattha nandiṃ na samanvāgameti[^221-5]. Evaṃvedano siyaṃ, evaṃsañño siyaṃ, evaṃsaṃkhāro siyaṃ, evaṃviññāṇo siyaṃ anāgatam addhānan ti, tattha nandiṃ na samanvāgameti[^221-5], evam pi nirāsattī anāgate. Athavā iti me cakkhu siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti rūpā ti, appaṭiladdhassa paṭilābhāya cittaṃ na paṇidahati, cetaso appaṇidhānapaccayā na tad abhinandati, na tad abhinandanto, evam pi nirāsattī anāgate. Iti me sotaṃ siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti saddā ti . . . pe . . . iti me mano siyā anāgatam addhānaṃ, iti dhammā ti, appaṭiladdhassa paṭilābhāya cittaṃ na paṇidahati, cetaso appaṇidhāna-


<!-- PTS Page 222 -->
<a id="page-222"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 222]*

paccayā na tad abhinandati, na tad abhinandanto, evam pi nirāsattī anāgate. Athavā imināhaṃ sīlena vā vattena[^222-1] vā tapena vā brahmacariyena vā devo vā bhavissāmi devaññataro [^222-2]vā ti[^222-2], appaṭiladdhassa paṭilābhāya cittaṃ na paṇidahati[^222-3], cetaso appaṇidhānapaccayā na tad abhinandati, na tad abhinandanto, evam pi nirāsattī anāgate. Atītaṃ nānusocatī ti vipariṇataṃ vā vatthu na socati. Vipariṇatamhi[^222-4] vā vatthusmiṃ na socati: cakkhuṃ me vipariṇatan ti na socati, sotaṃ me, ghānaṃ me, jivhā me, kāyo me, rūpā me, saddā me, gandhā me, rasā me, phoṭṭhabbā me, kulaṃ me, gaṇaṃ[^222-5] me, āvāso me, lābho me, yaso me, pasaṃsā me, sukhaṃ me, cīvaraṃ me, piṇḍapāto me, senāsanaṃ me, gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāro me, mātā me, pitā me, bhātā me, bhaginī me, putto me, dhītā me, mittāmaccā[^222-6] me, ñātakā me, sālohitā me vipariṇatā ti na socati, na kilamati, na paridevati, na urattāḷiṃ kandati, na sammohaṃ āpajjatī ti, atītaṃ nānusocati. Vivekadassī phassesū ti. Phasso ti cakkhusamphasso sotasamphasso ghānasamphasso jivhāsamphasso kāyasamphasso manosamphasso adhivacanasamphasso paṭighasamphasso sukhavedanīyo samphasso[^222-7] dukkhavedanīyo samphasso[^222-8] adukkha-m-asukhavedanīyo samphasso[^222-8] kusalo phasso akusalo phasso abyākato phasso kāmāvacaro phasso rūpāvacaro phasso arūpāvacaro phasso suññato phasso animitto phasso appaṇihito phasso lokiyo phasso lokuttaro phasso atīto phasso anāgato phasso paccuppanno phasso; yo evarūpo phasso phusanā samphusanā samphusitattaṃ; ayaṃ vuccati phasso. Vivekadassī phassesū ti cakkhusamphassaṃ vivittaṃ passati attena vā attaniyena vā niccena vā dhuvena vā sassatena vā avipariṇāmadhammena vā. Sotasamphassaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 223 -->
<a id="page-223"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 223]*

vivittaṃ passati, ghānasamphassaṃ vivittaṃ pāssati, jivhāsamphassaṃ vivittaṃ passati, kāyasamphassaṃ vivittaṃ passati, manosamphassaṃ vivittaṃ passati, adhivacanasamphassaṃ vivittaṃ passati, paṭighasamphassaṃ vivittaṃ passati, sukhavedanīyaṃ samphassaṃ[^223-1], dukkhavedanīyaṃ samphassaṃ[^223-1], adukkha-m-asukhavedanīyam samphassaṃ[^223-1], kusalaṃ phassaṃ [^223-2]vivittaṃ passati[^223-2], akusalaṃ phassaṃ [^223-2]vivittaṃ passati[^223-2], abyākataṃ phassaṃ [^223-2]vivittaṃ passati[^223-2], kāmāvacaraṃ phassaṃ, rūpāvacaraṃ phassaṃ, arūpāvacaraṃ phassaṃ, lokiyaṃ phassaṃ, [^223-3]lokuttaraṃ phassaṃ[^223-3] vivittaṃ passati attena vā attaniyena vā niccena vā dhuvena vā sassatena vā avipariṇāmadhammena vā. Athavā atītaṃ phassaṃ anāgatehi ca[^223-4] phassehi[^223-2] paccuppannehi ca phassehi[^223-2] vivittaṃ passati; anāgataṃ phassaṃ atītehi ca phassehi[^223-5] paccuppannehi ca phassehi vivittaṃ passati; paccuppannaṃ phassaṃ atītehi ca phassehi[^223-5] anāgatehi ca phassehi vivittaṃ passati. Athavā ye te phassā ariyā anāsavā lokuttarā suññatapaṭisaṃyuttā[^223-6], te phasse vivitte passati rāgena dosena mohena kodhena upanāhena makkhena paḷāsena issāya macchariyena māyāya sāṭheyyena thambhena sārambhena mānena atimānena madena pamādena sabbakilesehi sabbaduccaritehi sabbadarathehi sabbapariḷāhehi sabbasantāpehi sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārehi vivitte passatī ti, vivekadassī phassesu. Diṭṭhīsu ca na niyyatī ti tassa dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni pahīnāni samucchinnāni vūpasantāni paṭipassaddhāni abhabbuppattikāni ñāṇagginā daḍḍhāni, so diṭṭhiyā na yāyati, na niyyati, na vuyhati, na saṃhariyati, na pi taṃ diṭṭhigataṃ sārato pacceti paccāgacchatī ti, diṭṭhīsu ca na niyyati. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:


<!-- PTS Page 224 -->
<a id="page-224"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 224]*

Nirāsattī anāgate atītaṃ nānusocatī, vivekadassī phassesu diṭṭhīsu ca na niyyatī ti.

Sn_IV,10.5(=852): Patilīno akuhako, apihālu amaccharī, appagabbho ajeguccho, pesuṇeyye ca no yuto.  || Nidd_I.10:5 ||

Patilīno akuhako ti. Patilīno ti rāgassa pahīnattā patilīno, dosassa [^224-1]pahīnattā patilīno[^224-1], mohassa pahīnattā patilīno, kodhassa upanāhassa makkhassa paḷāsassa issāya macchariyassa . . . pe . . . sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārānaṃ pahīnattā, patilīno[^224-2]. Vuttaṃ h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā: *Kathañ ca bhikkhave bhikkhu patilīno hoti? Idha[^224-3] bhikkhave bhikkhuno asmimāno pahīno hoti ucchinnamūlo tālāvatthukato [^224-4]anabhāvaṃ gato[^224-4] āyatiṃ anuppādadhammo; evaṃ kho bhikkhave[^224-5] bhikkhu patilīno hotī ti, patilīno[^224-5]. Akuhako[^224-6] ti. Tīṇi kuhanavatthūni, paccayapaṭisevanasaṃkhātaṃ kuhanavatthu, iriyāpathasaṃkhātaṃ kuhanavatthu, sāmantajappanasaṃkhātaṃ kuhanavatthu. Katamaṃ paccayapaṭisevanasaṃkhātaṃ kuhanavatthu? Idha gahapatikā bhikkhuṃ nimantenti cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārehi. So pāpiccho icchāpakato atthiko cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ, bhiyyo kamyataṃ upādāya cīvaraṃ paccakkhāti, piṇḍapātaṃ paccakkhāti, senāsanaṃ paccakkhāti, gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāraṃ paccakkhāti. So evam āha: kiṃ samaṇassa mahagghena cīvarena? Etaṃ sāruppaṃ yaṃ samaṇo susānā vā saṅkārakūṭā vā pāpaṇikā vā nantakāni[^224-7] uccinitvā[^224-8] saṃghāṭiṃ katvā[^224-9] dhāreyya. Kiṃ samaṇassa mahagghena


<!-- PTS Page 225 -->
<a id="page-225"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 225]*

piṇḍapātena? Etaṃ sāruppaṃ yaṃ samaṇo uñchācariyāya piṇḍiyālopena jīvikaṃ kappeyya. Kiṃ samaṇassa mahagghena senāsanena? Etaṃ sārūppaṃ yaṃ samaṇo rukkhamūliko vā assa, sosāniko vā abbhokāsiko vā. Kiṃ samaṇassa mahagghena gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārena? Etaṃ sārūppaṃ yaṃ samaṇo pūtimuttena vā hariṭakīkhaṇḍena vā osadhaṃ[^225-1] kareyyā ti, tadupādāya lūkhaṃ cīvaraṃ dhāreti, lūkhaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjati[^225-2], lūkhaṃ senāsanaṃ paṭisevati, lūkhaṃ gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāraṃ paṭisevati. Tam enaṃ gahapatikā evaṃ jānanti: ayaṃ samaṇo appiccho santuṭṭho pavivitto asaṃsaṭṭho āraddhaviriyo dhutavādo ti; bhiyyo bhiyyo nimantenti cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārehi. So evam āha: tiṇṇaṃ sammukhībhāvā saddho kulaputto bahuṃ puññaṃ pasavati. Saddhāya sammukhībhāvā saddho kulaputto bahuṃ puññaṃ pasavati; deyyadhammassa sammukhībhāvā saddho kulaputto bahuṃ puññaṃ pasavati; dakkhiṇeyyānaṃ sammukhībhāvā saddho kulaputto bahuṃ puññaṃ pasavati. Tumhākañ c'; evāyaṃ saddhā atthi. Deyyadhammo cāyaṃ[^225-3] saṃvijjati. Ahañ ca paṭiggāhako. Sac'; āhaṃ na paṭiggahessāmi, evaṃ tumhe puññena parihīnā[^225-4] bhavissatha. Na mayhaṃ iminā attho, api ca tumhākañ ñeva anukampāya patiggaṇhāmī ti, tad upādāya bahum pi cīvaraṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, bahum pi piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, bahum pi senāsanaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, bahum pi gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāraṃ paṭiggaṇhāti; yā evarūpā bhākuṭikā bhākuṭiyaṃ kuhanā kuhāyanā kuhitattaṃ[^225-5]; idaṃ vuccati[^225-6] paṭisevanasaṃkhātaṃ[^225-7] kuhanavatthu. Katamaṃ iriyāpathasaṃkhātaṃ kuhanavatthu? Idh' ekacco pāpiccho icchāpakato sambhāvanādhippāyo: evaṃ maṃ jano sambhāvissatī[^225-8] ti gamanaṃ saṇṭhapeti, ṭhānaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 226 -->
<a id="page-226"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 226]*

saṇṭhapeti, nisajjaṃ saṇṭhapeti[^226-1], sayanaṃ[^226-2] saṇṭhapeti[^226-3]; paṇidhāya gacchati, paṇidhāya tiṭṭhati, paṇidhāya nisīdati, paṇidhāya seyyaṃ kappeti, samāhito viya gacchati, samāhito viya tiṭṭhati, samāhito viya nisīdati, samāhito viya seyyaṃ kappeti, āpādakajjhāyī ca[^226-4] hoti; yā evarūpā iriyāpathassa āṭhapanā[^226-5] saṇṭhapanā bhākuṭikā bhākuṭiyaṃ kuhanā kuhāyanā kuhitattaṃ; idaṃ iriyāpathasaṃkhātaṃ kuhanavatthuṃ. Katamaṃ sāmantajappanasaṃkhātaṃ kuhanavatthuṃ? Idh'; ekacco pāpiccho icchāpakato sambhāvanādhippāyo: evaṃ maṃ jano sambhāvissatī[^226-6] ti ariyadhammasannissitaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati. Yo evarūpaṃ cīvaraṃ dhāreti, so samaṇo mahesakkho ti bhaṇati, yo evarūpaṃ pattaṃ dhāreti, lohathālakaṃ dhāreti, dhammakarakaṃ[^226-7] dhāreti, parisāvanaṃ dhāreti, kuñcikaṃ dhāreti, upāhanaṃ dhāreti, kāyabandhanaṃ dhāreti, āyogaṃ dhāreti, so samaṇo mahesakkho ti bhaṇati. Yassa evarūpo upajjhāyo, so samaṇo mahesakkho ti bhaṇati; yassa evarūpo ācariyo, evarūpā samānupajjhāyakā samānācariyakā mittā sandiṭṭhā sambhattā sahāyā, so samaṇo mahesakkho ti bhaṇati. Yo evarūpe vihāre vasati, so samaṇo mahesakkho ti bhaṇati. Yo evarūpe aḍḍhayoge vasati, pāsāde[^226-8] vasati, hammiye vasati, guhāyaṃ vasati, leṇe vasati, kuṭiyā vasati, kūṭāgāre vasati, aṭṭe vasati, māḷe vasati, uddaṇḍe vasati, upaṭṭhānasālāyam[^226-9] vasati, maṇḍape vasati, rukkhamūle vasati, so samaṇo mahesakkho ti bhaṇati. Athavā korajikakorajiko bhākuṭikabhākuṭiko[^226-10] kuhakakuhako lapakalapako mukhasambhāvito ayaṃ samaṇo imāsaṃ evarūpānaṃ santāṇaṃ vihārasamāpattīnaṃ lābhī[^226-11] tādisaṃ gambhīraṃ guyhaṃ[^226-12] nipuṇṇaṃ paṭicchannaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 227 -->
<a id="page-227"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 227]*

lokuttarasuññatapaṭisaṃyuttaṃ kathaṃ katheti; yā evarūpā bhākuṭikā bhākuṭiyaṃ kuhanā kuhāyanā kuhāyitattaṃ[^227-1], idaṃ sāmantajappanasaṃkhātaṃ kuhanavatthuṃ. Yass'; imāni tīṇi kuhanavatthūni pahīnāni samucchinnāni vūpasantāni paṭipassaddhāni abhabbuppattikāni nāṇagginā daḍḍhāni, so vuccati akuhako ti, patilīno akuhako. Āpihālu amaccharī ti. Pihā vuccati taṇhā; yo rāgo sārāgo . . . pe . . . abhijjhā lobho akusalamūlaṃ. Yass' esā pihā[^227-2] pahīnā samucchinnā vūpasantā paṭippassaddhā abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā, so vuccati apihālu. So rūpe na piheti, sadde gandhe rase phoṭṭhabbe kulaṃ gaṇaṃ āvāsaṃ lābhaṃ yasaṃ pasaṃsaṃ sukhaṃ cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātaṃ senāsanaṃ gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāraṃ kāmadhātuṃ rūpadhātuṃ arūpadhātum kāmabhavaṃ rūpabhavaṃ arūpabhavaṃ saññābhavaṃ asaññābhavaṃ nevasaññānāsaññābhavaṃ ekavokārabhavaṃ catuvokārabhavaṃ pañcavokārabhavaṃ atītaṃ anāgataṃ paccuppannaṃ diṭṭhasutamutaviññātabbe dhamme na piheti, na icchati, na sādiyati[^227-3], na pattheti, nābhijappatī ti, apihālu. Amaccharī ti pañca macchariyāni, āvāsamacchariyaṃ kulamacchariyaṃ lābhamacchariyaṃ vaṇṇamacchariyaṃ dhammamacchariyaṃ; yaṃ evarūpaṃ macchariyaṃ[^227-4] maccharāyanā maccharāyitattaṃ vevicchaṃ kadariyaṃ kaṭukañcakatā[^227-5] aggahitattaṃ cittassa, idaṃ vuccati macchariyaṃ. Api ca khandhamacchariyam pi macchariyaṃ, dhātumacchariyam pi macchariyaṃ, āyatanamacchariyam pi macchariyaṃ, gāho vuccati macchariyaṃ. Yass'; etaṃ pahīnaṃ[^227-6] vūpasantaṃ paṭippassaddhaṃ abhabbuppattikaṃ ñāṇagginā daḍḍhaṃ, so vuccati amaccharī ti, apihālu amaccharī.


<!-- PTS Page 228 -->
<a id="page-228"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 228]*

Appagabbho ajeguccho ti. Pāgabbhiyan[^228-1] ti tīṇi pāgabbhiyāni, kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ, vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ, cetasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ. Katamaṃ kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ? Idh'; ekacco saṅghagato pi kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti, gaṇagato pi kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti, bhojanasālāyaṃ pi kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti, jantāghare[^228-2] pi kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti, udakatitthe pi kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti, antaragharaṃ pavisanto pi kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti, antaragharaṃ paviṭṭho pi kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti. Kathaṃ saṅghagato kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti? Idh'; ekacco saṅghagato acittikārakato there bhikkhū ghaṭṭayanto pi tiṭṭhati, ghaṭṭayanto pi nisīdati, purato pi tiṭṭhati, purato pi nisīdati, ucce pi āsane nisīdati, sasīsaṃ pārupitvā[^228-3] pi nisīdati, ṭhitako pi bhaṇati, bāhāvikkhepako pi bhaṇati; evaṃ saṅghagato kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti. Kathaṃ gaṇagato kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti? Idh' ekacco gaṇagato acittikārakato therānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anupāhanānaṃ caṅkamantānaṃ sa-upāhano[^228-4] caṅkamati, nīce caṅkame caṅkamantānaṃ ucce caṅkame caṅkamati, chamāyaṃ[^228-5] caṅkamantānaṃ caṅkame caṅkamati, ghaṭṭayanto pi tiṭṭhati, ghaṭṭayanto pi nisīdati; purato pi tiṭṭhati, purato pi nisīdati, ucce pi āsane nisīdati, sasīsaṃ pārupitvā pi nisīdati, ṭhitako pi bhaṇati, bāhāvikkhepako pi bhaṇati; evaṃ gaṇagato kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti. Kathaṃ bhojanasālāya kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti? Idh'; ekacco bhojanasālāya acittikārakato there bhikkhū anupakhajja nisīdati, nave pi bhikkhū āsanena patibāhati, ghaṭṭayanto pi tiṭṭhati, ghaṭṭayanto pi nisīdati, purato pi tiṭṭhati, purato pi nisīdati, ucce pi āsane nisīdati, sasīsaṃ pārupitvā pi nisīdati, ṭhitako pi bhaṇati, bāhāvikkhepako pi bhaṇati; evaṃ bhojanasālāya kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti.


<!-- PTS Page 229 -->
<a id="page-229"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 229]*

Kathaṃ jantāghare kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti? Idh'; ekacco jantāghare acittikārakato there bhikkhū ghaṭṭayanto pi tiṭṭhati, ghaṭṭayanto pi nisīdati, purato pi tiṭṭhati, purato pi nisīdati, ucce pi āsane nisīdati, anāpucchaṃ pi anajjhiṭṭho pi kaṭṭhaṃ pakkhipati, dvāraṃ[^229-1] pidahati, bāhāvikkhepako pi bhaṇati; evaṃ jantāghare kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti. Kathaṃ udakatitthe kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti? Idh'; ekacco udakatitthe acittikārakato there bhikkhū ghaṭṭayanto pi otarati, purato pi otarati, ghaṭṭayanto pi nhāyati[^229-2], purato pi nhāyati[^229-2], uparito[^229-3] pi nhāyati[^229-2], ghaṭṭayanto pi uttarati, purato pi uttarati, uparito[^229-3] pi uttarati; evaṃ udakatitthe kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti. Kathaṃ antaragharaṃ pavisanto kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti? Idh'; ekacco antaragharaṃ pavisanto acittikārakato there bhikkhū ghaṭṭayanto pi gacchati, purato pi gacchati, vokkamma pi therānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ purato[^229-4] gacchati; evaṃ antaragharaṃ pavisanto kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti. Kathaṃ antaragharaṃ paviṭṭho kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti? Idh'; ekacco antaragharaṃ paviṭṭho na pavisa bhante ti vuccamāno pavisati, na tiṭṭha bhante ti vuccamāno tiṭṭhati, na nisīda[^229-5] bhante ti vuccamāno nisīdati, anokāsaṃ pi[^229-6] pavisati, anokāse pi tiṭṭhati, anokāse pi nisīdati; yāni[^229-7] tāni honti kulānaṃ ovarakāni[^229-8] guḷhāni[^229-9] ca paticchannāni ca, yattha kulitthiyo kuladhītaro[^229-10] kulasuṇhāyo kulakumāriyo nisīdanti, tattha pi sahasā pavisati, kumārakassa pi sīsaṃ[^229-11] parāmasati; evaṃ antaragharaṃ paviṭṭho kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti; idaṃ kāyikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ.


<!-- PTS Page 230 -->
<a id="page-230"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 230]*

Katamaṃ vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ? Idh'; ekacco saṅghagato pi vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti, gaṇagato pi[^230-1] vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti, antaragharaṃ paviṭṭho pi vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti. Kathaṃ saṅghagato vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti? Idh'; ekacco saṅghagato[^230-2] acittikārakato there bhikkhū anāpucchaṃ vā anajjhiṭṭho vā dhammaṃ bhaṇati, pañhaṃ visajjeti, pātimokkhaṃ uddisati, ṭhitako[^230-3] pi bhaṇati, bāhāvikkhepako pi bhaṇati; evaṃ saṅghagato vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti. Kathaṃ gaṇagato vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti? Idh'; ekacco gaṇagato acittikārakato there bhikkhū anāpucchaṃ vā anajjhiṭṭho vā ārāmagatānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ bhaṇati, pañhaṃ visajjeti, ṭhitako pi bhaṇati, bāhāvikkhepako pi bhaṇati, ārāmagatānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ dhammaṃ bhaṇati, pañhaṃ visajjeti, ṭhitako pi bhaṇati, bāhāvikkhepako pi bhaṇati; evaṃ ganagato vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti. Kathaṃ antaragharaṃ paviṭṭho vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti? Idh'; ekacco antaragharaṃ paviṭṭho itthiṃ[^230-4] vā kumāriṃ[^230-5] vā āha: itthannāme itthaṃgotte kiṃ atthi? Yāgū atthi, bhattaṃ atthi, khādanīyaṃ atthi? Kiṃ pivissāma, kiṃ bhuñjissāma, kiṃ khādissāma, kiṃ vā atthi, kiṃ vā me dassathā? ti vippalapati. Yo evarūpo vācāpalāpo vippalāpo lālappo lālappāyanā[^230-6] lālappāyitattaṃ[^230-7]; evaṃ antaragharaṃ paviṭṭho vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ dasseti; idaṃ vācasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ. Katamaṃ cetasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ? Idh'; ekacco na uccākulā pabbajito samāno uccākulā[^230-8] pabbajitena saddhiṃ sadisaṃ attānaṃ dahati cittena; na mahākulā pabbajito samāno mahākulā pabbajitena saddhiṃ sadisaṃ attānaṃ dahati cittena; na mahābhogakulā pabbajito samāno mahābhogakulā pabbajitena saddhiṃ sadisaṃ attānaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 231 -->
<a id="page-231"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 231]*

dahati cittena; na uḷārabhogakulā pabbajito samāno, na suttantiko samāno suttantikena saddhiṃ sadisaṃ attānaṃ dahati cittena, na vinayadharo[^231-1] samāno, na dhammakathiko samāno, āraññiko samāno, na piṇḍapātiko samāno, na paṃsukūliko samāno, na tecīvariko samāno, na sapadānacāriko samāno, na khalupacchābhattiko samāno, na nesajjiko samāno, na yathāsanthatiko samāno, na paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhī samāno paṭhamassa jhānassa lābhinā saddhiṃ sadisaṃ attānaṃ dahati cittena . . . pe . . . na nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhī samāno nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasamāpattiyā lābhinā saddhiṃ sadisaṃ attānaṃ dahati cittena; idaṃ cetasikaṃ pāgabbhiyaṃ. Yass'; imāni tīṇi pāgabbhiyāni pahīnāni samucchinnāni vūpasantāni paṭipassaddhāni abhabbuppattikāni ñāṇagginā daḍḍhāni, so vuccati appagabbho ti, appagabbho. Ajeguccho ti. Atthi puggalo jeguccho, atthi ajeguccho. Katamo ca puggalo jeguccho? Idh'; ekacco puggalo dussīlo hoti pāpadhammo asucisaṅkassarasamācāro paṭicchannakammanto[^231-2] assamaṇo samaṇapaṭiñño abrahmacārī brahmacāripaṭiñño antoputi avassuto kasambujāto; ayaṃ[^231-3] vuccati puggalo jeguccho. Athavā kodhano hoti upāyāsabahulo, appam pi vutto samāno abhisajjati kuppati[^231-4] byāpajjati patiṭṭhiyati kopañ ca dosañ ca appaccayañ ca pātukaroti; ayaṃ vuccati puggalo jeguccho. Athavā kodhano hoti, upanāhī hoti[^231-5], makkhī hoti, paḷāsī hoti[^231-6], issukī hoti, maccharī hoti[^231-6], saṭho hoti, māyāvī hoti[^231-5], thaddho hoti, atimānī hoti[^231-5], pāpiccho hoti, micchādiṭṭhi hoti[^231-5], sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hoti, ādhānagāhī[^231-7] duppaṭinissaggī hoti; ayaṃ vuccati puggalo jeguccho. Katamo ca puggalo ajeguccho? Idha bhikkhu sīlavā hoti, pātimokkhasaṃvarasaṃvuto viharati ācāragocara-


<!-- PTS Page 232 -->
<a id="page-232"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 232]*

sampanno, aṇumattesu vajjesu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadesu; ayaṃ vuccati puggalo ajeguccho. Athavā akkodhano hoti anupāyāsabahulo[^232-1], bahum pi vutto samāno na abhisajjati, na kuppati na byāpajjati na patiṭṭhiyati na kopañ ca dosañ ca appaccayañ ca pātukaroti. Ayaṃ vuccati puggalo ajeguccho. Athavā akkodhano hoti, anupanāhī hoti[^232-2], amakkhī hoti, apaḷāsī hoti[^232-2], anissukī[^232-3] hoti, amaccharī hoti[^232-4], asaṭho hoti, amāyāvī hoti[^232-4], athaddho hoti, anatimānī hoti[^232-2], na pāpiccho hoti, na micchādiṭṭhi hoti[^232-2], asandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hoti, anādhānagāhī[^232-5] hoti[^232-2], suppaṭinissaggī hoti[^232-2]; ayaṃ vuccati puggalo ajeguccho. Sabbo[^232-6] kalyāṇaputhujjanaṃ[^232-7] upādāya ariyapuggalo ajeguccho ti, appagabbho ajeguccho. Pesuṇeyye ca no yuto ti pesuññan ti idh'; ekacco pisuṇavāco hoti, ito sutvā amutra akkhātā imesaṃ bhedāya, amutra vā sutvā imesaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhedāya; iti samaggānaṃ[^232-8] vā bhettā[^232-9], bhinnānaṃ vā anuppadātā, vaggārāmo vaggarato vagganandī vaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hoti; idaṃ vuccati pesuññaṃ. Api ca dvīhi kāraṇehi pesuññaṃ upasaṃharati, piyakamyatāya vā bhedādhippāyo vā. Kathaṃ piyakamyatāya pesuññaṃ upasaṃharati? Imassa piyo bhavissāmi, manāpo bhavissāmi, vissāsiko bhavissāmi, abbhantariko bhavissāmi, suhadayo bhavissāmī ti; evaṃ piyakamyatāya pesuññaṃ upasaṃharati. Kathaṃ bhedādhippāyo pesuññaṃ upasaṃharati? Kathaṃ ime nānā[^232-10] assu, [^232-11]vinā assu[^232-11], vaggā assu, dvedhā[^232-12] assu, dvejjhā assu, dvepakkhā assu, bhijjeyyuṃ na samāgaccheyyuṃ, dukkhaṃ na phāsu vihareyyun ti;


<!-- PTS Page 233 -->
<a id="page-233"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 233]*

evaṃ bhedādhippāyo pesuññaṃ upasaṃharati. Yass' etaṃ pesuññaṃ pahīnaṃ samucchinnaṃ vūpasantaṃ paṭipassaddhaṃ abhabbuppattikaṃ ñāṇagginā daḍḍhaṃ, so pesuññe[^233-1] na yutto na-ppayutto nāyutto[^233-2] na samāyutto ti, pesuṇeyye ca no yuto. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Paṭilīno akuhako, apihālu amaccharī, appagabbho ajeguccho, pesuṇeyye ca no yuto ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,10.6(=853): Sātiyesu anassāvī atimāne ca no yuto, saṇho ca paṭibhāṇavā, na saddho na virajjati.  || Nidd_I.10:6 ||

Sātiyesu anassāvī ti sātiyā vuccanti pañca kāmaguṇā. Kiṃkāraṇā sātiyā vuccanti pañca kāmaguṇā? Yebhuyyena devamanussā pañca kāmaguṇe icchanti sādiyanti patthayanti pihayanti abhijappanti, taṃkāraṇā sātiyā vuccanti pañca kāmaguṇā. Yesaṃ esā sātiyā taṇhā appahīnā, tesaṃ cakkhuto rūpataṇhā savati[^233-3] pasavati sandati pavattati, sotato saddataṇhā, ghānato gandhataṇhā, jivhāto rasataṇhā, kāyato phoṭṭhabbataṇhā, manato dhammataṇhā savati pasavati[^233-4] sandati pavattati. Yesaṃ esā sātiyā taṇhā pahīnā samucchinnā vūpasantā paṭipassaddhā abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā, tesaṃ cakkhuto rūpataṇhā na savati, [^233-5]na pasavati[^233-5], na sandati, na pavattati, sotato saddataṇhā . . . pe . . . manato dhammataṇhā na savati, [^233-6]na pasavati[^233-6], na sandati, na pavattatī ti, sātiyesu anassāvī. Atimāne ca no yuto[^233-7] ti. Katamo atimāno? Idh'; ekacco paraṃ atimaññati jātiyā vā gottena vā . . . pe . . . aññataraññatarena vā vatthunā; yo evarūpo māno, maññanā maññitattaṃ uṇṇati uṇṇamo dhajo sampaggāho[^233-8] ketu-


<!-- PTS Page 234 -->
<a id="page-234"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 234]*

kamyatā cittassa, ayaṃ vuccati atimāno. [^234-1]Yass'; eso[^234-1] atimāno pahīno[^234-2] samucchinno vūpasanto paṭipassaddho abhabbuppattiko ñāṇagginā daḍḍho, so atimāne[^234-3] no yutto[^234-4], na-ppayutto, nāyutto[^234-5], na samāyutto ti, atimāne ca no yuto. Saṇho ca paṭibhāṇavā ti. [^234-6]Saṇho ti[^234-6] saṇhena kāyakammena samannāgato ti saṇho, saṇhena vacīkammena[^234-7], saṇhena manokammena samannāgato ti saṇho, saṇhehi satipaṭṭhānehi samannāgato ti saṇho, saṇhehi sammappadhānehi, saṇhehi iddhippādehi, saṇhehi[^234-2] indriyehi, saṇhehi balehi, saṇhehi bojjhaṅgehi samannāgato ti saṇho, saṇhena ariyena aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena samannāgato ti saṇho. Paṭibhāṇavā ti. [^234-6]Tayo paṭibhāṇavanto[^234-6], pariyattipaṭibhāṇavā paripucchāpaṭibhāṇavā adhigamapaṭibhāṇavā. Katamo pariyattipaṭibhāṇavā? Idh'; ekaccassa[^234-8] pariyāpuṭaṃ hoti suttaṃ geyyaṃ veyyākaraṇaṃ gāthā udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jāṭakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vedallaṃ. Tassa pariyattiṃ nissāya paṭibhāyati; ayaṃ pariyattipaṭibhāṇavā. Katamo paripucchāpaṭibhāṇavā? Idh'; ekacco paripucchako[^234-9] hoti attatthe ca ñāyatthe ca lakkhaṇe ca kāraṇe ca ṭhānāṭhāne ca. Tassa taṃ paripucchaṃ nissāya paṭibhāyati; ayaṃ paripucchāpaṭibhāṇavā. Katamo adhigamapaṭibhāṇavā? Idh'; ekaccassa adhigatā honti cattāro satipaṭṭhānā, cattāro sammappadhānā, cattāro iddhippādā, pañc'; indriyāni, pañca balāni, satta bojjhaṅgā, ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo[^234-10], cattāri[^234-11] sāmaññaphalāni, catasso[^234-11] paṭisambhidā[^234-12], cha abhiññā[^234-12]; tassa attho ñāto, dhammo ñāto, nirutti ñātā, atthe ñāte attho


<!-- PTS Page 235 -->
<a id="page-235"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 235]*

paṭibhāyati, dhamme ñāte dhammo paṭibhāyati, niruttiyā ñātāya nirutti paṭibhāyati; imesu tīsu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhāṇapaṭisambhidā. Imāya paṭibhāṇapaṭisambhidāya upeto samupeto upagato samupagato upapanno samupapanno[^235-1] samannāgato hoti[^235-2]; so vuccati paṭibhāṇavā. Yassa pariyatti n'; atthi, paripucchā n'; atthi, adhigamo n'; atthi, kiṃ tassa paṭibhāyissatī ti, saṇho ca paṭibhāṇavā. Na saddho na virajjatī ti. Na saddho ti sāmaṃ sayam abhiññātaṃ attapacchakkhaṃ dhammaṃ na kassaci saddhahati[^235-3] aññassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā devassa vā mārassa vā [^235-4]narassa vā[^235-4] brahmuno vā. Sabbe saṃkhārā aniccā ti sāmaṃ sayam abhiññātaṃ [^235-5]attapacchakkhaṃ dhammaṃ na kassaci saddahati aññassa samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā devassa vā mārassa vā narassa vā brahmuno vā[^235-5]. Sabbe saṃkhārā dukkhā ti, sabbe dhammā anattā ti, avijjāpaccayā saṃkhārā ti . . . pe . . . jātipaccayā jarāmaraṇan ti avijjānirodhā saṃkhāraṇirodho ti . . . pe . . . jātinirodhā jarāmaraṇanirodho ti. Idaṃ dukkhan ti . . . pe . . . ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti. Ime āsavā ti . . . pe . . . ayaṃ āsavanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ti. Ime dhammā abhiññeyyā ti . . . pe[^235-6] . . . ime dhammā sacchikātabbā ti[^235-7]. Channaṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ ca aṭṭhaṅgamañ ca assādañ ca ādīnavañ ca nissaraṇañ ca, pañcannaṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ samudayañ ca [^235-8]aṭṭhaṅgamañ ca assādañ ca ādīnavañ ca nissaraṇañ ca[^235-8], catunnaṃ mahābhūtānaṃ samudayañ ca aṭṭhaṅgamañ ca assādañ ca ādīnavañ ca nissaraṇañ[^235-9] cā ti[^235-9]. Yaṃ kiñci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbantaṃ nirodhadhamman ti sāmaṃ sayam abhiññātaṃ attapaccakkhaṃ dhammaṃ na kassaci saddahati aññassa


<!-- PTS Page 236 -->
<a id="page-236"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 236]*

samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā devassa vā mārassa vā narassa vā brahmuno vā[^236-1]. Vuttaṃ h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā: *Saddahasi tvaṃ Sāriputta saddhindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyosānaṃ, viriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkatam amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyosānan? ti[^236-2]. Na khvāhaṃ ettha bhante Bhagavato saddhāya gacchāmi, saddhindriyaṃ viriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyosānaṃ. Yesam nūn'; etaṃ bhante añātaṃ assa adiṭṭhaṃ aviditaṃ asacchikataṃ aphusitaṃ[^236-3] paññāya, te tattha paresaṃ saddhāya gaccheyyuṃ, saddhindriyam . . . pe[^236-4] . . . paññindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyosānan ti[^236-2]. Yesañ ca kho etaṃ[^236-5] bhante ñātaṃ diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phusitaṃ[^236-6] paññāya, nikkaṃkhā te tattha nibbicikicchā, saddhindriyaṃ viriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyosānan ti; mayhañ ca kho etaṃ[^236-5] bhante ñātam diṭṭhaṃ viditaṃ sacchikataṃ phusitaṃ[^236-7] paññāya, nikkaṃkho[^236-8] 'haṃ tattha nibbicikiccho, saddhindriyaṃ viriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyosānan ti. Sādhu sādhu Sāriputta. Yesaṃ h'; etaṃ Sāriputta añātaṃ assa adiṭṭhaṃ aviditaṃ asacchikataṃ aphusitaṃ[^236-9] paññāya, te tattha paresaṃ saddhāya gaccheyyuṃ, saddhindriyaṃ . . . pe . . .


<!-- PTS Page 237 -->
<a id="page-237"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 237]*

paññindriyaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ amatogadhaṃ hoti amataparāyanaṃ amatapariyosānan ti. *Assaddho akataññū ca sandhicchedo ca yo naro, hatāvakāso vantāso, sa ve[^237-1] uttamaporiso ti. Na[^237-2] virajjatī ti sabbe bālaputhujjanā rajjanti. Kalyāṇaputhujjanaṃ[^237-3] upādāya satta sekhā virajjanti. Arahā n' eva rajjati, no virajjati; virato[^237-4] so khayā rāgassa vītarāgattā, khayā dosassa vītadosattā, khayā mohassa vītamohattā; so vuṭṭhavāso[^237-5] ciṇṇacaraṇo . . . pe[^237-6] . . . n'; atthi tassa punabbhavo ti, na saddho na virajjati. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Sātiyesu anassāvī atimāne ca no yuto, saṇho ca paṭibhāṇavā, na saddho na virajjatī ti.

Sn_IV,10.7(=854): Lābhakamyā na sikkhati, alābhe ca na kuppati, aviruddho ca taṇhāya rase ca nānugijjhati.  || Nidd_I.10:7 ||

Lābhakamyā na sikkhati, alābhe ca na kuppatī ti. Kathaṃ lābhakamyā na sikkhati? Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati lābhiṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ. Tassa evaṃ hoti: kena nu kho ayam āyasmā lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānan ti? [^237-7]Tassa evaṃ hoti: ayaṃ kho āyasmā suttantiko, tena ayam āyasmā lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānan ti[^237-7]. So lābhahetu lābhapaccayā lābhakāraṇā lābhābhinibbattiyā lābhaṃ paripācento suttantaṃ pariyāpuṇāti; evam pi lābhakamyā sikkhati.


<!-- PTS Page 238 -->
<a id="page-238"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 238]*

Athavā bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati lābhiṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ. Tassa evaṃ hoti: kena nu kho ayam āyasmā lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānan ti? Tassa evaṃ hoti: ayaṃ kho āyasmā vinayadharo . . . pe . . . ābhidhammiko[^238-1], tena ayam āyasmā lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānan ti, so lābhahetu lābhapaccayā lābhakāraṇā labhābhinibbattiyā lābhaṃ paripācento abhidhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti; evam pi lābhakamyā sikkhati. Athavā bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ passati lābhiṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānaṃ. Tassa evaṃ hoti: kena nu kho ayam āyasmā lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānan ti? Tassa evaṃ hoti: ayaṃ kho āyasmā āraññiko piṇḍapātiko paṃsukūliko tecīvariko sapadānacāriko khalupacchābhattiko nesajjiko yathāsanthatiko, tena ayam āyasmā lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasenāsanagilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhārānan ti. So lābhahetu lābhapaccayā lābhakāraṇā lābhābhinibbattiyā lābhaṃ paripācento āraññiko hoti . . . pe . . . yathāsanthatiko hoti; evam pi lābhakamyā sikkhati. Kathaṃ na lābhakamyā sikkhati? Idha bhikkhu na lābhahetu, na lābhapaccayā, na lābhakāraṇā, na lābhābhinibbattiyā, na lābhaṃ paripācento yāvad eva attadamathāya attasamathāya attaparinibbāpanatthāya suttantaṃ pariyāpuṇāti, vinayaṃ pariyāpuṇāti, abhidhammaṃ pariyāpuṇāti; evam pi na lābhakamyā sikkhati. Athavā bhikkhu na lābhahetu, na lābhapaccayā, na lābhakāraṇā, na lābhābhinibbattiyā, na lābhaṃ paripācento yāvad eva appicchañ ñeva nissāya, santuṭṭhiñ ñeva nissāya, sallekhañ ñeva nissāya, pavivekañ ñeva nissāya, idamatthitañ ñeva nissāya āraññiko hoti, piṇḍapātiko hoti, paṃsukūliko hoti, tecīvariko hoti, sapadānacāriko hoti, khalupacchābhattiko hoti, nesajjiko hoti, yathāsanthatiko hoti; evam pi na lābhakamyā sikkhatī ti, lābhakamyā na sikkhati.


<!-- PTS Page 239 -->
<a id="page-239"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 239]*

Alābhe [^239-1]ca na[^239-1] kuppatī ti. Kathaṃ alābhe kuppati? Idh'; ekacco kulaṃ vā na labhāmi, gaṇaṃ vā na labhāmi, āvāsaṃ vā na labhāmi, lābhaṃ vā na labhāmi, yasaṃ vā na labhāmi, pasaṃsaṃ vā na labhāmi, sukhaṃ vā na labhāmi, cīvaraṃ vā na labhāmi, piṇḍapātaṃ vā na labhāmi, senāsanaṃ vā na labhāmi, gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāraṃ vā na labhāmi, gilānupaṭṭhākaṃ vā na labhāmi, appañāto 'mhī ti kuppati byāpajjati patiṭṭhiyati[^239-2], kopañ ca dosañ ca appaccayañ ca pātukaroti[^239-3]; evaṃ alābhe kuppati. Kathaṃ alābhe na kuppati? Idha bhikkhu kulaṃ vā na labhāmi, gaṇaṃ vā na labhāmi, [^239-4]āvāsaṃ vā na labhāmi, lābhaṃ vā na labhāmi, yasaṃ vā na labhāmi, pasaṃsaṃ vā na labhāmi, sukhaṃ vā na labhāmi, cīvaraṃ vā na labhāmi, piṇḍapātaṃ vā na labhāmi, senāsanaṃ vā na labhāmi, gilānapaccayabhesajjaparikkhāraṃ vā na labhāmi, gilānupaṭṭhākaṃ vā na labhāmi[^239-4], appañāto 'mhī ti na kuppati, na byāpajjati na patiṭṭhiyati[^239-5], na kopañ ca na dosañ ca appaccayañ ca pātukaroti; evaṃ alābhe na kuppatī ti, lābhakamyā na sikkhati, alābhe ca na kuppati. Aviruddho ca taṇhāya rase ca nānugijjhatī ti. Viruddho ti yo cittassa āghāto[^239-6] paṭighāto[^239-7] anuvirodho[^239-8] kopo pakopo sampakopo doso padoso sampadoso cittassa byāpatti manopadoso[^239-9] cittassa[^239-10] kodho kujjhanā kujjhitattaṃ doso dussanā dussitattaṃ byāpatti byāpajjanā byāpajjitattaṃ virodho paṭivirodho caṇḍikkaṃ assuropo anattamanatā cittassa; ayaṃ vuccati virodho. [^239-11]Yass'; eso[^239-11] virodho pahīno samucchinno vūpasanto paṭipassaddho abhabbuppattiko ñāṇagginā daḍḍho, so vuccati aviruddho.


<!-- PTS Page 240 -->
<a id="page-240"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 240]*

Taṇhā ti rūpataṇhā saddataṇhā [^240-1]gandhataṇhā rasataṇhā phoṭṭhabbataṇhā dhammataṇhā. Raso ti mūlaraso khandharaso tacaraso pattaraso puppharaso phalaraso[^240-1] ambilaṃ[^240-2] madhuraṃ tittikaṃ[^240-3] kaṭukaṃ loṇikaṃ khārikaṃ lambilaṃ kasāvo sāduṃ asāduṃ sītaṃ uṇhaṃ. Sant'; eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā rasagiddhā, te jivhaggena rasaggāni pariyesantā āhiṇḍanti. Te ambilaṃ labhitvā anambilaṃ pariyesanti, anambilaṃ labhitvā ambilaṃ pariyesanti. Madhuraṃ labhitvā amadhuraṃ pariyesanti, amadhuraṃ labhitvā madhuraṃ pariyesanti. Tittikaṃ[^240-3] labhitvā atittikaṃ pariyesanti, atittakaṃ labhitvā tittakaṃ pariyesanti. Kaṭukaṃ[^240-4] labhitvā akaṭukaṃ pariyesanti. Akaṭukaṃ labhitvā kaṭukaṃ pariyesanti. Loṇikaṃ labhitvā aloṇikaṃ pariyesanti. Aloṇikaṃ labhitvā loṇikaṃ pariyesanti. Khārikaṃ labhitvā akhārikaṃ pariyesanti. Akhārikaṃ labhitvā khārikaṃ pariyesanti. Lambilaṃ[^240-5] labhitvā kasāvaṃ pariyesanti. Kasāvaṃ labhitvā lambilaṃ[^240-5] pariyesanti. Sāduṃ labhitvā asāduṃ pariyesanti. Asāduṃ labhitvā sāduṃ pariyesanti. Sītaṃ labhitvā uṇhaṃ pariyesanti. Uṇhaṃ labhitvā sītaṃ[^240-6] pariyesanti. Te yaṃ yaṃ labhitvā tena na[^240-7] santussanti, aparāparaṃ pariyesanti, manāpikesu rasesu rattā giddhā gadhitā mucchitā ajjhopannā [^240-8]laggā laggitā[^240-8] palibuddhā[^240-9]. Yassa[^240-10] rasataṇhā pahīnā samucchinnā vūpasantā paṭipassaddhā abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā, so paṭisaṃkhā yoniso āhāraṃ āhāreti n'; eva davāya, na madāya, na maṇḍanāya, na vibhūsanāya; yāvad eva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya vihiṃsuparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya iti purāṇañ ca vedanaṃ paṭikaṃkhāmi[^240-11], navañ ca vedanaṃ na uppādessāmi; yātrā ca me bhavissati anavajjatā ca phāsuvihāro [^240-12]cā ti[^240-12].


<!-- PTS Page 241 -->
<a id="page-241"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 241]*

Yathā vaṇaṃ[^241-1] ālimpeyya yāvad eva ropanatthāya, yathā vā pana akkhaṃ abbañjeyya[^241-2] yāvad eva bhārassa nittharaṇatthāya, yathā vā pana puttamaṃsa-āhāraṃ[^241-3] āhāreyya yāvad eva kantārassa nittharaṇatthāya; evam eva bhikkhu patisaṃkhā yoniso āhāraṃ āhāreti n'; eva davāya, [^241-4]na madāya, na maṇḍanāya, na vibhūsanāya; yāvad eva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya, vihiṃsuparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya, iti purāṇañ ca vedanaṃ paṭikaṃkhāmi, navañ ca vedanaṃ na uppādessāmi; yātrā ca me bhavissati anavajjatā ca[^241-4] phāsuvihāro cā ti, rasataṇhaṃ pajahati vinodeti byantīkaroti[^241-5] anabhāvaṃ gameti; rasataṇhāya ārato[^241-6] virato paṭivirato nikkhanto nissaṭṭho vippamutto visaññutto vimariyādikatena cetasā viharatī ti, aviruddho ca taṇhāya rase ca nānugijjhati. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Lābhakamyā na sikkhati, alābhe [^241-7]ca na[^241-7] kuppati, aviruddho ca taṇhāya rase ca nānugijjhatī ti.
