Sn_IV,12.4(=881): Sandiṭṭhiyā [^288-5]ce pana vīvadātā[^288-5] saṃsuddhapaññā kusalā matīmā[^288-6], [^288-7]na tesaṃ[^288-7] koci parihīnapañño, diṭṭhī hi tesaṃ[^288-8] pi tathā samattā.  || Nidd_I.12:4 ||

Sandiṭṭhiyā [^288-9]ce pana vīvadātā[^288-9] ti sakāya diṭṭhiyā, sakāya khantiyā, sakāya ruciyā, sakāya laddhiyā vīvadātā[^288-10] [^288-11]pariyodātā[^288-12] asaṅkiliṭṭhā[^288-13] ti, sandiṭṭhiyā [^288-9]ce pana vīvadātā[^288-9].


<!-- PTS Page 289 -->
<a id="page-289"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 289]*

Saṃsuddhapaññā[^289-1] kusalā matīmā ti. Suddhapaññā visuddhapaññā parisuddhapaññā vodātapaññā pariyodātapaññā. Athavā suddhadassanā visuddhadassanā[^289-2] parisuddhadassanā vodātadassanā pariyodātadassanā[^289-2] ti, saṃsuddhapaññā. Kusalā ti. Kusalā[^289-3] paṇḍitā paññavanto buddhimanto ñāṇino vibhāvino medhāvino ti, saṃsuddhapaññā kusalā. Matīmā ti matimā[^289-4] paṇḍitā paññavanto buddhimanto ñāṇino vibhāvino medhāvino ti, saṃsuddhapaññā kusalā matīmā[^289-4]. [^289-5]Na tesaṃ[^289-5] koci parihīnapañño ti tesaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ na koci hīnapañño nihīnapañño omakapañño lāmakapañño jatukkapañño[^289-6] parittapañño. Athavā[^289-7] sabb'; eva aggapaññā[^289-8] seṭṭhapaññā viseṭṭhapaññā pāmokkhapaññā uttamapaññā pavarapaññā ti,[^289-5]na tesaṃ[^289-5] koci parihīnapañño. Diṭṭhī hi tesaṃ pi tathā samattā ti tesaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ diṭṭhi tathā samattā samādinnā gahitā parāmaṭṭhā abhiniviṭṭhā ajjhositā adhimuttā ti, diṭṭhī hi tesaṃ pi tathā samattā. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Sandiṭṭhiyā [^289-9]ce pana vīvadātā[^289-9] saṃsuddhapaññā kusalā matīmā[^289-4], [^289-5]na tesaṃ[^289-5] koci parihīnapañño, diṭṭhī hi tesaṃ pi tathā samattā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,12.5(=882): Na vāham[^289-10] etam tathivan[^289-11] ti brūmi, yam āhu bālā[^289-12] mithu aññamaññaṃ: sakaṃ sakaṃ diṭṭhim akaṃsu saccaṃ, tasmā hi bālo ti paraṃ dahanti.  || Nidd_I.12:5 ||


<!-- PTS Page 290 -->
<a id="page-290"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 290]*

Na vāham[^290-1] etaṃ tathivan[^290-2] ti brūmī ti. Nā ti paṭikkhepo. Etan ti dvāsaṭṭhidiṭṭhigatan ti nāhaṃ etaṃ [^290-3]tathaṃ tacchaṃ[^290-3] bhūtaṃ yāthāvaṃ aviparittan ti brūmi ācikkhāmi desemi paññāpemi paṭṭhapemi vivarāmi vibhajāmi uttānīkaromi pakāsemī ti, na vāham[^290-4] etaṃ tathivan[^290-2] ti brūmi. Yam āhu bālā[^290-5] mithu aññamaññan ti. Mithū ti dve janā, dve kalahakārakā, dve bhaṇḍanakārakā, dve bhassakārakā, dve vivādakārakā, dve adhikaraṇakārakā, dve vādino, dve sallāpakā. Te aññamaññaṃ [^290-6]bālo hīno nihīno omako lāmako jatukko[^290-6] paritto[^290-7] ti evam āhaṃsu, evaṃ kathenti, evaṃ bhaṇanti, evaṃ dīpayanti, evaṃ voharantī ti, yam āhu bālā mithu aññamaññaṃ[^290-8]. Sakaṃ sakaṃ diṭṭhiṃ akaṃsu saccan ti sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti sakaṃ sakaṃ diṭṭhim akaṃsu saccaṃ. Asassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti, sakaṃ sakaṃ diṭṭhim akaṃsu saccaṃ. Tasmā hi bālo ti paraṃ dahantī ti. Tasmā ti tasmā taṃkāraṇā taṃhetu taṃpaccayā taṃnidānā paraṃ [^290-9]bālo hīno nihīno omako lāmako jatukko[^290-10] paritto[^290-9] ti dahanti passanti dakkhanti olokenti nijjhāyanti upaparikkhantī ti, tasmā hi bālo ti paraṃ dahanti. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Na vāham[^290-4] etaṃ tathivan[^290-11] ti brūmi, yam āhu bālā[^290-12] mithu aññamaññaṃ: sakaṃ sakaṃ diṭṭhim akaṃsu saccaṃ, tasmā hi bālo ti paraṃ dahantī ti.

_________________________________


<!-- PTS Page 291 -->
<a id="page-291"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 291]*

Sn_IV,12.6(=883): Yam āhu saccaṃ tathivan[^291-1] ti eke, tam āhu aññe pi[^291-2] tucchaṃ musā ti, evam pi viggayha vivādiyanti[^291-3], kasmā na ekaṃ[^291-4] samaṇā vadanti?  || Nidd_I.12:6 ||

Yam āhu saccaṃ tathivan[^291-1] ti eke ti yaṃ dhamman diṭṭhiṃ paṭipadaṃ maggaṃ eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā: idaṃ saccaṃ tacchaṃ[^291-5] bhūtaṃ[^291-6] yāthāvaṃ[^291-7] aviparittan ti evaṃ āhaṃsu, evaṃ kathenti, evaṃ bhaṇanti, evaṃ dīpayanti, evaṃ voharantī ti, yam āhu saccaṃ tathivan ti eke. Tam āhu aññe pi[^291-8] tucchaṃ musā tī[^291-9] ti tam eva dhammaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭipadaṃ maggaṃ eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā: etaṃ[^291-8] tucchaṃ, etaṃ musā, etaṃ abhūtaṃ, etaṃ alikaṃ, etaṃ ayāthāvan[^291-10] ti evam āhaṃsu evaṃ kathenti, evaṃ bhaṇanti, evaṃ dīpayanti, evaṃ voharantī ti, tam āhu aññe pi[^291-8] tucchaṃ musā ti. Evam pi viggayha vivādiyantī[^291-11] ti evaṃ gahetvā uggahetvā gaṇhitvā parāmasitvā abhinivisitvā vivādiyanti, kalahaṃ karonti, bhaṇḍanaṃ karonti, viggahaṃ karonti, vivādaṃ karonti, medhagaṃ karonti: na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi . . . pe . . . nibbedhehi vā sace pahosī ti, evam pi viggayha vivādiyanti. Kasmā na ekaṃ samaṇā vadantī? ti. Kasmā ti kasmā kiṃkāraṇā kiṃhetu kiṃpaccayā kiṃnidānā[^291-12] kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā[^291-13] kiṃpabhavā na ekaṃ vadanti, nānā vadanti, vividhaṃ vadanti, aññoññaṃ vadanti, puthu vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, kasmā na ekaṃ samaṇā vadanti. Ten'; āha so nimmito:


<!-- PTS Page 292 -->
<a id="page-292"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 292]*

Yam āhu saccaṃ tathivan[^292-1] ti eke, tam āhu aññe pi[^292-2] tucchaṃ musā ti, evam pi viggayha vivādiyanti, kasmā na ekaṃ samaṇā vadantī? ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,12.7(=884): Ekaṃ hi saccaṃ na dutīyam atthi, yasmiṃ pajā no vivade pajānaṃ, [^292-3]nānā te[^292-3] saccāni sayaṃ thunanti, tasmā na ekaṃ samaṇā vadanti.  || Nidd_I.12:7 ||

Ekaṃ hi saccaṃ na dutīyam atthī ti. Ekaṃ saccaṃ vuccati dukkhanirodho nibbānaṃ, yo so sabbasaṃkhārasamatho sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhakkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānaṃ. Athavā ekaṃ saccaṃ vuccati maggasaccaṃ niyyānasaccaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo, seyyathīdaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṃkappo sammāvācā sammākammanto sammā-ājīvo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhī ti, ekaṃ hi saccaṃ na dutīyam atthi. Yasmiṃ pajā no vivade pajānan ti. Yasmin ti yamhi[^292-4] sacce. Pajā[^292-5] ti sattādhivacanaṃ. Pajā[^292-6] yaṃ saccaṃ pajānantā ājānantā vijānantā paṭivijānantā pativijjhantā na kalahaṃ kareyya[^292-7] na bhaṇḍanaṃ kareyya, na viggahaṃ kareyya, na vivādaṃ kareyya, na medhagaṃ kareyya, kalahaṃ bhaṇḍanaṃ viggahaṃ vivādaṃ medhagaṃ pajaheyya[^292-8] vinodeyya[^292-9] byantīkareyya[^292-10] anabhāvaṃ gameyyā[^292-11] ti, yasmiṃ pajā no vivade pajānaṃ. [^292-12]Nānā te[^292-12] saccāni sayaṃ thunantī ti nānā te saccāni sayaṃ thunanti vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharanti: sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan


<!-- PTS Page 293 -->
<a id="page-293"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 293]*

ti sayaṃ thunanti vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharanti; asassato loko . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti sayaṃ thunanti vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, nānā te saccāni sayaṃ thuṇanti. Tasmā na ekaṃ samaṇā vadantī ti. Tasmā ti tasmā taṃkāraṇā taṃhetu taṃpaccayā taṃnidānā na ekaṃ vadanti, nānā vadanti, vividhaṃ vadanti, aññoññaṃ vadanti, puthu vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, tasmā na ekaṃ samaṇā vadanti. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Ekaṃ hi saccaṃ na dutīyam atthi, yasmiṃ pajā no vivade pajānaṃ, nānā te saccāni sayaṃ thunanti, tasmā na ekaṃ samaṇā vadantī ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,12.8(=885): Kasmā nu saccāni vadanti nānā pavādiyāse kusalā vadānā? saccāni suttāni[^293-1] bahūni[^293-2] nānā, udāhu te takkam anussaranti?  || Nidd_I.12:8 ||

Kasmā nu saccāni vadanti nānā ti. Kasmā ti kasmā kiṃkāraṇā kiṃhetu kiṃpaccayā kiṃnidānā [^293-3]saccāni [^293-4]nānā[^293-3] vadanti, vividhāni vadanti, aññoññāni vadanti, puthūni vadanti, kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, kasmā nu saccāni vadanti nānā. Pavādiyāse kusalā vadānā ti. Pavādiyāse ti vippavadantī ti pi[^293-5] pavādiyāse. Athavā sakaṃ sakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ pavadanti[^293-6] kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharanti: sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ mogham aññan ti pavadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharanti: asassato loko . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ mogham aññan ti pavadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharanti.


<!-- PTS Page 294 -->
<a id="page-294"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 294]*

Kusalā vadānā ti kusalavādā paṇḍitavādā dhīravādā[^294-1] ñāṇavādā[^294-2] hetuvādā lakkhaṇavādā kāraṇavādā ṭhānavādā sakāya laddhiyā ti, pavādiyāse kusalāvadānā. Saccāni suttāni bahūni nānā ti saccāni sutāni bahūni[^294-3] nānāni vividhāni aññoññāni [^294-4]puthūnī ti, saccāni suttāni bahūni nānā[^294-4]. Udāhu te takkam anussarantī? ti udāhu[^294-5] takkena vitakkena[^294-6] saṃkappena yāyanti niyyanti vuyhanti saṃhariyantī ti, evam pi udāhu te takkam anussaranti? Athavā takkapariyāhataṃ vīmaṃsānucaritaṃ sayaṃ pāṭibhānaṃ vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, evam pi udāhu te takkam anussaranti. Ten'; āha so nimmito:

Kasmā nu saccāni vadanti nānā pavādiyāse kusalā vadānā? saccāni suttāni bahūni nānā, udāhu te takkam anussarantī? ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,12.9(=886): Na h'; eva saccāni bahūni nānā, aññatra saññāya niccāni[^294-7] loke, takkañ ca diṭṭhīsu pakappayitvā saccaṃ musā ti dvayadhammam āhu.  || Nidd_I.12:9 ||

Na h'; eva saccāni bahūni nānā ti na h'; eva saccāni bahukāni nānāni vividhāni aññoññāni puthūnī ti, na h'; eva saccāni bahūni nānā. Aññatra saññāya niccāni loke ti. Aññatra saññāya[^294-8] niccagāhā ekañ ñeva saccaṃ loke [^294-9]kathiyati bhaṇiyati dīpayati vohariyati[^294-9]: dukkhanirodho nibbānaṃ, yo so sabbasaṃkhārasamatho sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhakkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānaṃ. Athavā ekaṃ saccaṃ vuccati maggasaccaṃ niyyānasaccaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā, ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo, seyyathīdaṃ sammā-


<!-- PTS Page 295 -->
<a id="page-295"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 295]*

diṭṭhi . . . pe  . . . sammāsamādhī ti, aññatra saññāya niccāni loke. Takkañ ca diṭṭhīsu pakappayitvā[^295-1] saccaṃ musā ti dvayadhammam āhū ti takkaṃ vitakkaṃ saṃkappaṃ takkayitvā vitakkayitvā[^295-2] saṃkappayitvā diṭṭhigatāni janenti sañjanenti nibbattenti abhinibbattenti; diṭṭhigatāni janetvā sañjanetvā nibbattetvā abhinibbattetvā: mayhaṃ saccaṃ, tuyhaṃ musā ti evam āhaṃsu, evaṃ kathenti, evaṃ bhaṇanti, evaṃ dīpayanti, evaṃ voharantī ti, takkañ ca diṭṭhīsu pakappāyitvā saccaṃ musā ti dvayadhammam āhu. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Na h'; eva saccāni bahūni nānā, aññatra saññāya niccāni loke, takkañ ca diṭṭhīsu pakappayitvā[^295-3] saccaṃ musā ti dvayadhammam āhū ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,12.10(=887): Diṭṭhe sute sīlavate mute vā [^295-4]ete ca[^295-4] nissāya vimānadassī vinicchaye ṭhatvā pahassamāno[^295-5] bālo paro akusalo ti c'; āha.  || Nidd_I.12:10 ||

Diṭṭhe sute sīlavate mute vā ete ca nissāya vimānadassī ti diṭṭhaṃ vā diṭṭhisuddhiṃ[^295-6] vā sutaṃ vā sutasuddhiṃ vā sīlaṃ vā sīlasuddhiṃ[^295-7] vā vattaṃ[^295-8] vā vattasuddhiṃ[^295-9] vā mutaṃ vā mutasuddhiṃ vā nissāya upanissāya gaṇhitvā parāmasitvā abhinivisitvā ti, diṭṭhe sute sīlavate mute vā. Ete ca nissāya vimānadassī ti na sammānetī ti pi vimānadassī, athavā domanassaṃ janetī ti pi vimānadassī ti, diṭṭhe sute sīlavate mute vā ete ca nissāya vimānadassī. Vinicchaye ṭhatvā pahassamāno[^295-10] ti. Vinicchayā vuc-


<!-- PTS Page 296 -->
<a id="page-296"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 296]*

canti dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni. Diṭṭhivinicchaye[^296-1] vinicchitadiṭṭhiyā[^296-2] ṭhatvā patiṭṭhahitvā gaṇhitvā parāmasitvā abhinivisitvā ti, vinicchaye ṭhatvā. Pahassamāno[^296-3] ti tuṭṭho hoti, haṭṭho pahaṭṭho attamano paripuṇṇasaṃkappo. Athavā dantavidaṃsakaṃ hassamāno[^296-4] ti, vinicchaye ṭhatvā pahassamāno. Bālo paro akusalo ti c'; āhā ti paro bālo hīno nihīno omako lāmako jatukko[^296-5] paritto akusalo avidvā[^296-6] avijjāgato añāṇī avibhāvī amedhāvī[^296-1] duppañño ti evam āha, evaṃ katheti, evaṃ bhaṇati, evaṃ dīpayati, evaṃ voharatī ti, bālo paro akusalo ti c'; āha. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Diṭṭhe sute sīlavate mute vā ete ca nissāya vimānadassī vinicchaye ṭhatvā pahassamāno bālo paro akusalo ti c'; āha ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,12.11(=888): Yen'; eva bālo ti paraṃ dahāti, ten'; ātumānaṃ kusalo ti c'; āha; sayam attanā so kusalo[^296-7] vadāno aññaṃ vimāneti, tath'; eva pāvā.  || Nidd_I.12:11 ||

Yen'; eva bālo ti paraṃ dahātī ti yena hetunā, yena paccayena, yena kāraṇena, [^296-8]yena pabhavena[^296-8] paraṃ bālato hīnato nihīnato omakato lāmakato jatukkato[^296-9] parittato[^296-10] dahati passati dakkhati oloketi nijjhāyati upaparikkhatī ti, yen'; eva bālo ti paraṃ dahāti. Ten'; ātumānaṃ kusalo ti c'; āhā ti. Ātumā[^296-11] vuccati attā. So pi ten'; eva hetunā, tena paccayena, tena kāraṇena, tena pabhavena attānaṃ āha[^296-12]: aham asmi kusalo


<!-- PTS Page 297 -->
<a id="page-297"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 297]*

paṇḍito paññavā buddhimā ñāṇī vibhāvī medhāvī ti, ten' ātumānaṃ kusalo ti c'; āha. Sayam attanā so kusalo[^297-1] vadāno ti sayaṃ[^297-2] attānaṃ kusalavādo paṇḍitavādo dhīravādo[^297-3] ñāṇavādo[^297-4] hetuvādo lakkhaṇavādo kāraṇavādo ṭhānavādo sakāya laddhiyā ti, sayam attanā so kusalo[^297-1] vadāno. Aññaṃ vimāneti, tath'[^297-5] eva pāvā[^297-6] ti. Na sammānetī[^297-7] ti pi aññaṃ vimāneti[^297-8]; athavā domanassaṃ janetī ti pi, aññaṃ vimāneti. Tath'[^297-9] eva pāvā[^297-10] ti. Tath'[^297-9] eva taṃ diṭṭhigataṃ pāvadati: [^297-11]iti p'; ayaṃ[^297-11] puggalo micchādiṭṭhiko viparittadassano ti, aññaṃ vimāneti tath'[^297-12] eva pāvā[^297-13]. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Yen'; eva bālo ti paraṃ dahāti, ten'; ātumānaṃ kusalo ti c'; āha; sayam attanā so kusalo[^297-14] vadāno aññaṃ vimāneti, tath'[^297-12] eva pāvā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,12.12(=889): Atīsaraṃdiṭṭhiyā[^297-15] so samatto mānena matto paripuṇṇamānī sayam eva sāmaṃ manasābhisitto, diṭṭhī hi sā tassa tathā samattā.  || Nidd_I.12:12 ||

Atīsaraṃdiṭṭhiyā[^297-15] so samatto ti. Atisāradiṭṭhiyo[^297-16] vuccanti dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni. Kiṃkāraṇā atisāradiṭṭhiyo[^297-17] vuccanti dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni? Sabbā tā[^297-18] diṭṭhiyo [^297-19]kāraṇātikkantā lakkhaṇātikkantā hīnātikkantā[^297-19] taṃkāraṇā atisāradiṭṭhiyo[^297-20] vuccanti dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni.


<!-- PTS Page 298 -->
<a id="page-298"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 298]*

Sabbe pi titthiyā[^298-1] atisāradiṭṭhiyā[^298-2]. Kiṃkāraṇā sabbe pi titthiyā[^298-1] [^298-3]atisāradiṭṭhiyā[^298-4]? Te aññamaññaṃ atikkamitvā samatikkamitvā[^298-5] vītivattetvā[^298-6] diṭṭhigatāni janenti sañjanenti nibbattenti abhinibbattenti; taṃkāraṇā sabbe pi titthiyā[^298-1] [^298-7]atisāradiṭṭhiyā[^298-8]. So samatto ti atisāradiṭṭhiyā[^298-9] samatto[^298-10] paripuṇṇo anomo ti, atīsaraṃdiṭṭhiyā[^298-11] so samatto. Mānena matto paripuṇṇamānī ti sakāya diṭṭhiyā mānena matto pamatto ummatto adhimatto ti, mānena matto. Paripuṇṇamānī ti paripuṇṇamānī[^298-12] samattamānī[^298-13] anomamānī ti, mānena matto paripuṇṇamānī. Sayam eva sāmaṃ manasābhisitto ti. Sayam eva attānaṃ cittena abhisiñcati: aham asmi kusalo paṇḍito paññavā buddhimā ñāṇī vibhāvī medhāvī ti, sayam eva sāmaṃ manasābhisitto. Diṭṭhī hi sā tassa tathā samattā ti tassa sā diṭṭhi tathā samattā samādiṇṇā gahitā parāmaṭṭhā abhiniviṭṭhā ajjhositā adhimuttā ti, diṭṭhī hi sā tassa tathā samattā. Ten' āha Bhagavā:

Atīsaraṃdiṭṭhiyā[^298-14] so samatto mānena matto paripuṇṇamānī sayam eva sāmaṃ manasābhisitto, diṭṭhī hi sā tassa tathā samattā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,12.13(=890): Parassa ce hi vacasā nihīno, tumo[^298-15] sahā[^298-16] hoti nihīnapañño; athavā[^298-17] sayaṃ vedagū hoti dhīro, na koci bālo samaṇesu atthi.  || Nidd_I.12:13 ||


<!-- PTS Page 299 -->
<a id="page-299"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 299]*

Parassa ce hi vacasā nihīno ti. Parassa ce vācāya vacanena ninditakāraṇā garahitakāraṇā upavaditakāraṇā paro bālo hoti hīno[^299-1] nihīno omako lāmako jatukko[^299-2] paritto ti, parassa ce hi vacasā nihīno. Tumo sahā hoti nihīnapañño ti so pi ten'; eva saha[^299-3] hoti hīnapañño[^299-1] nihīnapañño omakapañño lāmakapañño jatukkapañño parittapañño ti[^299-4], tumo[^299-5] sahā hoti nihīnapañño. Athavā sayaṃ vedagū hoti dhīro ti. [^299-6]Athavā sayam vedagū hoti[^299-6] dhīro paṇḍito paññavā buddhimā ṇāṇī vibhāvī medhāvī ti, athavā sayaṃ vedagū hoti dhīro. Na koci bālo samaṇesu atthī ti samaṇesu na koci bālo hīno nihīno omako lāmako jatukko paritto atthi; sabb' eva aggapaññā[^299-7] seṭṭhapaññā viseṭṭhapaññā pāmokkhapaññā[^299-8] uttamapaññā pavarapaññā ti, na koci bālo samaṇesu atthi. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Parassa ce hi vacasā nihīno, tumo[^299-9] [^299-10]sahā hoti[^299-10] nihīnapañño; athavā sayaṃ vedagū hoti dhīro, na koci bālo samaṇesu atthī ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,12.14(=891): Aññaṃ ito yābhivadanti dhammaṃ, aparaddhā suddhim [^299-11]akevalī te[^299-11], evam pi[^299-12] titthyā puthuso vadanti sandiṭṭhirāgena [^299-13]hi tyābhirattā[^299-13].  || Nidd_I.12:14 ||

Aññaṃ ito yābhivadanti dhammaṃ aparaddhā suddhim akevalī te ti ito aññaṃ dhammaṃ [^299-14]diṭṭhiṃ paṭipadaṃ[^299-14]


<!-- PTS Page 300 -->
<a id="page-300"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 300]*

maggaṃ ye abhivadanti, te suddhimaggaṃ visuddhimaggaṃ parisuddhimaggaṃ vodātamaggaṃ pariyodātamaggaṃ viraddhā aparaddhā khalitā gaḷitā[^300-1] [^300-2]aññāya aparaddhā[^300-2] akevalī te, asamattā te[^300-3], aparipuṇṇā te, hīnā nihīnā omakā lāmakā jatukkā parittā ti, aññaṃ ito yābhivadanti dhammaṃ aparaddhā suddhim akevalī te. Evam pi titthyā puthuso vadantī ti. Titthaṃ vuccati diṭṭhigataṃ; titthyā[^300-1] [^300-4]vuccanti diṭṭhigatikā; puthudiṭṭhiyā[^300-4] puthudiṭṭhigatāni vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, evam pi titthyā puthuso vadanti. Sandiṭṭhirāgena hi tyābhirattā ti sakāya diṭṭhiyā diṭṭhirāgena[^300-5] rattā abhirattā ti, sandiṭṭhirāgena hi tyābhirattā. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Aññaṃ ito yābhivadanti dhammaṃ, aparaddhā suddhim akevalī te, evaṃ pi titthyā puthuso vadanti sandiṭṭhirāgena hi tyābhirattā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,12.15(=892): Idh'; eva suddhiṃ[^300-6] iti vādiyanti, nāññesu dhammesu visuddhim āhu[^300-7], evam pi titthyā puthuso niviṭṭhā sakāyane tattha daḷhaṃ vadānā.  || Nidd_I.12:15 ||

Idh'; evā suddhiṃ iti vādiyantī ti idha suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharanti: sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti idha suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharanti: asassato loko . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti idha suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ vadanti kathenti


<!-- PTS Page 301 -->
<a id="page-301"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 301]*

bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, idh'; eva suddhiṃ iti vādiyanti. Nāññesu dhammesu visuddhim āhū ti attano satthāraṃ dhammakkhānaṃ gaṇaṃ [^301-1]diṭṭhiṃ paṭipadaṃ[^301-1] maggaṃ ṭhapetvā sabbe paravāde khipanti ukkhipanti parikkhipanti: so satthā na sabbaññū, dhammo na svākkhāto, gaṇo na supaṭipanno, diṭṭhi na bhaddikā, paṭipadā na supaññattā, maggo na niyyāniko; n'; atth'; ettha suddhi vā visuddhi vā parisuddhi vā mutti vā vimutti vā parimutti vā, n'; atth'; ettha sujjhanti vā visujjhanti vā parisujjhanti vā muccanti vā vimuccanti vā parimuccanti vā, hīnā nihīnā omakā lāmakā jatukkā[^301-2] parittā ti, evam āhaṃsu, evaṃ kathenti, evaṃ bhaṇanti, evaṃ dīpayanti, evaṃ voharantī ti, nāññesu dhammesu visuddhim āhu. Evam pi titthyā[^301-3] puthuso niviṭṭhā ti. [^301-4]Titthaṃ vuccati diṭṭhigataṃ[^301-4]; titthyā[^301-5] vuccanti diṭṭhigatikā; puthudiṭṭhiyā puthudiṭṭhigatesu niviṭṭhā patiṭṭhitā allīnā upagatā ajjhositā adhimuttā ti, evam pi titthyā[^301-3] puthuso niviṭṭhā. Sakāyane tattha daḷhaṃ vadānā ti. Dhammo sakāyanaṃ, diṭṭhi sakāyanaṃ, paṭipadā sakāyanaṃ, maggo sakāyanaṃ[^301-6]; sakāyane daḷhavādā thiravādā balikavādā avatthitavādā ti, sakāyane tattha daḷhaṃ vadānā. Ten' āha Bhagavā:

Idh'; eva suddhiṃ iti vādiyanti, nāññesu dhammesu visuddhim āhu, evam pi titthyā puthuso niviṭṭhā sakāyane tattha daḷhaṃ vadānā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,12.16(=893): Sakāyane vā[^301-7] pi daḷhaṃ vadāno [^301-8]kam ettha[^301-8] [^301-9]bālo ti[^301-9] paraṃ daheyya? [^301-10]sayam eva[^301-10] so medhagaṃ āvaheyya paraṃ vadaṃ[^301-11] bālam asuddhidhammaṃ.  || Nidd_I.12:16 ||


<!-- PTS Page 302 -->
<a id="page-302"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 302]*

Sakāyane vā pi daḷhaṃ vadāno ti. Dhammo sakāyanaṃ, diṭṭhi sakāyanaṃ, paṭipadā sakāyanaṃ, maggo sakāyanaṃ[^302-1]; sakāyane daḷhavādo thiravādo balikavādo avatthitavādo ti, sakāyane vā pi daḷhaṃ vadāno. [^302-2]Kam ettha[^302-2] bālo ti paraṃ daheyyā? ti. Etthā[^302-3] ti sakāya diṭṭhiyā, sakāya khantiyā, sakāya ruciyā, sakāya laddhiyā, paraṃ bālato hīnato nihīnato omakato lāmakato jatukkato[^302-4] parittato kaṃ daheyya, kaṃ passeyya, kaṃ dakkheyya, kam olokeyya, kaṃ nijjhayeyya, kaṃ upaparikkheyyā ti, kam ettha bālo ti paraṃ daheyya? [^302-5]Sayam eva so[^302-5] medhagaṃ[^302-6] āvaheyya paraṃ vadaṃ[^302-7] bālam asuddhidhamman ti paro bālo hīno nihīno omako lāmako jatukko paritto asuddhidhammo avisuddhidhammo aparisuddhidhammo avodātadhammo ti evaṃ vadanto, evaṃ kathento, evaṃ bhaṇanto, evaṃ dīpayanto, evaṃ voharanto, sayam eva kalahaṃ bhaṇḍanaṃ viggahaṃ[^302-8] vivādaṃ medhagaṃ[^302-9] āvaheyya samāvaheyya[^302-8] āhareyya samāhareyya ākaḍḍheyya samākaḍḍheyya gaṇheyya parāmaseyya abhiniviseyyā ti, [^302-10]sayam eva[^302-10] so medhagaṃ[^302-9] āvaheyya paraṃ vadaṃ bālaṃ asuddhidhammaṃ. Ten' āha Bhagavā:

Sakāyane vā pi daḷhaṃ vadāno kam ettha bālo ti paraṃ daheyya? [^302-10]sayam eva[^302-10] so medhagaṃ āvaheyya paraṃ vadaṃ[^302-7] bālam asuddhidhamman ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,12.17(=894): Vinicchaye ṭhatvā sayaṃ pamāya uddhaṃ so[^302-11] lokasmiṃ[^302-12] vivādam eti, hitvāna sabbāni[^302-13] vinicchayāni na medhagaṃ kurute[^302-14] jantu loke ti[^302-15].  || Nidd_I.12:17 ||


<!-- PTS Page 303 -->
<a id="page-303"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 303]*

Vinicchaye ṭhatvā sayaṃ pamāyā ti. Vinicchayā vuccanti dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni. Vinicchaye[^303-1] vinicchitadiṭṭhiyā[^303-2] ṭhatvā patiṭṭhahitvā gaṇhitvā parāmasitvā abhinivisitvā ti, vinicchaye ṭhatvā. Sayaṃ pamāyā ti sayaṃ pamāya paminitvā, ayaṃ satthā sabbaññū ti sayaṃ pamāya paminitvā; ayaṃ dhammo svākkhāto, ayaṃ gaṇo supaṭipanno, ayaṃ diṭṭhi bhaddikā, ayaṃ paṭipadā supaññattā, ayaṃ maggo niyyāniko ti sayaṃ pamāya paminitvā ti, vinicchaye ṭhatvā sayaṃ pamāya. Uddhaṃ so[^303-3] lokasmiṃ vivādam etī ti. Uddhaṃ[^303-4] vuccati anāgataṃ; attano vādaṃ uddhaṃ ṭhapetvā sayam eva kalahaṃ bhaṇḍanaṃ viggahaṃ vivādaṃ medhagaṃ eti upeti upagacchati gaṇhāti parāmasati abhinivisatī ti, evam pi uddhaṃ so[^303-3] lokasmiṃ vivādam eti. Athavā aññena uddhaṃ vādena saddhiṃ kalahaṃ karoti, bhaṇḍanaṃ karoti, [^303-5]viggahaṃ karoti[^303-5], vivādaṃ karoti, medhagaṃ karoti: na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi . . . pe . . . nibbedhehi vā sace pahosī ti, evam pi uddhaṃ so[^303-3] lokasmiṃ vivādam eti. Hitvāna sabbāni[^303-6] vinicchayānī ti Vinicchayā vuccanti dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni; sabbā[^303-7] vinicchitadiṭṭhiyo[^303-2] hitvā cajjitvā[^303-8] pariccajitvā[^303-9] jahitvā[^303-1] pajahitvā vinoditvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gametvā ti, hitvāna sabbāni vinicchayāni. Na medhagaṃ kurute[^303-10] jantu loke ti na kalahaṃ karoti, na bhaṇḍanaṃ karoti, na viggahaṃ karoti, na vivādaṃ karoti, na medhagaṃ karoti. Vuttaṃ h'; etaṃ Bhagavatā: *Evaṃ vimuttacitto kho Aggivessana bhikkhu na kenaci saṃvadati, na kenaci vivadati. Yañ ca loke vuttaṃ tena ca voharati aparāmasan ti. Jantū ti satto naro mānavo


<!-- PTS Page 304 -->
<a id="page-304"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 304]*

poso puggalo jīvo jagū[^304-1] jantu indagū manujo. Loke ti apāyaloke [^304-2]manussaloke devaloke khandhaloke dhātuloke[^304-2] āyatanaloke ti, na medhagaṃ kurute[^304-3] jantu loke ti. Ten' āha Bhagavā:

Vinicchaye ṭhatvā sayaṃ pamāya uddhaṃ so[^304-4] lokasmiṃ vivādam eti hitvāna sabbāni vinicchayāni na medhagaṃ kurute[^304-3] jantu loke ti.

DVĀDASAMO[^304-5] CŪḶAVIYŪHASUTTANIDDESO NIṬṬHITO[^304-6].


<!-- PTS Page 305 -->
<a id="page-305"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 305]*

TERASAMO[^305-1] MAHĀVIYŪHASUTTANIDDESO[^305-2].

Sn_IV,13.1(=895): Ye kec'; ime diṭṭhiparibbasānā idam eva saccan ti pavādiyanti[^305-3], sabb'; eva te nindam anvānayanti atho pasaṃsam pi labhanti tattha.  || Nidd_I.13:1 ||

Ye kec'; ime diṭṭhiparibbasānā ti. Ye kecī ti sabbena sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbaṃ asesaṃ nissesaṃ pariyādāyavacanam[^305-4] etaṃ, ye kecī ti. Diṭṭhiparibbasānā ti. Sant'; eke[^305-5] samaṇabrāhmaṇā diṭṭhigatikā. Te dvāsaṭṭhiyā diṭṭhigatānaṃ aññataraññataraṃ diṭṭhigataṃ gahetvā uggahetvā gaṇhitvā parāmasitvā abhinivisitvā sakāya sakāya diṭṭhiyā vasanti saṃvasanti āvasanti parivasanti; yathā āgārikā vā gharesu vasanti, sāpattikā vā āpattīsu vasanti, sakilesā[^305-6] vā kilesesu vasanti, evam eva sant'; eke [^305-7]samaṇabrāhmaṇā diṭṭhigatikā. Te dvāsaṭṭhiyā diṭṭhigatānaṃ aññataraññataraṃ diṭṭhigataṃ gahetvā uggahetvā gaṇhitvā parāmasitvā abhinivisitvā sakāya sakāya diṭṭhiyā vasanti saṃvasanti āvasanti[^305-7] parivasantī ti, ye kec'; ime diṭṭhiparibbasānā. Idam eva saccan ti pavādiyantī[^305-8] ti. Sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharanti. Asassato loko . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti, na na hoti tathāgato paraṃ maraṇā, idam eva


<!-- PTS Page 306 -->
<a id="page-306"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 306]*

saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, idam eva saccan ti pavādiyanti. Sabb'; eva te nindam anvānayantī ti sabb'; eva te samaṇabrāhmaṇā nindam eva anventi, garaham eva anventi[^306-1], akittim eva anventi, sabbe ninditā yeva honti, garahitā yeva honti, akittitā yeva hontī ti, sabb'; eva te nindam anvānayanti. Atho pasaṃsam pi labhanti tatthā ti. Tattha sakāya diṭṭhiyā, sakāya khantiyā, sakāya ruciyā, sakāya laddhiyā, pasaṃsaṃ thomanaṃ kittiṃ vaṇṇahārikaṃ labhanti paṭilabhanti [^306-2]adhigacchanti vindantī ti[^306-2], atho pasaṃsam pi labhanti tattha. Ten'; āha so nimmito:

Ye kec'; ime diṭṭhiparibbasānā idam eva saccan ti pavādiyanti[^306-3], sabb'; eva te nindam anvānayanti atho pasaṃsam pi labhanti tatthā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.2(=896): Appaṃ hi etaṃ [^306-4]na alaṃ[^306-4] samāya, duve[^306-5] vivādassa phalāni brūmi, evam[^306-6] pi disvā na vivādiyetha khemābhipassaṃ avivādabhummaṃ[^306-7].  || Nidd_I.13:2 ||

Appaṃ hi etaṃ [^306-8]na alaṃ[^306-8] samāyā ti. [^306-9]Appaṃ hi etaṃ ti[^306-9] appakaṃ etaṃ, omakaṃ[^306-10] etaṃ, thokaṃ[^306-11] etaṃ, lāmakaṃ etaṃ, jatukkaṃ etaṃ, parittakaṃ etan ti, appaṃ hi etaṃ. Na alaṃ samāyā ti nālaṃ rāgassa samāya, dosassa samāya, mohassa samāya, kodhassa upanāhassa makkhassa paḷāsassa issāya macchariyassa māyāya sāṭheyyassa, thambhassa sārambhassa mānassa atimānassa madassa pamādassa sabbakilesānaṃ sabbaduccaritānaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 307 -->
<a id="page-307"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 307]*

sabbadarathānaṃ sabbapariḷāhānaṃ sabbasantāpānaṃ sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārānaṃ samāya upasamāya vūpasamāya nibbānāya paṭinissaggāya paṭipassaddhiyā ti, [^307-1]appaṃ hi etaṃ[^307-1] na alaṃ samāya. Duve[^307-2] vivādassa phalāni brūmī ti diṭṭhikalahassa diṭṭhibhaṇḍanassa diṭṭhiviggahassa diṭṭhivivādassa diṭṭhimedhagassa dve phalāni honti. Jayaparājayo hoti, lābhālābho hoti, yasāyaso hoti, nindāpasaṃso hoti, sukhadukkhaṃ hoti, somanassadomanassaṃ hoti, iṭṭhāniṭṭhaṃ hoti, anunayapaṭighaṃ hoti, ugghātinigghāti hoti, anurodhavirodho hoti. Athavā taṃ kammaṃ nirayasaṃvattanikaṃ tiracchānayonikasaṃvattanikaṃ[^307-3] pittivisayikasaṃvattanikan[^307-4] ti brūmi ācikkhāmi desemi paññāpemi[^307-5] paṭṭhapemi vivarāmi vibhajāmi uttānīkaromi pakāsemī ti, duve vivādassa phalāni brūmi. Etam pi disvā na vivādiyethā[^307-6] ti. [^307-7]Etam pi disvā ti[^307-7] etaṃ ādīnavaṃ disvā passitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā diṭṭhikalahesu diṭṭhibhaṇḍanesu diṭṭhiviggahesu diṭṭhivivādesu diṭṭhimedhagesū ti, etam pi disvā. Na vivādiyethā ti na kalahaṃ kareyya, na bhaṇḍanaṃ kareyya, na viggahaṃ kareyya, na vivādaṃ kareyya, na medhagaṃ kareyya, [^307-8]kalahabhaṇḍanaviggahavivādamedhagaṃ[^307-8] pajaheyya vinodeyya byantīkareyya anabhāvaṃ gameyya, [^307-9]kalahabhaṇḍanaviggahavivādamedhagā[^307-9] ārato assa[^307-10], virato paṭivirato[^307-11] nikkhanto paṭinissaṭṭho[^307-12] vippamutto visaṃyutto vimariyādikatena[^307-13] cetasā vihareyyā ti, etam pi disvā na vivādiyetha[^307-14].


<!-- PTS Page 308 -->
<a id="page-308"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 308]*

Khemābhipassaṃ avivādabhumman ti. Avivādabhummaṃ vuccati amatanibbānaṃ, yo so sabbasaṃkhārasamatho sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggo taṇhakkhayo virāgo nirodho nibbānaṃ. Etaṃ avivādabhummaṃ khemato tāṇato leṇato saraṇato abhayato accutato amatato nibbānato passanto dakkhanto olokento nijjhāyanto upaparikkhanto ti, khemābhipassaṃ avivādabhūmmaṃ. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Appaṃ hi etaṃ na alaṃ samāya, duve vivādassa phalāni brūmi, etam pi disvā na vivādiyetha[^308-1] khemābhipassaṃ avivādabhumman ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.3(=897): Yā kāc'; imā sammutiyo[^308-2] puthujjā, sabbā va etā na upeti vidvā, anūpayo so upayaṃ kim eyya diṭṭhe sute khantim akubbamāno?  || Nidd_I.13:3 ||

Yā kāc'; imā sammutiyo puthujjā ti. Yā kācī ti sabbena sabbaṃ sabbatthā sabbaṃ asesaṃ nissesaṃ pariyādāyavacanam[^308-3] etaṃ, yā kācī ti. Sammutiyo ti sammutiyo vuccanti dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni, [^308-4]diṭṭhisammutiyo.[^308-4] Puthujjā ti puthujjanehi janitā [^308-5]vā tā[^308-5] sammutiyo ti puthujjā, puthunānājanehi janitā vā tā[^308-6] sammutiyo ti puthujjā ti, yā kāc' imā sammutiyo puthujjā. Sabbā va etā na upeti vidvā ti vidvā[^308-7] vijjāgato ñāṇī vibhāvī medhāvī sabbā va etā diṭṭhisammutiyo n'; eti, na upeti, na upagacchati, na gaṇhātī, na parāmasati, nābhinivisatī ti, sabbā va etā na upeti vidvā. Anūpayo so upayaṃ kim eyyā? ti. Upayo ti dve upayā, taṇhūpayo ca diṭṭhūpayo ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhūpayo . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhūpayo. Tassa taṇhūpayo pahīno, diṭṭhupayo paṭinissaṭṭho, taṇhūpayassa pahīnattā, diṭṭhupayassa paṭinissaṭṭhattā, anupayo puggalo kiṃ rūpaṃ upeyya upagaccheyya gaṇheyya parāmaseyya


<!-- PTS Page 309 -->
<a id="page-309"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 309]*

abhiniviseyya: attā me ti? Kiṃ vedanaṃ, kiṃ saññaṃ, kiṃ saṃkhāre, kiṃ viññāṇaṃ, kiṃ gatiṃ, kiṃ uppattiṃ, kiṃ paṭisandhiṃ, kiṃ bhavaṃ, kiṃ saṃsāraṃ, kiṃ vaṭṭaṃ upeyya upagaccheyya gaṇheyya parāmaseyya abhiniviseyyā? ti, anūpayo so upayaṃ kim eyya? Diṭṭhe sute [^309-1]khantim akubbamāno[^309-1] ti diṭṭhe vā diṭṭhasuddhiyā vā, sute vā sutasuddhiyā vā, mute vā mutasuddhiyā vā, khantiṃ[^309-2] akubbamāno[^309-3], chandaṃ akubbamāno, pemaṃ akubbamāno, rāgaṃ akubbamāno[^309-3] ajanayamāno asañjanayamāno anibbattayamāno nābhinibbattayamāno[^309-4] ti, diṭṭhe sute khantim akubbamāno. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Yā kāc'; imā sammutiyo puthujjā, sabbā va etā na upeti vidvā, anūpayo so upayaṃ kim eyya diṭṭhe sute khantim akubbamāno? ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.4(=898): Sīluttamā saṃyamen'; āhu suddhiṃ vattaṃ[^309-5] samādāya upaṭṭhitāse idh'; eva sikkhema, ath'; assa suddhiṃ bhavūpanītā kusalā vadānā.  || Nidd_I.13:4 ||

Sīluttamā saṃyamen'; āhu suddhin ti. Sant'; eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sīluttamavādā. Te sīlamattena[^309-6] saṃyamamattena[^309-7] saṃvaramattena avītikkamamattena suddhiṃ visuddhim parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ āhu[^309-8] vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharanti. *Samaṇamuṇḍikāputto evam āha: Catūhi kho ahaṃ thapati[^309-9] dhammehi samannāgataṃ purisapuggalaṃ paññāpemi sampannakusalaṃ paramakusalaṃ uttamapatti-


<!-- PTS Page 310 -->
<a id="page-310"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 310]*

pattaṃ samaṇaṃ ayojjhaṃ. Katamehi catūhi? Idha thapati[^310-1] na kāyena pāpakammaṃ karoti, na pāpikaṃ[^310-2] vācaṃ bhāsati, na pāpakaṃ saṃkappaṃ saṃkappati[^310-3], na pāpakaṃ ājīvaṃ ājīvati[^310-4]. Imehi kho ahaṃ thapati[^310-1] catūhi dhammehi samannāgataṃ purisapuggalaṃ paññāpemi sampannakusalaṃ paramakusalaṃ uttamapattipattaṃ samaṇaṃ ayojjhaṃ. Evam eva[^310-5] sant'; eke {samaṇabrāhmaṇā} sīluttamavādā; te sīlamattena saṃyamamattena[^310-6] saṃvaramattena avitikkamamattena suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ āhu vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, sīluttamā saṃyamen'; āhu suddhiṃ. Vattaṃ samādāya upaṭṭhitāse ti. Vattan ti hatthivattaṃ vā assavattaṃ vā govattaṃ vā ajavattaṃ vā kukkuravattaṃ vā kākavattaṃ vā vāsudevavattaṃ vā puṇṇabhaddavattaṃ vā maṇibhaddavattaṃ vā aggivattaṃ vā nāgavattaṃ vā supaṇṇavattaṃ vā yakkhavattaṃ vā asuravattaṃ vā gandhabbavattaṃ vā mahārājavattaṃ vā candavattaṃ vā suriyavattaṃ vā indavattaṃ vā brahmavattaṃ vā devavattaṃ vā disāvattaṃ[^310-7] vā ādāya samādāya ādayitvā[^310-8] gaṇhitvā parāmasitvā abhinivisitvā upaṭṭhitā paccupaṭṭhitā allīnā upagatā ajjhositā adhimuttā ti, vattaṃ samādāya upaṭṭhitāse. Idh'; eva sikkhema ath'; assa suddhin ti. Idhā ti sakāya diṭṭiyā, sakāya khantiyā, sakāya ruciyā, sakāya laddhiyā[^310-9] sikkhema[^310-10] ācarema samācarema samādāya vattemā ti, idh'; eva sikkhema. Ath'; assa suddhin ti; [^310-11]athavā 'ssa[^310-11] suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttin ti, idh'; eva sikkhema ath'; assa suddhiṃ. Bhavūpanītā kusalā vadānā ti. Bhavūpanītā ti bhavūpanītā bhavūpagatā bhava-ajjhositā bhavādhimuttā ti,


<!-- PTS Page 311 -->
<a id="page-311"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 311]*

bhavūpanītā. Kusalā vadānā ti kusalavādā paṇḍitavādā dhīravādā[^311-1] ñānavādā[^311-2] hetuvādā lakkhaṇavādā kāraṇavādā ṭhānavādā sakāya laddhiyā ti, bhavūpanītā kusalā vadānā. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Sīluttamā saṃyamen'; āhu suddhiṃ vattaṃ samādāya upaṭṭhitāse idh'; eva sikkhema, ath'; assa suddhiṃ bhavūpanītā kusalā vadānā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.5(=899): Sace cuto sīlavatāto[^311-3] hoti, [^311-4]sa vedhatī[^311-4] kamma[^311-5] virādhayitvā, [^311-6]sa jappatī[^311-6] [^311-7]patthayatī ca[^311-7] suddhiṃ satthā va hīno pavasaṃ gharamhā.  || Nidd_I.13:5 ||

Sace cuto sīlavatāto[^311-3] hotī ti. Dvīhi kāraṇehi sīlabbatato[^311-8] cavati: paravicchindanāya vā cavati, anabhisambhuṇanto vā cavati. Kathaṃ paravicchindanāya cavati? Paro vicchindati: so satthā na sabbaññū, dhammo na svākkhāto, gaṇo na supaṭipanno, diṭṭhi na bhaddikā, paṭipadā na supaññattā, maggo na niyyāniko, n'; atth'; ettha suddhi vā [^311-9]visuddhi vā[^311-9] parisuddhi vā mutti vā vimutti vā parimutti vā, n' atth'; ettha sujjhanti vā visujjhanti vā parisujjhanti vā muccanti[^311-10] vā vimuccanti vā parimuccanti vā, hīnā nihīnā omakā lāmakā jatukkā parittā ti, evaṃ paro vicchindati; evaṃ vicchindayamāno[^311-11] satthārā cavati, dhammakkhānā cavati, gaṇā cavati, diṭṭhiyā cavati, paṭipadāya cavati, maggato cavati, evaṃ paravicchindanāya cavati.


<!-- PTS Page 312 -->
<a id="page-312"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 312]*

Kathaṃ anabhisambhuṇanto cavati? Sīlaṃ anabhisambhuṇanto sīlato cavati, vattaṃ anabhisambhuṇanto vattato cavati, sīlabbataṃ anabhisambhuṇanto sīlabbatato cavati, evaṃ anabhisambhuṇanto cavatī ti, sace cuto sīlavatāto hoti. [^312-1]Sa vedhatī[^312-1] kamma virādhayitvā ti. Sa vedhatī ti: sīlaṃ vā vattaṃ vā sīlabbataṃ vā viraddhaṃ mayā, aparaddhaṃ mayā, khalitaṃ mayā, gaḷitaṃ mayā, aññāya aparaddho ahan ti vedhati pavedhati[^312-2] saṃpavedhatī ti, sa vedhatī kamma virādhayitvā ti. Puññābhisaṃkhāraṃ vā [^312-3]apuññābhisaṃkhāraṃ vā[^312-3] āneñjābhisaṃkhāraṃ vā viraddhaṃ mayā, aparaddhaṃ mayā, khalitaṃ mayā, gaḷitaṃ mayā, aññāya aparaddho ahan ti vedhati[^312-4] pavedhati sampavedhatī ti, sa vedhatī kamma virādhayitvā. [^312-5]Sa jappatī[^312-5] patthayatī[^312-6] ca suddhin ti. Jappatī[^312-7] ti sīlaṃ vā jappati[^312-8], vattaṃ vā jappati, sīlabbataṃ vā jappati pajappati abhijappatī ti, sa jappati. Patthayatī ca suddhin ti sīlasuddhiṃ vā pattheti, vattasuddhiṃ vā pattheti, sīlabbatasuddhiṃ vā pattheti piheti abhijappatī ti, sa jappatī patthayatī ca suddhiṃ. Satthā va hīno pavasaṃ gharamhā ti yathā puriso gharato nikkhanto, satthena[^312-9] saha[^312-10] vasanto, satthā ohīno, taṃ vā satthaṃ anubandhati sakaṃ vā gharaṃ paccāgacchati[^312-11]; evam eva so diṭṭhigatiko taṃ vā satthāraṃ gaṇhāti, [^312-12]aññaṃ vā satthāraṃ gaṇhāti[^312-12]; taṃ vā dhammakkhānaṃ gaṇhāti, [^312-12]aññaṃ vā dhammakkhānaṃ gaṇhāti[^312-12]; taṃ vā gaṇaṃ gaṇhāti,[^312-3]aññaṃ vā gaṇaṃ gaṇhāti[^312-3]; taṃ vā diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti, aññaṃ vā diṭṭhiṃ gaṇhāti; taṃ vā paṭipadaṃ gaṇhāti, aññaṃ vā paṭipadaṃ gaṇhāti; taṃ vā maggaṃ gaṇhāti, aññaṃ vā maggaṃ gaṇhāti parāmasati


<!-- PTS Page 313 -->
<a id="page-313"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 313]*

abhinivisatī ti, satthā va hīno pavasaṃ gharamhā. Ten' āha Bhagavā:

Sace cuto sīlavatāto hoti, sa vedhatī kamma virādhayitvā, sa jappatī patthayatī ca suddhiṃ satthā va hīno pavasaṃ gharamhā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.6(=900): Sīlabbataṃ vā pi pahāya sabbaṃ kammañ ca sāvajjanavajjam etaṃ suddhiṃ asuddhin ti apatthayāno virato care santim anuggahāya.  || Nidd_I.13:6 ||

Sīlabbataṃ vā pi pahāya sabban ti sabbā sīlasuddhiyo pahāya pajahitvā vinoditvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamitvā, sabbā vattasuddhiyo pahāya pajahitvā vinoditvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamitvā, sabbā sīlabbatasuddhiyo pahāya pajahitvā vinoditvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamitvā ti, sīlabbataṃ vā pi pahāya sabbaṃ. Kammañ ca sāvajjanavajjam etan ti. Sāvajjakammaṃ vuccati kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ, anavajjakammaṃ vuccati sukkaṃ sukkavipākaṃ. Sāvajjañ ca kammaṃ [^313-1]anavajjañ ca kammaṃ[^313-1] pahāya pajahitvā vinoditvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamitvā ti, kammañ ca sāvajjanavajjam etaṃ. [^313-2]Suddhiṃ asuddhin[^313-2] ti apatthayāno ti. [^313-1]Asuddhin ti[^313-1] asuddhiṃ patthenti, akusale dhamme patthenti, [^313-3]suddhiṃ [^313-4]patthenti, pañca kāmaguṇe patthenti, asuddhiṃ patthenti, akusale dhamme patthenti, pañca kāmaguṇe patthenti, suddhiṃ patthenti; dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni patthenti, asuddhiṃ patthenti, akusale dhamme patthenti[^313-3], pañca kāmaguṇe patthenti, dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni patthenti, suddhiṃ patthenti, tedhātuke kusale dhamme patthenti, asuddhiṃ patthenti, akusale dhamme patthenti, pañca kāmaguṇe patthenti, dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni patthenti, tedhātuke kusale dhamme patthenti, suddhiṃ patthenti, kalyāṇaputhujjanā[^313-5] niyāmāvakkantiṃ patthenti, sekkhā agga-


<!-- PTS Page 314 -->
<a id="page-314"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 314]*

dhammaṃ arahattaṃ patthenti, arahattappatte[^314-1] arahā n' eva akusale dhamme pattheti, na pi pañca kāmaguṇe pattheti, na pi dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni pattheti, na pi tedhātuke kusale dhamme pattheti, na pi niyāmāvakkantiṃ pattheti, na pi aggadhammaṃ arahattaṃ pattheti, patthanaṃ[^314-2] samatikkanto arahā vuddhiparihāniṃ[^314-3] vītivatto, so vuṭṭhavāso ciṇṇacaraṇo . . . pe[^314-4] . . . n'; atthi tassa punabbhavo ti, suddhiṃ asuddhin ti apatthayāno. Virato care santim anuggahāyā ti. Virato ti suddhiasuddhiyā ārato[^314-5] virato paṭivirato nikkhanto nissaṭṭho[^314-6] vippamutto visaṃyutto vimariyādikatena cetasā viharatī ti, virato. Care ti careyya vicareyya[^314-7] iriyeyya vatteyya pāleyya yapeyya yāpeyyā ti, virato care. Santim anuggahāyā ti. Santiyo vuccanti dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni, diṭṭhisantiyo agaṇhanto aparāmasanto anabhinivisanto ti, virato care santim anuggahāya. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Sīlabbataṃ vā pi pahāya sabbaṃ kammañ ca sāvajjanavajjam etaṃ [^314-8]suddhiṃ asuddhin[^314-8] ti apatthayāno virato care santim anuggahāyā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.7(=901): Tamūpanissāya[^314-9] jigucchitaṃ vā athavā pi [^314-10]diṭṭhaṃ va[^314-10] sutaṃ mutaṃ vā uddhaṃsarā suddhim anutthunanti[^314-11] avītatanhāse bhavābhavesu.  || Nidd_I.13:7 ||


<!-- PTS Page 315 -->
<a id="page-315"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 315]*

Tamūpanissāya jigucchitaṃ vā ti. Sant'; eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā tapojigucchavādā tapojigucchasārā[^315-1] [^315-2]tapojigucchaṃ nissitā[^315-2] sannissitā[^315-3] allīnā upagatā ajjhositā adhimuttā ti, tamūpanissāya jigucchitaṃ vā. Athavā pi diṭṭhaṃ va sutaṃ mutaṃ va ti diṭṭhaṃ vā diṭṭhasuddhiṃ vā sutaṃ vā sutasuddhiṃ vā mutaṃ vā mutasuddhiṃ vā nissāya upanissāya gaṇhitvā parāmasitvā abhinivisitvā ti, athavā pi diṭṭhaṃ va sutaṃ mutaṃ vā. Uddhaṃsarā suddhim anutthunantī ti. Sant'; eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhaṃsarāvādā. Katame te[^315-4] samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhaṃsarāvādā? Ye te samaṇabrāhmaṇā accantasuddhikā saṃsārasuddhikā akiriyadiṭṭhikā[^315-5] sassatavādā, ime te[^315-6] samaṇabrāhmaṇā uddhaṃsarāvādā, te saṃsārena[^315-7] suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ[^315-8] anutthunanti[^315-9] vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, uddhaṃsarā suddhim anutthunanti. Avītataṇhāse bhavābhavesū ti. Taṇhā ti rūpataṇhā saddataṇhā gandhataṇhā rasataṇhā phoṭṭhabbataṇhā dhammataṇhā. Bhavābhavesū ti bhavābhave kammabhave punabbhave[^315-7], kāmabhave kammabhave, kāmabhave punabbhave, rūpabhave kammabhave, rūpabhave punabbhave, arūpabhave kammabhave, arūpabhave punabbhave punappunaṃ bhave punappunaṃ gatiyā punappunaṃ upapattiyā punappunaṃ paṭisandhiyā punappunaṃ attathāvābhinibbattiyā. [^315-10]Avītataṇhāse ti[^315-10] avītataṇhā[^315-11] avigatataṇhā[^315-12] acattataṇhā avantataṇhā[^315-8] amuttataṇhā appahīnataṇhā[^315-12] appaṭinissaṭṭhataṇhā ti, avītataṇhāse bhavābhavesu. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:


<!-- PTS Page 316 -->
<a id="page-316"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 316]*

Tamūpanissāya jigucchitaṃ vā athavā pi diṭṭhaṃ va sutaṃ mutaṃ vā uddhaṃsarā suddhim anutthunanti avītataṇhāse bhavābhavesū ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.8(=902): Patthayamānassa hi jappitāni saṃvedhitaṃ[^316-1] vā[^316-2] pi pakappitesu: cutūpapāto idha yassa n'; atthi, sa kena vedheyya kuhiṃ pajappe[^316-3]?  || Nidd_I.13:8 ||

Patthayamānassa hi jappitānī ti. Patthanā vuccati taṇhā, yo rāgo sārāgo . . . pe . . . abhijjhā lobho akusalamūlaṃ. Patthayamānassā ti patthayamānassa icchamānassa[^316-4] sādiyamānassa pihayamānassa abhijappayamānassā ti, patthayamānassa hi[^316-5]. Jappitānī ti jappanā vuccati taṇhā, yo rāgo sārāgo . . . pe abhijjhā lobho akusalamūlan ti, patthayamānassa hi jappitāni. Saṃvedhitaṃ vā pi pakappitesū ti. Pakappanā ti dve pakappanā, taṇhāpakappanā ca, diṭṭhipakappanā ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhāpakappanā . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhipakappanā[^316-6]. Vatthu-acchedasaṃkino[^316-7] pi vedhenti, acchijjante pi vedhenti, acchinne pi vedhenti[^316-8]; vatthuvipariṇāmasaṃkino[^316-9] pi vedhenti, vipariṇāmante pi vedhenti, vipariṇate pi vedhenti pavedhenti sampavedhentī ti, saṃvedhitaṃ vā pi pakappitesu. Cutūpapāto idha yassa n'; atthī ti. Yassā ti arahato khīṇāsavassa[^316-10] āgamanaṃ gamanaṃ[^316-5] gamanāgamanaṃ kālaṃ gati bhavābhavo cuti ca upapatti ca nibbatti ca bhedo ca jātijarāmaraṇaṃ[^316-11] n'; atthi, na santi, na saṃvijjanti, n'; upalabbhanti, pahīnā samucchinnā vūpasantā paṭipassaddhā


<!-- PTS Page 317 -->
<a id="page-317"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 317]*

abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā ti, cutūpapāto idha yassa n'; atthi. Sa kena vedheyya [^317-1]kuhiṃ pajappe[^317-1]? ti, so[^317-2] kena rāgena vedheyya, kena dosena vedheyya, kena mohena vedheyya, kena mānena vedheyya, kāya diṭṭhiyā vedheyya, kena uddhaccena vedheyya, kāya vicikicchāya vedheyya, kehi anusayehi vedheyya ratto ti vā duṭṭho ti vā mūḷho ti vā vinibandho[^317-3] ti vā parāmaṭṭho ti vā vikkhepagato ti vā aniṭṭhaṅgato[^317-4] ti vā thāmagato ti vā? Te abhisaṃkhārā pahīnā; abhisaṃkhārānaṃ pahīnattā, gatiyā kena vedheyya pavedheyya[^317-5] nerayiko ti vā tiracchānayoniko ti vā pittivisayiko ti vā manusso ti vā devo ti vā rūpī ti vā arūpī ti vā saññī ti vā asaññī ti vā nevasaññīnāsaññī ti vā? So hetu n'; atthi, paccayo n'; atthi, kāraṇam n'; atthi, yena[^317-6] vedheyya pavedheyya sampavedheyyā ti, sa kena vedheyya? Kuhiṃ [^317-7]pajappe ti[^317-7] kimhi jappeyya, [^317-8]kattha jappeyya[^317-8] pajappeyya abhijappeyyā ti, sa kena vedheyya kuhiṃ pajappe? Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Patthayamānassa hi jappitāni saṃvedhitaṃ vā pi pakappitesu: cutūpapāto idha yassa n'; atthi, sa kena vedheyya kuhiṃ pajappe? ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.9(=903): Yam āhu dhammaṃ paraman ti eke, tam eva hīnan ti pan'; āhu aññe: sacco nu vādo katamo imesaṃ? sabb'; eva hīme kusalā vadānā.  || Nidd_I.13:9 ||

Yam āhu dhammaṃ paraman ti eke ti yaṃ dhammaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭipadaṃ maggaṃ eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā: idaṃ paramaṃ aggaṃ seṭṭhaṃ visetthaṃ pāmokkhaṃ uttamaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 318 -->
<a id="page-318"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 318]*

pavaran ti evam āhaṃsu, evaṃ kathenti, evaṃ bhaṇanti, evaṃ dīpayanti, evaṃ voharantī ti, yam āhu dhammaṃ paraman ti eke. Tam eva hīnan ti pan'; āhu aññe ti tam eva dhammaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭipadaṃ maggaṃ eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā: hīnaṃ etaṃ, nihīnaṃ etaṃ, omakaṃ etaṃ, lāmakaṃ etaṃ, jatukkaṃ etaṃ, parittakaṃ etan ti evam āhaṃsu, evaṃ kathenti, evaṃ bhaṇanti, evaṃ dīpayanti, evaṃ voharantī ti, tam eva hīnan ti pan'; āhu aññe. Sacco nu vādo katamo imesan? ti imesaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ vādo katamo sacco taccho tatho bhūto[^318-1] yāthāvo aviparitto ti, sacco nu vādo katamo imesaṃ? Sabb'; eva hīme kusalā vadānā ti [^318-2]sabb'; eva hīme[^318-2] samaṇabrāhmaṇā kusalavādā paṇḍitavādā dhīravādā[^318-3] ñāṇavādā[^318-4] hetuvādā lakkhaṇavādā kāraṇavādā ṭhānavādā sakāya laddhiyā ti, sabb'; eva hīme kusalā vadānā. Ten' āha Bhagavā[^318-5]:

Yam āhu dhammaṃ paraman ti eke, tam eva hīnan ti pan'; āhu aññe: sacco nu vādo katamo imesaṃ? Sabb'; eva hīme kusalā vadānā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.10(=904): Sakaṃ hi dhammaṃ paripuṇṇam āhu aññassa dhammaṃ pana hīnam āhu, evam pi viggayha vivādiyanti[^318-6] sakaṃ sakaṃ sammutim[^318-7] āhu saccaṃ.  || Nidd_I.13:10 ||

Sakaṃ hi dhammaṃ paripuṇṇam āhū ti sakaṃ hi[^318-8] dhammaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭipadaṃ maggaṃ eke[^318-9] samaṇabrāhmaṇā: idaṃ samattaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ anoman ti evam āhaṃsu [^318-10]evaṃ kathenti, evaṃ bhaṇanti, evaṃ dīpayanti[^318-10], evaṃ voharantī ti, sakaṃ hi dhammaṃ paripuṇṇam āhu.


<!-- PTS Page 319 -->
<a id="page-319"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 319]*

Aññassa dhammaṃ pana hīnam āhū ti aññassa dhammaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭipadaṃ maggaṃ eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā: hīnaṃ etaṃ, nihīnaṃ etaṃ, omakaṃ etaṃ, lāmakaṃ etaṃ, jatukkaṃ etaṃ, parittakaṃ etan ti evam āhaṃsu, evaṃ kathenti evaṃ bhaṇanti, evaṃ dīpayanti, evaṃ voharantī ti, aññassa dhammaṃ pana hīnam āhu. Evam pi viggayha vivādiyantī ti evaṃ gahetvā[^319-1] gaṇhitvā parāmasitvā abhinivisitvā vivādiyanti, kalahaṃ karonti, bhaṇḍanaṃ karonti, viggahaṃ karonti, vivādaṃ karonti, medhagaṃ karonti: na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi . . . pe . . . nibbedhehi vā sace pahosī ti, evam pi viggayha vivādiyanti. Sakaṃ sakaṃ sammutim āhu saccan ti sassato loko[^319-2] . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti, na na hoti tathāgato paraṃ maraṇā, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti, sakaṃ sakaṃ sammutim āhu saccaṃ. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Sakaṃ hi dhammaṃ paripuṇṇam āhu aññassa dhammaṃ pana hīnam āhu, evam pi viggayha vivādiyanti[^319-3] sakaṃ sakaṃ sammutim āhu saccan ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.11(=905): Parassa ce vambhayitena hīno, na koci dhammesu visesi assa, puthū hi aññassa vadanti dhammaṃ nihīnato, samhi dalhaṃ vadānā.  || Nidd_I.13:11 ||

Parassa ce vambhayitena hīno ti parassa ce[^319-4] vambhayitakāraṇā ninditakāraṇā[^319-5] garahitakāraṇā upavaditakāraṇā paro bālo hoti hīno nihīno omako lāmako jatukko paritto ti, parassa ce vambhayitena hīno. Na koci dhammesu visesi assā ti dhammesu[^319-6] na koci aggo seṭṭho viseṭṭho pāmokkho uttamo pavaro assā ti, na koci dhammesu visesi assa.


<!-- PTS Page 320 -->
<a id="page-320"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 320]*

Puthū hi aññassa vadanti dhammaṃ nihīnato ti bahukā[^320-1] pi bahunnaṃ[^320-2] dhammaṃ vadanti upavadanti nindanti garahanti hīnato nihīnato omakato lāmakato jatukkato parittato; bahukā pi ekassa dhammaṃ vadanti upavadanti nindanti garahanti hīnato nihīnato omakato lāmakato jatukkato parittato; eko pi bahunnaṃ[^320-3] dhammaṃ vadati upavadati nindati garahati hīnato nihīnato omakato lāmakato jatukkato parittato; eko pi ekassa dhammaṃ vadati upavadati nindati garahati hīnato nihīnato omakato lāmakato jatukkato parittato ti, puthū hi aññassa vadanti dhammaṃ nihīnato[^320-4]. Samhi daḷhaṃ vadānā ti. Dhammo sakāyanaṃ, diṭṭhi sakāyanaṃ, paṭipadā sakāyanaṃ, maggo sakāyanaṃ; sakāyane daḷhavādā thiravādā balikavādā avatthitavādā ti[^320-5], samhi daḷhaṃ vadānā. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Parassa ce vambhayitena hīno, na koci dhammesu visesi assa, puthū hi aññassa vadanti dhammaṃ nihīnato, samhi daḷhaṃ vadānā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.12(=906): Sadhammapūjā ca panā tath'; eva: yathā pasaṃsanti sakāyanāni, [^320-6]sabbe pavādā[^320-6] tathivā bhaveyyuṃ, suddhī hi[^320-7] nesaṃ paccattam eva.  || Nidd_I.13:12 ||

Sadhammapūjā ca panā tath'; evā ti. Katamā sadhammapūjā? Sakaṃ satthāraṃ sakkaroti[^320-8] garukaroti[^320-9] māneti pūjeti: ayaṃ satthā sabbaññū ti; ayaṃ sadhammapūjā. Sakaṃ dhammakkhānaṃ, sakaṃ gaṇaṃ, sakaṃ diṭṭhiṃ, sakaṃ paṭipadaṃ, sakaṃ maggaṃ sakkaroti garukaroti[^320-10] māneti pūjeti: ayaṃ maggo niyyāniko ti; ayaṃ


<!-- PTS Page 321 -->
<a id="page-321"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 321]*

sadhammapūjā. [^321-1]Sadhammapūjā[^321-2] ca panā tath'; evā ti[^321-1] sadhammapūjā tathā tacchā bhūtā yāthāvā aviparittā ti, sadhammapūjā ca panā tath'; eva. Yathā pasaṃsanti sakāyanānī ti. Dhammo sakāyanaṃ, diṭṭhi sakāyanaṃ, paṭipadā sakāyanaṃ, maggo sakāyanaṃ; sakāyanāni pasaṃsanti thomenti kittenti vaṇṇentī ti, yathā pasaṃsanti sakāyanāni. Sabbe pavādā tathivā bhaveyyun ti sabbe pavādā tathā tacchā bhūtā yāthāvā aviparittā bhaveyyun ti, sabbe pavādā tathivā bhaveyyuṇ. Suddhī hi nesaṃ paccattam evā ti paccattam eva tesaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ suddhi visuddhi parisuddhi mutti vimutti parimuttī ti, suddhī hi nesaṃ paccattam eva. Ten' āha Bhagavā:

Sadhammapūjā ca panā tath'; eva: yathā pasaṃsanti sakāyanāni, sabbe pavādā tathivā bhaveyyuṃ, suddhī hi nesaṃ paccattam evā ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.13(=907): Na brāhmaṇassa paraneyyam atthi dhammesu niccheyya samuggahītaṃ, tasmā vivādāni upātivatto, na hi seṭṭhato passati dhammam aññaṃ.  || Nidd_I.13:13 ||

Na brāhmaṇassa paraneyyam atthī ti. Nā ti paṭikkhepo. Brāhmaṇo ti sattannaṃ dhammānaṃ bāhitattā brāhmaṇo . . . pe . . . anissito[^321-3] tādi pavuccati[^321-4] brahmā. Na brāhmaṇassa paraneyyam atthī ti brāhmaṇassa paraneyyatā n'; atthi; brāhmaṇo na paraneyyo, na parapattiyo, na parapaccayo[^321-5], na parapaṭibaddhagu[^321-6] jānāti passati, asammūḷho sampajāno patissato: sabbe saṃkhārā aniccā ti brāhmaṇassa paraneyyatā n'; atthi; brāhmaṇo na paraneyyo, na parapattiyo, na parapaccayo, na parapaṭibaddhagu[^321-7]


<!-- PTS Page 322 -->
<a id="page-322"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 322]*

jānāti passati, asammūḷho sampajāno patissato: sabbe saṃkhārā dukkhā ti . . . pe . . . yaṃ kiñci samudayadhammaṃ sabban taṃ nirodhadhamman ti brāhmaṇassa paraneyyatā[^322-1] n'; atthi; brāhmaṇo na paraneyyo, na parapattiyo, na parapaccayo, na parapaṭibaddhagu[^322-2] jānāti passati, asammūḷho sampajāno patissato ti, na brāhmaṇassa [^322-3]paraneyyam atthi[^322-3]. Dhammesu niccheyya samuggahītan ti. Dhammesū ti dvāsaṭṭhidiṭṭhigatesu. Niccheyyā ti nicchinitvā vinicchinitvā[^322-4] vicinitvā pavicinitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā, odhiggāho[^322-5] vilaggāho varaggāho koṭṭhāsaggāho uccayaggāho samuccayaggāho: idaṃ saccaṃ [^322-6]tathaṃ tacchaṃ[^322-6] bhūtaṃ yāthāvaṃ aviparittan ti gahitaṃ parāmaṭṭhaṃ abhiniviṭṭhaṃ ajjhositaṃ adhimuttaṃ n'; atthi[^322-7], na saṃvijjati, n'; upalabbhati; pahīnaṃ samucchinnaṃ vūpasantaṃ paṭipassaddhaṃ abhabbuppattikaṃ ñāṇagginā daḍḍhan ti, dhammesu niccheyya samuggahītaṃ. Tasmā vivādāni upātivatto ti. Tasmā ti tasmā taṃkāraṇā taṃhetu tappaccayā taṃnidānā[^322-8], diṭṭhikalahāni diṭṭhibhaṇḍanāni diṭṭhiviggahāni[^322-9] diṭṭhivivādāni diṭṭhimedhagāni upātivatto atikkanto samatikkanto vītivatto ti, tasmā vivādāni upātivatto. Na hi seṭṭhato passati dhammam aññan ti: aññaṃ satthāraṃ dhammakkhānaṃ gaṇaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭipadaṃ maggaṃ, aññatra satipaṭṭhānehi, aññatra sammappadhānehi, aññatra iddhippādehi, aññatra indriyehi, aññatra balehi, aññatra bojjhaṅgehi, aññatra ariyā {aṭṭhaṅgikā} maggā, aggaṃ seṭṭhaṃ viseṭṭhaṃ pāmokkhaṃ uttamaṃ pavaraṃ dhammaṃ na passati, na dakkhati, na oloketi, na nijjhā-


<!-- PTS Page 323 -->
<a id="page-323"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 323]*

yati, na upaparikkhatī ti, na hi seṭṭhato passati dhammam aññaṃ. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Na brāhmaṇassa paraneyyam[^323-1] atthi dhammesu niccheyya samuggahītaṃ, tasmā vivādāni upātivatto, na hi seṭṭhato passati dhammam aññan ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.14(=908): Jānāmi passāmi, tath'; eva etaṃ: diṭṭhiyā eke paccenti suddhiṃ, addakkhi[^323-2] ce, [^323-3]kiṃ hi[^323-3] tumassa tena? atisitvā aññena vadanti suddhiṃ.  || Nidd_I.13:14 ||

Jānāmi passāmi tath'; eva etan ti. Jānāmī ti paracittañāṇena vā jānāmi pubbenivāsānussatiñāṇena vā jānāmi. Passāmī ti maṃsacakkhunā vā passāmi, dibbena vā cakkhunā passāmi. Tath'; eva etan ti etaṃ[^323-4] tathaṃ tacchaṃ bhūtaṃ yāthāvaṃ aviparittan ti, jānāmi passāmi tath'; eva etaṃ. Diṭṭhiyā eke paccenti suddhin ti diṭṭhiyā eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ paccenti: sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti diṭṭhiyā eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ paccenti; asassato loko . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato paraṃ {maraṇā}, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti diṭṭhiyā eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ paccentī ti, diṭṭhiyā eke paccenti suddhiṃ. Addakkhi ce, kiṃ hi tumassa tenā? ti. Addakkhī ti paracittañāṇena vā addakkhi, pubbenivāsānussatiñāṇena vā addakkhi; maṃsacakkhunā vā addakkhi, dibbena vā cakkhunā addakkhī ti, addakkhi ce. Kiṃ hi tumassa tenā ti tassa tena dassanena kiṃ kathaṃ dukkhapariññā[^323-5] atthi? Na samudayassa pahānaṃ atthi, na maggabhāvanā atthi,


<!-- PTS Page 324 -->
<a id="page-324"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 324]*

na phalasacchikiriyā atthi, na rāgassa samucchedapahānaṃ atthi, na dosassa samucchedapahānaṃ atthi, na mohassa samucchedapahānaṃ atthi, na kilesānaṃ samucchedapahānaṃ atthi, na saṃsāravaṭṭassa ucchedo[^324-1] atthī ti, addakkhi ce, kiṃ hi tumassa tena? Atisitvā aññena vadanti suddhin ti te titthiyā[^324-2] suddhimaggaṃ visuddhimaggaṃ parisuddhimaggaṃ vodātamaggaṃ parivodātamaggaṃ[^324-3] atikkamitvā samatikkamitvā vītivattitvā, aññatra satipaṭṭhānehi, aññatra sammappadhānehi, aññatra iddhippādehi, aññatra indriyehi, aññatra balehi, aññatra bojjhaṅgehi, aññatra ariyā aṭṭhaṅgikā maggā, suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, evam pi atisitvā aññena vadanti suddhiṃ. Athavā Buddhā ca buddhasāvakā ca paccekabuddhā ca tesaṃ titthiyānaṃ[^324-4] asuddhimaggaṃ avisuddhimaggaṃ aparisuddhimaggaṃ avodātamaggaṃ aparivodātamaggaṃ[^324-5] atikkamitvā samatikkamitvā vītivattitvā, catūhi satipaṭṭhānehi, catūhi sammappadhānehi, catūhi iddhippādehi, pañcahi indriyehi, pañcahi balehi, sattahi bojjhaṅgehi, ariyena aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena, suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ vadanti kathenti bhaṇanti dīpayanti voharantī ti, evam pi atisitvā aññena vadanti suddhiṃ. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Jānāmi passāmi, tath'; eva etaṃ: diṭṭhiyā eke paccenti suddhiṃ, addakkhi ce, kiṃ hi tumassa tena? atisitvā aññena vadanti suddhin ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.15(=909): Passaṃ naro dakkhati[^324-6] nāmarūpaṃ, disvāna vāññassati[^324-7] tāni-m-eva: kāmaṃ bahuṃ passatu appakaṃ vā, na hi tena suddhiṃ kusalā vadanti.  || Nidd_I.13:15 ||


<!-- PTS Page 325 -->
<a id="page-325"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 325]*

Passaṃ naro dakkhati nāmarūpan ti. Passaṃ naro[^325-1] ti paracittañāṇena vā passanto, pubbenivāsānussatiñāṇena vā passanto, maṃsacakkhunā vā passanto, dibbene [^325-2]vā cakkhunā[^325-2] passanto, [^325-3]nāmarūpañ ñeva[^325-3] dakkhati niccato sukhato attato; na tesaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayaṃ vā atthaṅgamaṃ vā assādaṃ vā ādīnavaṃ vā nissaraṇaṃ vā dakkhatī ti, passaṃ naro dakkhati nāmarūpaṃ. Disvāna vāññassati tāni-m-evā ti. [^325-4]Disvā ti[^325-4] paracittañāṇena vā disvā, pubbenivāsānussatiñāṇena vā disvā, maṃsacakkhunā vā disvā, dibbena vā cakkhunā disvā, nāmarūpañ ñeva disvā, ñassati[^325-5] niccato sukhato attato; na tesaṃ dhammānaṃ samudayaṃ vā atthaṅgamaṃ vā assādaṃ vā ādīnavaṃ vā nissaraṇaṃ vā ñassatī[^325-6] ti, disvāna vāññassati tāni-m-eva. Kāmaṃ bahuṃ passatu appakaṃ vā ti kāmaṃ bahukaṃ vā passanto nāmarūpaṃ appakaṃ vā niccato sukhato attato ti, kāmaṃ bahuṃ passatu appakaṃ vā. Na hi tena suddhiṃ kusalā vadantī ti. Kusalā ti ye te khandhakusalā dhātukusalā āyatanakusalā paṭiccasamuppādakusalā satipaṭṭhānakusalā sammappadhānakusalā iddhippādakusalā indriyakusalā balakusalā bojjhaṅgakusalā maggakusalā phalakusalā nibbānakusalā, te kusalā paracittañāṇena vā pubbenivāsānussatiñāṇena vā maṃsacakkhunā vā dibbena vā cakkhunā nāmarūpadassanena suddhiṃ visuddhiṃ parisuddhiṃ muttiṃ vimuttiṃ parimuttiṃ na vadanti, na kathenti, na bhaṇanti, na dīpayanti, na voharantī ti, na hi tena suddhiṃ kusalā vadanti. Ten' āha Bhagavā:

Passaṃ naro dakkhati nāmarūpaṃ, disvāna vāññassati tāni-m-eva: kāmaṃ bahuṃ passatu appakaṃ vā, na hi tena suddhiṃ kusalā vadantī ti.

_________________________________


<!-- PTS Page 326 -->
<a id="page-326"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 326]*

Sn_IV,13.16(=910): Nivissavādī na hi subbināyo[^326-1] pakappitaṃ[^326-2] diṭṭhi purekkharāno, yaṃ nissito, tattha subhaṃ[^326-3] vadāno suddhiṃvado[^326-4] tattha tath'; addasā so.  || Nidd_I.13:16 ||

Nivissavādī na hi subbināyo ti. Sassato loko, idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti nivissavādī; assassato loko[^326-5] . . . pe . . . n'; eva hoti na na hoti tathāgato paraṃ maraṇā idam eva saccaṃ, mogham aññan ti nivissavādī. Na hi subbināyo ti nivissavādī dubbinayo duññāpayo[^326-6] dunnijjhāpayo duppekkhapayo duppasādayo ti, nivissavādī na hi subbināyo. Pakappitaṃ diṭṭhi purekkharāno ti[^326-7] [^326-8]pakappitaṃ abhisaṃkhataṃ saṇṭhapitaṃ[^326-8] diṭṭhiṃ purakkhato purato[^326-9] katvā carati; diṭṭhidhajo diṭṭhiketu diṭṭhādhipateyyo diṭṭhiyā parivārito caratī ti, pakappitaṃ diṭṭhi purekkharāno. Yaṃ nissito, tattha subhaṃ vadāno ti. Yaṃ nissito ti[^326-10] yaṃ satthāraṃ dhammakkhānaṃ gaṇaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭipadaṃ maggaṃ nissito sannissito[^326-11] allīno upagato ajjhosito adhimutto ti, yaṃ nissito. Tatthā ti sakāya diṭṭhiyā, sakāya khantiyā, sakāya ruciyā, sakāya laddhiyā. Subhaṃ vadāno ti subhavādo sobhaṇavādo paṇḍitavādo dhīravādo ñāṇavādo hetuvādo lakkhaṇavādo kāraṇavādo ṭhānavādo sakāya laddhiyā ti, yaṃ nissito tattha subhaṃ vadāno. Suddhiṃvado tattha tath'; addasā so ti. Suddhiṃvado ti


<!-- PTS Page 327 -->
<a id="page-327"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 327]*

suddhivādo visuddhivādo parisuddhivādo vodātavādo pariyodātavādo; athavā suddhidassano visuddhidassano parisuddhidassano vodātadassano pariyodātadassano ti, suddhiṃ-vado. Tatthā ti sakāya diṭṭhiyā, sakāya khantiyā, sakāya ruciyā, sakāya laddhiyā. [^327-1]Tath'; addasā so ti[^327-1] tathaṃ tacchaṃ bhūtaṃ yāthāvaṃ aviparittan ti addassa[^327-2] addakkhi apassi paṭivijjhī ti, suddhiṃvado tattha tath' addasā so. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Nivissavādī na hi subbināyo pakappitaṃ diṭṭhi purekkharāno, yaṃ nissito, tattha subhaṃ vadāno suddhiṃvado tattha tath'; addasā so ti.

_________________________________

Sn_IV,13.17(=911): Na brāhmaṇo kappam upeti saṃkhaṃ na diṭṭhisārī, na pi ñāṇabandhu, ñatvā ca so sammutiyo[^327-3] puthujjā upekkhatī[^327-4], uggahaṇanti-m-aññe[^327-5].  || Nidd_I.13:17 ||

Na brāhmaṇo kappam upeti saṃkhan ti. Nā ti paṭikkhepo. Brāhmaṇo ti sattannaṃ dhammānaṃ bāhitattā brāhmaṇo . . . pe . . . anissito[^327-6] tādi pavuccate[^327-7] brahmā. kappā ti dve kappā, taṇhākappo ca diṭṭhikappo ca . . . pe . . . ayaṃ taṇhākappo . . . pe . . . ayaṃ diṭṭhikappo. Saṃkhā vuccati ñāṇaṃ, yā paññā[^327-8] pajānanā . . . pe[^327-9] . . . amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi. Na brāhmaṇo kappam upeti saṃkhan ti brāhmaṇo saṃkhāya jānitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā: [^327-10]sabbe saṃkhārā aniccā ti, sabbe saṃkhārā dukkhā ti[^327-11] . . . pe . . . yaṃ kiñci samudayadhammaṃ sabban taṃ nirodhadhamman ti saṃkhāya jānitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā[^327-10] taṇhākappaṃ vā diṭṭhi-


<!-- PTS Page 328 -->
<a id="page-328"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 328]*

kappaṃ vā na upeti, na upagacchati, na gaṇhāti, na parāmasati, nābhinivisatī ti, na brāhmaṇo kappam upeti saṃkhaṃ. Na diṭṭhisārī na pi ñāṇabandhū ti tassa dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni pahīnāni samucchinnāni vūpasantāni paṭipassaddhāni abhabbuppattikāni ñāṇagginā daḍḍhāni; so diṭṭhiyā na yāyati, na niyyati, na vuyhati, na saṃhariyati, na pi taṃ diṭṭhigataṃ sārato pacceti paccāgacchatī ti, na diṭṭhisārī. Na pi ñāṇabandhū ti aṭṭhasamāpattiñāṇena vā pañcābhiññāñāṇena[^328-1] vā micchāñāṇena vā taṇhābandhaṃ vā diṭṭhibandhaṃ vā na karoti, na janeti, na sañjaneti, na nibbatteti, nābhinibbattetī ti, na diṭṭhisārī na pi ñāṇabandhu. Ñatvā ca so sammutiyo puthujjā ti. [^328-2]Ñatvā ti[^328-2] ñatvā jānitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā: sabbe saṃkhārā aniccā ti, sabbe saṃkhārā dukkhā ti . . . pe . . . yaṃ kiñci samudayadhammaṃ sabban taṃ nirodhadhamman ti ñatvā jānitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā ti, ñatvā ca so. Sammutiyo ti vuccanti dvāsaṭṭhī diṭṭhigatāni[^328-3]. Puthujjā ti puthujjanehi janitā vā tā sammutiyo ti puthujjā, [^328-4]puthunānājanehi janitā[^328-4] vā tā sammutiyo ti puthujjā ti, ñatvā ca so sammutiyo puthujjā. Upekkhatī uggahaṇanti-m-aññe ti aññe taṇhāvasena diṭṭhivasena gaṇhanti parāmasanti abhinivisanti, arahā upekkhati, na gaṇhāti, na parāmasati, nābhinivisatī ti, upekkhatī uggahaṇanti-m-aññe. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Na brāhmaṇo kappam upeti saṃkhaṃ na diṭṭhisārī, na pi ñāṇabandhu, ñatvā ca so sammutiyo puthujjā upekkhatī, uggahaṇanti-m-aññe ti.

_________________________________


<!-- PTS Page 329 -->
<a id="page-329"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 329]*

Sn_IV,13.18(=912): Visajja[^329-1] ganthāni[^329-2] munīdha loke vivādajātesu na vaggasārī santo asantesu upekkhako so anuggaho, uggahaṇanti-m-aññe.  || Nidd_I.13:18 ||

Visajja ganthāni[^329-3] munīdha loke ti. [^329-4]Ganthā ti[^329-4] cattāro ganthā: abhijjhā kāyagantho[^329-5], byāpādo kāyagantho, sīlabbataparāmāso kāyagantho; idaṃsaccābhiniveso kāyagantho. Attano diṭṭhiyā rāgo, abhijjhā kāyagantho; paravādesu āghāto appaccayo, byāpādo kāyagantho; attano sīlaṃ vā vattaṃ vā sīlabbataṃ vā parāmasati[^329-6], sīlabbataparāmāso kāyagantho; attano diṭṭhi[^329-7], idaṃsaccābhiniveso kāyagantho. Visajjā ti ganthe[^329-8] vossajjitvā vā[^329-9] visajja; athavā ganthe gathite[^329-10] bandhe vibandhe ābandhe lagge laggite palibuddhe bandhane phoṭayitvā vā visajja. [^329-11]Yathā vayham[^329-12] vā rathaṃ vā sakaṭaṃ vā sandamānikaṃ vā sajjaṃ visajjaṃ[^329-13] karonti vikopenti, evam eva ganthe vossajjitvā vā visajja; athavā ganthe gathite [^329-14] bandhe vibandhe ābandhe lagge laggite palibuddhe bandhane phoṭayitvā vā visajja[^329-11]. Munī ti monaṃ vuccati ñāṇaṃ, [^329-15]yā paññā pajānanā[^329-15] . . . pe . . . saṅgajālam aticca so muni. Idhā ti imissā diṭṭhiyā . . . pe . . . imasmiṃ manussaloke ti, visajja ganthāni munīdha loke. Vivādajātesu na vaggasārī ti vivādajātesu[^329-16] [^329-17]sañjātesu nibbattesu abhinibbattesu pātubhūtesu[^329-17], chandāgatiṃ gacchantesu dosāgatiṃ gacchantesu[^329-18] mohāgatim gac-


<!-- PTS Page 330 -->
<a id="page-330"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 330]*

chantesu bhayāgatiṃ gacchantesu na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dosāgatiṃ gacchati[^330-1], na mohāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, na rāgavasena gacchati, na dosavasena gacchati, na mohavasena gacchati, [^330-2]na mānavasena gacchati[^330-2], na diṭṭhivasena gacchati, na uddhaccavasena gacchati, na vicikicchāvasena gacchati, na anusayavasena gacchati, na vaggehi dhammehi yāyati niyyati vuyhati saṃhariyatī ti, vivādajātesu na vaggasārī. Santo asantesu upekkhako so ti. Santo ti rāgassa santattā santo, dosassa santattā santo, mohassa santattā santo . . . pe . . . sabbākusalābhisaṃkhārānaṃ santattā samitattā vūpasamitattā vijjhātattā nibbutattā vigatattā paṭipassaddhattā santo upasanto vūpasanto nibbuto paṭipassaddho ti, santo. Asantesu ti asantesu anupasantesu avūpasantesu anibbutesu apaṭipassaddhesū ti, santo asantesu. Upekkhako so ti arahā chaḷaṅgupekkhāya samannāgato: cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā n'; eva sumano hoti, na dummano, upekkhako viharati sato sampajāno; sotena saddaṃ sutvā . . . pe . . .kālaṃ kaṃkhati bhāvito sudanto[^330-3] ti, santo asantesu upekkhako so. Anuggaho uggahaṇanti-m-aññe ti aññe taṇhāvasena diṭṭhivasena [^330-4]gaṇhanti parāmasanti abhinivisanti[^330-4]; arahā upekkhati, na gaṇhāti, na parāmasati, nābhinivisatī ti, anuggaho uggahaṇanti-m-aññe. Ten'; āha Bhagavā:

Visajja ganthāni munīdha loke vivādajātesu na vaggasārī santo asantesu upekkhako so anuggaho, uggahaṇanti-m-aññe ti.
